moheñco-tarō allatu cintuveḷi nākarikam — moheñco-tarō allatu cintuveḷi nākarikam


					ṭākṭar. mā. irācamāṇikkaṉār





1941





12 jūṉ, 2024 aṉṟu vikkimūlattil iruntu pativiṟakkappaṭṭatu





﻿ulakaḷāviya potuk kaḷa urimam (CC0 1.0)

itu caṭṭa ēṟpuṭaiya urimattiṉ curukkam maṭṭumē. muḻu uraiyai https://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/legalcode eṉṟa mukavariyil kāṇalām.

patippurimai aṟṟatu

inta ākkattuṭaṉ toṭarpuṭaiyavarkaḷ, ulakaḷaḷāviya potup payaṉpāṭṭukku eṉa patippurimaic caṭṭattukku uṭpaṭṭu, taṅkaḷ aṉaittup patippurimaikaḷaiyum viṭuvittuḷḷaṉar.

nīṅkaḷ ivvākkattaip paṭiyeṭukkalām; mēmpaṭuttalām; pakiralām; vēṟu vaṭivamāka māṟṟalām; vaṇikap payaṉkaḷum aṭaiyalām. ivaṟṟukku nīṅkaḷ opputal ētum kōrat tēvaiyillai.

***

﻿patippurai



vaḷattukkum vāṇikattukkum intiyā eppōtum ulaka makkaḷiṉ kaṇṇil niḻalāṭikkoṇṭirukkiṟatu; appaṭiyē kalai, nākarikam mutaliya pala talaiyāṉa ārāyccikaḷukkum ulaka aṟiñarkaḷiṉ uḷḷattai eṉṟum atu kavarntu koṇṭirukkiṉṟatu. intiya nāṭṭiṉ ellai, intiya makkaḷiṉ paṉṉuṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa cāti camaya moḻip pirivukaḷ, avvavaṟṟiṉ paḻaimai perumai, vaḷarcci taḷarccikaḷ mutaliya karuttukkaḷaik kuṟittu mēlaināṭṭavarum kīḻaināṭṭavarum pala kālamākavē ārāyntu varukiṉṟaṉar. kaṭalāḻaṅ kāṇpatum intiy nākarika āḻaṅ kāṇpatum oṉṟu.

cila kālattukkumuṉ varaiyil ārāycciyāḷar intiya nilaikaḷaip paṟṟip paṟpala karuttuṭaiyarāyiruntaṉar. ‘intiya makkaḷil āriya reṉpavarē nākarikamuṭaiyavar; avaratu moḻiyē vaṭamoḻi yeṉpatu; atuvē tamiḻ uṭpaṭa ellā intiya moḻikaḷukkun tāy; āriya nākarikamē ulaka nākarikaṅkaṭku aṭippaṭai’ eṉpaṉa akkaruttukkaḷiṟ cila. ārāycciyāḷar kaṇṇukku vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷē intiyāviṉ talai nūlkaḷ eṉṟu appōtu kāṇappaṭṭu vantamaiyē ataṟkuk kāraṇam.

piṉpu, paṇṭaik kālattil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa, tamiḻ nūlkaḷ cila veḷippaṭalāyiṉa. avaṟṟiliruntu, tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ paḻaimaiyum cevviyum ārāyappaṭṭu, itaṟku āriyamoḻi tāy aṉṟu eṉpatum, immoḻiyāḷariṉ ﻿nākarikam āriya nākarikattiṉum vēṟu eṉpatum, uyarntatu eṉpatum, vēṟu cilavum viḷaṅkiṉa.

itaṟkuḷ nūlārāycciyē yallāmal, ñāla ārāycci, kalveṭṭu ārāycci, akapporu ḷārāycci mutalāṉa palavakai ārāyccikaḷ vaḷarccipeṟalāyiṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ payaṉāka, oru kālattil intiyāviṉ vaṭapakutiyum, imayamalaiyum kaṭaluḷ iruntaṉa; appōtu intiyāviṉ kumarimuṉaikkut teṟkē intumāk kaṭalil kumarik kaṇṭam eṉṟu oru periya nilapparappu iruntatu; ulakattilēyē makkaṭ piṟaviyiṉ tōṟṟam mutalmutal ak kumarik kaṇṭattilēyē uṇṭāyiṟṟu; piṉpu, akkaṇṭam ciṟitu ciṟitākak kaṭaluḷ. āḻntu vaṭakkē imayamalai eḻuntapōtu, ak kaṇṭattilirunta makkaḷ vaṭakkē vantu ulakeṅkum paraviṉar. ippōtu teṉṉintiyāvāka uḷḷa tirāviṭa nilappakuti eññāṉṟum aḻiyāta mikappaḻaiya nilamātaliṉ, vaṭakkilum ulakattilum paraviya makkaḷ ittirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrē yākiṉṟaṉar; imaya malaikku vaṭakkēyum vaṭamēṟkēyum ceṉṟa am muṉṉōrē piṉṉorukāl vēṟṟumoḻiyāḷarpōla māṟi mīṇṭum intiyāvukkuḷ pukuntapōtu, avarkaḷ aṅkaṅkumiruntu vantavarākak kūṟa iṭamāyiṟṟēyaṉṟi vēṟillai; ākavē tirāviṭa nākarikamē ulaka nākarikattukku aṭippaṭai eṉṉum uṇmaikaḷellām tiṭṭamākak kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟaṉa; tamiḻarkaḷiṉ nalviṉaippayaṉāl 2000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa caṅkat tamiḻ nūlkaḷ pala ikkālaṅkaḷil mēlumēlum veḷivantu, avaṟṟiṉ āḻnta ariya ārāyccikaḷālum mēluṇmaikaḷ naṉṟāka valuppeṟalāyiṉa; kumarikkaṇṭa nākarikam ikkāla ﻿nākarikattiṉum uyarntateṉpatu, tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ perumai akkālattilēyē iṉṉum mikuntiruntateṉpatum, tamiḻvaḷartta tamiḻc caṅkaṅkaḷil mutaṟcaṅkam kumarik kaṇṭattiliruntatu eṉpatum, itupōṉṟa pala aruṅkuṟippukaḷellāmum caṅka nūlārāyccikaḷiliruntu veḷippaṭṭaṉa.

eṉiṉum, kāṇṭal, karutal, uraiyaḷavaikaḷil, ārāycci eṉpatu karutalaḷavaiyilum caṅkat tamiḻ nūlkaḷ uraiyaḷavaiyilum aṭaṅkutaliṉ kāṇṭalaḷavaiyiluntakka cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaikkumāyiṉ, intiyā muḻuvatumē oru kālattil tirāviṭa nāṭu; tirāviṭa makkaḷē piṟkālattiṟ palvēṟu intiyak kiḷaiyiṉarāyiṉar tirāviṭamoḻiyē tirintu tirintu palvēṟu intiya moḻikaḷāyiṟṟu: tirāviṭa nākarikamē ulaka nākarikattiṟku aṭippaṭai iṉṉum tirāviṭa nākarikamē uyarntatāka uḷḷatu’ eṉṉum ivvaḷavu periya putai poruḷuṇmaikaḷai mikat teḷivākat tuṇintu koḷvataṟku aiyuṟavillāta nilaippu ēṟpaṭum. oru karuttait tuṇivataṟku iṅṅaṉam mūvakaiyaḷavaikaḷum mutaṉmaiyāṉavai.

‘tamiḻ’ eṉpataip piṟar ‘tirāviṭam’ eṉṟaṉar; tamiḻ makkaḷ aṅkaṅkum pōkkuvaraviṉṟit taṅkiyakālattil avarkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyē palvēṟu vakaiyāṉa intiya moḻikaḷākat tirintu niṉṟaṉa; oṉṟōṭoṉṟu kalantu piṉṉum palavāyiṉa. mikutiyākat tirintuviṭṭavai vaṅkāḷam mutaliya vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ; oraḷavil tirintavai teluṅku mutaliya tirāviṭamoḻikaḷ; moḻitiriyavē tamiḻmakkaḷum avvam moḻik kuriyavarāy ﻿vaṭa intiyarum teṉṉintiyarumākat tirintu pala cātiyiṉarāyiṉar; itaṉāl intiyar yār eṉpatē terintu koḷvatu aritāyiṟṟu.

ittakaiya uṟṟa nērattilētāṉ, intiyāviṉ vaṭamēṟkē cintu āṟṟiṉ karaimaruṅku iṟṟaikku 5000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ ciṟanta tirāviṭa nākarikattōṭu viḷaṅkiyiruntu piṉ maṇmūṭuṇṭa ‘moheñco-tarō’ ‘harappā’ mutaliya peru nakaraṅkaḷ, akaḻntu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. takka nākarikac cāṉṟukaḷ avaṟṟiliruntu nēriṟ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuc ciṟanta ārāycci yaṟiñarkaḷāl naṉku cōtaṉai ceyyappeṟṟaṉa: cintuveḷi nākarikam tirāviṭa nākarikamē eṉpatu kāṇṭalaḷavaiyālum malaiyilakkākat tulaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu. iṟṟaikku 4500 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ intiyā muḻumaiyum tirāviṭamē yeṉṟu viḷakki, nilappaṭap puttakattiṟ (Atlas) paṭamum eḻutappeṟṟirukkiṟatu.

tirāviṭar ippōtu ennilaiyi lirukkiṉṟaṉar. tam muṉ nilaikaḷaic ciṟitēṉum eṇṇip pārppārāyiṉ, intiyāvil cātiyālum moḻiyālum camayattālum pala piriviṉarāy oṟṟumaiyiṉṟi eḻucciyiṉṟi mēlumirukka oruppaṭuvarō! tirāviṭar tam toṉmaiyaiyum vaṉmaiyaiyum ulakaṟiya vaittu vāḻavaṉṟō muṟpaṭuvar! iv vuṇarcciyum muyaṟciyum uṇṭākum poruṭṭē im mohañco-tarō ārāyccikaḷai viḷakkamākat tamiḻmoḻiyil eḻutuvittu veḷiyiṭukiṉṟōm. iv veḷiyīṭu ak karuttaic cevvaṉam niṟaivēṟṟumeṉṟu nampukiṟōm. ﻿

nāṅkaḷ virumpiyataṟku ēṟpa, innūlaip pala talaippukaḷil takutiyākap pākupāṭu ceytu paṉṉūl ārāyccikaḷaiyum naṉṟākak kōvaiceytu porutti ēṟṟa mēṟkōṭ kuṟippukkaḷuṭaṉ mikavum teḷivāka mika eḷiya tamiḻ naṭaiyil nalluḻaippōṭu eḻutiyutaviya itaṉ āciriyar ṭākṭar. mā. irācamāṇikkaṉār, M.A., M.O.L., L.T., Ph.D. avarkaḷiṉ toṇṭu tamiḻakattārāṟ peritum pārāṭṭaṟ pālatākum. acciṭuṅ kālattil, innūlai naṉku pārvaiyiṭṭutaviyavar tarumaiyātīṉa vittuvāṉ, tiruvāḷar. kāḻi. civa. kaṇṇucāmip piḷḷai B.A. avarkaḷāvar. ivvaṟiñariruvarkkum eṅkaḷ naṉṟiyai aṉpuṭaṉ terivittuk koḷḷukiṟōm.

caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattār.





﻿uḷḷuṟai

1. putaiporuḷ ārāycci pakkam 1 - 17

putai poruḷ ārāycci ārāycci avā uṇṭāvatēṉ? ārāyccikkuriya iṭaṅkaḷ - mālṭṭā - ekiptu - pālastīṉam - aciriyā - pāpilōṉiyā - mecoppoṭṭēmiyā - ēlam - innāṉku iṭaṅkaḷilum naṭanta ārāycci ‘ur’ nakaril ārāycci - pāracīkam - intiyāvil ārāycci - cintuveḷi nākarikam.

2. cintuveḷiyiṟ putaiyuṇṭa nakaraṅkaḷ 18- 34

cintu yāṟu - pañcāp maṇṭilam - cintu maṇṭilam - kīrtar malaittoṭar - harappā - moheñco - tarō - stupattaiyuṭaiya maṇmēṭu - 1925-1934 varai naṭaipeṟṟa ārāycci - kuṟippiṭattakka ceytikaḷ - cāṉha tarō - lohāñco - tarō - takañco - tarō - cakpūrpāṉi epiyāl - alimurāt - pāṇṭivāhi amri. kōṭlā nihāṅ kāṉ nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa maṇmēṭukaḷ.

3. piṟamaṇṭilaṅkaḷiṉ putaiporuḷkaḷ 35 - 53

ārāycci iṭaṅkaḷiṟ pāyum āṟukaḷ kaṅkai narumatai tapati yāṟukaḷ - teṉṉāṭu kōtāvari kiruṣṇai - kāviri tāmiraparaṇi - periyāṟu -āṟṟuveḷikaḷil paṇṭai makkaḷ - aikkiya maṇṭilattup putai poruḷkaḷ - keḷacāmpi tōṉṟiya kālam - pīkār maṇṭilttup putaiporuḷkaḷ - naṭu maṇṭilattu veḷḷit ﻿tāmpāḷaṅkaḷ - orissāvil iraṭṭaik kōṭarikaḷ - ṭekkāṉ - ceṉṉai maṇṭilam - makiḻccikkuriya maṇmēṭu - putukkōṭṭaiyil tāḻikaḷ - tiruvitāṅkūr - ṭekkāṉ pakuti taṉittiruntatā? - paṇṭait tamiḻ nakaraṅkaḷ caṅku cāṉṟu pakarum . mēṉāṭṭār muyaṟci nammavar kaṭamai.

4. nakara amaippum āṭci muṟaiyum 54 - 65

maṟaintāriṉ maṇ mēṭukaḷ - āṟṟōram amainta nakaram nakaram amainta iṭam - terukkaḷiṉ amaippu - kālvāy amaippu - cuvarukkuḷ kaḻinīrk kuḻai - mūṭappeṟṟa kālvāykaḷ iṭai iṭaiyē perun toṭṭikaḷ - matakuḷḷa kālvāykaḷ - paṉmuṟai uyarttap peṟṟa kālvāykaḷ - malam kaḻikka otukkiṭam nakara āṭci muṟai avaciyamē ഷിക്കവ vaḷarppatu piṟa nāṭukaḷil illāta aṟputa nakara amaippu.

5. kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ 66 - 80

kaṭṭiṭa amaippu muṟai aṇi aṇiyāṉa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ - parutta cuvarkaḷ neṭuñ cuvarkaḷ - palavakaik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ - eḷiyavar illaṅkaḷ periya muṟṟamuṭaiya illaṅkaḷ pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷ vāḻnta vīṭukaḷ celvar tam ‘ māṭa māḷikaikaḷ - aracaṉatu araṇmaṉaiyō! - taiyalārkkut taṉi aṟaikaḷ - nīrāṭum aṟaikaḷ - camaiyal aṟaikaḷ aṅkāṭiyō? ampalamō? kaḷḷukkaṭaiyō? taṇṇirp pantalō? uṇṭic cālaiyō? - vēṭkōvar viṭutikaḷ! - vīṭṭuvāyilkaḷ - tūṇkaḷ- harappāvil kaṭṭiṭa amaippu iruvakai illaṅkaḷ - peruṅ ﻿kaḷañciyam - toḻilāḷar illaṅkaḷ - vīṭṭiṟku uriya vīṭukaḷē.

6. kiṇaṟukaḷ - ceykuḷam - ceṅkaṟkaḷ 81 - 89

5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa kiṇaṟukaḷ iṉṟum curappuṭaiya kiṇaṟukaḷ - māḷikaikaḷil uḷḷa kiṇaṟukaḷ . kayiṟum uruḷaikaḷum palamuṟai uyarttappaṭṭa kiṇaṟukaḷ - aḻakiya ceykuḷam kuḷattiṉ aṭimaṭṭamum uṭcuvarkaḷum - nilakkīl - kuḷattiṟku vaṭakkē - kuḷattiṟku nīr vacati ceṅkaṟkaḷ - paṇṭai nāṭukaḷ - ceṅkaṟkaḷ cuṭappaṭṭa muṟaikaḷ ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷ - ceṅkaṟkaḷiṉ aḷavukaḷ.

7. vīṭṭup poruḷkaḷum viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷum 90 - 104

vīṭṭukkuriya poruḷkaḷ - maṭpāṇṭa māṇpu palavakai maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ - kaḷimaṇ kalavai vēṭkōvar. uruḷaikaḷ kāḷavāy merukiṭum karuvikaḷ pala niṟap pāṇṭaṅkaḷ niṟaṅkaḷaip pūcuvāṉēṉ? - oviyam koṇṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ - eṅkum illā eḻiluṟu oviyam vēṟu pala oviyaṅkaḷ maṭpāṇṭa vakaikaḷ pūcaikkuriya maṭpāṇṭam kaṉal caṭṭi - kumiḻikaḷ koṇṭa tāḻi - veṇkaṟ pāṉaikaḷ kaippiṭi koṇṭa kalaṉkaḷ - maikkūṭukaḷ purimaṉaikaḷ putaikkappaṭṭa tāḻikaḷ - elippoṟikaḷ pīṅkāṉ ceyyum muṟai ammi, entiram, ural palavakai viḷakkukaḷ - piṟa poruḷkaḷ - viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ iṉṟum ūtum ūtukuḻal talai acaikkum erutu - vaṇṭikaḷ - cokkaṭṭāṉ karuvikaḷ. ﻿

8. kaṇip poruḷkaḷ 105-116

payaṉpaṭṭa kaṇip poruḷkaḷ - poṉṉum veḷḷiyum - cempil īyak kalavai-cempil nikkal kalavai - cempu kalanta maṇ - veṇkalam - veḷḷiyam - kārīyam - makkaḷiṉ mati nuṭpam - vēlai muṟai - cempu, veṇkalap :oruḷkaḷ īṭṭikaḷ - uṭaivāḷkaḷ iṭai vāḷkaḷum kattikaḷum vēlkaḷ - ampu muṉaikaḷ - irampam - uḷikaḷ - tōl cīvum uḷikaḷ - kōṭarikaḷ - vāycci - maḻittaṟ kattikaḷ - uḻu karuvikaḷ tūṇṭil muṭkaḷ piṟa karuvikaḷ - cāṇaikkal eṇ iṭappaṭṭa karuvikaḷ.

9. vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavaikaḷum 117 - 123

maṉitaṉukku muṉ tōṉṟiya vilaṅkukaḷ kālattiṟ kēṟṟa māṟupāṭu - cintup piratēca vilaṅkukaḷ - yāṉai - erutukaḷ - nāykaḷ - pūṉaikaḷ - paṉṟikaḷ - āṭukaḷ - kaḻutaikaḷ - māṉkaḷ . erumaikaḷ - oṭṭakam muyalkaḷ - āmai mutaliyaṉa - paṟavaikaḷ - nākaṅkaḷ.

10. uṇavum uṭaiyum 124 - 130

viḷaiporuḷkaḷ - maruta nilamum nakar vaḷamum pulāl uṇṭa makkaḷ - piṟa uṇavup poruḷkaḷ camaiyaṟ poruḷkaḷ - uṇavu - koṇṭa muṟai - uṭaikaḷ aṇaṅkukaḷiṉ āṭaicciṟappu - muṇṭācu kaṭṭiya makaḷirkāl caṭṭaiyō - kūtta makaḷ.

11. aṇikalaṉkaḷ 131 - 151

aṇikalaṉkaḷ - putaikkappaṭṭa nakaikaḷ putaiyuṇṭa veḷḷikkalaṉkaḷ - putaiyuṇṭa cempukkalaṉkaḷ ﻿kāṭcik kiṉiya kaḻuttumālai maṟṟum iru kaḻuttu mālaikaḷ - harappāvil kiṭaitta kaḻuttu mālaikaḷ - aḻakoḻukum iṭaip paṭṭaikaḷ - tuḷai iṭṭa pēraṟivu - āṟu caraṅkoṇṭa aḻakiya kaiyaṇi - uḷḷē arakkiṭṭa kāppukaḷ palavakaippaṭṭa vaḷaiyalkaḷ - oviyam tīṭṭappaṭṭa vaḷaiyalkaḷ - kāl kāppukaḷ - neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ kātaṇikaḷ - mūkkaṇikaḷ - mōtiraṅkaḷ pottāṉkaḷ - talaināṭākkaḷ - koṇṭai ūcikaḷ - camayattoṭarpuḷḷa patakkam - palavakaik kaṟkaḷ - maṇikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭa vitam oviyam amainta maṇikaḷ - paṭṭai veṭṭappaṭṭa maṇikaḷ kaṟkaḷiṟ pulamai - taṅkak kavacam koṇṭa maṇikaḷ - cippukaḷ - kaṇṇāṭikaḷ makaḷir kūntal oppaṉai - maintar kūntal oppaṉai - mīcai illā āṭavar kaṇṇukku mai - mukattiṟkup poṭi.

12. vāṇipam 152 – 165

uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipam - veḷināṭṭu vāṇipam nanti vaḻipāṭu ekiptiyaruṭaṉ vāṇipam - nīrvaḻi vāṇipam nilavaḻi vāṇipam paṇṭaikkālap palucistāṉam cintuveḷi makkaṭkup piṉ niṟaik kaṟkaḷ niṟai aḷavukaḷ - tarācukaḷ aḷavukkōl muttirai patittal.

13. viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷ - toḻilkaḷ- kalaikaḷ 166-187

palavakai viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷ - vēṭṭaiyāṭal - kōḻi, keḷatāric caṇṭaikaḷ - kottu vēlai - maṭpāṇṭat toḻil - kaltaccar toḻil marat taccar toḻil - kaṉṉāra vēlai - araṇmaṉaiyuḷ kāḷavāykaḷ - poṟkollar toḻil -irattiṉak kal cōtaṉai - cetukkuvēlai ﻿caṅkut toḻil - mīṉ piṭittal - vaṇṭi oṭṭutal - nāvitat toḻil - tōṭṭi vēlai - kāval toḻil - kappal toḻil - payirttoḻil - necavut toḻil - taiyalum piṉṉalum - tanta vēlai - maṇi ceyyun toḻil pāy piṉṉutal - eḻutak kaṟṟavar - vāṇipat toḻil - ciṟpak kalai - oviyak kalai - toṭarntu varum toṉmai nākarikam - icaiyum naṭaṉamum - kaṇitap pulamai - maruttuvak kalaivāṉanūṟ pulamai uṭaṟpayiṟci nakara makkaḷ.

14. camaya nilai 188 - 205

cāṉṟukaḷ - taraip peṇ vaṇakkam - kaviṉpeṟu kaṟpaṉai narapali uṇṭā? - talaiyil viciṟip pākai - civa vaṇakkam liṅka vaḻipāṭu - puttar perumāṉā? kaṇṇapirāṉā? kompuḷḷa teyvaṅkaḷ - nāṉku kait teyvaṅkaḷ - camaṇa camayamum paṇṭaiyatō? nanti vaḻipāṭu oṟṟaik kompu erutu - āṟutalai vilaṅku - katiravak kaṭavuḷ kalappu uruvaṅkaḷ piṟa vilaṅkukaḷ nāka vaṇakkam - puṟā vaṇakkam karuṭa vaṇakkam mara vaṇakkam. - mara tēvataikaḷ - āṟṟu vaṇakkam - pali iṭum paḻakkam - tāyittu aṇital - camayap patakkam - kaṭavuḷ uruvaṅkaḷiṉ ūrvalam - nērttik kaṭaṉ - icaiyum naṭaṉamum - paṭaittal paḻakkam - kōvil vaḻipāṭu - cintu veḷic camayam yātu? - caivattiṉ paḻaimai.

15 iṭutalum cuṭutalum 206-211

cuṭutal - iṭutal - īrāṉiyar paḻakkam - tāḻikaḷ mītu oviyaṅkaḷ mayilkaḷ teyvīkat taṉmai peṟṟavaiyā? ﻿paṟavaimuka maṉita uruvaṅkaḷ - tāḻikaḷuṭ pala poruḷkaḷ - uṭaṉ iṟakkum vaḻakkam - muṭivu.

16. cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷ 212-225

eḻuttu ārāycciyāḷar - paṭikka muṭiyāta eḻuttukaḷ eḻuttukaḷaip peṟṟuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ - eḻutum muṟai eḻuttukaḷāl aṟiyappaṭuvaṉa . rā.) eḻuttukaḷ - eḻuttukaḷ muṭivurai.

17. cintuveḷi makkaḷ yāvar? 226-261

paṇṭai intiya makkaḷ - nīkrōvar . āsṭrēliyar melaṉēviyar - mattiyataraik kaṭaliṉar . maṅkōviya . alpaiṉar moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta elumpuk kūṭukaḷ - kālam yātu im makkaḷ vāḻkkai āriyar - āriyar allātavar (anāriyar) - āriyar - anāriyar pōrkaḷ - inta anāriyar cintuveḷi makkaḷē - cintuveḷi makkaḷ yāvar? - moḻi ārāycci kūṟum uṇmai - piṟa cāṉṟukaḷ - intiya makkaḷ paṟṟiya aṟikkai neṭuṅkālam vāḻnta makkaḷ - muṭivurai.

itaṉkaṇ eṭuttāḷap peṟṟa mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷ 262





﻿moheñco-tarō allatu

cintu veḷi nākarikam

1 . putaiporuḷ ārāycci



putaiporuḷ ārāycci

mikap paḻaiya maṉitaṉ vīṭu kaṭṭa aṟiyātu malai muḻaikaḷil vāḻntu vantāṉ. appōtu avaṉ iyalpākak kiṭaitta kaṟkaḷaiyē taṉ karuvikaḷākak koṇṭāṉ. avaṉ nāḷaṭaivil ulaka iyalpai kūṟupāṭṭai aṟintu, cempu, veṇkalam, irumpu mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭāṉ: avaṟṟaik koṇṭu taṉakkuvēṇṭiya palavakaipporuḷkaḷaic ceytu koṇṭāṉ.[1] āṟṟaṅkaraikaḷilum camaveḷikaḷilum iyalpākak kiṭaitta kaḷimaṇṇaik koṇṭu vīṭukaḷai amaittāṉ. āṟṟu ōraṅkaḷil ōṅki vaḷarnta kōraip pullaikkoṇṭu kūraikaḷai amaittāṉ taṉ vāḻviṟkēṟṟa pāṇṭaṅkaḷai maṇṇālēyē ceytu koṇṭāṉ; taṉ. aṟivukku eṭṭiya aḷavil, tāṉ. pēcivanta moḻiyaip pulappaṭuttac cittira eḻuttukkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāṉ; av veḻuttukkaḷaip paṇpaṭuttappaṭṭa kaḷimaṇ taṭṭukaḷ mītum



* * *



﻿1. The Indus Valley Civilization. ↑ 2. ikkālam kaṟkālam eṉappaṭum. 3. ikkālaṅkaḷ muṟaiyē cempuk kālam, veṇkalak kālam, irumpuk kālam eṉappaṭum.



maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītum cuṇṇāmpuk kaṟkaḷāl āṉa pāttiraṅkaḷ mītum piṟavaṟṟiṉmītum poṟittu vaittāṉ; akkāla aṟivunilaikku ēṟṟavāṟu kaṟkaḷaikkoṇṭum paccai maṇṇaik koṇṭum, piṉṉarc cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷaik koṇṭum. taṉ maṉattiṟkiṉiya kōvilkaḷai amaittuk kaṭavuḷarai uṇṭākkiṉāṉ;tāṉ vāḻnta maruta nilattai uḻutu payir ceyyalāṉāṉ; taṉakku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷai vaittukkoṇṭu eñciyavaṟṟai, avai kiṭaikkappeṟāta malai nāṭukaḷilum pālaivaṉaṅkaḷilum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum irunta makkaṭku utavi, taṉṉiṭam illāmal avarkaḷiṭamē ciṟappāka irunta pal poruḷkaḷaip paṇṭamāṟṟākap peṟṟu vāḻntu vantāṉ. ippaḻakkamē nāḷaṭaivil vāṇipamāka vaḷarntōṅkiyatu. iṅṅaṉam oru pakutiyil vāḻum makkaḷ maṟṟap pakutikaḷil vāḻum mkkaḷōṭu vāṇipam ceyyuṅkāl irutiṟattārum avvappōtu kalantu uṟavāṭa vāyppukaḷ uṇṭātal iyalpu. akkūṭṭuṟaviṉāl oru cārāriṭam kāṇappaṭum nalliyalpukaḷ palavaṟṟaippiṟitoru cārār kaikkōṭal iyalpu:irucārārum nāḷaṭaivil peṇ koḷvatilum koṭuppatilum īṭupaṭṭuk kalappuṟutalum uṇṭu. ikkalappiṉāl iruvēṟupaṭṭa paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, kalaikaḷ, mata uṇarccikaḷ iṉṉa piṟavum kalap puṟutalum iyalpē. ikkalappu nākarikattiṉiṉṟum teḷivupeṟṟa putiyatōr nākarikam tōṟṟam eṭuttalum uṇṭu. iḵtu ulaka iyaṟkai iṅṅaṉam ulakiṉ pala pakutikaḷil makkaṭ kalappum nākarikak kalappum paṇṭaikkālattilēyē uṇṭāyiṉa.

iṅṅaṉam ulakattiṉ pala pakutikaḷil vāḻnta paṇṭai makkaḷ, taṉittum kalantum palavakai nākarikaṅkaḷai vaḷarttu vantaṉar. kaṟkāla makkaḷ cempaip payaṉpaṭuttat toṭaṅkiyatum, kallāl ceyyappaṭṭa pala poruḷkaḷaip puṟakkaṇittu viṭṭaṉar; payaṉaṟṟa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷai vilakkiṉar. iṅṅaṉam nīkkappaṭṭa apporuḷkaḷ, kavaṉippāraṟṟu nāḷaṭaivil maṇṇuḷ putaiyuṇṭaṉa.kaṟkāla makkaḷ maṇṇāl tāḻikaḷaic ceytu iṟantavar uṭampukaḷai avaṟṟuḷ vaittu ഥങ്ങജൂൺ putaittu vantaṉar. teṉ intiyāvil ivvakait tā, kaḷ putukkōṭṭaiyil ērāḷamākak kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. paṇṭai makkaḷ nalliṭaṅkaḷ tēṭi aṭikkaṭi iṭam māṟit tirintaṉar. ātaliṉ, āṅkāṅkup paḻutuṟṟa poruḷkaḷaip pōṭṭup pōyiṉar. apporuḷkaḷ nāḷaṭaivil maṇṇuḷ maṟaippuṇṭaṉa. paḻaṅkāla maṉṉar uṭalaṅkaḷ putaikkap peṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil kaṟkōpuraṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mītu akkālac cittira eḻuttukkaḷum pala cittiraṅkaḷum poṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. āṟṟu ōraṅkaḷilum kaṭaṟkaraiyai aṭuttum oḻuṅkāṉa muṟaiyil aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷai amaittuk koṇṭu vāḻnta cempu - veṇkalak kāla makkaḷ muṟaiyē āṟṟu veḷḷattiṟkum kaṭaliṉ kontaḷippiṟkum añci annakaraṅ *տոպմ tam poruḷkaḷaiyum viṭṭuviṭṭu ōṭiviṭṭamaiyum uṇṭu. vēṟu cila iṭaṅkaḷ, vēṟṟu makkaḷ paṭaiyeṭuppukku añcit tuṟakkappaṭṭirukkalām. cila nakaraṅkaḷ erimalaikaḷiṉ cēṭṭaiyāl aḻivuṟṟu irukkalām. cila nakaraṅkaḷ maṇ māriyāl aḻintatuṇṭu. iṅṅaṉam palvēṟu kāraṇaṅkaḷāl appaṇṭai makkaḷ vāḻntu vanta kirāmaṅkaḷum nakaraṅkaḷum kaiviṭappaṭṭu, nāḷaṭaivil maṇ mūṭappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa. pala nakaraṅkaḷ kaṭaluḷ āḻntaṉa. pala āṟṟaṅkaraikaḷiṉ aṭiyil putaiyuṇṭu viṭṭaṉa. pala camaveḷikaḷil maṇ mēṭiṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.

iṅṅaṉam maṇ mēṭiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷait tōṇṭip pārttu, avviṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappeṟum palatiṟappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷai. ārāyntu avaṟṟaippaṟṟiya uṇmaic ceytikaḷaiyum, avaṟṟaip payaṉpaṭuttiya paṇṭai makkaḷaippaṟṟiya cuvai payakkum ceytikaḷaiyum aṟiyum muyaṟciyē. putaiporuḷ ārāycci eṉappaṭum. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, palaporuḷkaḷaic ceytu nākarikattait tōṟṟuvitta paḻaṅkāla makkaḷatu varalāṟṟaik kaṇṭaṟivatē putaiporuḷ ārāycciyākum.’[1] ivvārāyccikkup pakkapalamākac ciṟappuṟṟu iruppavai maṇṭaiyōṭṭaic cōtikkum kalai, vilaṅkukaḷiṉ elumpukaḷaic cōtikkum kalai, ‘nilanūl aṟivu’ eṉṉum mūṉṟākum.

ārāycci avā uṇṭāṉatēṉ?

mikka paḻaṅkāla makkaḷ taṅkaḷ varalāṟukaḷai eḻuti vaittirāviṭiṉum, pala iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum camātikaḷ mītum kaṟkampaṅkaḷ mītum appaḻaṅkāla moḻikaḷil pala ceytikaḷ eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avarkaḷaip paṟṟiya cila kuṟippukaḷ ceviyāṟāka vantu ulakattup paḻaiya nūlkaḷ eṉṟu karutappaṭum piṟkālattut tōṉṟiya paipiḷ, rikvētam, purāṇaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. appaḻaṅkāla makkaḷ naṭattiya pōrkaḷ, kaṭal kerntaḷippu, pala nakaraṅkaḷ aḻintmai pōṉṟa cila kuṟippukaḷ piṟkāla makkaḷiṉ kavaṉattai īrttaṉ. kaṭal kontaḷippāl uṇṭāṉa tīmai paipiḷilum rikvētattilum purāṇaṅkaḷilum īḻa nāṭṭavar paḻaiya nūlkaḷilum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paipiḷ, rikvētam, purāṇaṅkaḷ mutaliya nūlkaḷil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷa aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷ pala iṉṟu kāṇappaṭavillai. pala nakaraṅkaḷ teyvaṅkaḷatu cīṟṟattukkuṭpaṭṭu erikkappaṭṭum kaṭaluḷ āḻttappaṭṭum viṭṭaṉa eṉṉum kuṟippukkaḷaip paṭikkum aṟivuḷḷa maṉitaṉ, avai iruntaṉavākak kūṟappaṭum iṭaṅkaḷait tēṭik kaṇṭaṟiya avāvutal iyalṭēyaṉṟō? ‘hōmar’ eṉṉum kirēkka kaviñar eḻutiyuḷḷa ‘iliyaṭ’ eṉṉum nūlil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷa ṭrāy nakaram eṅkē? appoṉṉakaraik kaṇṭu, ataṉ amaippaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum aṟiyiṉ, appaḻaṅkāla nakarattil vāḻnta makkaḷaippaṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷai aṟital kūṭumaṉṟō? eṉṟu aṟivuṭaiyavar eṇṇutal iyalpēyākum. nācarēttūr aṭikaḷ vāḻntu pala aṟputac ceyalkaḷaic ceyta iṭaṅkaḷ paipiḷil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, avviṭaṅkaḷ iṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟila. avai maṇmūṭi iruttal vēṇṭum. aṅkuc ceṉṟu maṇmēṭukaḷaik kaṇṭaṟintu, avaṟṟait tōṇṭi, uṇmaiyai uṇartal vēṇṭum eṉṉum avā aṟiñar uḷḷattē vērūṉṟutal iyalpu tāṉē!

intiya nāṭṭup paḻaiya nūlkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa nālantā, takṣacīlam, kōcāmpi, pāṭalipuram, koṟkai, kāyal, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam, vañcimānakaram, uṟaiyūr mutaliya varalāṟṟuc ciṟappuṭaiya nakaraṅkaḷai akaḻntu kaṇṭu, nam muṉṉōraippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷai aṟintu iṉpuṟa nām virumputal iyalpaṉṟō? “tirāviṭa moḻikaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa ‘kōṇṭ’ eṉpatu mattiya mākāṇattilum ‘kūy’ eṉpatu ceṉṉai mākāṇattiṉ vaṭa kōṭiyilum, ‘kuruk’ eṉpatum ‘irāj mahāl’ eṉpatum vaṅkāḷa mākāṇattilum, pala tirāviṭac coṟkaḷaiyuṭaiya ‘prāhi’ eṉpatu palucistāṉattilum iruttalaik kāṇiṉ, vaṭa intiyāvil mikap paḻaiya kālattil mūlat tirāviṭamoḻi pēcappaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum. prāhiyil uḷḷa tirāviṭac coṟkaḷai nōkkuṅkāl, palucistāṉattiṟku aṇmaiyil tirāviṭar iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṉum eṇṇam tōṉṟukiṟatu”[2] eṉpataip paṭikkum poḻutu, tirāviṭa makkaḷākiya nām, nam muṉṉōr cintu veḷiyil vāḻntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇukiṟōm. uṭaṉē, avarkaḷ vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷ akappaṭāvō? avai eṉṉa vāyiṉa? maṇṇuḷ maṟaintaṉavō? avartam paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ nammiṭam iṉṟu iruppavaitāmā? avaṟṟai aṟiya vēṇṭumē eṉap palavāṟu puttuṇarcci peṟukiṉṟōm. ivvuṇarcci tōṉṟaṟku aṟiñarkaḷ aṉpuṭaṉ eḻuti vaittuḷḷa ariya nūlkaḷē kāraṇamākum.

nām iṉṟu koṇṭuḷḷa paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ tiṭīreṉa vantirā. avaṟṟuḷ cila mikap paḻaiya kālantoṭṭē vantiruttal vēṇṭum. oru vakai nākarikam tōṉṟum poḻutu aḵtu, ataṟku muṟpaṭṭa nākarikattiṉ oru pakutiyait taṉṉakattuk koṇṭē tōṟṟameṭukkum. inta uṇmai, varalāṟṟiṉ uyir nāṭiyākum. varalāṟu eṉpatu eṉṟum toṭarpuṟṟu varum iyakkamākum. ‘ittuṭaṉ ellām muṭivaṭaintuviṭṭaṉa; iṉinammaiccārvaṉa evaiyum illai’ eṉṟu enta iṭattilum enta nērattilum kūṟa, uṇmai varalāṟu iṭamtarātu. iṉṟaiya airōppā, paḻaiya urōmappēraraciliruntu tōṉṟiyatākum. anta urōmap pēraracum aleksāṇṭaratu peru veṟṟiyiliruntu piṟantatākum. aleksāṇṭaratu pēravā, pāracīka maṉṉarkaḷiṉ taṉiyaracu uṇarcciyiṉiṉṟum piṟantatākum. pāracīka vēntarkaḷ ataṉai āciriyarpāl kaṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar. iṅṅaṉam uḷḷa pala uṇmaikaḷaik kāṇum pōtu, varalāṟu eṉṟum toṭarpuṭaiyatē eṉṉum uṇmaic ceytiyai uḷḷavāṟu uṇaralām”.[3]

ivvuṇarcci nam uḷḷattē eḻumāyiṉ, namatu varalāṟṟut toṭarpaik kaṇṭaṟiya nām avāvi niṟṟal iyalpē ākum. avā nāḷaṭaivil ceyalil muṭikiṉṟatu. aḵtāvatu, putaintu kiṭakkum iṭaṅkaḷait tōṇṭi maṇ mūṭuṇṭa nākarikattai aṟiya maṉam nāṭukiṉṟatu: ceyalāṟṟip pala uṇmaikaḷaik kaṇṭaṟintu taḷarntuḷḷa varalāṟṟuc caṅkiliyai muṟukkuṭaiyatāka ākkukiṉṟatu.

ārāyccikkuriya iṭaṅkaḷ

kaṟkāla makkaḷum cempu veṇkala irumpuk kāla makkaḷum kuṭiyēṟi vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷ ellām ārāyccikku uriyaṉavē ākum. ammakkaḷ perumpālum āṟṟup pāyccal uḷḷa camaveḷikaḷiṟṟāṉ vāḻntu vantārkaḷ. malaippāṅkāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil peritum kaṟkāla maṉitar nākarikac ciṉṉaṅkaḷē mikkirukkum. camaveḷikaḷil piṟkāla makkaḷ ciṉṉaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. ikkuṟippaik koṇṭum, nām mēṟkūṟiya paipiḷ mutaliya paḻaiya nūlkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷaik koṇṭum ārāyntāl, ovvoru kaṇṭattilum ārāyccikku uriya iṭaṅkaḷ iruttal uṇmai. avaṟṟai aṟiñar kaṇṭaṟintu varukiṉṟaṉar. īṇṭu nām eṭuttukkoṇṭa poruḷukku uriyaṉavum ataṟkuc cārpāṉavaiyumāka iruppavai mattiyataraik kaṭaliliruntu intiyā aṭaṅka uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷ ākum. avai mālṭṭā, ekiptu, pālastīṉam, aciriyā, pāpilōṉiyā, ēlam, pāracīkam, cintup piratēcam, kaṅkaic camaveḷi, ṭekkāṉ, teṉ intiyā eṉpaṉa ākum. ivai paṟṟiya iṉṟiyamaiyāta kuṟippukkaḷai maṭṭum īṇṭuk kāṇpōm.

taṭitta eḻuttukkaḷ

mālṭṭā eṉpatu mattiya taraik kaṭalil iṭṭālikkum vaṭa āprikkak karaikkum iṭaiyil uḷḷa paḻantīvu. itaṉ talainakaram ‘lāveṭṭā’ eṉpatu. iḵtu, ‘araṇmaṉaikaḷaik koṇṭuḷḷa nakaram’ eṉappaṭum. itil uḷḷa pala māṭa māḷikaikaḷ jeruslēttiṉ makāṉāṉa jāṉ eṉpāruṭaiya vīrarkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭavai. itu mikap paḻaiya kāla mutalē ciṟappuṟṟa tīvākum. itaṉ oru puṟam ārāycci vēlai naṭaipeṟṟatu. ataṉ palaṉāka, ārāycciyāḷar karuttaik kavarnta maṭkalaṅkaḷum palavakai maṇikaḷum veḷippaṭṭaṉa. ārāycci muṟṟuppeṟavillai. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu palavakaiccittiraṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pala pāṇṭaṅkaḷ uṭainta nilaiyil kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avai, oṟṟaik kaippiṭiyum iraṭṭaik kaippiṭiyum uṭaiyaṉavāka iruntaṉa. cuṇṇāmpuk kallālāṉa liṅkam oṉṟu kiṭaittatu.[4] mālṭṭā nākarikam veṇkalak kālattatu eṉṟu ārāycci aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

ekiptu

āprikkāviṉ vaṭa pakutiyil nīla āṟu pāyum marutanila veḷiyē ekiptu eṉpatu. ulakattil eṅkumē iruntirāta mika uyarnta nākarikam aṅku irunta teṉpataṟku uriya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ mikap pala uṇṭu. āyiṉum, avai ēṟakkuṟaiya nūṟāṇṭukaṭku muṉ varai kavaṉikkappaṭṭila. keyrō mutal ‘laksar’ varaiyuḷḷa 960 ki. mī. tolaivuvarai paḻutupaṭṭa paṇṭaikkālattuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ paṇṭai aracar kallaṟaikaḷ pala kōvilkaḷ pala kal mēṭukaḷ pala uṭainta cilaikaḷ pala ki. mu. 4000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉum piṉṉum irunta ekiptiya aracarkaḷ taṅkaṭkeṉak kaṭṭiya kallaṟaikaḷē piramiṭkōpuraṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa. avai cumār 80 ākum. avaṟṟuḷ mikap periyatu 6,37,600 ca.ce.mī parappum 14,400 ce.mī. uyaramum uṭaiyatu. atil uḷḷa kaṟkaḷ 70 laṭcam ṭaṉ niṟaiyuḷḷavai nāṉku allatu aintu aṭukkukaḷaiyuṭaiya 22 āyiram vīṭukaḷaik koṇṭa nakaram oṉṟaik kaṭṭi muṭikkap pōtumāṉavai; eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar. ivviyappūṭṭum piramiṭ kōpuraṅkaḷaik kaṭṭiya pēraracar tam nākarikattai eṉṉeṉpatu ārāycciyiṟ kiṭaitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; pala niṟaṅkaḷ pūcappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; pāṉaikaḷum piṟavum perumpālum taṭṭaiyāṉa aṭiyuṭaiyaṉavākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; piramiṭ kōpuraṅkaḷilum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum, ap paṇṭai makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya cittira eḻuttukkaḷum vilaṅku paṟavaikaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ārāycciyāḷar avaṟṟaip pala āṇṭukaḷ muyaṉṟu paṭittu muṭittaṉar; ataṉ piṉṉarē ekiptiṉ varalāṟṟai oḻuṅkāka eḻutalāyiṉar.

pālastīṉam

ippakuti, ulakattiluḷḷa kiṟistava camayattiṉar aṉaivarum pōṟṟattakkatākum. ātaliṉ, ippakutiyil uḷḷa ārāyccikkuriya maṇmēṭukaḷait tōṇṭi uṇmaic ceytikaḷ palavaṟṟai aṟiya avākkoṇṭa aṟiñar kuḻu ārāycci vēlaiyait toṭaṅkiyatu. akkuḻu piriṭṭiṣ aṟiñar cilaraiyum amerikka aṟiñar cilaraiyum koṇṭatākum. paipiḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa nakaraṅkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittalē ivvaṟiñarkaḷiṉ mukkiya nōkkamākum. ivarkaḷ ārāycci naṭattiyatiṉ payaṉāyp pala nakaraṅkaḷ veḷippaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cotam (Sodum), komorāh (Gomorrah), jerikō (Jericho), mamri (Mamre), kirjāt cepar (Kiriath - Sepher) mutaliyaṉa kuṟippiṭattakkaṉa. paipiḷil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷ innakaraṅkaḷil iruttalaik kaṇṭu aṟiñarkaḷ viyappum makiḻcciyum eytiṉar. cila nakaraṅkaḷil irunta cavappeṭṭikaḷuḷ cippi ōṭukaḷ, maṇikaḷ, pala niṟaṅkoṇṭa kaṇṇāṭikaḷ, vilai uyarnta kaṟkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl āṉa mālaikaḷ mutaliyaṉa kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟōṭu mālaikaḷil iṇaikkappaṭum patakkaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avai muttuc cippi, elumpu, muttukkaḷ paṉṉiṟak kaṇṇāṭit tuṇṭukaḷ ivaṟṟaik koṇṭu ceyyap paṭṭavai ākum palavakai maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. kal entiraṅkaḷum, kalvaṅkaḷum kuḻavikaḷum, vēṟupala uruṇṭaik kaṟkaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa.[5]

aciriyā

innilappakuti mecoppoṭṭēmiyāvukkuk kiḻakkilum kurtistāṉattukku mēṟkilum pāpilōṉiyāvukku vaṭakkilum armīṉiyāvukkut teṟkilum amaintiruppatu itaṉ talainakaram acur eṉpatu. innāṭṭiṉ mukkiya nakaraṅkaḷ mōcul, niṉaveh, kalā, recaṉ mutaliyaṉavām. innāṭu mikka ceḻippuḷḷatu. iṅkuk kaḷimaṇ mikutiyākak kiṭaittamaiyāl, paṇṭai aciriyar, ceṅkaṟkaḷāl vīṭukaḷaik kaṭṭikkoṇṭu uyarnta nākarikattil vāḻntu vantaṉar. ceṅkaṟkaḷilum karuṅkaṟkaḷilum innāṭu pāpilōṉiyāvaiviṭac ciṟappuṭaiyatē ākum. mēṟ kuṟippiṭṭa nakaraṅkaḷ oru kālattil mikac cīrum ciṟappum peṟṟu iruntu, piṉṉar maṇmēṭukaḷāy maṅkip pōṉavai ākum.

pāpilōṇā

innilappakuti yūprēṭis, ṭaikris yāṟukaḷukku iṭaiyil uḷḷa marutanilap pakutiyākum. iṅkuc ciṟanta kaḷimaṇ kiṭaittamaiyāl, iṅku vāḻnta paṇṭai makkaḷ maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ ceyvatilum ceṅkaṟkaḷ ceyvatilum ciṟappuṟṟiruntaṉar. āṟṟōraṅkaḷil vaḷarnta kōrai kūraiyākavum kūṭaiyākavumpāyākavum payaṉpaṭṭatu. iṅku viḷai poruḷkaḷ atikamākum. pāpilōṉiyar, ivai nīṅkalāka uḷḷa kaṟkaḷ, tēkkamaram, perṉ veḷḷi mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaic cuṟṟup pakkattu nāṭukaḷiliruntu varavaḻaittuk koṇṭaṉar. eṉavē, avarkaḷ ciṟanta vāṇipattiṟamai uṭaiyavarkaḷāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. mecoppoṭṭēmiyā

innilappakuti skāṭlaṇṭ aḷavuṭaiyatu; ṭaikris yāṟṟukku mēṟkē amaintiruppatu itaṉ perum pakuti ciṟu malait toṭarkaḷaik koṇṭa pālai nilamākum. ‘kappūr, palīk’ eṉṉum yāṟukaḷ innāṭṭiṟ pāyntu yūprēṭis yāṟṟil kalakkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ karaiyōraṅkaḷil maṭṭumē payir ceyya vacati uṇṭu. itaṟku vaṭakiḻakkē koḷḷaiyaṭikkum malai nāṭṭār vacittu vantaṉar. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, mecop poṭṭēmiyā ciṟitu vaḷamuṭaiyatu - peritum vaḷamillātatu; ciṟitaḷavu payir uṭaiyatu - peritaḷavu pāṟai uṭaiyatu; cila iṭaṅkaḷil nīr vacati uṭaiyatu pala iṭaṅkaḷil nīr vacati aṟṟatu.

ēlam

ēlam eṉpatu pāpilōṉiyāvukkuk kiḻakkē uḷḷa ciṟiya nilapparappākum; ciṟitaḷavu malaippakuti uṭaiyatu. itaṉ talainakaram cucā. maṟṟoru ciṟanta nakaram ‘aṉcaṉ’ eṉpatu. ip pakutiyil vāḻnta makkaḷ aṭikkaṭi vaṭakkē irunta aciriyarōṭu pōriṭṭu vantaṉar. aciriyar ki.mu.650-il ippakutiyil irunta makkaḷai veṉṟu aṭippaṭuttiṉar. aciriyaratu pēraracu vīḻnta piṟaku ēlam, pāracīkap pēraracil kalantu maṟaintatu.

innāṉku iṭaṅkaḷilum naṭanta ārāycci

in nāṉku nilap pakutikaḷilum paṟpala iṭṅkaḷil maṇmēṭukaḷ iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa. mēṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ mutaṉ mutalāka aciriyāvil uḷḷa mōcul eṉṉum iṭattiṟku arukil irunta ‘niṉaveh’ eṉṟa paḻaiya nakarattaiyum pāpilōṉiyāvil ‘hillah’ eṉṉum iṭattiṟku arukil irunta maṇmēṭṭaiyum veṭṭi ārāycci naṭattiṉar. iv vārāycciyiṉāl aciriyaraip paṟṟiyum pāpilōṉiyaraip paṟṟiyum ivviruvarkkum muṟpaṭṭa cumēriyaraip paṟṟiyum cemiṭṭiyaraippaṟṟiyum pala ceytikaḷ veḷippōntaṉa. [6]aṅkuk kiṭaitta eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷaic cōtittup pārtta eṭvarṭ hiṅks (Edward Hicks) eṉpavar, ‘pāpilōṉiyar kaiyāṇṭa eḻuttukkaḷ avarkaḷuṭaiyaṉa alla’ eṉṟu karutiṉār. avveḻuttukkaḷai naṉku ārāynta āpparṭ eṉpār. ‘ivai cumēriyaruṭaiyaṉa’ eṉṟu muṭivu kūṟiṉār. mēlum ārāycci naṭattiyatil, pāpilōṉiyarum aciriyarum taṅkaṭku muṟpaṭṭa cumēriyariṭamiruntē kalai, matam, moḻi mutaliya ellām peṟṟaṉar eṉpataṟku uriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. 1855 il ṭeylar eṉpavar naṭattiya ārāycciyil pāpilōṉiyāvil eritu (Eridu) ur (Ur) eṉṉum cumēriya nakaraṅkaḷ veṭṭi eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. 1874 il nikaḻttappaṭṭa ārāycciyiṉ piṟakē cumēriyaruṭaiya varalāṟu, kalaikaḷ, moḻi mutaliyaṉa naṉku terintaṉa. ki.mu.2800 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa ciṟpaṅkaḷum mikap paḻaiya cācaṉaṅkaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. piṟaku jermāṉiyar pala maṇmēṭukaḷait tōṇṭi ārāycci naṭattiṉar. acur nakaram tōṇṭappaṭṭuc cumēriyaraip paṟṟiya pala uṇmaikaḷ veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. piṉṉar amerikkar cila maṇmēṭukaḷai ārāyat toṭaṅkiṉar. 1889il nippūr tōṇṭappaṭṭatu. atil kāṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ kōvilum, pala kālaṅkaḷaic cērnta 50,000 cācaṉaṅkaḷum kiṭaittaṉa. ceṉṟa airōppiyap pōrukkup piṉṉar eritu, ur, kiṣ, tal, asmar, kapjē mutaliya cumēriyar nakaraṅkaḷ ārāyccikku uṭpaṭṭaṉa. avviṭaṅkaḷilirunta maṇmēṭukaḷil pala aṭukkukaḷaiyuṭaiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ki.mu.3000kku muṟpaṭṭa nākarikattai uṭaiya cumēriyaruṭaiya ciṟappukaḷ kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭaṉa.

cumēriyar cittira eḻuttukkaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. nīṇṭa uruṇṭai muttiraikaḷai upayōkittaṉar; kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷukku aṭippaṭaiyākak karuṅkallaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. 70 aṭi uyarattil ceykuṉṟukaḷ amaittu avaṟṟiṉmītu ciṟiyakōvilkaḷaik kaṭṭiṉar; tarai mītu periya kōvilkaḷaik kaṭṭiṉar; avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil cittiraṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai. cumēriyar mikap paḻaiya kālattil āṭṭut tōlāṭai uṭuttiyiruntaṉar; kalliṉ mītu cetukkum vēlaiyil ciṟantiruntaṉar. itaiyum vaḻipāṭṭukkuriya ceyalkaḷaiyum ceyyum poḻutu perumpālum āṭaiyiṉṟi iruntaṉar eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

cumēriyar vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷ mitu cittiraṅkaḷ tīṭṭak kaṟṟiruntaṉar; erutukaḷum kaḻutaikaḷum pūṭṭappeṟṟa vaṇṭikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; cempu mutaliya poruḷkaḷāl āṉa - āyutaṅkaḷai vaittiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ eṇṇuvatil oru kuṟippiṭṭa muṟaiyaik kaiyāṇṭu vantaṉar;[7] orē kuṟippiṭṭa vaṭivamuḷḷa ceṅkaṟkaḷaiyē ceytuvantaṉar;[8] iṟantavarkaḷait tāḻikaḷiṟ putaittuvantaṉar. avarkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭu vanta teyvaṅkaḷ palavākum. orē kōvilil payirkkaṭavuḷāṉa apu, akkaṭavuḷiṉ maṉaiviyāṉa iṉaṉṉā, avartam makaṉ āka immūṉṟu teyvaṅkaḷum vaittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa.[9] ak kōviliṉ aṭiyil cuṇṇāmpuk kallālum paḷiṅkālum āṉa 12 teyvaṅkaḷiṉ cilaikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avai or aṭi uyaram mutal iraṇṭarai aṭivarai vēṟupaṭṭiruntaṉa. iccumēriyartam moḻi turukki tamiḻ pōṉṟa oṭṭu[10] moḻiyākum.[11]

‘ur’ nakaril ārāycci

inta nakaram cumēriya nakaraṅkaḷuḷ mikac ciṟantatākum. innakarattil naṭaipeṟṟa ārāycciyiṉ palaṉāka ariya kuṟippukaḷ pala veḷippaṭṭaṉa. innakaril tiṅkaṭ kaṭavuḷ tam maṉaiviyōṭu eḻuntaruḷiyuḷḷa kōvil oṉṟum nilamakaḷ kōvilum katiravaṉ kōyilum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. nilamakaḷ kōvilil cempālāya erutum paṟavaiyum kiṭaittaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ kālam ki.mu.4300 ākum. tēr, vīṇai, irampaṅkaḷ, uḷikaḷ, malar ēntum cāṭikaḷ, poṉṉālāṉa ariya poruḷkaḷ, mutaliyaṉa eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ kallaṟaiyum araciyiṉ kallaṟaiyum ārāyccikku uṭpaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉaip putaitta iṭattil aṭutta piṟaviyil avaṉōṭu iruppataṟkeṉṟu āṭavar peṇṭir āka 60 pēr paliyiṭappaṭṭaṉar eṉpatum, cila erutukaḷ paliyiṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatum aṅkirunta cittiraṅkaḷālum elumpukaḷālum aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.araciyiṉatu cavappeṭṭiyil talaiyai maṟaikkum poṉṉālāṉa talaiyaṇi oṉṟum, kūntaliṉ mēṟpuṟam aṇiyat takka mālaipōṉṟa aṇi oṉṟum kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. aracaṉatu cavap peṭṭiyil kiṭantavāṟē iṅkum elumpuk kūṭukaḷ kiṭantaṉa. ēḻaiyiṉ cavappeṭṭi oṉṟil maṇ pommaikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.

inta nakarattil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ palavum.mēl varum pakkaṅkaḷil pala iṭaṅkaḷil cuṭṭappaṭum ātaliṉ, avaipaṟṟi iṅku virivākak kūṟavēṇṭuvatillai. innakara ārāycci paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ palavākum. putaiporuḷ ārāycciyiṉ puṇṇiya stalamāka viḷaṅkum - cumēriyar

nākarikattait teṟṟeṉa viḷakkum - innakarac ciṟappai virinta nūlkaḷiṟ kaṇṭu makiḻka.[12]

pāracīkam

yūprēṭis, ṭaikris yāṟukaḷ pāyappeṟum nilap pakutikkuk kiḻakkē āpkāṉistāṉamvarai uḷḷa nilapparappē pāracīkam eṉpatu. itaṉ perum pakuti pālaivaṉam; ataik kaṭantāl kaṇṇukkiṉiya kāṭcikaḷai nalkum kuṉṟukaḷum, malait toṭarkaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. āṅkāṅku aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. iṅkup paḻaiya kāla makkaṭkuriya ciṉṉaṅkaḷum eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟai mutaṉ mutal 1885 il ārāycci naṭattiyavar karṉal sar heṉṟi irāliṉsaṉ eṉpavar. ivar pāracīkariṉ paṇṭai eḻuttukkaḷai aritiṉ muyaṉṟu paṭittār; pāracīkaraippaṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷai aṟintu ulakiṟku uṇarttiṉār.[13]

intiyāvil ārāycci

nam nāṭṭil ārāyccikkuriya iṭaṅkaḷ evai? mekastaṉis, pāhiyaṉ. hiyūṉ - saṅ, pḷainī, tālami pōṉṟa ayalnāṭṭār intiyāvil iruntapōtum intiyāviṟku vanta pōtum kaṇṭaṉavākak kūṟappaṭṭa paṇṭai nakaraṅkaḷ iṉṟu maṇṇuḷ maṟaintum urumāṟiyum aḻintu uḷḷaṉa. puttar kālattil ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkiya paṇṭai nakaraṅkaḷ iṉṟu eṅkē irukkiṉṟaṉa? tamiḻ aracar āṇṭa talainakaraṅkaḷ eṅkē? ivviṭaṅkaḷil ārāycci naṭatta araciyalār taṉikkuḻu oṉṟai amaittaṉar.akkuḻu intiyap putaiporuḷ ārāyccikkuḻu[14] eṉappaṭum.akkuḻuvil ciṟappuṟṟa ārāycciyāḷarāka iruntavar sar aleksāṇṭar kaṉṉiṅhām eṉpavar āvar. avar vaṭaintayāvil pala iṭaṅkaḷil ārāycci nikaḻttiṉār. avaratu uḻaippiṉ payaṉāy nālantā, takṣacīlam, pāṭalipuram, kōcāmpi mutaliya paṇṭai nakaraṅkaḷaip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ cilavum, teṉ intiyāvil amarāvati eṉṉum iṭattil viyakkattakka poruḷkaḷum kalveṭṭukaḷum kiṭaittaṉa. mēṟkoṇṭu ārāycci naṭatta araciyalāriṭam, paṇam illai. ātaliṉ - intiya ārāycci vēlai mantamākavē naṭaipeṟṟu vantatu.

cintuveḷi nākarikam

intiyāviṟ kuṭipukunta āriyar mutaṉ mutal taṅkiyirunta iṭam cintuveḷiyē yākum. avviṭattiṟṟāṉ avarkaḷ rikvētam pāṭiṉar. avarkaḷ, aṅkut taṅkaṭkumuṉ irunta paṇṭai makkaḷōṭu pōr ceyya vēṇṭiyavar āyiṉar. ‘appakaivar nalla nakaraṅkaḷai amaittukkoṇṭu māṭa māḷikaikaḷil ciṟanta celvapperukkattōṭu vāḻntu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ vēḷvi ceyyātavarkaḷ; uruvavaḻipāṭu koṇṭavar; taṭṭai mukkuṭaiyavar; kuḷḷarkaḷ; māyā jālaṅkaḷil vallavarkaḷ; vāṇipat tiṟamai uṭaiyavarkaḷ’ eṉṟellām rikvētam kūṟukiṉṟatu. ikkuṟippukaḷāl āriyarkku muṟpaṭṭa intiya makkaḷ cintu veḷiyil ciṟanta paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷai amaittukkoṇṭu vāḻntaṉar eṉṉum ceyti pulaṉākiṉṟatu. iḵtu uṇmaiyē eṉpatai uṇarttavē pōlum, cintuveḷiyil uḷḷa harappāvum moheñco-tarōvum aṟiñar kaṇkaṭkuk kāṭci aḷittaṉa.[15]

1920 ām āṇṭil pañcāp maṇṭilattil, māṉṭ kōmarik kōṭṭattil, rāvi - caṭlej yāṟukaḷukku iṭaiyil, lāhūr - mulṭṭāṉ pukai vaṇṭippātaiyil harappā eṉṉum ārāyccikkuriya iṭam akappaṭṭatu. 1922il cintu maṇṭilattil uḷḷa larkkāṉāk kōṭṭattil 2100 ce.mī. uyaram uṭaiya maṇmēṭu oṉṟu kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭatu. ataṉuḷ putaintuḷḷa nakaramē moheñco-tarō eṉpatu. ammēṭṭiṉmēl neṭuntūṇ oṉṟu niṉṟiruntatu. iv viraṇṭu iṭaṅkaṭkum iṭaippaṭṭatolaivu 640 ki.mī. ākum. iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum ciṟitaḷavu tōṇṭip pārtta poḻutu, ‘ivai ārāyccikkuriya iṭaṅkaḷ’ eṉpatai aṟiñar aṟintu koṇṭaṉar; iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum palucistāṉattilum kiṭaitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷai oppiṭṭup pārtta ārāyccik kuḻuviṉ talaivarāṉa sar jāṉ mārṣal eṉṉum pēraṟiñar peruviyappuk koṇṭār; apporuḷkaḷai ārāycci ceytu tām aṟinta ceytikaḷai mēṉāṭṭup pattirikai oṉṟil veḷiyiṭṭār. intiya aracāṅkam makiḻntu paṇa utavi ceytatu. ataṉāl harappāvum moheñco tarōvum cuṟucuṟuppōṭu ārāycci ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ivviraṇṭu iṭaṅkaṭkum iṭaippaṭṭa palvēṟu. iṭaṅkaḷilum cintuyāṟu kaṭalōṭu kalakkum iṭam varaiyuḷḷa, iṭaṅkaḷilum kīrtar malaittoṭarai aṭuttuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilum ciṟitaḷavu ārāycci vēlaikaḷ naṭaipeṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ virivāṉa ceytikaḷai aṭutta pakutiyiṟ kāṇka.

﻿2. cintu veḷiyiṟ putaiyuṇṭa nakaraṅkaḷ

cintu yāṟu

ip pēriyāṟu tipēt pīṭapūmiyil uḷḷa kailāca malaikaḷil, kaṭalmaṭṭattiṟku mēl āṟu ilaṭcam ce. mī. uyarattil uṟpattiyākiṉṟatu. itaṉ arukil caṭlej eṉṉum itaṉ kiḷaiyāṟum piramaputtirā eṉṉumpēriyāṟum uṟpatti ākiṉṟaṉa. itiṉiṉṟum 104 ki. mī. tolaiviṟṟāṉ kaṅkaip pēriyāṟu uṟpatti ākiṉṟatu. ‘cintu’yāṟu tipēt, kāṣmīr nāṭukaḷil vaṭamēṟkāka 1280 ki.mī. ceṉṟu, kiljiṭ haṉsā eṉṉum iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaṭku iṭaiyil teṟku nōkkit tirumpip pañcāp maṇṭilattai aṭaikiṉṟatu. 80 ki.mī. teṟkē oṭiya piṉṉar, āpkāṉistāṉattu yāṟākiya ‘kāpūl’ yāṟu itaṉuṭaṉ aṭṭāk eṉṉum iṭattiṟku arukil kalakkiṉṟatu. aṭṭākkukku 752 ki. mī. teṟkē, pañcāp maṇṭilattai maruta nilamākat tikaḻac ceytu varukiṉṟa jīlam, cīṉāp, rāvi, piyās, caṭlej eṉṉum aiyāṟukaḷum cintuvil kalakkiṉṟaṉa. cintu āṟu kaṭaliṟ kalakkum iṭattil uḷḷa pukār nilam (Delta) 3000 caturak kal parappuṭaiyatu. cintu yāṟu aṭittukkoṇṭu varum ērāḷamāṉa maṇalum maṇṇum vantu cērvatālum, cintuyāṟu aṭikkaṭi taṉ pōkkai māṟṟikkoḷvatālum āṟṟu mukattil uḷḷa kiḷai yāṟukaḷ aṭikkaṭi tam pōkkai māṟṟik koḷkiṉṟaṉa. cintu yāṟṟiṉ nīḷam ēṟakkuṟaiya 3200 ki. mī. ākum.itaṉ pāyccal peṟṟuḷḷa nilam 960,000 ca.kimī ākum. āṇṭil mē mātam mutal ākasṭ mātamvarai imaya malaiyil uḷḷa paṉikkaṭṭikaḷ urukuvatāl, cintu yāṟu aḷavu kaṭanta veḷḷap perukkeṭuttu mikka vēkamāka ōṭi varum. ivveḷḷattāl uṇṭāṉa aḻivukaḷ pala; aḻinta nakaraṅkaḷ pala; inta yāṟṟil mīṉkaḷ mikkuḷḷaṉa. nīṇṭa mukkuṭaiy mutalaikaḷ mikutiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. inta vaṟṟāta vaḷamuṭaiya yāṟṟāl cintu veḷi mikka viḷaiccalaiyuṭaiya ciṟanta maruta nilamākak kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟatu.[1]

cintu yāṟu itukāṟum patiṉeṭṭu muṟai taṉ pōkkai māṟṟikkoṇṭatām. iḵtu 1928 varai moheñco tarōvukku aintāṟu kaṟkaḷukku ōṭikkoṇṭiruntatu; avvāṇṭil, tiṭīreṉat taṉ pōkku māṟi, moheñcetarēvukku 1.5 ki. mī. : tolaiviṟkuḷ ōṭalāyiṟṟu. itaṉāl, ippoḻutu tōṇṭik kaṇṭeṭukkappeṟṟa moheñco-tarōvukkum aṅkuḷḷa putai poruḷ kāṭcic cālaikkum ūṟu nērātirukka, nūṟāyiram rūpāyc celavil 300 ce. mī. uyarattiṟkuc cuvar oṉṟai 1.5 ki.mī . cuṟṟaḷavil eḻuppuvateṉṟu intiya aracāṅkattār muṭivu ceytaṉar.

pañcāp maṇṭilam

cintu yāṟu pāyum veḷi iṉṟu pañcāp, cintu eṉṉum iraṇṭumaṇṭilaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭu irukkiṉṟatu. pañcāp maṇṭilam, cintu māṟum ataṉ upanatikaḷākiya mēṟcoṉṉa aiyāṟukaḷum pāykiṉṟa vaḷamuṭaiya nāṭṭakum. payir viḷaiccalukkuriya cattuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ inta yāṟukaḷāl aṭittukkoṇṭu varappeṟṟu immaṇṭilam ciṟappuṟṟu irukkiṉṟatu. iṅkuppayirttoḻil nirampa naṭaipeṟukiṉṟatu; kōtumai, nel mutaliya kūla vakaikaḷum parutti, eṇṇey vitaikaḷ, karumpu mutaliyaṉavum ērāḷamākap payirākiṉṟaṉa; kālnaṭaip paṇṇaikaḷ pala naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, āṉāl, kaṉipporuḷkaḷ arukiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

cintu maṇṭilam

cintu maṇṭilam eṉpatu ‘tār’ pālaivaṉattukku mēṟkilum, palucistāṉattiṟkuk kiḻakkilum , pañcāp maṇṭalattiṟkut teṟkilum, ‘kaṭc’ vaḷaikuṭāviṟku vaṭakkilum uḷḷa nilapparappākum. itaṉ parappu ēṟakkuṟaiya 53 āyiram catūrakkal ākum.[2] iṅku nel, kōtumai, pārli mutaliya kūla vakaikaḷum piṟavum viḷaikiṉṟaṉa. āyirak kaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaṭku muṉ cintu maṇṭilam kāṭukaḷ ceṟintu iruntatu; mikunta maḻai peṟṟiruntatu: akkālattil cintu yāṟum ki. pi. 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai oṭikkoṇṭirunta ‘mahāmihrāṉ’ eṉṉum yāṟum pāyappeṟṟup peruvaḷam peṟṟatāka iruntatu. eṉiṉum, immaṇṭilam aṭikkaṭi veḷḷak kēṭṭiṟku nilaikkaḷaṉāka iruntamai varuntaṟkuriyatu.

kīrtar malait toṭar

immalaittoṭar cintuviṉ mēṟkil irukkiṉṟatu.itaṉ mēṟkil palucistāṉam uḷḷatu. immalai nāṭṭil paṇṭaik kālattil palavakai vilaṅkukaḷ vāḻntiruntaṉa. aṭivārap pakutikaḷil kaṟkāla maṉitarum cempuk kāla maṉitarum vāḻn tiruntaṉar eṉpataṟkuriya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. innūlil ārum ceytikaṭku immalaittoṭaraip paṟṟiya aṟivu iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum. cintu maṇṭilattaippaṟṟiya ippotuvivaraṅkaḷai immaṭṭōṭu niṟutti, cintu veḷiyiṟ kaṇṭaṟiyappeṟṟa paṇṭai nakaraṅkaḷaippaṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷaik kāṇpōm.

harappā

harappā eṉṉum paṇṭai nakaram pañcāp maṇṭilattil iraṇṭu yāṟukaṭku iṭaiyē amaintiruttalāl, mikap paḻaiya kālattil uyarnta nākarikam vāynta paṭṭaṇamāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. innakaram maṇmēṭiṭṭup pala āyiram āṇṭukaḷ ākiviṭṭaṉa. atikārikaḷ itaṉ arukil pukaivaṇṭip pātai pōṭṭa poḻutu, appātaikku vēṇṭiya kaṟkaḷai, immaṇmēṭṭait tōṇṭi uḷḷē irunta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷi liruntu eṭuttup payaṉpaṭuttikkoṇṭaṉar. itaṉāl,harappā nakaram citainta nilaiyai eytiyatu; itaṉ muḻu nilaimai irunta vitattai nām iṉṟu aṟiyakkūṭavillai. epar aleksāṇṭar kaṉṉiṅhām eṉṉum ārāycci nipuṇarkkuc cittira eḻuttukkaḷaik koṇṭa cila muttiraikaḷaiyē innakaram īntatu. atu mutal innakaram ārāycciyāḷar kavaṉattait taṉpāl iḻuttatu. iṅku 1920il naṭaipeṟṟa ārāycciyiṉ payaṉāy iṅku vāḻntavarākak karutappaṭṭa makkaḷiṉ kallaṟai oṉṟum maṭṭāṇṭaṅkaḷum vilaṅkukaḷiṉ elumpukaḷum vēṟu cila muttiraikaḷum kiṭaittaṉa. atē camayattil 1922 il cintu maṇṭilattu larkkāṉāk kōṭṭattil uḷḷa moheñco-tarō eṉṉum nakarattiṉ oru pakuti tōṇṭappaṭṭatu. aṅkuc cila poruḷkaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. ivviraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷ oṉṟāka iruttalai aṟinta ārāycci nipuṇarkaḷ, iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum mummuramāka ārāycci ceyyalāyiṉar. eṉavē, harappā nakarattil pukaivaṇṭippātai amaittōrāl toṭappaṭātirunta pakutikaḷ tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa.

paṇṭai harappā nakarattiṉ cuṟṟaḷavu 4 ki.mīṭṭarākalām. aṅku āṟu maṇ mēṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ periyatu 29,100 ce.mī.nīḷam, 23,400 ce.mī. akalam uṭaiyatu. mēṭukaḷiṉ uyaram 750 ce. mī. mutal 1,800 ce. mī. aṭivarai iṭattiṟku ēṟṟavāṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. harappā eṭṭu aṭukkukaḷaiyuṭaiya nakaram: aḵtāvatu eṭṭumuṟai putuppikkappaṭṭa nakaram. itaṉ kālam ki. mu. 3500-ki.mu. 2,750 eṉṉalām. itu moheñco-tarōvai viṭac ciṟitu muṟpaṭṭatu.[3]

mātō carup vāṭs eṉṉum pēraṟiñar innakara ārāycciyil pērukkam kāṭṭiṉār; pala poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟiya viyattaku ceytikaḷai intiyap putaiporuḷ ārāycci nilaiya veḷiyīṭukaḷil veḷiyiṭṭār; iṟutiyil harppāvaippaṟṟiya ārāycciyai iraṇṭu pakutikaḷāka aṇmaiyil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. avar veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa ceytikaḷ piṉ varum pakutikaḷil āṅkāṅkuk kūṟappaṭum ātaliṉ, iṅkuk kūṟātu viṭappaṭṭaṉa.

moheñco-tarō (1922-23il naṭaipeṟṟa ārāycci)

itu maṇ mūṭupaṭṭu irunta āriyar varuvataṟku muṉpu mika uyarnta nākarikattuṭaṉ irunta nakaramākum. iḵtu iṉṟu cintu āṟṟiṉ mēṟkē irukkiṉṟatu. itaṉaic cuṟṟilum vēṟu cila maṇmēṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ 2100 ce. mī. uyaramuḷḷa oṉṟaṉmītu peḷatta stūpi oṉṟu kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. ataṉaik kaṇṭa pāṉarji eṉṉum ārāycciyāḷar. ataṉ cuṟṟuppuṟattait tōṇṭum vēlaiyil īṭupaṭṭār. ammaṇ mēṭṭiṉuḷ nāṟkōṇamuḷḷa muṟṟam oṉṟaik kaṇṭār; ammuṟṟattaic cūḻ irunta muppatu ciṟṟaṟaikaḷaik kaṇṭār. avaṟṟuḷ or aṟaiyil akkaṭṭiṭattiṉ kālattaik kuṟippaṉapōlac cila nāṇayaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avai kuṣāṉ aracarkālattavai vācutēvamaṉṉaṉāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭavai. pāṉarji mikka makiḻcci aṭaintavarāy mēlum maṇmēṭṭai akaḻntār. ammēṭu taraimaṭṭa aḷavukku veṭṭappaṭṭa pōtu, aṅkoru cittira eḻuttukkaḷaikkoṇṭa muttirai kiṭaittatu. avarmēlum nilattai akaḻnta pōtu, iraṇṭu muttiraikaḷaik kaṇṭeṭuttār.

immūṉṟu muttiraikaḷum ārāyccik kūṭattai aṭaintaṉa. atē kālattil harappāvil ārāycci nikaḻttiya irāy pakatūr tayārām sahṉi eṉṉum pēraṟiñar, harappāvil kaṇṭeṭutta cittira eḻuttukkaḷaikkoṇṭa muttiraikaḷai ārāyccik kūṭattiṟku aṉuppiṉār. ivviraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭaitta muttiraikaḷaiyum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum naṉku cōtittu, avaṟṟiṉ orumaippāṭṭai uṇarnta ārāyccik kuḻuttalaivar sar jāṉ mārṣal viyappuṟṟār; avaṟṟōṭu palucistāṉattil kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷum oṉṟupaṭṭiruttalaik kaṇṭu peruviyappuṟṟār. aṉṟu (1924) mutal moheñco-tarō maṇ mēṭukaḷ tam meyyuruvai mella mella ulakattiṟku uṇarttalāyiṉa.

stūpattaiyuṭaiya maṇmēṭu

peḷatta stuāpattaik koṇṭa maṇ mēṭṭiṟkuk kāla kal tolaiviṟṟāṉ moheñco-tarō nakarattaikkoṇṭa maṇ mēṭu irukkiṟatu. stupattaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa maṇ mēṭukaḷ ēṟakkuṟaiya 750 ce mī uyaramuṭaiyaṉa. moheñco-tarō nakarattiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷ inta maṇmēṭukaṭkuḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttākum. stupattaik koṇṭa maṇmēṭu uḷḷa iṭamē, moheñco-tarō ceḻippuṭaṉ irunta kālattilum ciṟanta iṭamāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum: ataṉ aḻiviṟku 2500 āṇṭukaṭkup piṉṉarum avviṭam uyariya nilaiyil iruntamaiyāṟṟāṉ, peḷattarkaḷ aṅku stuāpiyai eḻuppiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. anta maṇ mēṭṭil stupiyum peḷattar paḷḷiyum naṉku eḻuppappaṭṭu iruttaliṉ, avaṟṟai ivvamayam eṭukkakkūṭavillai. eṉiṉum, avai nāḷaṭaivil eṭukkappaṭum. avaṟṟiṉ aṭiyil eṉṉa ariya poruḷkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉavō, aṟintavar yāvar?

1925-1934 varai naṭaipeṟṟa ārāycci

aṟiñar pāṉarji stāpaṅkoṇṭa maṇmēṭṭil ciṟitiṭattait tōṇṭiyapiṉṉar, iṉṟaiya moheñco-tarō nakaram putaiyuṇṭirunta maṇ mēṭṭait tōṇṭum paṇiyil irāvpahatūr kē. eṉ. tīkṣit eṉpavar. muṉaintār. avarukku utaviyāḷar palarum iruntaṉar. aṉaivarum ceyta ārāycci vivaraṅkaḷ car jāṉ mārṣal mēṟpārvaiyil mūṉṟu pakutikaḷaiyuṭaiya peru nūlāka veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu.[4] 1927il ivvārāyccik keṉṟē ṭākṭar ī. jē. hec. makkē eṉpavar amarttappaṭṭār. avar ippakutiyil ārāycciyai naṭattiṉār; tam ārāycciyiṟ kaṇṭavaṟṟaippaṟṟi virivāṉa nūlkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷār.[5] avarukkuppiṉ, moheñco-tarōvil putitāka vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa poruṭkāṭcic cālaiyiṉ talaivarākiya kē. eṉ. pūri eṉpavarum, kyū. em. moṉīr (Q.M.Moneer) eṉpavarum vēṟu cilarum ārāyccip paṇiyil iṟaṅkiṉar. ipperumakkaḷiṉ iṭaiviṭā uḻaippāl, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷ maṇṇuḷ maṟaintu kiṭanta mānakaramākiya moheñco-tarōviṉ pattil oru pākam veḷip paṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu. appakutiyiliruntu ulakattai viyappāl tiṭukkiṭac ceyyum pala ariya vivaraṅkaḷ veḷippaṭṭaṉ.

kuṟippiṭattakka ceytikaḷ

maṇṇuḷ maṟainta mānakarattaippaṟṟiya ariya vivaraṅkaḷ aṭuttuvarum pakutikaḷil virivākak kūṟappaṭumātaliṉ, īṇṭuk kuṟippiṭattakka cilavaṟṟaiyē kūṟutum:

innakaram harappāvaippōlap pala aṭukkukaḷai uṭaiyatu. aṟiñar ituvarai ēḻu aṭukkukaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷaṉar; ‘avaṟṟiṉ aṭiyilum pala aṭukkukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, nīr ūṟum iṭmāka iruppatāl avaṟṟait tōṇṭik kāṇal iyalātupōlum! eṉṟu kūṟi varuntukiṉṟaṉar.

immānakaram harappāvaip pōla aḻivuṟavillai. nakara amaippu, terukkaḷiṉ amaippu illaṅkaḷiṉ amaippu ivai kaṇṭu iṉpuṟat takkavai. varicai varicaiyākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa illaṅkaḷ, māṭamāḷikaikaḷ, maṇṭapaṅkaḷ, nīrāṭum kuḷam, kaḻitīrppātai, aḵtu amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa uyariya muṟai. iṉṉa piṟavum kaṇṇaik kavarvaṉavākum. oviyaṅkaḷum cittirak kuṟiyōṭukūṭiya eḻuttukkaḷum koṇṭa kaṇakkaṟṟa muttiraikaḷ aṟiñarai meymaṟakkac ceytaṉa. pala niṟaṅkoṇṭa palavaṭivamainta maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ, pommaikaḷ, viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ, paṇpaṭṭa maṇṇālum cuṇṇāmpuk kallālum amainta palavakaipporuḷkaḷ, caṅku-cippi-paṇpaṭṭa maṇ. uyarnta kaṟkaḷ ivaṟṟālāya aṇi vakaikaḷ mutaliyaṉa ulaka ārāycciyāḷar tam kavaṉattait tampāl īrttaṉa. ip poruḷkaḷiṟ pala, nām muṟpakutiyiṟ kūṟiya aciriyā, pāpilōṉiyā, mecopoṭṭōmiyā, ēlam, pāracīkam mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṟ kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷaip peritum ottiruttalaik kaṇṭa ārāycci aṟiñar tiṭukkiṭṭaṉar. ivvariya poruḷkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiya makkaḷatu kālam ēṟak kuṟaiya ki.mu.3250-ki.mu.2750 āka irukkalām eṉa matippiṭṭaṉar. immānakaram paṟṟiya ceytikaḷai ittuṭaṉ niṟutti, iccintu veḷiyilēyē putaiyuṇṭu kiṭakkum piṟa nakaraṅkaḷaip paṟṟik kavaṉippōm.

cāṉhu-tarō

aṟiñar eṉjī.majūmtār, cintu āṟṟiṉ kiḻakkuppuṟattil oru putiya iṭattaik kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷār. apputiya iṭattil mūṉṟu maṇmēṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. anta iṭam 30000 ce. mī. nīḷamum 2100 ce.mī. akalamum uṭaiyatu, maṇmēṭukaḷ mūṉṟum 300, 510, 570 ce.mī. uyaram iruntaṉa. anta iṭam oru kālattil ‘periyatoru nakaramāka iruntiruttal kūṭum’ eṉpatu aṟiyakkiṭakkiṟatu. aṟiñarkaḷ ataṟkuc cāṉhu-tarō eṉṟu peyar iṭṭuḷḷaṉar. aṟiñar majūmtār, cāṉhu-tarōvil ārāycci nikaḻttiya aḷavil, vevvēṟu vakaiyāṉa poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟaik kaṇṭeṭuttuḷḷār.

aṅkuḷḷa vīṭukaḷ pala ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭavai; cuṭṭa ceṅkal koṇṭu kaṭṭappaṭṭavaiyum cila uḷa. aṅku akaḻappaṭṭa varaiyil 300 poruḷkaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ irattiṉa maṇikaḷ kuṟippiṭat takkavai. immaṇikaḷmītu ‘8’ pōṉṟa vaṭivam kāṇappaṭṭatu. aṅkuc cippiyālāṉa vaḷaiyal tuṇṭukaḷ pala kiṭaittaṉa. oru vaḷaiyalil iraṇṭu tuḷaikaḷ iruntaṉa. aḻakiya pantu oṉṟu vaḷainta kōṭukaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭṭatu. muttiraikaḷ pala kiṭaittaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟil iru maṉitar villum ampum ēnti niṟpatupōlac cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. avarkaḷ arukil oru malaiyāṭu niṟpatu pōlak kāṇappaṭṭatu. viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ pala kiṭaittaṉa. ciṟu tēr uruḷaikaḷum uṭainta tērkaḷ iraṇṭum maṇṇil ceytu cūḷai iṭappaṭṭavai. erumaittalai oṉṟu cevvaṇṇam pūcik kaḻuttil tuḷaiyiṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. itu pōlat tuḷaiyiṭappaṭṭa kuraṅku kāṇṭā mirukam mutaliyaṉavum kiṭaittaṉa. paṟavai oṉṟu mikka aḻakāyc ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ōr ūtukuḻal naṉṉilaiyil kāṇap paṭṭatu. ivai aṉaittum cenniṟam pūcappaṭṭavai ākum. ivai tavirak kaḷimaṇṇāṟ ceyta ‘taraippeṇ’ tēvataiyiṉ patumai oṉṟu kiṭaittatu. iḵtu 15 ce. mī. uyaramuḷḷatu. aṅku 198 maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kiṭaittaṉa avai pala niṟaṅkaḷil palvakai oviyaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ orupuṟak kaippiṭiyuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ orupuṟak kaippiṭiyuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭṭa pāṇṭam oṉṟē ciṟantatākum. avaṟṟuṭaṉ cempuk katti, vaḷaiyal tuṇṭu, uḷi, īṭṭimuṉai mutaliyaṉavum nirampak kiṭaittaṉa.

cāṉhu-tarōvil kiṭaitt poruḷkaḷ, moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaittavaṟṟukku oppākavē irukkiṉṟaṉ. aṅkuk kiṭaitta tāḻikaḷil tīṭṭappeṟṟa oviyaṅkaḷ moheñco-tarō oviyaṅkaḷai ottuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟil maramum maṟṟoṉṟil veḷḷāṭum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa iraṇṭu muttiraikaḷ aṅkuk kiṭaittaṉa. eṉavē, cāṉha-tarōvai mēlum naṉku akaḻntu ārāycci nikaḻttiṉāl, paṇṭaik kālattiṟku uriya poruḷkaḷ pala kiṭaittal kūṭum. āyiṉ, apputiya iṭattai akaḻntu pārkkum urimaiyai intiya aracāṅkattāriṭamiruntu, or amerikka ārāyccik kaḻakattiṉar peṟṟuviṭṭaṉar. iṉi akkaḻakattiṉar. tam poruṭcelavil avviṭattu ārāyccippaṇi āṟṟuvar pōlum!

lohuñ-co-tarō

cintu āṟṟiṉ mēṟkuk karaiveḷiyil, piyārōkōtattiṟku arukil moheñco-tarōvukku 96 ki.mī. teṟkil oru periya maṇmēṭu iruppatākat terintatu. avviṭam irunta maṇmēṭu 27,000 ce.mī.nīḷamum 18000 ce.mī. akalamum 690 ce.mī. uyaramum uṭaiyatu. ammaṇmēṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyait taraimaṭṭam varaiyil tōṇṭi ataṟkuk kīḻum 330 ce.mī. varai aṟiñar majūmtār cōtaṉai purintār. aṅkuc ceṅkaṟkaḷālākiya - kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṟku aṭiyil avaṟṟukkum muṟpaṭṭa kālattucciṉṉaṅkaḷ pala kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ ciṟantavai maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷākum. ivai cenniṟam pūcappaṭṭavai. accenniṟattiṉ mītu kaṟuppu vaṇṇattil cittiraṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.ittakaiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ pala moheñco-tarōvilum kiṭiait tuḷḷamai kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu, ippāṇṭaṅkaṭku mēṟpuṟattil kāṇappaṭṭa vēṟuvakaip pāṇṭaṅkaḷ piṟkāla nākarikattaic cērntavai ākum eṉṟu majumtār karutukiṉṟār. ākavē, ip pakutiyilum iru vēṟupaṭṭa nākarikam iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu karutattakkatu. piṟa poruḷkaḷuḷ kuṟippiṭat takkavai kaḷimaṇṇālāya erutup paṭivaṅkaḷ ākum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu kaḻuttil tuḷaiyiṭppaṭṭuḷḷatu. mukkōṇa vaṭivil amainta kaḷimaṇ appaṅkaḷ, uyarnta kaḷimaṇṇālāya vaḷaiyalkaḷ mutaliyaṉa kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. aṅkuk kiṭaitta palavakai maṇikaḷuḷ mākkal maṇikaḷ palavākum avai taṭṭai vaṭivattil amaintuḷḷaṉa. ammaṇikaḷ moheñco-tarōvilum mikutiyākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. mākkallālāṉa muttirai oṉṟum kiṭaittatu. ataṉmītuḷḷa eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ moheñco-tarōvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa muttiraikaḷil uḷḷa eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷaiyē ottuḷḷamai viyappūṭṭuvatākum.

ivvaṇṇam kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa putiya maṇmēṭu irunta iṭam mohāñco-tarō eṉṉum peyar koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. anta iṭaṅkaḷil ārāyccit toṇṭāṟṟiya aṟiñar majumtār piṟitōr uṇmaiyaiyum veḷippaṭutti yuḷḷār. aḵtāvatu, ‘akkāla makkaḷ, kuṉṟukaḷiṉ aṭip puṟaṅkaḷ vacippataṟku ēṟṟa taṭpa veppa nilaiyuṭaṉ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpataiyum taṟkāppu araṇ uṭaiyavaikaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpataiyum naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar’ eṉpatē ākum.[6]

itu cintu maṇṭilattil ‘ṣahaṭaṭpūr’ eṉṉum ūrukku vaṭamēṟkil 6.5 ki.mī. tolaivil uḷḷa periya maṇmēṭākum. itaṉ arukil 0.5 ki. mī. tolaivil vēṟoru maṇmēṭu irukkiṉṟatu. moheñco-tarō maṇmēṭṭiṉ mītu irukkum stūpam pōṉṟa peḷatta stūpi oṉṟu iṅkum irukkiṉṟatu. inta stūpam uḷḷa maṇmēṭṭaic cuṟṟilum ciṟiya maṇmēṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivai aṉaittum koṇṭa periya maṇ mēṭētakañcotarō eṉpatu. itaṉ parappu aintu ēkkar ākum. maṇmēṭṭiṉ uyaram 600 ce.mī. mutal 1200 ce.mī. ākum. iṅkuc cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ 10 ce.mī. kaṉamuṭaiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuc cittiram tīṭṭappeṟāta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷē mikutiyākak kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. immaṭ pāṇṭac citaivukaḷum ceṅkaṟkaḷumē ippakutiyil peruvāri yākak kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. cil ceṅkaṟkaḷ 28 ce.mī. caturamuṭaiyaṉa; 5.5 ce. mī. kaṉamuṭaiyaṉa. uyartaramaṇikaḷ iraṇṭu kiṭaittaṉa. inta iṭattai mēlum akaḻntu ārāycci ceyyavillai. cittiram tīṭṭap peṟāta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ iruttalaik kāṇiṉ, iṅku mikap paḻaiya nakaram iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu veḷiyākiṟatu.[7]

cak pūrpāṉi siyāl

harappāvukkut teṉkiḻakkē ēṟakkuṟaiya 20.5 ki. mī. tolaivil piyās āṟṟiṉ ularnta paḻaiya paṭukkai oṉṟil kāṇappaṭṭa maṇmēṭukaḷai aṟiñar vāṭs eṉpavar akaḻntu pārttār. anta iṭam ‘car pūrpāṉisiyāl’ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkap paṭukiṟatu. aṅku ērāḷamāṉa uṭainta maṭpāṇṭa oṭukaḷum, ceṅkaṟ kaṭṭikaḷum, aṭippuṟam kuvintuḷḷa nīr aruntum ēṉaṅkaḷum, niṟan tīṭṭappaṭṭa pāṇṭac citaivukaḷum, uruṭṭu vaṭivāṉa ēṉaṅkaḷum, mōtiraṅkaḷum, pīṭaṅkaḷ mītu vaikkum kalaṉkaḷum, muṭṭai vaṭivak kōppaikaḷum, tāmpāḷaṅkaḷum, vaḷaiyalkaḷum, kuraṅku aṇil, kompuṭaiya erutu, kallāl kaṇkaḷ vaikkappeṟṟa mayil ākiya paṭivaṅkaḷum, talaiyaṟṟa peṇ paṭivaṅkaḷum, kātaṇikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya ciṟṟurukkaḷum, iruntaṉa. aṅku mēlum pala niṟaṅkaḷuṭaiya cuṭṭa kaḷimaṇ maṇikaḷ, vaḷaiyalkaḷ, mākkallāl ceyyappaṭṭa maṇikaḷ, irattiṉak kal maṇikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum kiṭaittaṉa. potuvil avviṭattil nikaḻttiya ārāycciyiṉ mūlam aṅkuk kiṭaittirukkum poruḷkaḷ aṉaittum moheñco. tarō harappā ākiyavaṟṟiṉ nākarikattiṟku iyaintavaiyākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu aṟiñar vāṭsiṉ karuttākum.[8]

ali murāt

cintu veḷiyil ārāyccikkuriya maṟṟōr iṭam ali murāt eṉpatu. aṅkuc carittira kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa iraṇṭu periya maṇmēṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ nīḷam 33000 ce.mī, akalam 33000 ce.mī. uyaram 810 ce.mī. ākum. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiṉ uyaram 750 ce. mī. ākum. ataṉ teṉ pakutiyait tōṇṭiya poḻutu kallāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa kiṇaṟu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭatu. ataṉ uṭpuṟak kuṟukkaḷavu 20 ce. mī. ākum. akkiṇaṟṟai aṭuttu vaḷaivāṉa kaṟcuvar oṉṟu 12.5 ce.mī. uyarattil kāṇap paṭṭatu. ataṉ nīḷam 5100 ce.mī. eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu kōṭṭaikkamainta matiṟ cuvarāka iruntirukkalām. accuvarkku uṭpaṭṭa iṭattil pala kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ iruntamaik kuriya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai yāvum kaṟkālattai niṉaippūṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. avviṭattil kiṭaitta maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa pala iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭaitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaip pōlavē vaṇṇamiṭappaṭṭum cittiram tīṭṭappaṭṭum irukkiṉṟaṉa. avviṭattil kaḷimaṇ appaṅkaḷ āyirak kaṇakkil kiṭaittuḷḷamai peritum viyappūṭṭu vatākum.ivaiyaṉṟi, muḻuvēlaippāṭu amaintuḷḷa pīppāy vaṭivam koṇṭa uyartarak kal oṉṟu 5 ce.mī.nīḷattil kāṇap paṭṭatu. itē vaṭivaṅkoṇṭacemmaṇi oṉṟum kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭatu. avviṭam muḻuvatum tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭiṉ, vēṟu pala poruḷkaḷum piṟavum aṟital kūṭumeṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[9]

pāṇṭi vāhi

ippeyaruṭaiya ciṟiya kirāmam larkkāṉākkōṭṭattil kīrtar malaittoṭariṉ aṭivārattil uḷḷatu. itaṉ arukil itaṉ peyaraiyē koṇṭi maṇmēṭu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. atu 13500 ce. mī. nīḷamum 7500 ce. mī. akalamum 630 ce. mī. uyaramum uṭaiyatu. eṉiṉum, ataṉ paḻaiya aḷavu mikap periyatāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ammaṇ mēṭṭil kuṟippiṭṭa mūṉṟu iṭaṅkaḷil ārāycci naṭaipeṟṟatu. mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭaitta maṭpāṇṭap poruḷkaḷum piṟavum avviṭattum kiṭaittaṉa. ammaṇmēṭum muṉ collappaṭṭamēṭukaḷaippōlavē muḻu ārāyccikku uṭpaṭumāyiṉ, pala uṇmaikaḷ veḷiyākum.[10]

amri

amri eṉpatu karāccik kōṭṭattil vaṭakku ellaiyil cintu āṟṟiṉ karaiyil uḷḷa ciṟṟuār ākum. cintu, āṟṟil pirayāṇam ceyta parṉas eṉṉum mēṉāṭṭu aṟiñar cumār 100 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē, ivviṭattaippaṟṟik kīḻ varumāṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār: “amri eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ iṉṟu viḷaṅkum ciṟṟur, oru kālattil mikap periya nakaramāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. itu cintuviṉ veḷḷattiṟku aṭikkaṭi iraiyāki maṟaintiruttal vēṇṭum. eṉiṉum, iṉṟuḷḷa kirāmattiṉarukil 1200 ce.mī. uyarattil maṇ mēṭu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉait tōṇṭip pārppiṉ pala uṇmaikaḷ veḷippaṭal kūṭum”.

ivvaṟiñar oru nūṟu āṇṭukaṭku muṉ iṅṅaṉam eḻutic ceṉṟamai iṉṟu uṇmai ākiviṭṭatu. ivar āppi maṇmēṭu, kirāmattiṉ vaṭamēṟkil amaintuḷḷatu. ataṉ arukil vēṟu pala maṇ mēṭukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟil ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭac paṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila maṇmēṭukaḷil cuṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaccitaivukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.avviṭattai ārāycci ceyyac ceṉṟa majumtār oru maṇmēṭṭil 1500 ce. mī. nīḷamum 360 ce. mī. akalamum 150 ce. mī āḻamum uḷḷa kuḻi citaivukaḷ kāṇap ceṅkaṟkaḷāl ākiya oṉṟait tōṇṭip pārttār. mūṉṟu kaṟcuvarkaḷiṉ pakutikaḷ teṉpaṭṭaṉa. ārāycciyā ḷar accuvar kaḷiṉ aṭimaṭṭam varai tōṇṭavē, avviṭam taraimaṭṭattiṉum kīḻnōkkic ceṉṟatu: akkuḻiyiliruntu 253 poruḷkaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cittiram tīṭṭappaṭṭa viḷaiyāṭṭu vaṇṭikaḷ, maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ, patumaikaḷ, vaḷaiyal tuṇṭukaḷ, maṇikaḷ, maṇ appaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa ciṟappittukkūṟat takuvaṉa. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ 214 kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.ivai palavakai niṟaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. anniṟaṅkaḷukkumēl mīṉ cetil, vaṭṭa malar, māvilai, aracilai, alakil malaruṭaṉ viḷaṅkum mayil, caturaṅkappalakai, muṭṭai, paṭṭaikaḷ, paṭṭaikaḷukkuḷ mukkōṇa vaṭivam mutaliya ōviyaṅkaḷ paṟpala vitamākat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. potuvāka, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ cenniṟamē pūcappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaittavaṟṟai ottirukkiṉṟaṉa.

piṟa poruḷkaḷil nīṇṭu uruṇṭa paccai maṇikaḷum, kumiḻpōṉṟa maṇikaḷum kuṟippiṭat takkavai. immaṇikaḷ mītu vaḷaiyaṅkaḷ varaiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vaḷaiyal tuṇṭukaḷ 25 kiṭaittaṉa. ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟil cenniṟap puḷḷikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.patumaikaḷuḷ citaint tērkaḷ 12 kiṭaittaṉa; erutuppatumaikaḷ 8 kiṭaittaṉa. or erutut talai kaḻuttil tuḷaiyuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭṭatu. ittalaikkuriya uṭal kāṇappaṭa villai. ivai tavira, 10 maṇ appaṅkaḷ kiṭaittirukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuk kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷ moheñco-tarō nākarikattuṭaṉ peritum ottuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.[11]

kōṭlā nihāṅ kāṉ

‘civālik malaikku aṭippuṟam uḷḷa ‘ampālā’ kōṭṭattil ‘rupār’ nakarukku arukil, cintu āṟṟiṉ kiḷaiyākiya sṭlej āṟṟiṉ paṭukkaiyil irukkum kōṭlā nihāṅ kāṉ eṉṉum ciṟṟuāraic cārntuḷḷa maṇmēṭṭai aṟiñar vāṭs akaḻntu ārāycci nikaḻttiṉār. ammēṭu 12 ēkkar parappuḷḷatu; 360 ce. mī. mutal 900 ce.mī. varai uyaramuṭaiyatu. aṅku avar kaṇṭeṭutta poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟai, harappāvilum moheñco-tarōvilum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭṭup pārttapōtu, avai peritum oṉṟupaṭṭa orē nākarikattiṟku uriyavaiyāka iruntaṉa. kōṭlā nihāṅ kāṉil kiṭaitta kaḷi maṇṇālāya ceṅkaṟkaḷ, kaḷimaṇ koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa maṇikaḷ, appaṅkaḷ, mōtiraṅkaḷ, curaikkuṭukkai pōṉṟa nīrkkuvaḷaikaḷ, tāmpāḷaṅkaḷ, cāṭikaḷ vaikkum pīṭaṅkaḷ, maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ, ivaṟṟiṉ uṭainta pakutikaḷ, vaḷaiyal tuṇṭukaḷ, ciṟu niṟaik kaṟkaḷ, muṭṭai vaṭivattil irunta nīr aruntum kuvaḷaikaḷ mutaliyaṉa cintu veḷimakkaḷ nākarikattaiyē oruvāṟu oppaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa.[12]

nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa maṇmēṭukaḷ

cintu āṟṟiṉ kiḻakkuk karaimītuḷḷa ‘kairppūr’ camattāṉattilum, kattiyavāriluḷḷa ‘limpṭi’ camattāṉattaic cērnta, ‘araṅpūr’ eṉṉum iṭattilum, larkkāṉāvukku arukil uḷḷa ‘jukār’ eṉṉum iṭattilum, kājiṣā eṉṉum iṭattilum ivvakai ārāyccip paṇi naṭaipeṟṟatu. innāṉku iṭaṅkaḷilum nikaḻttiya ārāyccit toṇṭiṉ palaṉākak kiṭaitta paṇṭaip poruḷkaḷai nōkkiṉ, ivaṟṟiṟkum moheñco-tarō, harappā ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷukkum, iṭaiyē ciṟu vēṟupāṭum illai eṉalām. ivai muḻuvatum cintu veḷi nākarikattuṭaṉ iyaintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

cintu veḷiyil maṭṭum oru nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa maṇmēṭukaḷum paḻaiya carittira kālattiṟkuriya iṭaṅkaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa.ivaṟṟai ārāycci ceytu pārppiṉ, iṉṟuḷḷa intiya varalāṟu aṟputa māṟutalai aṭaiyum eṉpatil aiyamillai.
