﻿3. piṟa maṇṭilaṅkaḷiṉ putai poruḷkaḷ

ārāycci iṭaṅkaḷiṟ pāyum āṟukaḷ

intiyāvil ārāyccikkuriya piṟa iṭaṅkaḷai aṟiyumuṉ, avviṭaṅkaḷ amaintuḷḷa āṟṟu veḷikaḷaip paṟṟi aṟital iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum. ātaliṉ, cintu nīṅkalākavuḷḷa kaṅkai, narumatai,kōtāvari,kiruṣṇai, kāviri, tāmiraparaṇi, periyāṟu eṉṉum yāṟukaḷaip paṟṟiyum avai pāyum iṭaṅkaḷaip paṟṟiyum īṇṭu mutalil kuṟippiṭuvōm.

kaṅkai

imayamalaiyiṉ teṉ paḷḷattākkukaḷiliruntu puṟappaṭum ‘pākīrati alakaṇṭā’ eṉṉum iraṇṭu ciṟṟāṟukaḷiṉ kalappē kaṅkai yāṟu ākum. iḵtu allakapātai aṭaiyumvarai mikka nīrōṭṭam uṭaiyataṉṟu. allakapātil mikap periya yāṟākiya yamuṉai eṉpatu kaṅkaiyiṟ kalakkiṉṟatu. aṅkiruntu kaṅkaiyāṟu aikkiya maṇṭilattilum vaṅkāḷa maṇṭilattilum pāyntu vaṅkakkaṭaliṟ kalakkiṉṟatu. itaṉ motta nīḷam 2490 ki.mī. ākum. itil kalappuṟum yāṟukaḷ-cartā, irām kaṅkā, kumṭi, kōkrā, cōṉai, kaṇṭakkuci eṉpaṉa. itaṉ caṅkamukat tuṟaiyil uḷḷa pukar nila veḷi 128 mutal 352 ki. mī. akalamuṭaiyatu. itaṉ pāyccalaip peṟum iṭam 39,000 caturak kaṟkaḷ ākum. itu mikap paḻaiya kāla mutalē ciṟappuṭaiyāṟākak karutappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. itu pāykiṉṟa nilam kaṅkai veḷi eṉappaṭum. itaṉ karaiyōrattil irunta paṇṭai nakaraṅkaḷ (vaṭa) maturai, keḷacāmpi, pirayākai, vāraṇāci (kāci) mutaliyaṉa.

cintu veḷiyiṟ kuṭiyēṟiya āriyar nāḷaṭaivil kaṅkaiyamuṉai yāṟukaṭku iṭaiyil uḷḷa nilappakutiyilum kuṭiyēṟiṉar. aṅkaṉam avarkaḷ kuṭiyēṟum poḻutu aṅku vāḻntirunta paṇṭai makkaḷōṭu pōriṭṭaṉar. “kiruṣṇaṉ eṉpavaṉait talaivaṉākak koṇṭa ‘kiruṣṇar’ (kaṟuppar) yamuṉaik karaiyil vāḻntiruntaṉar. avaruḷ aimpatu āyiram pērai āriyar talaivaṉāṉa intiraṉ koṉṟāṉ”[1] eṉṟu rikvētam kūṟuvatiliruntu, kaṅkai veḷiyiṟ pala iṭaṅkaḷil paṇṭai makkaḷ nakaraṅkaḷai amaittukkoṇṭu vāḻntiruntaṉar[2] eṉṉum uṇmaiyai uṇaralām.

narumatai-tapati yāṟukaḷ

ivai vaṭa intiyāviṟkut teṉ ellaiyākavuḷḷa vintiya-cātpūrā malait toṭarkaḷiliruntu puṟappaṭṭu mēṟku nōkkic ceṉṟu arapik kaṭalil kalakkiṉṟaṉa. naṭu intiyāviṉ teṟkil uḷḷa malaik kōṭṭaṅkaḷum, naṭu maṇṭilaṅkaḷiṉ vaṭakkil uḷḷa malaikkōṭṭaṅkaḷum muṟaiyē vintiyacātpūrā malai nāṭukaḷiṟ kalantuḷḷaṉa. ivviru malait toṭarkaṭkum iṭaiyē ciṟanta yāṟākiya tarumatai oṭikkoṇṭirukkiṟatu.inta irumalait toṭarkaḷaic cārnta nāṭukaḷil, pala vakaippaṭṭa malaivāṇar vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. immalai nāṭukaḷil, aṭarnta kāṭukaḷ mikkuḷḷaṉa ivaṟṟaik kaṭantupōtal iyalāta ceyal, pōka virumpupavar, karaiyōraṅkaḷ vaḻiyākavum kaṭal vaḻiyākavum pōkalām. immalaip pakutikaḷāṟṟāṉ vaṭa intiya varalāṟu vēṟākavum teṉ intiya varalāṟu vēṟākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa.

teṉṉāṭu

vintiya-cātpūrā malaittoṭarkaṭkut teṉpāṟpaṭṭa nilappakuti teṉṉāṭu allatu ‘ṭekkāṉ’ eṉappaṭum. im mēṭṭup pakuti kiḻakku nōkkiyum teṉkiḻakku nōkkiyum carintuḷḷatu. ataṉāl makā nari, kōtāvari, kiruṣṇai, kāviri eṉṉum periya yāṟukaḷ kiḻakku nōkki ōṭi vaṅkak kaṭalil kalakkiṉṟaṉa. inta yāṟukaḷ pāyum nilappakutikaḷ vaḷamuṭaiyaṉavākum makkaḷ tokuti mikkuṭaiyaṉavākum. inta yāṟukaḷ mēṟku malait toṭarcciyil uṟpattiyāvaṉa.

kōtāvari

intap periyāṟu 1440 ce mī nīḷamuṭaiyatu. itaṉ camaveḷiyil arici, pukaiyilai, eṇṇey vitaikaḷ, karumpu mutaliyaṉa peruvāriyāka viḷaikiṉṟaṉa. itaṉai aṭuttuḷḷa kāṭukaḷil tēkka maraṅkaḷ mikutiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. inta yāṟṟiṉ neṭunturam haitarāpāt nāṭṭil aṭaṅki iruttalāl, inta yāṟṟāl uṇṭākum perumpayaṉai annāṭē peṟṟuvarukiṉṟatu.

kiruṣṇai

itu 1280 ce mī nīḷa muṭaiyatu. 180 ce. mī. tolaivu pampāy maṇṭilattil oṭippiṉpu haitarāpāttiṟkuṭ pukuntu, piṉṉarc ceṉṉai maṇṭilattai aṭaikiṉṟatu. itaṉ pāyccalaip peṟṟa maruta nilaṅkaḷil nel mutaliya palavakaip poruḷkaḷ vaḷamuṟap payirākiṉṟaṉ.

kāviri

inta yāṟē tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṟ ciṟappup peṟṟatākum. itu mēṟku malait toṭaril tōṟṟamāki, maicūrnāṭṭiṉ vaḻiyē tamiḻ nāṭṭai aṭaintu vaṅkak kaṭalil kalakkiṉṟatu. itaṉ nīḷam 760 ce. mīṭṭarākum. itaṉ payaṉai aṭaintuvarum nilam 76,800 ca. ki. mī parappuṭaiyatu. itu tañcāvūrk kōṭṭattil kaṭalōṭu kalakkiṉṟatu. itu poṉṉaik koḻikkum perumai yuṭaiyatātaliṉ, ‘poṉṉi eṉap peyar peṟṟatu.

tāmiraparaṇi

inta yāṟum poṉ koḻikkum perumai peṟṟatu. itu tirunelvēlikkōṭṭattiṟ pāykiṉṟatu. itaṉai aṭuttuḷḷa maruta nilaṅkaḷ mikka viḷaiccalai nalkukiṉṟaṉa. itu pāyum “tirunelvēlik kōṭṭattil paṇṭaip pukaḻpeṟṟa koṟkai mutaliya pāṇṭiyar nakaraṅkaḷ maṟaintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.

pēriyāṟu

itumēṟku malaittoṭarcciyil tōṉṟi mēṟku nōkki ōṭi arapik kaṭalil kalakkiṉṟatu; tiruvāṅkūr nāṭṭil tōṉṟik kocci nāṭṭiṟ pāykiṉṟatu.

āṟṟu veḷikaḷil paṇṭai makkaḷ

intiyāvil uḷḷa inta yāṟukaḷ pāyap peṟṟuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilum malai nāṭukaḷilum paḻaiya kaṟkāla maṉitarum putiya kaṟkāla maṉitarum cempukkāla maṉitarum veṇkalakkāla maṉitarum irumpukkāla maṉitarum kirāmaṅkaḷaiyum nakaraṅkaḷaiyum amaittuk koṇṭu vāḻntiruntaṉar eṉpatu kūṟāmalē viḷaṅkum. ivarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam vāḻntiruntaṉar eṉpataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaitta vaṇṇam irukkiṉṟaṉa. iṉi, accāṉṟukaḷai oraḷavu muṟaiyē kāṇpōm.

aikkiya maṇṭalattup putai poruḷkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvum harappāvum tōṇṭik kaṇṭupiṭippataṟku muṉṉarē-cintuveḷi nākarikam paravi irunta uṇmai veḷippaṭuvataṟku muṉṉarē-kaṅkai āṟṟup pāyccal nāṭukaḷil cempiṉālāya vāṭkaḷum, varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṉ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa kattikaḷum, maṉita uruvampōlac ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. maṉita uruvam pōṉṟa poruḷ moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaitta muttiraikaḷ cilavaṟṟuḷ kāṇappaṭum maṉita uruvam pōṉṟē iruntaṉa.

jeṉaral kaṉṉiṅkām eṉpārum piṟarum kaṅkai āṟṟup paḷḷattākkukaḷākiya kājippūr, kāci ākiya kōṭṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa pala iṭaṅkaḷil nikaḻttiya ārāycci mūlam, cempiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa karuvikaḷ maṭṭum iṉṟip pala oviyaṅkaḷum civappuk kaṟkaḷālāya maṇikaḷum, cintu āṟṟup paḷḷattākkukaḷil kiṭaittavaṟṟai otta pala poruḷkaḷum veḷippaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. vāraṇāci kācik kōṭṭattil cila cempuk karuvikaḷum cakkimukkik kaṟkaḷum kiṭaittaṉa. ivaipōlavē, kaṅkai āṟṟiṉ etir karaiyiluḷḷa kājippūrk kōṭṭattilum pala poruḷkaḷ kiṭaittirukkiṉṟaṉa.

keḷacāmpi tōṉṟiya kālam

aikkiya maṇṭilattiṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷil paṇṭaikkālap poruḷkaḷ pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ōriṭattil oṉpatu vāyccikaḷ uṭpaṭap patiṉāṟu ceppuk karuvikaḷ kiṭaittaṉa; irupuṟamum avvāṟu kaṇaikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya cempālāya ampu muṉaikaḷ akappaṭṭaṉa.vaṭamaturaiyil oriṭattil irunta maṇmēṭṭait tōṇṭiyapōtu cempālāya pala vāyccikaḷum ampu, vēl, pōṉṟa eṟipaṭaikaḷum kiṭaittiṉa. ‘ṣājahāṉpūr’ kōṭṭattil cempālāya īṭṭimuṉai oṉṟu kiṭaittatu. intac cempu īṭṭi ippoḻutu ilakṣmaṇapuri-poruṭ kāṭci nilaiyattil kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟatu. itupōla, ‘iṭṭavā’ kōṭṭattil kiṭaitta piṟitōr īṭṭi ippoḻutu piriṭṭiṣ poruṭkāṭci nilaiyattil uḷḷatu. piṟitōr iṭattil maṉita uruvam pōṉṟa karuvi oṉṟum nīṇṭa kaṉatta vāḷ oṉṟum kiṭaittaṉa. kaṅkai āṟṟaṅkaraiyil uḷḷa ōriṭattil pala ampu muṉaikaḷum kalluḷikaḷum kiṭaittaṉa. kāṉpūr kōṭṭattil cila iṭaṅkaḷil cempālāya maṉita uruvaṅkaḷum ampu muṉaikaḷum īṭṭikaḷum kalluḷikaḷum kiṭaittaṉa. piṟitōriṭattil ciṟuttuk kuṟukiya uḷi oṉṟu kiṭaittatu. iḵtu 12.5 ce. mī. uyaramuṭaiya kalluḷi ākum. intac cempuk kālattiṟṟāṉ keḷacāmpi eṉṉum varalāṟṟuc ciṟappuṭaiya nakaram kaṭṭappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu irāvpakatūr tīkṣajit karutukiṉṟār. sar jāṉ mārṣal eṉṉum aṟiñar ‘pīṭṭar’ eṉṉum ūril oru pakutiyai maṭṭum akaḻntu ārāycci naṭattiyataṉ mūlam, kallālum cempālum ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟaik kaṇṭeṭuttaṉar.

pīkār maṇṭilattup putai poruḷkaḷ

pīkār maṇṭilattil kaṅkaiyum cōṉaiyāṟum kalakkum iṭattil mōriyar talainakaramāṉa pāṭalipuram amaintiruntatu. atu carittirap pukaḻpeṟṟa paṇṭai nakaramākum.aḵtu iṉṟuḷḷa pāṭalipurattiṟkum hājippūrkkum iṭaiyil irunta nakaram. ataṉ perum pakuti kaṅkaiyāṟṟāl cētamuṟṟuviṭṭatu eṉṟu aṅkuḷḷār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. eṉiṉum, ārāycciyāḷar nilattai akaḻntu pārttapōtu, acōkaṉatu araṇmaṉaiyiṉ citaivukaḷaiyum piṟa ariya periya poruḷkaḷaiyum kaṇṭeṭuttaṉar. ippoḻutuḷḷa putiya pāṭalipura nakara capaiyiṉar aṇmaiyil putai kaḻinīr vaṭikālkaḷai nilattiṟku aṭiyil amaippataṟkāka nilattai akaḻnta pōtu mōriya vamicattavar.innāṭṭaiāṭcipurinta kālattiṟku uriyapaṇṭaik kuṟiyīṭukaḷ palavaṟṟaik kaṇṭeṭuttaṉar.[3]

naṭu maṇṭilattu veḷḷit tāmpāḷaṅkaḷ

naṭu maṇṭilattil or iṭattil orē camayattil 424 cempuk karuvikaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. cōṭṭā nākapurippakutiyil cempuk kālattiṟku uriya iṭaṅkaḷ pala iṉṉum akaḻntu pārkka vēṇṭupavaiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. immaṇṭilattil ōr ūril, tiruttamākac ceytu muṭikkappaṭāta uḷikaḷ mūṉṟu uṭpaṭap pala ciṟu cempuk karuvikaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. ārāycciyāḷar ivaṟṟai mikavum mukkiyamāṉavai eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. cōṭṭā nākapuriyiṉ teṉpākattil cempuc curaṅkaḷ iruppatāl, anta veḷiyil mēlum pala iṭaṅkaḷil paṇṭaik kālap poruḷkaḷō aṉṟic cempuc curaṅkaṅkaḷō iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar niṉaikkiṉṟaṉar. itē maṇṭilattil piṟitōriṭattil 162 veḷḷit tāmpāḷaṅkaḷ akappaṭṭaṉa. intiyāvil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa cempukkālattiṟku uriya poruḷkaḷuḷ ivaitām mikac ciṟantavai eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. jappalpūril veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭa kaltaccar uḷi oṉṟu kiṭaittatu. ivvuḷi nīṇṭu vaḷaintatāyk kūriya muṉai uṭaiyatāy irukkiṟatu.[4]

orissāvil iraṭṭaik kōṭarikaḷ

orissā (uriyā) maṇṭilattaic cērnta ‘mayūrppañc’, camattāṉattil irupuṟamum kūrmai uṭaiya cempālākiya iraṭṭaik kōṭarikaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. inta mākāṇattil kanta kiri (Khanda Giri) eṉṉum malaip piratēcattil aḻakiya vēlaippāṭamainta malaik kukaikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. inta iṭam ārāyccikku mikavum uriyatākum.[5] vaṅkāḷattilum cempālāṉa karuvikaḷ cila kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. ippakutikaḷil virivāṉa muṟaiyil ārāycci naṭaipeṟiṉ varalāṟu eḻutat toṭaṅkappaṭṭa kālattiṟkum ataṟku muṉṉaiya kālattiṟkum uḷḷa uṟavupaṟṟiya tuṇukkaṅkaḷ palavum, cempuk kālattil nam nāṭṭiṉ evvep pakutikaḷil makkaḷ evvennilaiyil iruntaṉar eṉpatum, ataṟkuppiṟ paṭṭa kāla vivaraṅkaḷum kiṭaikkappeṟalām eṉpatu aṟiñarkaḷ karuttākum. -

ṭekkāṉ

intiya nāṭṭiṉ vaṭapuṟattuc ceyti ivvaṇṇam iruppat teṉpuṟattaik kavaṉippōm; narumatai, tapati ākiya āṟukaḷ pāyum iṭaṅkaḷ payaṉ aṟṟavaiyā? ceyppūr, pikkāṉīr, jeysālmar, kāmpē vaḷaikuṭā, purōc ākiya iṭaṅkaḷaic cūḻntuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷ aṟiñar kavaṉattai īrkkavillaiyō? ‘illai’ eṉṟu kūṟiviṭal muṭiyātu. inta iṭaṅkaḷilum, cintu veḷi nākarikam paravi iruntatu eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ pala kiṭaittal kūṭum aṟiñar ituvarai ippakuti kaḷil tīviramāka ārāycci nikaḻttavillai. eṉiṉum, īṇṭum cintu veḷi nākarikam paravittāṉ iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu terikiṟatu. cintu āṟṟup pāṅkil vāḻnta makkaḷ kaṉi poruḷkaḷukkākavum vāṇipam paṟṟiyum intap pakutikaḷil vāḻntirunta makkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭṭuṟavu peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. kaṅkai āṟṟup paḷḷattākkukaḷaiviṭa intappakutikaḷil, ikkaruttai meyppippataṟku vēṇṭiya cāṉṟukaḷ pala veḷippaṭum eṉa ārāycciyāḷar nampukiṉṟaṉar.appaṇṭaik kālac civappumaṇ kaṟkaḷum, ceṅkaṟkaḷum, civappu maṇṇālāya maṇikaḷum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum, piṟa perruḷkaḷum karampē, purōc ākiya tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ mūlamākavē, veḷināṭṭārukku aṉuppappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttu cintu veḷi nākarik kālattil inta paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ veḷināṭṭu vāṇipattiṉ poruṭṭup peruñ ciṟappup peṟṟavaiyākavē amaintiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ārāyciciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

vintamalaip pakutikaḷil putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ kaiyāṇṭa nalluruvil amaintuḷḷa iyantiraṅkaḷ, kal uḷikaḷ, tāḻikaḷ, uralkaḷ, palvēṟu vakaippaṭṭa piṟa poruḷkaḷ, uyartara maṇikaḷālāṉa poruḷkaḷ, cittira varṇa vēlaippāṭamainta kalaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa kukaikaḷiliruntum maṇ mēṭukaḷiliruntum eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. kattiyavāril uḷḷa ‘limpṭi’ nāṭṭil ‘araṅppūr’ eṉṉum iṭattiṟ kiṭaitta ērāḷamāṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum piṟa poruḷkaḷum moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaittavai pōlavē iruttal kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. ippakutiyil mēlum virivāṉa ārāycci naṭaipeṟiṉ, cintu veḷi nākarikam narumatai-tapati yāṟukaḷ pāykiṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum paraviyirunta paṇpu pulaṉākum.

parōṭāvil putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ kaiyāṇṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ peruvāriyākak kiṭaittaṉa.avai cittirattai yuṭaiya palaniṟap pāṇṭaṅkaḷ cittiram illāta pāṇṭaṅkaḷ eṉa iruvakai yākum. avaṟṟuḷḷum karaṭumuraṭāṉavai, maḻa maḻappu uṭaiyavai, kaṇṇukku iṉiyavai, cittiram tīṭṭap peṟṟavai eṉa nāṉku vakai uṇṭu. ivai nērē kaṇṭu iṉpuṟat takkavaiyē aṉṟi eḻuttāl aṟintu iṉpuṟuttakkavai alla.

haitarāpātil pala iṭaṅkaḷil ittkaiya pal vakaippaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ‘pallārik kōṭṭattil uḷḷa irāmaṉ turkka malaikaḷaic cārnta tārvār-pāṟaikaḷiṉ mēṟkuppuṟa ellaiyil, putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ vaittirunta palavakai niṟaṅkaḷ koṇṭa kaḷimaṇ poruḷkaḷum piṟavum peruvāriyākak kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa’, eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

maicūr nāṭṭilum muṉ coṉṉa pala niṟappāṇṭaṅkaḷ pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa; paḻaiya kaṟkāla makkaḷum putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷum kaṭṭiviṭṭuc ceṉṟa oru vakaik kaṟkōvilkaḷum cavak kuḻikaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.

ceṉṉai maṇṭilam

pallāri, aṉantappūr, kaṭappai, karnūl eṉṉum nāṉku kōṭṭaṅkaḷilum putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷiṉ maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mēṟcoṉṉa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷaik koṇṭaṉa vākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. peruṅ kaṟkaḷai aṭukkik kaṭṭappaṭṭa palavakaic camātikaḷ pallārik kōṭṭattil mikkuḷḷaṉa. aṅku itukāṟum 2000kku mēṟpaṭṭa camātikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉa jē.ci.pireḷaṉ eṉṉum ārāycciyāḷar aṟaintuḷḷaṉar. aṉantappūrkkōṭṭattil ‘hintuppūr’ eṉṉum pukai vaṇṭi nilaiyattukkuk kiḻakkē 7 ki. mī. tolaivil ‘temakeṭiyapallē (paḷḷi)’ eṉṉum ciṟṟuār uḷatu.ataṉ kiḻakkilum teṟkilum pala ciṟiya kuṉṟukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avai ēṟattāḻa orē toṭarcciyāka uḷḷaṉ. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu kukaikaḷākac ceyyappaṭa muṟpaṭṭatu eṉpataṟkuriya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ teṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅku nāṟṟuk kaṇakkāṉa kalluḷikaḷ, vēṟu pala kaṟkaruvikaḷ, cuttikaḷ mutaliyaṉa akappaṭṭaṉa. kuṇṭakkal pukaivaṇṭi nilaiyattiṟku arukil maraccippu oṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭatu. atu putiya kaṟkālap peṇkaḷatu kūntal alaṅkārattiṟku vēṇṭap paṭṭatākum. aḵtu eṭukkappaṭṭa iṭattil matippukkuriya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ palavum tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. karnūl kōṭṭattil uḷḷa pillacurkkam kukaiyil paṟkaḷāl āṉa tāyittukaḷ kiṭaittaṉa.[6] ittakaiya malai muḻaikaḷai ārāyvatāl pala paṇṭaip poruḷkaḷ kiṭaikkum. ‘malaippiratēcaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kāṭukaḷil añcātu nuḻaintu, kukaikaḷaik kaṇṭaṟintu, ārāycci nikaḻttutal aṟiñar kaṭaṉākum eṉṟu puṭ (Foote) eṉṉum ārāycciyaṟiñar aṟaintuḷḷaṉar. kiruṣṇā cillāvil ‘kuṭivāṭā’vil irukkum periya maṇmēṭukaṭkuk kiḻakkup pakkattil, putiya kaṟkālattukku uriya cittiram tīṭṭap peṟṟa ‘kutir’ oṉṟu akappaṭṭatu. iḵtu ētēṉum oru kūlap poruḷaic cēmittu vaikkappeṟum periya ‘kutir’ ākum. itil paṭṭaiyāka niṟam tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. appaṭṭaikku iṇṭayil palavaṇṇa vēlaippāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paṇpaṭṭa kaḷimaṇ kāppukaḷ pala iṭaṅkaḷil akappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉmītu vēlaippāṭukaḷ niṟaintuḷḷaṉa. cippi vaḷaiyalkaḷ palavakai vēlaippāṭukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiyaṉavākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.

ceṉṉaikku aṭutta pallāvarattil kaḷimaṇṇum maṇalum kalantu ceyyappeṟṟa (terra cotta) maṉita uruvam oṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ kālkaḷ kuṭṭaiyāka iruntaṉa. aṅkup piṇam putaikkum tāḻikaḷ palavuṅ kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. cēlattil pallāri mutaliya kōṭṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭaitta maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷaip pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷē mikutiyākak kiṭaittaṉ, cenniṟa maṇṇālāya peṇ uruvaṅkaḷ cila kiṭaittaṉa. ippeṇ uruvaṅkaḷiṉ kūntal alaṅkāram ārāycciyāḷar karuttaik kavarntatu. kūntal pala curuḷ curuḷākat talaimuḻuvatum curuṭṭappaṭṭu, accuruḷkaḷ mītu uyarnta cīppukaḷ cerukappaṭṭamātiri kāṇappaṭṭatu.[7] ivvakai alaṅkāram koḷḷum peṇkaṭkuk kaḻuttaṇi (aṭṭikai pōṉṟatu) avaciyamākum. peṇkaḷ uṟaṅkumpoḻutum akkūntaṟ curuḷkaḷ aviḻāmal iruttaṟku kaḻuttaṇiyē vēṇṭaṟpālatu. iṅṅaṉam utavum kaḻuttaṇi oṉṟu maicūrppakutiyil kāviriyāṟṟiṉ karai arukil tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭatu.cēlam kōṭṭattil kuṭicaikaḷaip pōṉṟa piṇappeṭṭikaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkapṭṭaṉa.ivaipōṉṟavai pala kūrccarattilum akappaṭṭaṉa.[8]

makiḻccikkuriya maṇmēṭu

kōyamutturk kōṭṭam pallaṭam tālukāvil uḷḷa ceṭṭipāḷaiyam eṉṉum ūrukku aṇmaiyil vayal veḷiyil oru maṇmēṭu irukkiṉṟatu. ataṉ nīḷam 2550 ce.mī; akalam 2130 ce. mī. ataṉ uyaram mikak kuṟaivākum.ataṉ oru puṟam ciṟiya kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷiṉ kuviyal irukkiṉṟatu. ammēṭuḷḷa iṭittil maṭpāṇṭac citaivukaḷum pala vakai ulōkap poruḷkaḷum elumpukaḷum kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. mēṭṭiṉ naṭu iṭattil kaṟkukai pōṉṟu oṉṟu kāṭci aḷittukkoṇṭu iruntatu. ārāycciyāḷar ataṉait tōṇṭip pārtta pōtu itu kallālāṉa piṇakkōvil[9] eṉpatu terintatu. aḵtu oru nīḷa catura aṟaiyākum; 240 ce.mī. nīḷamum 142.5 ce.mī akalamum 15 ce. mī. uyaramum uṭaiyatu; peruṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. ataṉ kūraiyāka amainta kal 322.5 ce. mī nīḷamum 20 ce. mī. akalamum 30 ce. mī. uyaramum koṇṭiruntatu. akkōviliṉ uṭpuṟattil palavakaip paṇṭaipporuḷkaḷ iruntaṉa. maḻamaḻappāṉa kaṟuppuniṟa maṇpāṇṭaṅkaḷ nalla nilaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭṭamai kuṟippiṭattakkatu: nīr aruntum cempuk kōppai oṉṟu kiṭantatu; vēṟoru kōppaiyiṉ mūṭimītu āṭu (māṉ?) niṟpatu pōlac cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu; ik kōppai pāracīkattil “lūrittāṉ’ eṉṉum iṭattil kiṭaitta veṇkalap pāttiraṅkaḷ niṉaippūṭṭuvatu’ eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar.ivai kiṭaitta maṇmēṭṭukku arukil vēṟu pala maṇmēṭukaḷ āṅkāṅku irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivai aṉaittum ārāyccikku uṭpaṭumāyiṉ varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa teṉ intiya nākarikattai aṟiya vacati uṇṭākum eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[10]

putukkōṭṭaiyil tāḻikaḷ

putukkōṭṭaiyait tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ putai poruḷ ārāyccikkuriya tiruppatiyākac collalām. aṅku muṉ coṉṉa palavakai maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. aṅkup putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷuṭaiya piṇam putaikkum palavakai muṟaikaḷaik kāṇalām. maṇ tāḻiyil uṭal uṭkāra vaikkap paṭṭu, ataṉmēl maṇal pātiyaḷavu parappapṭṭu, avvaḷavukku mēl ariciyum piṟa kūlapporuḷkaḷum koṇṭa taṭṭoṉṟu vaikkappaṭṭut tāḻi putaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. attāḻiyiṉ pakkaṅkaḷil iṟantavaṉ payaṉpaṭuttiya kaṟkaruvikaḷum piṟavum vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tāḻi, maṇal nirampap parappappaṭṭu mūṭiyiṭṭup putaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tāḻiyaip putaitta kuḻi, maṇal pōṭappaṭṭup pāṟaiyāl mūṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ap pāṟaiyiṉ mītu mīṇṭum maṇalkoṭṭip pāti muṭṭaivaṭivam pōṉṟa pāṟai oṉṟāl mūṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.ippāṟaiyaic cuṟṟi oru muḻam uyaramuṭaiya kaṟkaḷ putaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. putukkōṭṭai camattāṉattil nīrppacaiyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷiṉ arukirla āyirakkaṇakkāṉa tāḻikaḷ putaikkappeṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷ pala kaṟkaḷ varai uḷḷaṉa. ivviṭaṅkaḷ, cuṟṟilum uḷḷa taraiyaiviṭc ciṟitu uyarntuḷḷaṉa. ippakutikaḷai mēlākat taṭṭiṉāl avaṟṟiṉ ocai naṉku kēṭkum. ittāḻikaḷ uruvattil palavāṟu vēṟupaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; cila 120 ce. mī. uyaramum 105 ce. mī. kuṟukkaḷavum koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. [11]cila tāḻikaḷ kōṭukaḷaik koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. irumpuk kālattuc cavap peṭṭikaḷ iraṇṭāka pakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oṉṟil piṇamum maṟṟoṉṟil avviṟantavar payaṉpaṭuttiya poruḷkaḷum vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. intat tāḻikaḷ uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷaic cōtittup pārttāl, paṇṭaikkāla makkaḷaippaṟṟic cuvai payakkum ceytikaḷ pala naṉkuṇaralām.

tiruccirāppaḷḷi, tirunelvēlik kōṭṭaṅkaḷilum mēṟcoṉṉa palavakaiyāṉavaiyum pala niṟamuḷḷavaiyumāṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. tirunelvēlik kōṭṭattaic cērnta āticca nallūril tāmiraparaṇiyāṟṟuk karaiyōram, ēkkar oṉṟil ēṟakkuṟaiya āyiram tāḻikaḷ vītam 114 ēkkarkaḷil tāḻikaḷ putaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cilavaṟṟuḷ irumpu, veṇkalam, poṉ ivaṟṟālāṉa poruḷkaḷ iruntaṉa. kuṭicait tāḻikaḷ (hut urns) cila cintu maṇṭilattil uḷḷa pirāmaṇa pāttilum kiṭaittuḷḷamai kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ṭekkāṉ pakuti vaṭa intiyāvaiviṭap paḻamaiyāṉatu; vaṭa intiyā kaṭaluḷ mūḻkiyiruntapōtu ṭekkāṉ pīṭapūmi taṉittiruntatu. ātaliṉ, atuvē putaiporuḷ ārāyccikkup peritum poruttamuḷḷa iṭam ākum eṉṟu nila nūl nipuṇar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar.

tiruvitāṅkūr

itu paṇṭaic cēranāṭṭiṉ perum pakutiyākum. ippakutiyil malaikaḷ pala. ammalaikaḷ mītum, avaṟṟai aṭuttuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilum paḻaiya kaṟkāla maṉitarum putiya kaṟkāla maṉitarum irumpukkāla maṉitarum iruntamaikkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaittavaṇṇam irukkiṉṟaṉa. ammakkaḷ, iṟantavar uṭalaṅkaḷai erittu uṇṭāṉa cāmpalaiyum elumpukaḷaiyum perum tāḻikaḷil aṭaittu avviṟantavar payaṉpaṭuttiya palavakaik karuvikaḷum pāṇṭaṅkaḷum piṟavum attāḻikaḷuḷ aṭakkip putaittaṉar. avviṭaṅkaḷukkumēl oḻuṅkāṉa pāṟaik kaṟkaḷai naṭṭuc ciṟu maṇṭapaṅkaḷ amaittuḷḷaṉar. ittakaiya maṇṭpaṅkaḷ pala malaiyaraiyar, mutuvar eṉṉum nākarikamaṟṟa makkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ōriṭattil naṭattiya ārāycciyil akappaṭṭa tāḻiyiṉ vāy 37.5 ce.mī. aṅkulam kuṟukkaḷavu uṭaiyatu; 192 ce. mī. uyaramuṭaiyatu. ataṉuḷ eṭṭuc ciṟu tāḻikaḷum, uṇavuṇṇum kalaṉkaḷum, nīraruntum kalaṉkaḷum, cāṭikaḷum, caṭṭikaḷum iruntaṉa. avaiyāvum karuppuk kaḷimaṇṇālum civappuk kaḷimaṇṇālum ceyyappaṭṭavai. attāḻiyiṉ mēṟpuṟattil 75 ce.mī nīḷamuḷḷa vāḷ oṉṟu kiṭaittatu.ataṉ kaippiṭi aḻintatu pōlum! attāḻikkuḷ iraṇṭu irumpu īṭṭit talaikaḷ kiṭantaṉa. piṟitōr iṭattil veṇkala viḷakkoṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkppaṭṭatu. ittakaiya paḻaṅkālap putaipporuḷkaḷaippaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ ārāycciyāḷarāl avvappoḻutu veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.

ṭekkāṉ pakuti taṉittiruntatā?

paḻaiya putiya kaṟkālaṅkaḷilum ivaṟṟai aṭuttut tōṉṟiya irumpuk kālattilum kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷaip piṟa nāṭukaḷiṟ kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷōṭu vaittu ārāykaiyil, ṭekkāṉ pakuti veḷi nāṭukaḷōṭu toṭarpuṟṟu iruntatu eṉṉum pēruṇmaiyai uṇaralākum: (1) pallāvarattil akap paṭṭa maṉita uruvam irākkil uḷḷa ‘pāktāt’ nakaril kaṇṭeṭukkappeṟṟa uruvaṅkaḷai ottuḷḷatu kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. (2)kāl koṇṭa maṭpāṇṭap poruḷkaḷ pala heṉṟik ṣlīmaṉ eṉpārāl tōṇṭi eṭukkappeṟṟa ‘ṭrāy’ nakarattil kiṭaitta maṭpāṇṭap poruḷkaḷai ottuḷḷaṉa. (3) maicūrp pakutiyil kaṇṭu eṭukkappeṟṟa ‘svastikā’ vaṭivil amainta poruḷ, ‘ṭarāy’ nakaril akappaṭṭa svastikaḷ vaṭivattaip peritum ottuḷḷatu.[12] (4) ‘ceṭṭipāḷaiyattiṟ kiṭaitta māṉ allatu āṭu niṟpatāka uṭaiya kōppai mūṭi oṉṟu pāracīkattillūristāṉil kiṭaitta veṇkalap poruḷkaḷai ottuḷḷatu. iṉṉa piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷāl appaṇṭaik kāla makkaḷ āciya, āppirikka, airōppiya nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ aṟiviyal tuṟaiyilum vāṇipat tuṟaiyilum nerukkamāṉa toṭarpu koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṉum pēruṇmai pulaṉākum.[13]

paṇṭait tamiḻ nakaraṅkaḷ

‘vaṭaintiyāvai viṭatteṉṉāṭu paḻaimai vāyntatātaliṉ, meyyāṉa ārāyccikku uriya iṭam teṉṉāṭē ākum’ eṉṟu, nilanūṟ pulavar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatai muṉṉarē kuṟippiṭṭōm allamō? ātalāl, muṉ coṉṉa teṉpakutikaḷil ārāycci ceytal iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum. mēlum, tamiḻakattai āṇṭa muṭiyuṭai mūvēntar tam talainakaraṅkaḷil pērārāycci naṭattal mikavum iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum. cōḻanāṭṭil uṟaiyūr, tiruvārūr, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam eṉpaṉa mikka paḻaimai vāyntaṉa. kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam neṭuṅkālamāka mēṉāṭukaḷuṭaṉum kīḻnāṭukaḷuṭaṉum vāṇipam ceytu vanta nakaramākum. atu ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil kaṭalukku iraiyāṉateṉṟu maṇimēkalai kūṟukiṉṟatu. tirumaṟaikkāṭu eṉappaṭum vētāraṇyam paḻaiya kālattil oru tuṟaimukap paṭṭiṉamāka iruntatu. anta iṭattiṟkum yāḻppāṇattiṟkum neṭunturamillai iṉṟum ciṟuvaṇikar tam poruḷkaḷaip paṭakukaḷil ēṟṟik koṇṭu yāḻpāṇat tīvukaṭkuc ceṉṟu vāṇipam ceykiṉṟaṉar. at tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉamum ārāyccikkuriyatākum. ataip pōlavē pāṇṭiyar paḻampatiyākiya alaivāy (kapāṭapuram) kaṭal kōḷāl aḻivuṟṟateṉṟu iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uraiyum, cilappatikāra uraiyum ceppukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭṭiṉam ciṟanta nākarikattōṭu irunta pāṇṭiyar paḻampati eṉṟu vāṉmīki irāmāyaṇam kūṟukiṉṟatu. irāmāyaṇattiṉ kālam ēṟakkuṟaiya ki.mu.1000kku muṟpaṭṭateṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē, irāmaṉatu cama kālattavarākiya vāṉmīki muṉivar coṉṉa ki.mu.1000kku muṟpaṭṭa ‘alaivāy’ irunta iṭam eṅkē? eṉpatai aṟiñar tēṭippārttu, avviṭattai ārāytal avaciyamaṉṟō? koṟkai, muttukkup peyarpōṉa paḻampaṭṭiṉamākum. iṅkiruntutāṉ vētakāla āriyarkku muttukkaḷ aṉuppappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum’ eṉpar tiru.pi.ṭi.cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār. eṉavē, koṟkaiyum mikka pḻaimai vāynta pakutiyākum.

cēra nāṭṭil arapikkaṭalil tuṟaimukap paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷāka viḷakkamuṟṟu iruntavai muciṟi, toṇṭi mutaliyaṉa. aṅkiruntu urōmap perunāṭṭiṟkum piṟa iṭaṅkaṭkum miḷaku, tantam, tēkku, akil, vācaṉaipporuḷkaḷ mutaliyaṉa ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. cumār ki.mu.1000il vāḻntirunta cālamaṉ maṉṉarkku mayil mutaliyaṉa aḷitta iṭam cēra nāṭākum. eṉavē, ittuṟaimukap paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷum paḻaimai vāyntaṉavē ākum. kocci nāṭṭil aḻinta nilaiyil uḷḷa vañci mānakaram paḻaiya cērar talainakaramākum. atu ki.mu.-vilēyē peruñ ciṟappu vāynta nakaramāka iruntatu. ataṉaiyum akaḻntu ārāycci purital naṟpalaṉai aḷikkum. ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu jillāvil uḷḷa mahāpalipuram oraḷavu paḻaimai vāynta tuṟaimukap paṭṭiṉam. aṅku arēpiyāviliruntu kutiraikaḷ iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.

ivaiyellām ki.mu.4 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉṉarē peruñ ciṟappuppeṟṟa paṇṭai nakaraṅkaḷ ākum. iv viṭaṅkaḷil virivāṉa ārāycci nikaḻttal cintu veḷi ārāyccikkup peruntuṇai purivatākum! tamiḻakattup paḻaimaiyaiyum kaṇikkap pērutavi purivatākum.

caṅku cāṉṟu pakarum

cintu āṟṟup pāyccal peṟṟa pakutikaḷil nikaḻttiya ārāycciyiliruntu, aṅku vāḻntirunta makkaḷ caṅkaip peritum payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉpatai aṟikiṟōm. ‘intac caṅkukaḷ cintuveḷi makkaḷukku eṅṅaṉam kiṭaittaṉa? ivaṟṟaip payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkam avarkaḷukku eṅkiruntu vantatu? caṅkaip payaṉpaṭuttum muṟai eṅkup peruki iruntatu? eṉpavaṟṟaik kavaṉittōmāyiṉ, cintu veḷi makkaḷukkum tamiḻnāṭṭu makkaḷukkum irunta toṭarpu teṟṟeṉat teriyum.

intiya nāṭṭiṉ teṉkiḻakkukkōṭiyil(tamiḻ nāṭṭil) vāḻntirunta tamiḻ makkaḷiṭamiruntē caṅkukaḷ cintup piratēcaṅkaḷil vāḻntirunta makkaḷukkup pōyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[14] ‘caṅku aṟukkum kulattavar eṉṟē oru pakuti makkaḷ, tamiḻ nāṭṭil mikap piṟpaṭṭa kālam varaiyilum iruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. caṅkukaḷum muttukkaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ ciṟanta vāṇipap poruḷkaḷāka iruntaṉa eṉpataic caṅka nūlkaḷālum piṟa cāṉṟukaḷālum aṟiyalām.

“vēḷāp pārppāṉ vāḷaran tumitta

vaḷaikaḷain toḻinta koḻuntu”





eṉṉum akanāṉūṟṟu aṭikaḷ cintikkaṟpālaṉa.

mēṉāṭṭār muyaṟci

ittakaiya ārāyccit toṇṭil namnāṭṭuc celvarkaḷ viruppaṅ koḷvatillai. mēṉāṭukaḷil uḷḷa celvarkaḷō iṅṅaṉam iruppatillai, putumaikaḷaik kāṇuvatil oru cārār īṭupaṭṭāl, toṉmaic ceytikaḷai aṟivatil piṟitoru cārār īṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivvulakiṉ pala mūlaikaḷukkum ceṉṟu āṅkāṅkuḷḷa viyattaku vivaraṅkaḷai ellām kaṇṭaṟintu, appōtaikkappōtuveḷiyiṭutaṟku eṉṟu mēṉāṭṭuccelvar cilar kūṭi ōr iyakkam tōṟṟuvittuḷḷaṉar. avviyakkattiṉar, tāṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkum putumaikaḷaiyellām avvāṟu āṅkāṅkē paṭam piṭittuc ceṉṟu, avaṟṟiṟku uriya vivaraṅkaḷuṭaṉ tiṅkaḷtōṟum or itaḻāka veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar.maṟṟoru piriviṉar ulakiluḷḷa uyarnta malaikaḷ mītēṟi āṅkuk kiṭaikkum aṟputa vivaraṅkaḷaic cērttu veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. ulakil mika uyarntatum, itukāṟum evarālum ataṉ ucciyil ceṉṟu kāṇutaṟku iyalātatumāka viḷaṅkip perumai peṟṟirukkum imayamalaimītu ēṟutaṟku mēṉāṭṭu aṟiñar palar muyaṉṟuḷḷaṉar; iṉṟum muyaṉṟu varukiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟum oru kūṭṭattiṉar kaṭaliṉ aṭippakutikaḷiṟ ceṉṟu, aṅku vāḻum uyirkaḷiṉ aṟputac ceyalkaḷaiyum nikaḻccikaḷaiyum aṟintu, avaṟṟiṉai veḷippaṭutti varukiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉamē nilattai akaḻntu maṇṇukkuḷ maṟaintu kiṭakkum pala paṇṭaip poruḷkaḷai veḷippaṭutti ārāycci ceyvatilum ammēṉāṭṭu makkaḷ ārvaṅkāṭṭi varukiṉṟaṉar.

nammavar kaṭamai

mēṉāṭṭāraip pōla nam nāṭṭuc celvarkaḷum aṟiñarkaḷum ittakaiya tuṟaikaḷil ārvaṅ kāṭṭi uṟcākamum ūkkamuṅ koṇṭu uḻaittāl, piṟa nāṭukaḷaiviṭa nam nāṭu pala vakaiyāṉa aṟputa vivaraṅkaḷai ulakukku viḷakki viyakkavaikkum eṉpatil aiyamillai.



↑ P.T.S.Iyengar’s ‘Stone Age in India’, p.51

↑ ippaṇṭai makkaḷ yāvar eṉpatai iṟutip pakutiyil ārāyvōm.

↑ L.A. Waddell’s ‘Report on the Excavations at Pataliputra’.

↑ K.N.Dikshit’s ‘Pre-historic Civilization of the Indus Valley’, pp.54, 55.

↑ M.M.Ganguly’s ‘Orissa and her Remains’.

↑ puliyiṉ pallaik kayiṟṟil kōttu aṇital paṇṭait tamiḻar marapu. atu pulippal tāli eṉappaṭum.

↑ itē kūntal alaṅkāramum cippuc cerukalum cintu veḷi mātariṭam irunta paḻakkaṅkaḷ ākum eṉpatu iṅku aṟiyat takkatu.

↑ P.T.S.Iyengar’s ‘Stone Age in India’, pp. 19-40.

↑ P.T.S.Iyengar’s ‘Stone age in India’ p.43.

↑ Dikshit’s ‘Pre-historic Civilization of the I. V’, pp. 12, 13.





* * *





﻿4. nakara amaippum āṭci muṟaiyum

maṟaintāriṉ maṇmēṭukaḷ

moheñco-tarō nakaram amaintuḷḷa iṭattil pala maṇmēṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ periyatu 117,000 ce.mī. nīḷamum 60,300 ce mī akalamum uṭaiyatu. itu teṟku vaṭakkāka amaintuḷḷatu. itaṟku 18000 ce mī mēṟkē itaṟkaṭutta periya maṇmēṭu oṉṟu irukkiṉṟatu. ituvum teṟku vaṭakkāka amaintuḷḷatu. itaṉ nīḷam 39,500 ce mī akalam 29700 ce.mī periya mēṭṭiṟku vaṭakkilum kiḻakkilum ciṟiya maṇ mēṭukaḷ pala irukkiṉṟaṉa.mutalil kuṟippiṭṭa iraṇṭuperiya maṇmēṭukaḷum mutalil oṉṟākavē iruntaṉavā. aṉṟi iraṇṭākavē iruntaṉavā eṉpatai iṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟal iyalātu. ivviraṇṭukkum iṭaiyil uḷḷa iṭattait tōṇṭip pārtta piṉṉarē muṭivu kūṟutal iyalum. ivviraṇṭu periya maṇmēṭukaḷum nakaramākavum, cuṟṟiyuḷḷa piṟa ciṟiya maṇ mēṭukaḷ nakarattaic cērnta pakutikaḷākavum irukkalām eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar. immaṇmēṭukaḷ ciṟitumaṅkiya cenniṟattuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

āṟṟōram amainta nakaram

putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷum cempukkāla makkaḷum aṟivu vaḷara vaḷara, āṟṟōraṅkaḷil nakaraṅkaḷai amaittukkoṇṭu vāḻntiruntaṉar eṉṟu mutaṟ pakutiyiṟ kūṟiṉōm. nām kūṟiyāṅkē, ivviru kālaṅkaḷaiyum cērnta cintu nāṭṭu mak kaḷ tam vāḻviṟku uriya iṭaṅkaḷai āṟṟōraṅkaḷil amaittuk koṇṭiruntamai kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. moheñco tarō cintu āṟṟiṉ karaiyil amaintiruntatu. harappā, rāvi āṟṟiṉ karaiyil amaintiruntatu. marakkalap pōkkuvarattu vacati karutiyē innakaraṅkaḷ āṟṟu ōraṅkaḷil amaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. moheñco-tarōvil paṭakukaḷil ēṟṟappaṭum poruḷkaḷ atē āṟṟil yātoru taṭaiyum iṉṟic ceṉṟu arapikkaṭalai aṭaiya vacatiyuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉamē veḷi nāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ kappalkaḷ mūlam vantu cintu maṇṭilat tuṟaimukattai aṭainta piṉ, apporuḷkaḷaip paṭakukaḷ ēṟṟikkoṇṭu moheñco-tarōvai aṭaitalum eḷitāṉatu. ippōkkuvaravu vacati karutiyē appaṇṭai vāṇipam pukaḻ peṟṟa makkaḷ āṟṟōraṅkaḷil aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷai amaittuk koṇṭu vāḻntaṉarātal vēṇṭum.

nakaram amainta iṭam

moheñco-tarō nakaram amaintuḷḷa iṭam mikap paranta camaveḷiyākum. innakaram amaintuḷḷa iṭam oru caturamail parappuṭaiyatu. itil pattil oru pākamē ippoḻutu tōṇṭappeṟṟu ārāycci naṭaipeṟṟuḷḷatu. tōṇṭappaṭāta pakuti 15, 29, 92, 160 ca. ce. mī. parappuṭaiyatu. appakutiyum tōṇṭappeṟṟu oḻuṅkāṉa muṟaiyil ārāycci naṭaipeṟumāyiṉ, innakarattaippaṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷ uḷḷavāṟu uṇartal kūṭum. āyiṉum, iṉṟuḷḷa nilaiyil tōṇṭi eṭukkappeṟṟa pattil oru pākattai ārāynta aḷavil innakarattaip paṟṟiya tam karuttukkaḷai ārāycci yāḷar virivākak kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.

terukkaḷiṉ amaippu

innakarattiṉ terukkaḷ kiḻakku mēṟkākavum, teṟku vaṭakkākavum amaintuḷḷaṉa. teṉkiḻakkup paruvakkāṟṟu, vaṭakiḻakkup paruvakkāṟṟu eṉpavaṟṟaik kavaṉittē - nalla kāṟṟōṭattai etirpārttē itterukkaḷ amaikkappaṭṭaṉa vātalvēṇṭum.kāṟṟunakariṉ akaṉṟaterukkaḷil vīcumpoḻutu, kuṟukkēyuḷḷa ciṟiya terukkaḷilum pukuntu ellā makkaṭkum naṟkāṟṟu nukara vacati uṇṭākal iyalpē. innōkkam paṟṟiyē ciṟiya terukkaḷ periya terukkaḷil vantu kalakkumāṟu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kāṟṟōṭṭam karuti iṅṅaṉam terukkaḷai amaittuḷḷa muṟai, uyariya nākarika makkaḷ eṉap pōṟṟap paṭṭa pāpilōṉiyar nakaraṅkaḷilum kāṇakkūṭavillai eṉpatu kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu.

periya terukkaḷaic ciṟiyaterukkaḷ orē nērāka veṭṭic ceṉṟuḷḷaṉa. oru perunteru mukkāl kal nīḷam uṭaiyatu.itu nakara maṇmēṭṭaiyē iraṇṭākap pirittuḷḷatu. itaṉ akalam 990 ce.mī. itu vaṇṭip pōkkuvaraviṟkē ivvaḷavu akalamāka amaikkappaṭṭiruttalvēṇṭum. itaṉ irupuṟaṅkaḷilum makkaḷ naṭantu cellum naṭaippātaiyum amaintuḷḷatu. itteruvil cintu maṇṭila vaṇṭikaḷ mūṉṟu orē varicaiyiṟ pōka vacati uṇṭeṉṟu aṟiñar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar.itaṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē ciṟiya terukkaḷ pala cantikkiṉṟaṉa. inneṭun teruviṉ mēṟkup puṟattil kuṟippiṭattakka periya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉavātal vēṇṭum. itaiviṭap periya teru oṉṟu ciṟitaḷavē tōṇṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉāl ataippaṟṟi oṉṟum ippoḻutu kūṟutaṟkillai.540 ce.mī akalamuṭaiya terukkaḷ cila irukkiṉṟaṉa. 390 ce.mī. akalamuḷḷa terukkaḷ cila, 270 ce. mī. akalam mutal 360 ce mī akalam varai uḷḷa terukkaḷ pala. 120 ce.mī. akalamuṭaiya cantukaḷ pala ikkuṟunterukkaḷ, neṭun terukkaḷaiyum naṭuttaramāṉa terukkaḷaiyum pala iṭaṅkaḷil iṇaittuḷḷaṉa. potuvākak kūṟiṉ, ellāt terukkaḷukkum. iṇaippu irukkiṉṟatu.

cila terukkaḷ uṭainta ceṅkal tuṇṭukaḷaiyum maṭpāṇṭac citaivukaḷaiyum koṭṭik keṭṭippaṭuttap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pala terukkaḷ puḻutipaṭintuḷḷaṉa. ciṟiya terukkaḷ periya terukkaḷaiviṭanaṉṉilaiyil amaintuḷḷaṉa. perutta vāṇipamē innilaimaikkuk kāraṇamākum. cila cantukaḷiṉ muṉai vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷ mītu, cumaitūkkic ceṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷ uṟaintu ceṉṟa aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vēṟu cila muṉai vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷ vaḷaivākavē amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itaṉāl poti ēṟṟic cellum vilaṅkukaḷum vaṇṭikaḷum cantaiviṭṭut tirumpumpōtu vīṭṭuc cuvaraip paḻutākka iṭamirātaṉṟō? mika nuṭpa aṟivuṭaṉ amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa iccuvar amaippuc cumēriyar nakaramāṉa pukaḻ vāynta ‘ur’ eṉpatil amaintuḷḷa cila muṉai vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷil kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. aiyāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpirunta ippaṇṭai makkaḷ, ivvaḷavu matinuṭpam vāykkap peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉpatai eṇṇa eṇṇa ārāycciyāḷar peruviyappuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.

kālvāy amaippu

moheñco-tarōvil kālvāy illāta neṭun teruvō kuṟunteruvō illai. kālvāykaḷ aṉaittum, orē aḷavil veṭṭic cuṭṭut tēyttu vaḻavaḻap pākkiya ceṅkaṟkaḷāl amaintavai. potuvāka ellāk kālvāykaḷum 50 ce. mī. āḻamum 22 ce. mī akalamum uṭaiyaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. ikkālvāykaḷaip pōlavē illaṅkaḷil amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa ciṟiya kaḻinīrk kālvāykaḷum ittakaiya ciṟanta muṟaiyiṟ ceyyappaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷaik koṇṭē kaṭṭappeṟṟavai ākum. ivvīṭṭu vaṭikālkaḷ terukkālvāyuṭaṉ cērum iṭaṅkaḷil, catura vaṭivil ceṅkaṟkaḷ koṇṭu kaṭṭappeṟṟ ciṟu kuḻikaḷ amain tuḷḷaṉa. akkuḻikaḷ 22 ce. mī. caturamum 45 ce. mī. āḻamum uṭaiyavai. akkuḻikaḷil 90 ce. mī. uyaramuṭaiya tāḻikaḷ putaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. attāḻikaḷiṉ aṭiyil ciṟiya tuḷaikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. vīṭṭu vaṭikālkaḷ vaḻiyē kaḻinīruṭaṉ kuppai kaḷaṅkaḷ vantu tāḻikaḷil viḻutal iyalpu. tāḻikaḷiṉ aṭiyil uḷḷa ciṟiya tuḷaikaḷ vaḻiyē kaḻivunīr toṭṭiyil nirampit teruk kālvāyil kalakkum. annīruṭaṉ vanta kuppai kūḷaṅkaḷ tāḻiyiṉ aṭiyilēyē taṅki viṭum nakarāṇmaik kaḻakap paṇiyāṭkaḷ akkuppai kūḷaṅkaḷai avvappōtu tāḻikaḷiliruntu appuṟappaṭuttit tūymai ceyvar. ‘ā! icciṟanta muṟai vēṟu entap paṇṭai nakarattilum iruntatāka yām kaṇṭatillai; kēṭṭatu millai’ eṉṟu cār jāṉ mārṣal pōṉṟār kūṟip peru viyappu eytiyuḷḷaṉar. ivviyattaku muṟai, nākarikam mikunta ikkālattilum ceṉṉai, kalkattā pōṉṟa periya nakaraṅkaḷil uṇṭē tavirap piṟa paṭṭaṇaṅkaḷil illai eṉpatu kavaṉikkat takkatu. ivvariya kālvāy amaippu muṟai 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa makkaḷāl kaikkoḷḷappaṭṭiruntatu eṉiṉ, avar tam aṟivu nuṭpamum cukātāra vāḻvil avarkkirunta ārvamum veḷḷiṭai malaiyām. ceṉṉai pōṉṟa periya nakaraṅkaḷil nilattiṉ aṭiyil cākkaṭaikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, moheñco-tarōvil taraimītē cākkaṭaikaḷ kaṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. innakarattuc cākkaṭai amaippu muṟaiyē nāḷaṭaivil pātāḷac cākkaṭaikaḷāka māṟiyuḷḷatu eṉpatum kavaṉittaṟ kuriyatu.

cuvarukkuḷ kaḻinīrk kuḻai

mēṉmāṭaṅkaḷiliruntu varum kaḻinīraik kīḻē uḷḷa tāḻiyiṟ koṇṭu cērkkac cuṭṭa kaḷimaṇṇālāya periya kuḻaikaḷ cuvar arukil patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl cila periya vīṭukaḷil ikkuḻaikaḷ veḷiyē tōṉṟātavāṟu cuvarukku uḷḷēyē amaintiruttal viyappiṉum viyappākum. tāḻiyiṉ arukil uḷḷa ikkuḻaikaḷiṉ vāy 8 ce. mī. akalamum 10 ce. mī. nīḷamum uṭaiyaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa.

mūṭappeṟṟa kālvāykaḷ

terukkaḷil oṭum periya kālvāykaḷ mītu nīṇṭa catura vaṭivil 30 ce. mī. nīḷamuṭaiya ceṅkaṟkaḷ patittu neṭuka mūṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil 56.5 ce. mī. nīḷamuḷḷa kaṟkaḷum mūṭuvataṟkākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu uḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ, iṭai iṭaiyē kaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷālum karuṅkaṟkaḷālum, cuṇṇak kaṟkaḷālum ceyyap peṟṟa mūṭikaḷ poruttappeṟṟu irukkiṉṟaṉa. kālvāykaḷil cakati paṭintu kaḻivunīr eḷitil oṭa iyalātavāṟu taṭaiyuṇṭākā tiruttalaip pārttuk koḷḷavē ikkaṟkaḷ aḻuttamāka vaittu mūṭappaṭāmal poruttappeṟṟu uḷḷaṉa eṉpatu eḷitiṟ pulaṉākiṉṟatu.

iṭai iṭaiyē perun toṭṭikaḷ

nīṇṭa kālvāykaḷukku iṭaiyiṭaiyē mūlai muṭukkukaḷil kaḻivunīr tēṅkuvataṟkup peruntoṭṭikaḷ amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiliruntu nīrai vēṟu vaḻiyē koṇṭu cellum kālvāykaḷ toṭṭikaḷuṭaṉ iṇaippuṇṭu irukkiṉṟaṉa. akkuṭṭaikaḷiliruntu nīṇṭa tāṭikaḷai viṭṭu iru puṟattuk kālvāykaḷaiyum iyaṉṟa aḷavu tūymai ceyyavē avai amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. akkuṭṭaikaṭkum akaṉṟa mēlmūṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. appaṇṭaik kāla ulakattaic cērnta enta nākarika nāṭṭilum icciṟanta kālvāy amaippu muṟai illai, illai eṉṟu ārāycci aṟiñar ovvoruvarum viyantu kūṟutal kavaṉittaṟ kuriyatākum.

matakuḷḷa kālvāykaḷ

periya kālvāykaḷ muṭivuṟum iṭaṅkaḷil 120 ce. mī. allatu 150 ce. mī. uyaramum 75 ce. mī. akalamum uḷḷa matakukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ammatakukaṭku mēl vaḷainta uttiraṅkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya kūrai vēyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ittakaiya matakukaḷaiyuṭaiya peruṅ kālvāykaḷ potuvāṉa nāṭkaḷil kaḻivu nīrukkākap payaṉpaṭuvatē pōṉṟu, veḷḷam varum kālaṅkaḷil avveḷḷa nīrai vaṭiyac ceyvataṟkum payaṉ palṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itukāṟum kūṟappeṟṟa ellāp poruḷkaḷum aḻiyāmal uṟutiyāka iruttalaikkoṇṭu ivai evvaḷavu uṟutiyākak kaṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉpatai eḷitil aṟintu koḷḷalām.

paṉmuṟai uyarttap peṟṟa kālvāykaḷ

moheñco-tarō nakarattiṉ vaḷappattiṟkum perumaikkum kāraṇamāk irunta cintu yāṟē ataṉ. aḻiviṟkum kāraṇamāka iruntatu eṉṉum uṇmai, annakaram paṉmuṟai tiruttiyum uyarttiyum amaikkappaṭṭiruppatiliruntu naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu. mutalil annakaraic cama taraiyil amaitta makkaḷ, piṉṉarc cintuyāṟṟiṉ veḷḷak koṭumaikku añci, annakarattiṉ aṭi maṭṭattaic ciṟitu ciṟitāka uyarttik koṇṭē pōyiṉar; piṟaku uyarnta mēṭṭil nakarattaip putuppittuk kaṭṭiṉar. iṅṅaṉam avarkaḷ pala aṭukkukaḷai kaṭṭiṉar eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷarkku naṉku pulaṉākiṟatu. aṟiñar ituvarai ēḻu aṭukkukaḷaik kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar. ‘ēḻāvataṉ aṭiyilum pala aṭukkukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl aṅku nīr ūṟṟam ēṟpaṭutalāl, tōṇṭip pārttal iyalātatāka irukkiṟatu’ eṉṟu. kūṟivarukiṉṟaṉar.[1] ‘mutalil amaikkap peṟṟa nakarttiṉ kālam ēṟakkuṟaiya ki.mu.2800 āka irukkalām; ēḻām muṟai amaikkappeṟṟa nakarattiṉ kālam cumār ki.mu. 2500 āka irukkalām’ eṉṟu makkē pōṉṟa ārāycci aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[2]

ivvāṟu kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ uyara uyarap paḻaiya kālvāykaḷ payaṉila āyiṉa, ataṉāl putiya kālvāykaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.pala iṭaṅkaḷil paḻaiya kālvāykaḷiṉ pakkac cuvarkaḷ uyarttap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil paḻaiya kālvāykaḷ mītē ceṅkaṟkaḷai aṭukkip putiya kālvāykaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila pakutikaḷil paḻaiya kālvāykaḷ iṭittut taḷḷappaṭṭup putiya kālvāykaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam kālvāykaḷ, paṉmuṟai tiruttappeṟṟum putiyaṉavāka amaikkappeṟṟum uḷḷaṉa.[ku 1]

malam kaḻikka otukkiṭam

moheñco-tarōvil uḷḷa perumpālāṉa illaṅkaḷil malam kaḻippataṟku uriya otukkiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai iṉṟum nam nāṭṭil uḷḷaṉa pōlavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ taḷa varicaiyum puṟaccuvarkaḷum cevvaiyākavum uṟutiyākavum amaikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avai nāḷtōṟum nakarāṇmaik kaḻakattār amarttiyirunta tōṭṭikaḷāl tūymai ceyyappeṟṟu vantaṉa eṉpataṟkuriya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ terikiṉṟaṉa. cila māḷikaikaḷiṉ mēṉ māṭaṅkaḷilēyē ittakaiya otukkiṭaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. aṅkiruntu malamum nīrum, cuvarukku uḷḷē allatu veḷiyē amaittuk kīḻē uḷḷa tāḻiyōṭu iṇaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa kuḻai vaḻiyē kīḻē ceṉṟuviṭa vacati yiruntatu eṉpatu terikiṟatu. ivvotukkiṭaṅkaḷ nīrōṭum aṟaiyai aṭuttu amaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai peritum iṉṟu cintu maṇṭilattil uḷḷa malam kaḻikkum iṭaṅkaḷaiyē ottuḷḷamai kuṟippiṭat takkatu.[3]

nakara āṭci muṟai

ikkāla intiyāvil nākarikam mikkuḷḷaṉavākak karutap paṭum nakaraṅkaḷ palavaṟṟilum kāṇa iyalāta cukātāra muṟaikaḷ 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa moheñco-tarōvil mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu peru viyappukku uriyatākum aṉṟō? ovvoru vīṭṭilum cantilum kuṟunteruvilum neṭunteruvilum cukātāra vitikaḷ kaṇṭippākak kaik koḷḷappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṉal oru pōtum mikaiyākātu. neṭun terukkaḷaiviṭak kuṟunterukkaḷum cantukaḷum mikka tuymaiyāka iruntaṉa eṉiṉ immuṟaikku nērmāṟāṉa muṟaiyil ikkāla nakaraṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉ - akkāla makkaḷiṉ oḻuṅkumuṟaiyum, kaṭṭut tiṭṭaṅkaṭku avarkaḷ aḷittu vanta peru matippum, cukātāra muṟaiyil vāḻavēṇṭu meṉṟu avarkaḷ virumpi naṭantuvantamaiyum, nakarāṇmaik kaḻakattāriṉ āṭcit tiṟaṉum naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa allavā? terukkaḷil puḻuti kiḷampātirukka nāḷum taṇṇir teḷikkap paṭṭu vantatu. pattup iitiṉaintu vīṭukaṭku oṉṟāka vaikkap paṭṭirunta toṭṭikaḷilēyē kuppai kūḷaṅkaḷ koṭṭap paṭṭu vantaṉa. nakarattiṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷil ikkālattiya potu iṭaṅkaḷ - ikkālattup pūmpoḻil (Park) pōṉṟavai - iruntaṉa eṉṟu niṉaippataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa.

orunakaram naṉmuṟaiyil amaintuḷḷatu. eṉiṉ, atu paranta iṭattil amaintirutal vēṇṭum nīṇṭu akaṉṟaterukkaḷaip peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum: ellāp pātaikaḷum vilakkiṉṟit tūymaiyāka iruttal vēṇṭum kaḻinīraic cevvaṉē koṇṭu cellum oḻuṅkāṉa kālvāykaḷ amaintiruttal vēṇṭum. vīṭṭu acuttap poruḷkaḷai uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ appuṟappaṭuttum ellā vakai vacatikaḷum naṉku amaintiruttal vēṇṭum; orō vaḻi makkaḷ kalantu iṉpuṟat takka potu iṭaṅkaḷ iruttal vēṇṭum. veḷḷa nīrai uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ veḷikkoṇṭu cellattakka periya cākkaṭaikaḷ naṉmuṟaiyil amaintiruttal vēṇṭum kaḻinīrp.pātaikaḷ mū ppaṭṭiruttal mikka avaciya mākum.ittakaiya ellā vacatikaḷaiyum peṟṟuḷḷa nakarmē cukātāra muṟaiyil amaikkappaṭṭa nakaram eṉappaṭum. ivvacatikaḷ aṉaittum moheñco-tarōvil iruntaṉa. eṉavē, innakara makkaḷ ciṟanta nākarikattaip pēṇiya perumakkaḷ eṉṉalām. innakara āṭciyiṉar, akkāla ulakil irunta piṟa nakara āṭciyiṉaraiviṭa mika uyarnta aṟivuṭaiyavar eṉṉal mikaiyākātu.

cukātāram oru pāl iruppa, nakariṉ pala pākaṅkaḷil kāvaṟ. kūṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatiliruntu, nakara āṭciyiṉar nakarattaip pātukātta muṟaiyum naṉku viḷaṅkum. pala periya terukkaḷiṉ kōṭikaḷil ittakaiya kāvaṟ kūṭaṅkaḷ amaintiruntaṉa. moheñco-tarō vāṇipam mikunta nakaram aṉṟō? aṅku vaṇikar taṅkuvataṟkākap periya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ arukil kāvaṟ kūṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. vāṇipap poruḷkaḷaic cēmittu vaikka viṭutikaḷ pala iruntaṉa; veḷināṭṭu vaṇikarum uḷnāṭṭu vaṇikarum celvapperukku uṭaiyavarkaḷ. ātaliṉ, poruḷukkum celvattukkum ūṟu nērātiruttaṟ poruṭṭē nakara āṭciyiṉar āṅkāṅkuk kāvaṟ kūṭaṅkaḷai amaittuk kāvalāḷikaḷai vaittu, nakarattaik kāttu vantaṉar.

nakarāṇmaik kaḻakattil perumpālār vaṇikarākavē iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. eṉṉai? innakaram vāṇipap. perukka muṭaiya nakaramātaliṉ eṉka. mōriyar āṭcik kālattil irunta makkaḷ piratinitikaḷ aṭaṅkiya maṉṟam[4] allatu kupta maṉṉar āṭcik kālattē irunta nakarāṇmaik kaḻakam[5] mutaliyavai moheñco-tarō pōṉṟa paḻaiya intiya nakaraṅkaḷiliruntē tōṉṟiṉavātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟaintu viyakkiṉṟaṉar.[6]

avaciyamē aṟivai vaḷarppatu

innakarattai amaitta paṇṭai makkaḷiṉ cuṟṟup puṟaṅkaḷil kīrtar malaiyaṭivārap pakutikaḷil irunta makkaḷ kaṟkukaikaḷilum kallāl āṉa vīṭukaḷilum vacittaṉarē aṉṟi nakaraṅkaḷai amaittukkoṇṭu vāḻntilar. eṉavē, moheñco-tarō, harappā pōṉṟa paṇṭai nakaraṅkaḷ annakara makkaḷiṉ aṟivu nuṭpattaik koṇṭē amaintaṉavātal vēṇṭum. avarkaḷ vēṟevaraiyum pārttu nakara amaippu muṟaiyaik kaṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar eṉṟu iṉṟuḷḷa ārāycci nilaiyaikkoṇṭu kūṟutal iyalātatu. ēṉeṉil, atē kālattil ciṟappuṟṟirunta ekiptiyar, cumēriyar nakaraṅkaḷ ivvaḷavu ciṟappuṭaiyaṉavāka illāmaiyē iṅṅaṉam vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamākum.

piṟa nāṭukaḷil illāta nakara amaippu

“nalla tiṭṭaṅkaḷ iṭṭuc ciṟanta cukātāra muṟaiyil nakaraṅkaḷai amaittavarkaḷ cintu maṇṭila makkaḷē āvārkaḷ. ittakaiya tiṭṭam ki.mu.2000 varai ‘ur’ eṉṉum nakaril tōṉṟiyatākak kūṟal iyalātu. atē kālattiṟṟāṉ pāpilōṉiyāvilum ittiṭṭam tōṉṟiyatu. ekiptil uḷḷa ‘kahuṉ’ eṉṉum nakaril paṉṉiraṇṭām araca paramparaiyiṉar āṇṭakālattētāṉ itu pōṉṟa tiṭṭam tōṉṟiyatu. eṉavē, mikka pukaḻpaṭaitta ekiptiyarum pāpilōṉiyarum cumēriyarum appaḻaṅkālattil kaṇṭirāta nakara amaippu muṟaiyē ic cintu makkaḷ kaiyāṇṭiruntaṉar eṉiṉ, avarkaḷ pala āyiram āṇṭukaḷāka innāṭṭilēyē iruntu immuṟaikaḷiṟ kaitērntavarātal vēṇṭum. cumār ki.mu.1500 il intiyāvukku vanta āriyarkku muṉṉarē pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka mika uyarnta nākarikattil tiḷaittiṉarātalvēṇṭum. avarkaḷatu nākarikam cumēriyar, ēlattavar tam nākarikaṅkaḷaiviṭa mikavum uyarntatākum.[7]

﻿5. kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ

kaṭṭiṭa amaippu muṟai

moheñco-tarō makkaḷ akaṉṟa kāṟṟōṭṭam mikka terukkaḷai amaittu, nakara amaippil taṅkaṭkirunta paṇpaṭṭa aṟivai ulakattiṟku uṇarttiyatu pōṉṟē, kaṭṭi amaippu muṟaiyilum taṅkaḷukku irunta paṇpaṭṭa aṟivait taṅkaḷ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ vāyilāka viḷakkiyuḷḷaṉar. apperumakkaḷ, taṅkaḷ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaṭkuc cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷaiyum ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷaiyum payaṉpaṭutti uḷḷaṉar. maḻai, veyil, veḷḷam, puyaṟkāṟṟu ivaṟṟukku eḷitil āṭpaṭupavai kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ puṟac cuvarkaḷē ākum. ātaliṉ avvaṟiñarkaḷ, appuṟac cuvarkaḷaic cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷālēyē amaittuḷḷaṉar. mēṟ coṉṉavaṟṟāl tuṉpuṟuttappaṭāta uṭpuṟac cuvarkaḷai ularnta cūḷaiyiṭappaṭāta ceṅkaṟkaḷāl amaittuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷ ikkālattiya cimeṉṭai aṟiyār: kāṉkrīṭ eṉpataiyum aṟiyār. ātaliṉ, akkāla muṟaikku ēṟṟapaṭi kaḷimaṇ cāntaiyē cuvarkaḷiṉ mēṟpūccākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. cila illac cuvarkaḷait taviṭu kalanta kaḷimaṇ cāntāl meḻukiyuḷḷaṉar. cuvar, tarai, kūrai immūṉṟum kaḷimaṇ cāntāl āṉavai. avarkaḷ mara uttiraṅkaḷaip pōṭṭu avaṟṟiṉ mītu nāṇaṟpāy parappi, appāykaḷ mītu kaḷimaṇ cāntaik kaṉamāyp pūcik kūrai amaittu vāḻntaṉar; uttiraṅkaḷai nuḻaippataṟkeṉṟē cuvarkaḷiṉ talaippuṟaṅkaḷil catura vaṭivil ciṟiya tuḷaikaḷai viṭṭiruntaṉar. nām avaṟṟai iṉṉum teḷivuṟak kāṇalām. kaṭṭiṭap pulavarkaḷ cuvarkaḷaiyum taraiyaiyum mēṟkūraiyaiyum oḻuṅkupaṭutta maṭṭap palakaiyaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. appalakaikaḷ pala moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai pala aḷavukaḷ uṭaiyaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷ, cāḷaraṅkaḷ vāyiṟpaṭikaḷ mutaliyaṉa oḻuṅkāka amaintiruntatiliruntu, akkālattuk kottarkaḷ kaṭṭiṭa amaippil niṟainta aṟivuṭaiyavarkaḷākat tikaḻntaṉarātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu.

aṇi aṇiyāṉa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ

ovvoru teruvilum vīṭukaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu iṭaiveḷiyiṉṟic cērntāṟpōlavē amaintuḷḷa . āyiṉ piṟkālatatil cintunatiyil veḷḷam uṇṭāki, ataṉāl oru muṟai nakara amaippukkup paṅkam ēṟpaṭṭatum, aṇi aṇiyāka amaikkappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ cila māṟutal kaḷaip peṟṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ taraimaṭṭam uyarttap paṭṭatu. iṅṅaṉam mummuṟai tarai maṭṭam uyarttappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. ivvāṟu taraimaṭṭam uyarttap paṭṭapiṉ kaṭṭappaṭṭa eḻuppappaṭṭa cuvarkaḷō vīṭukaḷō aṇi aṇiyāka iṉṟic ciṟitu uyarttum tāḻntum muṉṉum piṉṉumāka māṟiyuḷḷaṉa. nakara aṭimaṭṭam uyarttap paṭṭa piṉ pōtiya iṭam iṉmaiyiṉ, ivvāṟu tāṟumākak kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ eḻuppappaṭṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum. toṭakkattil amaintuḷḷa kaṭṭiṭa amaippum, piṉṉar amaintuḷḷa kaṭṭiṭa amaippum kāṇpōrkku naṉku kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉa. akaṉṟa neṭunterukkaḷil uḷḷa illaṅkaḷai viṭakkuṟun terukkaḷil uḷḷa illaṅkaḷē naṉṉilaiyil irukkiṉṟaṉa.

parutta cuvarkaḷ

perumpālum ellāk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷum paruttavaiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. avai 105 ce. mī. mutal 180 ce.mī. varai taṭittavaiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. cintu natiyiṉ veḷḷattai etirnōkkiyē iccuvarkaḷ ivvaḷavu kaṉamāka amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iraṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa mēṉ māṭaṅkaḷai amaikkumpoḻutu avaṟṟiṉ pārattait tāṅka vallavaiyāka ivai amaintiruttal vēṇṭum aṉṟō? an nōkkam paṟṟiyum iccuvarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟalām. oru periya kaṭṭiṭattiṉ puṟac cuvar 172.5 ce. mī. kaṉamuṭaiyatākavum, piṟa cuvarkaḷ 105 ce. mī, 145 ce. mī, 132.5 ce. mī. kaṉam uṭaiyaṉa vākavum amaintuḷḷaṉa. ivai avvappōtu putuppikkap paṭṭaṉa. ātaliṉ, iṅṅaṉam vēṟupaṭṭuk kāṇkiṉṟaṉa.

neṭuñcuvarkaḷ

kaṭṭiṭac cuvarkaḷ ōraḷavil illai. periya terukkaḷil uḷḷa kaṭṭiṭac cuvarkaḷ 540 ce. mī. uyaram uṭaiyaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. kuṟun terukkaḷil uḷḷa kaṭṭiṭac cuvarkaḷ 540ce.mī. mutal 750 ce. mī. varai uyaram uṭaiyaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. innakaram tōṇṭappaṭṭa poḻutu, ārāycciyāḷar mikunta kavaṉattuṭaṉ mēṟpārvai celutti vantamaiyiṉ, ikkaṭṭiṭac cuvarkaḷ ciṟitum paḻutukku uḷḷākāmal kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟai naṉku kavaṉippiṉ, taraimaṭṭam uyarttap paṭṭapōtellām ivaiyum uyarttappaṭṭu vantaṉa eṉpatai eḷitil uṇartal kūṭum.

palavakaik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil pala tiṟappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. cila iraṇṭu aṭukkum iraṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭavaiyum uṭaiya māṭivīṭukaḷ cila akaṉṟa muṟṟaṅkaḷai uṭaiya orē aṭukku uṭaiyavai. ivvillaṅkaḷ ellām mēlē taḷam iṭappaṭṭavai; cuṟṟilum kaippiṭik cuvarkaḷai uṭaiyavai; mēltaḷattil peyyum maḻainīr uṭaṉukkuṭaṉē kīḻē viḻak kuḻaikaḷaiyuṭaiyavai.ik kuḻaikaḷ maṇṇālum marattālum ceyyappaṭṭavai. cila kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ aṅkāṭikaḷāka iruntavai; cila kōvilkaḷ eṉṟu karutat takkavai: pala eḷiyavar illaṅkaḷ.

eḷiyavar illaṅkaḷ

ciṟiya illaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa nāṉkaintu aṟaikaḷ koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai 900 ce.mī nīḷamum 810 ce. mī. akalamum uṭaiyaṉa. ivai aṉaittum ceṅkaṟkaḷaik koṇṭē amaittavai ākum. ivaṟṟiṉ tarai cāṇattāl meḻukappaṭṭu vantatu. iṅṅaṉam ceṅkaṟkaḷaik koṇṭu uṟutiyuṭaiya illaṅkaḷ kaṭṭi vāḻa iyalāta makkaḷ, nakarattiṉ veḷip puṟattil uṟutiyaṟṟa poruḷkaḷaik koṇṭu kuṭilkaḷ amaittu vāḻntaṉarātal vēṇṭum.

periya muṟṟamuṭaiya illaṅkaḷ

cila periya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ naṭuvil periya-akaṉṟa muṟṟam irukkiṉṟatu. ataṉaic cūḻap pala aṟaikaḷ amaintuḷḷaṉa. avvaṟaikaḷ ikkālattup periya aṟaikaḷ pōṉṟavai alla; eṉiṉum kāṟṟōṭṭam koṇṭaṉavākavē amaintuḷḷaṉa. ovvōr aṟaiyilum kāṟṟum veḷiccamum pukat takkavāṟu cāḷaram amaintuḷḷatu. kāṟṟōṭṭam naṉku amaintirukkat takkavāṟu vīṭṭiṉ vāyiṟpaṭiyum kūraiyum uyarttik kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ittakaiya illaṅkaḷ ceṉṉaiyil vaṇṇārappēṭṭaiyilum irāyapurattilum kāṇalām. cintu mākāṇattilum pala iṭaṅkaḷil ittakaiya akaṉṟa muṟṟamuṭaiya illaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar.

pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷ vāḻnta vīṭukaḷ

periya vīṭukaḷiṟ cila iṭaiyē neṭuñcuvarkaḷ vaittup pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. attakaiya illaṅkaḷ uṭaṉ piṟantōr pirintu vāḻvataṟkeṉṟē amaikkappaṭṭavai ākum. cila iṭaṅkaḷil cilavīṭukaḷ avaṟṟiṉ tāyc cuvarkaḷukkum. aṭutta vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷukkum iṭaiyil ciṟu iṭaiveḷi viṭṭuk kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟāl akkāla makkaṭkuḷ cuvarkaḷāl ēṟpaṭum tollai iruntatu eṉpatum, ataṉai nīkkavē immuṟai kaiyāḷappaṭṭatu eṉpatum eḷitiṟ pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa. ittakaiya periya vīṭukaḷil kūlaṅkaḷaic cēmittuvaikkum kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ taraiyiṟ patikkappaṭṭu iruntaṉa. cila illaṅ kaḷil māṭaṅkaḷum cuvar aṟaikaḷum amaintuḷḷaṉa. cila vīṭukaḷil 1ṟ kalaṉkaḷ vaittaṟkuriya marap peṭ kaṅkaḷ (Cup boards) cuvarkaḷilēyē iṇaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

celvar tam māṭa māḷikaikaḷ

ivai, iraṇṭum iraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa aṭukkukaḷu - muṭaiya māḷikaikaḷ ākum. ivai periya kūṭaṅkaḷ, akaṉṟu nīṇṭa tāḻvāraṅkaḷ, akaṉṟa muṟṟaṅkaḷ, iṭai kaḻikaḷ, ciṟiya periya vāyilkaḷ, pala aṟaikaḷ ivaṟṟai uṭaiyaṉavākum. ivaṟṟiṉ mēṉmāṭaṅkaḷ ceṅkal taḷa varicai uṭaiyaṉa. cila māḷikaikaḷil uḷḷa mēṉmāṭaṅkaḷil paṭukkai aṟaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa; vēṟu cila māṭaṅkaḷil paṭukkai aṟaikaḷ, nīrāṭum aṟaikaḷ, malaṅkaḻikka otukkiṭam mutaliyaṉa amaintuḷḷaṉa. mēṉmāṭaṅkaṭkuc cellum paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ pala iṉṟum naṉṉilaiyiṟ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. avai iṉṟu nam vīṭukaḷil uḷḷa paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ pōlavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. paṭikkaṭṭu illāta māṭaṅkaḷ cila kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.avaṟṟukku mara ēṇikaḷ payaṉ paṭṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum. paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ perumpālāṉa vīṭukaḷil teruppuṟamē amaintuḷḷatai nōkka kīḻk kaṭṭiṭattil vēṟu palar kuṭiyiruntaṉar eṉṟu eṇṇa vēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu. cila vīṭukaḷil paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ uṭpuṟat tākavē amaintuḷḷaṉa. oru māḷikaiyil iraṇṭu paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu aṇmaiyilēyē amain tuḷḷaṉa. paṭikkāka viṭappaṭṭa palakai ovvoṉṟum 21 ce. mī akalamum 5.5 ce. mī. kaṉamum uṭaiyatu. ﻿ paṭikkaṭṭiṉ akalam 100 ce.mīṭṭarākum. iṅṅaṉam iraṇṭu paṭikkaṭṭu vaittuḷḷa illam perun talaivaṉuṭaiya illamākavē iruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttu. vēṟoru periya māḷikaiyiṉ muṉpuṟam 2550 ce. mī. akaṉṟuḷḷatu piṉpuṟam 290 ce mī akaṉṟuḷḷatu. iṅṅaṉam illattiṉ irupuṟamum paranta iṭamviṭṭuk kaṭṭum muṟai appaṇṭaik kālattilēyē vaḻakkil iruntateṉpatu peru viyappukkuriya ceytiyē aṉṟō?

aracaṉatu araṇmaṉaiyō?

itukāṟum tōṇṭik kaṇṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ aṉaittiṉum mikap periyatāyuḷḷa māḷikai oṉṟē aṟiñar kavaṉattai mikutiyum kavarntatākum. ammāḷikai 7260 ce.mī nīḷamum 3360 ce.mī. akalamum uṭaiyatu. atai aṭuttuc caṟṟuk kuṟainta aḷavu 350 ce.mī.nīḷamum 3480 ce.mī akalamum uṭaiya periya māḷikai oṉṟu uḷḷatu. ataṟku aṭuttāṟpōl maṟṟoru māḷikai irukkiṉṟatu. ataṉ vaṭapuṟac cuvar 2610 ce. mī nīḷam: teṉpuṟac cuvar 2800 ce. mī nīḷam mēṟkuppuṟaccuvar 140 ce.mī. nīḷam kiḻakkuppuṟac cuvar 1465 ce. mī nīḷam akkaṭṭiṭattuḷ pala aṟaikaḷum oru kiṇaṟum irukkiṉṟaṉa; ovvoru puṟattum 125 ce. mī akalamuḷḷa caturat tuṇkaḷ cila irukkiṉṟaṉa. ittūṇkaḷ akkaṭṭiṭa vāyiliṉ vaḷaivukaḷait tāṅkupavai eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar. akkaṭṭiṭattiṟku mēṉ māṭi iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. aḵtu iṉṟu kāṇumāṟillai.

akkaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷai aṭuttuc ciṟu viṭutikaḷ pala uḷḷaṉa. avai 1680 ce.mī nīḷamuṭaiyaṉa. avai ovvoṉṟilum naṭuvil neṭuntuṇkaḷaik koṇṭa maṇṭapamum pala kūṭaṅkaḷum uḷvaḻikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya pala aṟaikaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. anta aṟaikaḷ cāmāṉkaḷai vaippataṟkum, camaiyal ceyvataṟkum, paṭukkaikkum, uṇavu uṭkoḷḷavum, piṟa tēvaikaṭkākavum taṉittaṉiyē amaikkappaṭṭavai ākum. iṅṅaṉam amain tuḷḷa periya māḷikai āṭci urimaiyuṭaiya peruntalaivaṉatu araṇmaṉaiyākavum, ataṉai aṭutta māḷikaikaḷ avaṉukku aṭutta uttiyōkattarkaḷiṉ māḷikaikaḷākavum, immāḷikai kaḷiṉ veḷippuṟam uḷḷa viṭutikaḷ kāvalāḷar ēvalāḷar illaṅkaḷākavum iruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttākum.

taiyalārkkut taṉi aṟaikaḷ

illaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟil kālnaṭaikaḷaik kaṭṭuvataṟkut taṉi iṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉavākat teriyavillai. ātalāl ammakkaḷ vīṭṭu muṟṟuḷilēyē oru mūlaiyil avaṟṟaik kaṭṭiyiruttal vēṇṭum. ‘camaiyal aṟaikaḷ illāta illaṅkaḷil camaiyal vēḷaiyum muṟṟattilē naṭaipeṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum’, eṉṟu aṟiñar cilar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ karācci vikṭōriyāk kaṇkāṭcik cālaiyiṉ kāppāḷarākiya aṟiñar ci.ār.rāy eṉpār, ‘ovvoru vīṭṭiṟkum vāyiṟpaṭi uṇṭu. ataṉai aṭuttut tiṟanta ciṟṟaṟai oṉṟu vāyiṟ kāvalaṉukkākak kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉ piṉpuṟam amarntu pēcutaṟkuriya kūṭaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. avaṟṟiṟkup piṉpuṟam paṭukkai aṟaikaḷ, camaiyal aṟaikaḷ, taiyalār irukkat taṉi aṟaikaḷ mutaliyaṉa irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.[1]

nīrāṭum aṟaikaḷ

ovvōr illattiluma nīrāṭat taṉi aṟai irukkiṟatu. māḷikaikaḷil oṉṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa nīrāṭum aṟaikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivai teruppakkam amaintuḷḷaṉa; melliya ceṅkaṟkaḷāl taḷavaricai iṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa; ivaṟṟiliruntu kaḻinīr ōṭa vaṭikālkaḷ naṉmuṟaiyil amaintuḷḷaṉa. iv vaṭikālkaḷ teruk kālvāyuṭaṉ iṇaikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivvaṭikālukku uriya puḻiaiyuḷḷa iṭam nōkki nīrāṭum aṟaiyiṉ taraimaṭṭam carintu celvatu kuṟippiṭat takkatu. ittakaiya nīrāṭum aṟai oṉṟu eṣṉaṉṉāvil irunta akkēṭiyar araṇmaṉaiyuḷ iruntatu. akkēṭiyar nīrāṭum aṟai amaippataic cintup piratēca makkaḷiṭamiruntē kaṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatil aiyamillai.[2]

camaiyal aṟaikaḷ

ovvoru vīṭṭārum perumpālum muṟṟattaṇṭai camaiyal ceytu vanta pōtiṉum, camaiyalukkeṉṟu taṉi aṟaiyaiyum vaittiruntaṉar. anta aṟaiyil viṟku vaikkavum piṟa camaiyaṟ poruḷkaḷai vaikkavum taṉi mēṭaikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila periya vīṭṭuc camaiyal aṟaikaḷ akaṉṟu irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ periya tāḻikaḷ patikkappaṭ ṭiruntamaikkuriya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. attāḻikaḷ nīraic cēmittu vaikkap payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām. mēlum, camaiyal aṟaiyil camaiyalukkuriya cāmāṉkaḷai vaikkap pala maṇ cāṭikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ippaḻakkam iṉṟum moheñco-tarōvaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa kirāmattāriṭam iruppataik kāṇalām. camaiyal aṭuppukaḷ cilavē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avai mecopoṭṭēmiyāvil kiṭaitta aṭuppukaḷaip pōlavē iruttal viyappukku uriyatē.[3]

aṅkāṭiyō? ampalamō?

ōriṭattil mikap periya maṇṭapam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭatu. atu 76500 ca. ce. mī. parappuṭaiyatu. ataṉ kūraiyai irupatu ceṅkal tūṇkaḷ tāṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ivai nāṉku nāṉkāyccaturam caturamāka niṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. apperiya maṇṭapam potu aṅkāṭiyāka iruntirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñar cilar apippirāyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. cilar, ‘potu makkaḷiṉ iṟaivaṇakkattiṟkuriyatākavum, potu viṉaikku uriyatākavum iruntirukkalām’, eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. āyirakkaṇakkāṉa yāṇṭukaṭku muṉ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa oṉṟaippaṟṟi iṅṅaṉam palatiṟappaṭṭa karuttukkaḷ eḻutal iyalpaṉṟō?

cila iṭaṅkaḷil aṟaikkuḷ aṟaiyākap pala illaṅkaḷ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. avai aṅkāṭikaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, uḷḷaṟaikaḷ kaṭaic cāmāṉkaḷ vaittaṟ kuriyavai eṉṟum, aṟiñar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. vēṟu cila illac cuvarkaḷaic cuṟṟi 120 ce. mī. akalam taḷavaricai iṭappeṟṟa mēṭaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai cillaṟaik kaṭaikaḷ vaikkap payaṉpaṭṭavai eṉṟu aṟiñar cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. cilar, ‘avai veḷḷap pātukāppukkeṉṟē uyarttap paṭṭavai’ eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.

kaḷḷukkaṭaiyō? taṇṇīrppantalō? uṇṭiccālaiyō?

cila iṭaṅkaḷil periya aṟaikaḷaik koṇṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ cila nilattil aḻuntip patintuḷḷaṉa.ivai perumpālum teruk kōṭikaḷiṟṟām amaintuḷḷaṉa.ivaṟṟuḷ kaḷimaṇṇāl ceytu cūḷaiyiṭappaṭṭa periya tāḻikaḷ pala taraiyiṟ putaintu kiṭantaṉa. ivai kaḷḷuk kaṭaikaḷāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu cilar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. tīkṣat pōṉṟōr, ‘ivai nīr mōr, taṇṇir mutaliya pāṉaṅkaḷai vaḻippōkkarkku vilaiyiṉṟi vaḻaṅkum aṟaccāvaṭikaḷāka iruntiruttalvēṇṭum’, eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. ‘ivai nakara māntar oṉṟukūṭi aḷavaḷāvutaṟkamainta uṇṭic cālaikaḷāka irukkalām’ eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē kūṟukiṉṟār.

vēṭkōvar viṭutikaḷ

nakarattiṉ oru puṟam irunta illaṅkaṭku arukil pala kāḷavāykaḷum arai kuṟaiyākac cūḷaiyiṭappaṭṭa maṭṭāṇṭaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avviṭattil maṭpāṇṭat toḻilāḷar vēṭkōvar perumpāṉmaiyiṉarāka vāḻntaṉarātal vēṇṭum. toṭakkattil ammakkaḷ nakarattiṉ puṟampē iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟum (kāḷavāykaḷ nakaril iruttal cukātārak kuṟaivātaliṉ), nāḷaṭaivil caṉattokai kuṟaiyak kuṟaiya nakarattiṉ oru pakutiyiṟ kuṭiyēṟalāyiṉar eṉṟum aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[4]

vīṭṭu vāyilkaḷ

moheñ cotarō nakarattu illaṅkaḷiṉ vāyilkaḷ perumpālum perun terukkaḷil vaikkap paṭāmal kuṟukkup pātaikaḷilēyē amaintuḷḷaṉa. immuṟaiyāl terukkaḷ aḻakākak kāṇappaṭal iyalpē aṉṟō? cila periya māḷikaikaḷiṟṟām akaṉṟum uyarntum uḷḷa vāyilkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. ēṉaiya illaṅkaḷil ellām uyarattilum akalattilum kuṟukalākavuḷḷa vāyilkaḷē amaintuḷḷaṉa. periya vīṭṭu vāyil 235 ce.mī. akalamuṭaiyatu; āṉāl 150 ce. mīṭṭarukku mēṟpaṭṭa uyaramuṭaiyatāka illai. ipperiya vāyilkaḷai uṭaiya vīṭṭār pala erutukaḷaiyum kōvēṟu kaḻutaikaḷaiyum piṟa kālnaṭaikaḷaiyum vaittiruntavar ātal vēṇṭum. ivarkaḷ avaṟṟai vīṭṭu muṟṟattilēyē kaṭṭiyiruntaṉar. iṅku, avai pullaiyum vaikkōlaiyum tiṉṉa amaikkap paṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ irunta aṭaiyāḷam terikiṉṟatu. perumpālāṉa vīṭṭu vāyilkaḷ 120 ce. mī. uyaramē uḷḷaṉa. ivai iṅkaṉam amaintiruttaṟku iraṇṭu kāraṇaṅkaḷ kūṟalām: oṉṟu veḷḷam vīṭṭiṟkuḷ eḷitil varāmal taṭuppataṟkāka irukkalām; maṟṟoṉṟu, moheñco-tarō makkaḷ cumēriyaraip pōlak kuḷḷamāṉavarāka iruttal vēṇṭum. mēlum, avarkaḷ mēṉmāṭikku iṭṭa uttiraṅkaḷ taraimaṭṭattiliruntu 180 ce. mī.kku uḷḷēyē iruttalum kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. eṉavē piṉṉatē peritum poruttamuṭaiya kāraṇamākum.[5] vīṭṭuk katavukaḷ ellām marak katavukaḷēyākum. cintup piratēcattil appaḻaṅkālattil kāṭukaḷ mikutiyātaliṉālum vīṭṭuc cuvariṉ kaṉattiṟku ēṟṟa katavu amaittal kaṭaṉātalālum marakkatavukaḷ mikka parumaṉ uṭaiyaṉavāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē karutukiṟār.

tuṇkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kāṇappaṭṭa tuṇkaḷ ellām ceṅkaṟkaḷāl āṉavaiyē. avai perumpālum caturat turaṇkaḷēyākum. cila aṭiyil 2700 ca. ce. mī. rākavum, mēl pōkappōka 2250 ca. ce.mī.rākavum uḷḷaṉa. pala muṟṟum 2700 ca. ce mī parappuṭaiyaṉavākavē uḷḷaṉa. cumēriyāvil atē kālattil vaṭṭattuṇkaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. cumēriyarōṭu neruṅkiya vāṇipam ceytuvanta cintup piratēca makkaḷ avvaṭṭat tūṇkaḷait taṅkaḷ illaṅkaṭkup payaṉpaṭuttāmai viyappukkuriyatākum. tūṇkaḷait tāṅkum kalvaḷaiyaṅkaḷ pala kiṭaittaṉ. āṉāl avai ‘yōṉikaḷ’ eṉṟu tayārāma cahaṉi kūṟukiṟār.[6]

iṉi, harappā nakarattu illaṅkaḷaippaṟṟiya cila vivaraṅkaḷaik kāṇpōm:

harappāvil kaṭṭiṭa amaippu

moheñco-tarō makkaḷ cukavāḻviṟkēṟṟa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷaik kaṭṭikkoṇṭu vāḻntāṟ pōlavē harappā makkaḷum mikac ciṟanta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭi vāḻntaṉar. periya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭai mikka uṟutiyāka iruttal vēṇṭi, naṉku cūḷaiyiṭappaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, ciṟiya illaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭai iṅṅaṉam uṟutiyāka illai. avai uṭainta maṇ ōṭukaḷ, ceṅkaṟkaṭṭikaḷ, maṭpāṇṭa oṭukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaiyē koṇṭiruntaṉa. periya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷum taraikaḷum cākkaṭaikaḷum oruvitak kal koṇṭu naṉṟāka vaḻavaḻappākat tēyttu ikkālac ‘cimeṇṭ’ pūcappeṟṟaṉapōl amaintuḷḷaṉa. taḷavaricaiyil uḷḷa kaṟkaḷ aṉaittum oḻuṅkākavum vaḻavaḻappākavum aṟuttuc cūḷaiyiṭappaṭṭavaiyākum. māṭip paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ moheñco-tarōvil uḷḷavaipōlat tiṟampaṭa amaintuḷḷaṉa. kuppai koṭṭum toṭṭikaḷ, kaḻinīrt tēkkaṅkaḷ, kālvāykaḷ, vaṭikālkaḷ mutaliyaṉa moheñco-tarōvil iruppavaṟṟaiviṭa nalla nilaiyil uḷḷaṉa. ivaiyaṉṟik kiṇaṟukaḷ āṅkāṅkē kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai vīṭṭukkoṉṟāka amaintuḷḷamai kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

iruvakai illaṅkaḷ

harappāvil uḷḷa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷai iruvakaiyiṉavākap pirikkalām;avai kuṭi iruttaṟkuriya illaṅkaḷ, potuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa ākum. kuṭikku uriya illaṅkaḷ perumpālum ciṟantaṉavāyum māṭikaḷ uṭaiyaṉavāyum irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟil viruntiṉarkkut taṉi aṟaikaḷum taiyalārkkut taṉi aṟaikaḷum akaṉṟa muṟṟaṅkaḷum iruntamai kuṟippiṭat takkatu. potuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷil ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa vaṭṭavaṭimāṉa mēṭaikaḷ amaintuḷḷaṉa.

peruṅ kaḷañciyam

aṟiñar oru maṇmēṭṭait tōṇṭiyapōtu ataṉ aṭiyil kaḷañciyam oṉṟu irukkak kaṇṭaṉar. itu 50-10 ce.mī. nīḷamum 4050ce.mī akalamum uṭaiyatu. itaṉ cuvarkaḷ 1560 ce.mī. uyaramum 270 ce.mī. kaṉamum uṭaiyaṉa. ivai iraṇṭu varicai kaḷākak kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivviraṇṭu varicaikaṭku iṭaiyē uḷḷaturam 690 ce mī ākum. inta iṭaiveḷikkumēl kūrai iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. inta iru varicai neṭuñ cuvarkaḷuḷ ovvoru varicaiyilum āṟu maṇṭapaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru maṇṭapattaiyum iṇaikkum vakaiyil aintu iṭaikaḻikaḷ viṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ovvoru maṇṭapamum mūṉṟu neṭuñcuvarkaḷ eḻuppappeṟṟu nāṉku aṟaikaḷ pōlappirintu uḷḷatu. maṇṭapaṅkaḷiṉ taraip pakutiyil marappalakaikaḷ patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvaḷavu aṟputamāṉa vēlaippāṭṭuṭaṉ viḷaṅkum immaṇṭapaṅkaḷ tāṉiyaṅkaḷ koṭṭivaikkap payaṉpaṭṭa kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ ākum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttu. iṅkilāntilum cermaṉiyilum paṇṭamāṟṟu muṟaiyum, varikaḷaip paṇṭaṅkaḷākavē vāṅkum vaḻakkamum irunta paṇṭaik kālattil, araciyalār, apporuḷkaḷaic cēmittu vaippataṟkākak kaṭṭiyirunta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷaip pōlavē harappāvil kaṭṭappaṭṭa kaḷañciya maṇṭapaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl, ivai araciyalār araciṟaikkākat tiraṭṭappeṟṟa paṇṭaṅkaḷ vaikkum kaḷañciyaṅkaḷākavē iruttal kūṭum eṉpatu aṟiñar eṇṇamākum.

toḻilāḷar illaṅkaḷ

harappāvil toḻilāḷar illaṅkaḷ amaintuḷḷa muṟai kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. oru varicaiyil ēḻu vīṭukaḷ irukkumāṟu iraṇṭu varicaikaḷ oṉṟākak kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iraṇṭiṟkum iṭaiyil nīṇṭa kuṟukiya pātai oṉṟu celkiṉṟatu. appātai iru pakkaṅkaḷilum potut terukkaḷil kalakkiṉṟatu. ivvillaṅkaḷ ekiptil uḷḷa kel-el amarṉā[7] eṉṉum iṭattil amaintuḷḷa toḻilāḷar. illaṅkaḷaippōlavē iruttal kuṟippiṭat takkatu. āṉāl, piṉṉavai orē varicaiyil amaintuḷḷaṉa; iṭaiyil pātai viṭappaṭṭila. mēlum, harappāvil uḷḷa toḻilāḷar illam ovvoṉṟilum periya muṟṟamum mūṉṟu aṟaikaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam ekiptiya illaṅkaḷ amaintila. mēlum, harappāviṉ illaṅkaḷ ki.mu.2500kku muṟpaṭṭaṉa; āṉāl, ekiptiya illaṅkaḷ ki.mu.2000kkup piṟpaṭṭaṉa. ip piṟkāla illaṅkaḷaiviṭa harappāvil uḷḷa muṟkāla illaṅkaḷ cukātāra vacatiyōṭu piṟa vacatikaḷum poruntiyirukkumpaṭi amaikkappaṭṭiruttal peruviyappukku uriyatē ākum. veḷiyiliruntu pārppavarkku uṭpuṟam tōṉṟātavāṟu ivvillaṅkaḷiṉ talaivāyil vaḷainta cuvarkaḷuṭaṉ amaintuḷḷatu. ivviṭutikaḷil palavakait toḻilpuri makkaḷ vāḻntiruntaṉar eṉṟu eṇṇutaṟkuriya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa.

vīṭṭiṟku uriya vīṭukaḷē

‘aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu eṉṉum pēṟukaḷ nāṉkaṉuḷ, vīṭu’ eṉpatu ‘iṉpam’ allatu ‘mōkṣam’ eṉap poruḷpaṭum. maṉitaṉ ivvulakil taṉ uyirukkuyirāṉa peṟṟōruṭaṉum maṉaivi makkaḷuṭaṉum nīṇṭa nāḷ kalantu uṟavāṭi iṉpam nukarum iṭamāka uḷḷatu vīṭē aṉṟō? avvillam nalla cukātāra muṟaippaṭi amaintirukkumāyiṉ, nōy noṭiyiṉṟi uṭal vaḷam kuṉṟāmal cuṟucuṟuppāka viṉai yāṟṟip poruḷiṭṭi illaṟameṉṉum nallaṟam tuyttu iṉpa vāḻvu vāḻalām aṉṟō? iṅṅaṉam iṉpam (vīṭu) tarattakka illam amaittu vāḻntamaiyāṟṟāṉō, nam muṉṉōrāya paṇṭait tamiḻ makkaḷ mōkṣattai ‘vīṭu’ eṉṉum peyarāl aḻaittaṉar? ipporuṭ poruttam uṭaiyaṉavākavē harappā, moheñco-tarō eṉṉum iraṇṭu nakaraṅkaḷilum irunta paṇṭaik kāla vīṭukaḷ viḷakkamuṟṟiruntaṉa. ekiptiyar, cumēriyar mutaliyōr piramiṭ kōpuraṅkaḷaik kaṭṭuvatilum cittira vēlaippāṭu mikunta illaṅkaḷai amaippatilum taṅkaḷ karuttaic celuttiṉarē aṉṟic ciṟanta cukātāra muṟaikku ēṟṟavāṟu illaṅkaḷai amaittu vāḻntilar. ‘vīṭu’ (illam) eṉpatai vīṭu (mōkṣam) ākkiya perumai āriyarkku muṟpaṭṭa cintuppiratēca makkaṭkē uriyatākum.

↑ tamiḻnāṭṭu vēṭkōvar periya tāḻikaḷaic ceyvatil nipuṇar eṉpataip puṟanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷāl aṟika.

↑ Dr. Mackay’s ‘The Indus Civlilization’, p.202.

﻿6. kiṇaṟukaḷ - ceykuḷam - ceṅkaṟkaḷ

5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa kiṇaṟukaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil perumpālum vīṭṭukkoru kiṇaṟu iruntateṉak kūṟalām. ārāycciyāḷar nakarattait tōṇṭi ārāycci naṭattumpoḻutu pala kēṇikaḷ iruttalaik kaṇṭaṉar. āṉāl avai aṉaittum tuārntu kiṭantaṉa. harappāvil aṟiñar vāṭh eṉpār ārāycci nikaḻttiya pōtu oru peruṅ kiṇaṟṟil nīr iruttalaik kaṇṭār. akkiṇaṟṟil 245 ce.mī. uyaram taṇṇīr iruntatām. avvaṟiñar ataṉait tūymai ceytu aṅku vēlai ceytu vanta toḻilāḷarkkup payaṉpaṭumāṟu ēṟpāṭu ceytaṉarām. harappāvil piṟitōr iṭattil 180 ce. mī. cuṟṟaḷavuṭaiya kiṇaṟu oṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. pala kiṇaṟukaḷ nakara aḻiviṉāl aḻintu viṭṭaṉa. pala irunta iṭam teriyāmal maṇṇukkuḷ maṟaippuṇṭaṉa.

iṉṟum curappuṭaiya kiṇaṟukaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil uḷḷa kiṇaṟukaḷ 107.5 ce.mī. cuṟṟaḷavu mutal 270 ce.mī. cuṟṟaḷavu varai pala tiṟappaṭṭaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ pala maṇṇuḷ putaintuviṭṭaṉa. ārāycciyāḷar avaṟṟaik kaṇṭaṟintu avaṟṟil aṭaintuḷḷa maṇ kal mutaliyavaṟṟai appuṟappaṭutti, cakatiyai nīkkit tūymai ceyyum vēlaiyil īṭupaṭṭaṉar. cila kiṇaṟukaḷ naṉṉīrc curappuṭaiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. annīrē aṅku ārāycciyil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa aṟiñarkkum toḻilāḷarkkum uṇṇavum kuḷikkavum uriyatāyiṟṟu.kēṇi nīraip payaṉpaṭuttuvataṟku muṉ avarkaṭkut tēvaippaṭṭa nīr 3kimī tolaiviliruntu vaṇṭiyil koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. ippoḻutu attollai muṟṟum nīṅkiviṭṭatu. cumār 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa kiṇaṟukaḷ iṉṟum curappuṭaiyaṉavāka iruttal viyappukkuriyatē aṉṟō?

māḷikaikaḷil uḷḷa kiṇaṟukaḷ

cila māḷikaikaḷil uḷḷa paḻaṅkiṇaṟukaḷ teruppuṟam illāmal māḷikaikku aṇmaiyil amaintiruntaṉaveṉṟu terikiṟatu. annilaiyil avai māḷikai makkaṭkē payaṉ paṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. āṉāl ammāḷikaiyil piṟkālattil vīṭṭiṉ teruppuṟamākak kiṇaṟukaḷ amaintuḷḷaṉa. avai piṟkālattil potumakkaḷ nalaṅkarutiyē muṉpuṟam amaikkap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam teruppuṟam amaintuḷḷa kiṇaṟukaḷ cila aṟaikaḷil uḷḷamai kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. avvaṟaiyil kiṇaṟṟurukil periya taṇṇīrp pāṉaikaḷ putaikkappaṭṭiruntamaikkuriya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ terikiṉṟaṉa. avaṟaiyiṉ tarai naṉku paṇpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kiṇaṟṟarukil cintum nīr aṅkut tēṅkiyirāmal uṭaṉukkuṭaṉē teruk kālvāykkum iṭaiyē ciṟu vaṭikāl oṉṟu amaintuḷḷatu. nīr eṭukkum peṇkaḷ uṭkāruvataṟkuk kiṇaṟṟaṇṭai mēṭai oṉṟu amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu kiṇaṟṟaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa cuvar kuḻantaikaḷ eṭṭip pārkka muṭiyāta aḷavu uyarntuḷḷatu.

terumutal kiṇaṟuvarai ciṟiya vaḻi viṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. veḷiyār kiṇaṟṟaṇṭai varuvataṟkeṉṟē avvaḻi amaikkappaṭṭatu pōlum piṟar taṇṇīr eṭukkumpoḻutu māḷikaikkuriya maṭantaiyarum taṇṇir eṭukkavēṇṭumāyiṉ eṉceyvatu? ataṟkeṉṟu takka ēṟpāṭu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu. veḷiyārkkum vīṭṭārkkum kiṇaṟṟaṇṭai yātoru campantamum irātavāṟu kiṇaṟṟiṉ naṭuvil melliya maṟaivu amaikkappaṭṭiruntatu eṉpataṟkuriya aṭaiyāḷam terikiṟatu. kēṇikaḷiṉ mēṟpuṟamum uṭpuṟamum mikka kavaṉattuṭaṉum oḻuṅkuṭaṉum kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila kiṇaṟukaḷiṉ ōrac cuvarkaḷ mūṉṟu mutal aintu aṭukkuc ceṅkaṟkaḷ vaittuk kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ippātukāppukkuriya ēṟpāṭṭiṉāl kiṇaṟṟu nīr cuvarkaḷil taṅki ūṟu viḷaikkātu eṉpatu teḷivām.

kayiṟum uruḷaikaḷum

kiṇaṟukaḷiṟ kuṭaṅkaḷaiviṭṭu nīrai mukattal ikkālattum cila iṭaṅkaḷil vaḻakkamāka irukkiṉṟatu. itu pōṉṟē appaṇṭaik kālattum moheñco-tarōvil ivvaḻakkam iruntatu. akkāla makkaḷ taṭitta kayiṟukaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatu, kiṇṟukaḷiṉ mēṟcuvaril ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa urāyppukaḷ mūlam aṟital kūṭum.potuk kiṇaṟukaḷil makaḷir palar orē kālattil nīrai eṭukkum paḻakkam iṉṟum iruppatu pōlavē appaḻaṅkālattum iruntateṉpataik kiṇaṟṟiṉ mēṟcuvar mītuḷḷa aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu kūṟalām. pal kēṇikaḷil uruḷaikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu cuṭṭa kaḷimaṇṇālāya cila patumaikaḷ vāyilāka aṟiyakkiṭakkiṟatu.

palamuṟai uyarttappaṭṭa kiṇaṟukaḷ

cintu āṟṟiṉ veḷḷap perukkiṟku añci nakarattiṉ taraimaṭṭam uyarttappaṭṭapōtellām ikkiṇaṟukaḷum māṟupāṭu aṭaintukoṇṭu vantaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kiṇaṟukaḷ iṅkaṉam mummuṟai putuppikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatai, avaṟṟiṉ mēṟcuvarkaḷ mūlam naṉkaṟiyalām. vēṟu cila iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kiṇaṟukaḷ nakara aṭimaṭṭam uyarttappaṭṭa kālattum putuppikkappaṭavillai. cila iṭaṅkaḷil putuppikkap paṭṭa kiṇaṟukaḷ paḻaiyavaṟṟaiviṭa eḷiyaṉavākavē kāṇappaṭalāl, annakara makkaḷ putuppikkum toḻilil veṟuppuṟṟaṉar eṉṟō, allatu celva nilaiyil pātikkappaṭṭaṉar eṉṟō kūṟalām eṉpar ārāycciyāḷar. akkālattut taṇṇir maṭṭattiṟkum ikkālattut taṇṇir maṭṭattiṟkum 600 ce.mī. vēṟupāṭu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉāl, akkiṇaṟukaḷai aṭimaṭṭam varai tōṇṭik kāṇpatu māṭṭāmaiyāka irukkiṉṟatu. ‘cila kiṇaṟukaḷiṉ - aṭimaṭṭattaiyēṉum cōtittup pārttal vēṇṭum. avaṟṟiṉ aṭiyil kuḻantaikaḷum periyavarkaḷum kai tavaṟip pōṭṭa poruḷkaḷ cilavēṉum kiṭaittal kūṭum. nām avaṟṟaik koṇṭu pala uṇmaikaḷ uṇartal kūṭum’ eṉṟu ārāycci yāḷar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar. ārāycci uṭaiyārkku akappaṭṭa ciṟu poruḷum aṟputamākak kāṭciyaḷikkum aṉṟō!

aḻakiya ceykuḷam

moheñco-tarōvil itukāṟum naṭanta ārāycciyil veḷippaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷuṭ ciṟantavai araṇmaṉai eṉṟu karutattakka kaṭṭiṭam oṉṟum aḻakiya ceykuḷam oṉṟumē ākum. piṉṉatu ikkālap poṟivalāḷarum tikaippuṟumāṟu amaintuḷḷatu. itaṉai. car jāṉ mārṣal 1925-1926il kaṇṭaṟintār. itaṉil taṇṇir niṟkum iṭam maṭṭum cumār 1200 ce.mī nīḷamum 690 ce.mī. akalamum 240ce.mīāḻamum uṭaiyatu.ikkuḷattiṟku oḻuṅkāṉa paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ amaintuḷḷaṉa. ikkuḷattaic cuṟṟi nāṟpuṟamum naṭaivaḻi irukkumāṟu 135.ce.mī. akalamuḷḷa cuvar eḻuppappaṭṭuḷḷatu. accuvarukkum appāl kuḷattaic cuṟṟi 210 ce.mī akalamuḷḷa peruñcuvar oṉṟu amaintuḷḷatu. ataṉ mītum naṭaivaḻi amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

kuḷattiṉ aṭimaṭṭamum uṭcuvarkaḷum

icceykuḷattiṉ aṭimaṭṭam, naṉṟāy iḻaittu vaḻavaḻappākkappaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷum ikkālattuc ‘cimeṇṭ’ pōṉṟa oruvakai nilak kīlum[1] koṇṭu taḷavaricai iṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṭimaṭṭac cuvarkaḷum ivviraṇṭāl ākkappaṭṭaṉavē ākum. itai amaitta aṟiñar pārāṭṭutaṟku uriyavarē āvar eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttākum. apperumakkaḷ mutalil cūḷaiyiṭappaṭṭa vaḻavaḻappāṉa ceṅkaṟkaḷai oruvakai veḷḷik kaḷimaṇ[2] koṇṭu oṭṭavaittuḷḷaṉar; ivvaṇṇam nāṟpuṟamum 120 ce. mī. kaṉattil cuvarkaḷ eḻuppiṉar; piṉṉar accuvarkaḷ mītu 2.5 ce.mī. kaṉattil nilakkīl pūciyuḷḷaṉar; iccuvarukkup piṉpuṟam naṉṟāyc cuṭappaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷaik koṇṭu maṟṟoru varicaic cuvar kaṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar; ataṉai oṭṭic cuṭāta ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷālāṉa cuvar oṉṟai iṇaittuḷḷaṉar. taṇṇir ūṟippaḻutākāmal iruppataṟkākavē iccuvarkaḷ ivvaḷavu vēlaippāṭukaḷōṭu kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

nilak kīl

icceykuḷattil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa nilakkīl cintup piratēcattilō aṉṟi intiyāviṉ piṟapakutikaḷilō illai. eṉavē, itu veḷi nāṭṭiliruntē koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum. avveḷināṭu yātu? cumēriyāviṟṟāṉ innilakkīl uṇṭu. iḵtu aṅkētāṉ peritum ippaṇṭaik kālattil payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭatu. cumēriyar kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷai uṟutiyum aḻakum ceytatu innilakkīlē ākum. eṉavē, vāṇipattil cumēriyarōṭu nerukkam koṇṭirunta cintup piratēca makkaḷ innilakkīlai avarkaḷiṭamiruntē peṟṟaṉarātal vēṇṭum. iḵtu iṅṅaṉam neṭuntuārattiliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatāka iruntatāṟṟāṉ, kiṇaṟukaṭkō piṟavaṟṟukkō payaṉpaṭuttappaṭāmal ivvariya ceykuḷattiṟku maṭṭumē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa teṉpatai uṇartal vēṇṭum. ivvariya poruḷai anneṭuntura nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum koṇarntu amaikkappaṭṭa icceykuḷam, oru kuṟippiṭṭa naṟkāriyattiṟkākavē payaṉpaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum eṉpatum aṟiyattakum.

kuḷattiṟku vaṭakkē

icceykuḷattiṟku vaṭakkē eṭṭu aṟaikaḷ kaṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai mikka uṟutiyāṉa cuvarkaḷaik koṇṭavai: veḷiyiliruntu pārppavar uṭpuṟattaic ciṟitum kāṇātavāṟu iyaṉṟa uṟutiyāṉa katavukaḷai uṭaiyavai; mēlē ēṟap paṭikkaṭṭukaḷai uṭaiyavai. ovvōr aṟaiyiṉ mēlum aṟai iruntateṉpataṟku uriya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. mēl aṟaiyil iruntavar paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ vaḻiyē uṭpuṟamākavē kīḻ aṟaikku vantu, arukil uḷḷa kiṇaṟṟiliruntu cēvakaṉ cēmittu vaikkum nīril nīrāṭiviṭṭu mēlē pōyviṭuvar. avvaḻukku nīr ovvōr aṟaiyilum uḷḷa tuḷai vaḻiyē veḷiyil uḷḷa kaḻinīrk kālvāyaiccērum. iv aṟaikaḷil vāḻntavar camayaccārpuṭaiya perumakkaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum. ivaṟaikaḷum icceykuḷamum stūpi niṟkum iṭattiṟku aṇmaiyil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl,stuāpiyiṉ aṭiyil maṟaintukiṭappatu, ivviraṇṭiṟkum toṭarpuṭaiya kōvilākavō aṉṟic camayac cārpuṭaiya piṟitoru kaṭṭiṭamākavō iruttal vēṇṭum[3]

kuḷattiṟku nīr vacati

icceykuḷattiṟku arukē, itil nīrai nirapputaṟkeṉṟē amaintaṉa pōla mūṉṟu periya kiṇaṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ nīrē kuḷattiṟkup payaṉpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. kuḷattu nīr tūymai aṟṟavuṭaṉ appuṟappaṭuttappaṭal vēṇṭum aṉṟō? ataṟkākavē icceykuḷattiṉ mūlai oṉṟil catura vaṭivil puḻai oṉṟu amaintuḷḷatu. aḵtu oru maṉitaṉ tārāḷamāka nuḻaintu cellakkūṭiya aḷavu akaṉṟatāka irukkiṉṟatu. atu, vēṇṭum pōtu taṇṇirai vaṭiyāmal taṭuttu vaippataṟkākavum, vēṇṭātapōtu taṇṇīrai veḷiviṭutaṟkākavum amaintuḷḷa matakaippōlac ciṟanta vēlaippāṭuṭaiyatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, icceykuḷattiṉ vēlaippāṭu oṉṟē ārāycciyāḷaraip peritum viyappuṟac ceytateṉṉal mikaiyākātu. itaṉ arukil uḷḷa piṟa maṇ mēṭukaḷum tōṇṭappaṭṭa piṉṉarē itaṉ arumai perumaikaḷ mēlum viḷakkamākum.

ceṅkaṟkaḷ

vīṭukaḷ, kiṇaṟukaḷ, kuḷaṅkaḷ, kaḻinīrppātaikaḷ iṉṉa piṟavaṟṟukkum moheñco-tarōvil payaṉpaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷaippaṟṟiya cila vivaraṅkaḷai aṟital iṅku avaciyamākum. cintup piratēca makkaḷ cumēriyāviliruntu nilakkīlaik koṇṭuvantavāṟē ceṅkalceyyum muṟaiyiṉaiyum piṟariṭam kaṟṟaṉarā? aṉṟi iyalpākat tāmē uṇarntaṉarā? eṉṉum viṉākkaṭku viṭai kāṇal kaṭamaiyākum.

paṇṭai nāṭukaḷ

ekiptu, cumēriyā mutaliya paṇṭaip pukaḻ paṭaitta nāṭukaḷil cūḷaiyiṭappaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷaik kaṭṭiṭaṅkaṭkup payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkamē appaṇṭai nāṭkaḷil eḻuntilatu. urōma aracu ēṟpaṭṭa piṉṉarē ceṅkaṟkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttum muṟaiyai am mēṟkuppuṟa nāṭukaḷ aṟiyalāyiṉa. ekiptil kallaip pōṉṟa oruvakaik kaṭiṉamāṉa poruḷ mikutiyākak kiṭaittatu. ataṉāl, ammakkaḷ ceṅkaṟkaḷaic ceyyavēṇṭiya avaciyam eḻavillai. cumēriyarkkum aṅṅaṉamē. eṉavē, cintup piratēca makkaḷ ceṅkaṟkaḷaic ceyyum muṟaiyaip piṟa nāṭṭāriṭam kaṟṟilar: tāmākavē ammuṟaiyaik kaṇṭupiṭittaṉar eṉak kūṟal tavaṟākātu. ‘avaciyamē ārāycciyiṉ tāy’ eṉpatu uṇmai yaṉṟō? avarkaḷ, cintu āṟṟaṅkaraiyil iyaṟkaiyil kiṭaitta kaḷimaṇṇaippatamākki aṟuttukkāyavaittup payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; atu kaṭuveyilukkum maḻaikkum āṟṟātataik kaṇṭapiṉ, ataṉaic cūḷaiyil iṭṭuc cuṭṭup payaṉpaṭuttiṉarātal vēṇṭum iṅṅaṉam apperumakkaḷ kaiyāṇṭa muṟaiyē namnāṭṭil iṉṟaḷavum kaiyāḷappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.

ceṅkaṟkaḷ cuṭappaṭṭa muṟaikaḷ

appaṇṭai nāṭkaḷil veṭṭa veḷikaḷil kaḷimaṇ ceṅkaṟkaḷai aṟuttuk kāyavaittu, piṉ viṟakukaḷaikkoṇṭu tī mūṭṭic ceṅkaṟkaḷaic cuṭṭu vantaṉar. immuṟai kāḷavāykaḷ ēṟpaṭṭa piṉṉarum ikkālattum vaḻakkil iruttalāl, cintup piratēcattilum appaḻaṅkālattil orupāl immuṟaiyum piṟitorupāl kāḷavāyil cuṭum muṟaiyum iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. moheñco-tarō makkaḷ ceṅkaṟkaḷaic cūḷaiyiṭuvataṟkākavēp payaṉpaṭuttiya kāḷvāykaḷ vaṭṭa vaṭivil ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉpataṟkuriya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ikkāḷavāykaḷ aṭiyil neruppu muṭṭappaṭum. ivaṟṟiliruntu pukaiyai veḷippaṭutta pukaip pōkkikaḷ amaikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.

ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil cila vīṭukaḷiṉ uṭ puṟaccuvarkaḷ ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭavai. pala iṭaṅkaḷil nilattiṉ aṭimaṭṭa aḷavai uyarttuvataṟkāka ivvularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. iṅṅaṉam ivvularnta kaṟkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkam appaṇṭaik kālam mutal iṉṟaḷavum innāṭṭil iruntu varukiṉṟatu. iṉṟaiya cintu mākāṇattil immuṟai nirampak kaiyāḷappaṭukiṉṟatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

ceṅkaṟkaḷiṉ aḷavukaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil irunta ceṅkal aṟukkum toḻilāḷar matinuṭpam vāyntavarātal vēṇṭum. avarkaḷ ovvoru ceṅkallaiyum ataṉ payaṉukku ēṟṟavāṟu ceytuḷḷaṉar. ovvoṉṟum kōṇalum mēṭupaḷḷaṅkaḷum iṉṟi naṉṟāy aṟukkappaṭṭuc camaṉākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cūḷaiyiṭappaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ patamaṟintu cūḷaiyiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ovvoru ceṅkallum ēṟakkuṟaiya nīḷattiṟ pāti akalamum akalattiṟ pāti uyaramum (1 : ½ : ¼) uṭaiyatāka amaintiruttal peritum viyakkattakkatu. ivvaḷavil amaintuḷḷa ceṅkaṟkaḷē palavākum. kaḻinīrp pātaikaṭkuc ceyyappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷum piṟa kuṟippiṭṭa kāriyaṅkaṭkeṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷum aḷaviṟ periyaṉavum ciṟiyaṉavumāka irukkiṉṟaṉa; avaṟṟuḷ cila 55 ce. mī nīḷamum 28 ce. mī. akalamum 7.5 ce.mī. uyaramum uṭaiyavai; cila 5 ce. mī. nīḷamuṭaiyavai; cila 23 ce. mī. nīḷamuṭaiyavai. icceṅkaṟkaḷ tēvaikku ēṟṟa vaṭivil ceyyappaṭṭavai ākum. cila cama caturamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa; cila nīḷa caturamāka uḷḷaṉa; cila vaḷaivākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa; cila kaṇṇāṭipōla vaḻa vaḻappākkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila mūlaikaḷukku ēṟpa mukkōṇamāka veṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. “āriyar intiyāvil kālaṭi eṭuttu vaippataṟku muṉ intiyāvil nākarikam eṉṟu oṉṟu iruntatē illai” eṉṟu niṉaittavarum pēciyavarum eḻutiyavarumē viyakkumāṟu iccintup piratēca nākarikam ceṅkaṟkaḷilum ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu eṉiṉ, ippiratēcattup paṇṭaip perumaiyai eṉṉeṉpatu!



* * *





↑ Bitumen.

↑ Gypsum Mortar.

↑ Dr.Mackay’s ‘The Indus Civilization’, pp.57, 58.





* * *





﻿7. vīṭṭup poruḷkaḷum viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷum

vīṭṭukkuriya poruḷkaḷ

moheñco-tarō makkaḷ taṅkaḷ vāḻviṟkut tēvaiyāṉa ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉpatu aṅkuk kiṭaittuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiliruntu naṉkaṟiyalām. apporuḷkaḷ kaḷimaṇṇālum marattālum cempālum veṇkalattālum caṅku, veṇkal mutaliyavaṟṟālum ceyyap paṭṭavai. vīṭṭukkuriya perumpālāṉa poruḷkaḷ kaḷi maṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉavē ākum. ciṟappuṭai nāṭkaḷil caṅkālum veṇkallālum ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar. maṭṭāṇṭap poruḷkaḷē mikutiyāka iruttaliṉ, avaṟṟaip paṟṟi mutaṟkaṇ pēcuvōm.

maṭpāṇṭa māṇpu

enta iṭattil nilattai akaḻntu ārāycci ceyyiṉum, aṅkuk kiṭaikkum palatiṟappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷuḷ ārāycciyāḷar maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaiyē ciṟantavaiyāka matikkiṉṟaṉar. oru nakaram aḻivuṟumpōtu-tuṟakkappaṭumpōtu annakarattār viṭṭuc celvaṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷē ākum. piṟar paṭaiyeṭuppiṉālum cētamākātaṉavum kavarappaṭātaṉavum matikkappaṭātaṉavum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷē ākum. ivviru kāraṇaṅ kaḷālum ammaṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ citaivukaḷum annakara makkaḷiṉ uṇmainākarikattai uḷḷavāṟu uṇarttuvaṉavākum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttu, avaiyē ammakkaḷiṉ kālam, aṟivu, āṟṟal iṉṉa piṟavum uṇmaiyāka uṇarttum āṟṟal uṭaiyaṉa. ikkāraṇaṅkaḷāṟṟāṉ cintuppiratēca ārāycciyāḷar, cintup piratēcattil uḷḷa pala iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭaitta maṭṭāṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum citainta maṇ oṭukaḷaiyum viṭāmaṟ pātukāttu varukiṉṟaṉar; moheñco-tarōvil uḷḷa illaṅkaḷilum kaḻinīrp pātaikaḷilum citaintum citaiyāmalum kiṭaitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaic cēmittu vaittuḷḷaṉar.

palavakai maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ mutal vīṭṭiṟkup payaṉpaṭ maṭpāṇṭap poruḷkaḷ varai yāvum pala tiṟappaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷai uṭaiyaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. cila naṉṉilaiyil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa; cila araikuṟaiyāṉa nilaiyil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa; pala citainta nilaiyil irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟukkum harappāvil kiṭaittuḷḷa pala tiṟappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaṭkum ciṟitaḷavē vēṟupāṭu uḷḷatu. ivvēṟupāṭu koṇṭu, harappā nakaram moheñco-tarōvai viṭac ciṟitu muṟpaṭṭatāka irukkakkūṭumō eṉṟu aiyuṟuvārum uḷar. ivviraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum nāṭōṟum kaiyāḷappaṭṭa maṭṭāṇṭaṅkaḷaip pōṉṟavai ekiptilum pāpilōṉiyāvilum nāṭōṟum vīṭukaḷil payaṉpaṭṭaṉavākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, akkālattu nākarika nāṭukaḷil ellām immaṭṭāṇṭaṅkaḷ peritum oṉṟu pōlavē iruntaṉa eṉak kūṟal oruvāṟu poruntuvatākum.

kaḷimaṇ kalavai

cintu āṟṟukkaḷi maṇṇē moheñco-tarō makkaṭkup pērutavi purintatākum. ammaṇṇil ōraḷavu aprakamum cuṇṇāmpum aṇukkaḷ vaṭivil kalantiruntaṉa. maṭpāṇpu viṉaiyāḷar ammaṇṇai ōraḷvu teḷḷiya maṇaluṭaṉ kalantu naṉṟāka araittu, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷaiyum viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷaiyum ērāḷamākac ceytaṉar. ikkalavaiyiṉāl poruḷkaḷ uṟutiyāka irukkum eṉpatai avviṉaiyāḷar naṉku aṟintiruntamai viyappukku uriyatē ākum. ippalavakaip poruḷkaḷai ārāynta aṟiñarkaḷ, ‘immaṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷē ulakil paḻaimaiyum uṟutiyum aḻakum poruntiyavai’ eṉṟu kūṟi viyakkiṉṟaṉar.

vēṭkōvar uruḷaikaḷ

maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ aṉaittum vēṭkōvar uruḷaiyaik koṇṭē ceyyappaṭṭavai ākum. vēṭkōvar, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷai uruḷaikaḷ mūlam uruvākkiya piṉṉar, avaṟṟiṉmītu ceṅkāvi niṟam pūcik kāyavaittu, piṉṉar avaṟṟai vaḻavaḻappākki, iṟutiyiṟ cūḷaiyiṭṭaṉar. ivvēlaikaṭkup piṉṉarē ammaṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kaṇkavar vaṉappup peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam uruḷaikaḷ tuṇaikkoṇṭu ceyyappaṭāmal vēṭkōvar tam kaikaḷaik koṇṭē ceyyappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum cila kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai makaḷirāl ceyyappaṭṭu, vīṭṭiṟkuḷḷēyē cūḷaiyiṭappaṭṭaṉavāka iruttalkūṭum eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē pōṉṟa ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

kāḷavāy

vēṭkōvar payaṉpaṭuttiya uruḷaikaḷil oṉṟēṉum iṉṟu kiṭaittilatu. āyiṉum, avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiṉa kāḷavāykaḷ cila citainta nilaiyil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. avai vaṭṭavaṭivil 180 allatu 210 ce. mī. cuṟṟaḷavu uṭaiyaṉavāka amaintuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ aṭippākattil. niṟaiyat tuḷaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. cūḷaiyiṭappaṭa vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷ kāḷavāyiṉ mītuḷḷa camamāṉa iṭattil vaikkappaṭṭaṉa; aṭiyil tīmūṭṭappaṭṭatu. tī tuḷaikaḷ mūlam mēṟceṉṟu, mēlē vaikkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷai vēkac ceytatu, mēṟpuṟattil kuḻai oṉṟu iruntatu, ataṉ vaḻiyākavē pukai veḷicceṉṟatu. cūḷaiyiṭappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷaik kāṇkaiyil, kāḷavāykaḷ mutaṟṟaramāṉa muṟaiyil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatum, vēṭkōvar paṇpaṭṭa viṉaiyāḷar eṉpatum eḷitiṟ pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa. merukiṭum karuvikaḷ

vēṭkōvar maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷai merukiṭa eṉṟē oruvakaik karuviyaip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. akkaruvi elumpiṉāl āṉatu. atu 38 ce. mī nīḷamum 4 ce. mī. akalamum 10 ce.mī. kaṉamum uṭaiyatu. akkaruvi oṉṟu ārāycciyāḷarukkuk kiṭaittatu. ataippōlavē ciṟiya aḷavuḷḷa karuvikaḷ cilavum kiṭaittaṉa. ivai allāmal ciṟiya elumput tuṇṭukaḷum kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷai merukiṭuvataṟkākap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. harappāvil 42 ce. mī. nīḷamuḷḷa merukiṭum karuvi oṉṟu kiṭaittatu. ikkaruvikaḷ taraiyaiyum cuvarkaḷaiyum vaḻavaḻappākkavum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. vēṭkōvar illaṅkaḷil immerukiṭum paṇiyaip peṇpālārē ceytiruttal kūṭiyatē eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar: cūḷaiyiṭappaṭṭa pāṇṭaṅkaḷmītu aḻakiya niṟaṅkaḷaip pūciyavarum oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭiyavarum makaḷirākavē iruttalum kūṭiyatē. eṉṉai? iṉṟum moheñcertarōvaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa irāmaṅkaḷil ivvēlaikaḷaic ceytu varupavar peṇmaṇikaḷē ātaliṉ eṉka.[1]

palaniṟap paṇṭaṅkaḷ

moheñco-tarō makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya maṭkalaṉkaḷuḷ oru cila tavirap perumpālāṉa ētēṉum oruvakai niṟam tīṭṭap paṭṭaṉavākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuṭpala, cenniṟampūcappaṭṭuk kaṟuppup paṭṭaikaḷ aṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. civappu karumai eṉṉum iraṇṭu niṟaṅkaḷē peritum payaṉpaṭṭu iruppiṉum pacumai, veṇmai, cāmpal niṟaṅkaḷum mikutiyākavē payaṉpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mañcaḷ niṟam arumaiyākavē payaṉpaṭṭuḷḷatu. cila pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu veḷutta mañcaḷ paṭṭaimītu kaṟuppuppaṭṭai aṭikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. cila pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu paḻuppu niṟam tīṭṭappaṭṭu ataṉmītu kaṟuppuppaṭṭai aṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

ippaṉṉiṟa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ moheñco-tarōvukkut teṟkē 128 ce.mī. tolaivil uḷḷa amrīyilum kiṭaittaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ civappum kaṟuppum mikutiyākap pūcappaṭṭavaiyē palavākum. moheñco-tarōviṟku vaṭamēṟkil 176 ce. mī. tolaivil uḷḷa ‘nāl’ eṉṉum paḻampatiyiṟ kiṭaitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ pala paccai niṟam pūcappaṭṭavai ākum. iṅṅaṉam maṭpāṇṭaṅkaṭku niṟam pūcum vaḻakkam appaṇṭaikkālam mutal iṉṟu varai innāṭṭil iruntu varutal kavaṉikkat takkatu. ippaḻakkam iṉṟum moheñco-tarōvukku arukil uḷḷa cila kirāmaṅkaḷilum iruntu varukiṟatu.

niṟaṅkaḷaip pūcuvāṉēṉ?
