maṭkalaṅkaḷil kaṭpulaṉukkut teriyāta pala ciṟiya tuḷaikaḷ iruttal kūṭum. niṟaṅkaḷ uḷḷum puṟamum pūcappaṭuvatāl attuḷaikaḷ aṭaipaṭum; aḻakum koṭukkum. ivviru kāraṇaṅkaḷai naṉku uṇarnta appaṇṭai nakarattu vēṭkōvar tām ceyta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaṭku niṟam pūciṉar eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē karutukiṉṟār. iṅṅaṉamē taṇṇīr cēmittuvaikkap payaṉpaṭṭa periya tāḻikaḷ nilakkīl pūcappaṭṭiruntaṉavām.

oviyam koṇṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil oviyam tīṭṭappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. civappu niṟam pūcappaṭṭa pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu kaṟuppu niṟaṅkoṇṭu tīṭṭappaṭṭa oviyaṅkaḷ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa, ūrvaṉa, paṟappaṉa, naṭappaṉa, nīntuvaṉa mutaliyaṉa oviyaṅkaḷākat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṟavaikaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ uruvaṅkaḷē pala vilaṅkukaḷiṉ oviyam pul taraiyuṭaṉum kāṭukaḷuṭaṉum cērttu iyaṟkaik kāṭciyāka eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṟavaikaḷ marakkiḷaikaḷil iruttal pōlavum, maraṅkaṭku aṭiyil iruppaṉa pōlavum tīṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iraṇṭu kāṭṭukkōḻikaḷ putar arukil iruttalaippōla eḻutappaṭṭuḷa. oru tāḻiyiṉ mītu pala vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷa. ittakaiya cittiraṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ ēlam, cumēr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaitta pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu maṉita uruvam tīṭṭappaṭṭilatu. āṉāl harappāvil kiṭaitta uṭainta maṭkala oṭu oṉṟiṉ mītu maṉitaṉ uruvamum kuḻantai uruvamum tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai allāmal, moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaitta pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu mīṉtōl, oru vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ malar, vāyil malar vaittukkoṇṭu niṟkum mayil malarkkottukkaḷ, māvilai, aracilai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cilavaṟṟiṉmītu kaḻukukaḷ varicai varicaiyākap paṟappaṉa pōlavum, mayil oṉṟu paṟappatu pōlavum varaiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oṉṟiṉmītu ōṭam oṉṟu eḻutappaṭṭuḷatu. cintu āṟṟil pala ōṭaṅkaḷait tiṉamum kaṇṭa ammakkaḷ, ōṭattiṉ paṭattait tīṭṭap peritum virumpiṉarilar. eṉṉai? ōṭam tiṭṭappeṟṟa pāṇṭam itukāṟum oṉṟē kiṭaitta tātaliṉ eṉka.

eṅkum ellā eḻiluṟu oviyam

oru maṭpāṇṭattiṉmītu vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ vaṭṭam tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷatu. ivvaṭṭaṅkaḷ piḻaipaṭāmal amai vataṟkāka, mutalil caturak kōṭukaḷ iḻukkappaṭṭu, avaṟṟiṉ utaviyāl vaṭṭaṅkaḷ oḻuṅkāka amaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iv amaippu muṟai piṟa nāṭṭu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu kāṇumāṟu illai eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar. moheñco-tarōvilum immuṟai cila pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītē amaintuḷḷatu. vēṟu cila pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu kaiyāl vaṭṭaṅkaḷ iḻukkappaṭṭuḷa. eṉavē, avvaṭṭaṅkaḷ naṉṟāka amaintila.

vēṟu pala oviyaṅkaḷ

cila tāḻikaḷ mītu maraṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aṭimarattaik kuṟikkat taṭitta kōṭukaḷum kiḷaikaḷaik kuṟikka melliya vaḷainta kōṭukaḷum iḻuttuviṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu piṟaimati tiṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cilavaṟṟiṉ mītu - tiṟampaṭat tiṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa caturaṅka oviyam kuṟippiṭat takkatu. oru kaṭṭam kaṟuppākavum maṟṟoṉṟu civappākavum aḷavu piṟaḻāmalum varaiyappaṭṭiruttal vaṉappuṭaṉ kāṭci yaḷikkiṉṟatu. pala pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu mukkōṇam oṉṟākavum iraṇṭākavum mūṉṟākavum cērttu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

maṭpāṇṭa vakaikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaittuḷḷa maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷil cila vēṟeṅkumē illātavaiyāka iruttal kavaṉittataṟkuriyatu. avai cintup piratēcattiṟkē uriyavai eṉak kūṟal tavaṟākātu. nīr aruntap payaṉpaṭṭa pāṇṭaṅkaḷ, aṭiyil kumiḻ pōṉṟa kūriya vaṭivam peṟṟuk kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. avai nīr parukiya piṉṉark kaviḻttu vaikkappaṭṭaṉ pōlum! cila pāttiraṅkaḷ aṭippuṟam taṭṭaiyākavum mēṟpuṟam tiraṇṭu kūmpiya vaṭivattuṭaṉum kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. ivai palavakaik kiṇṇaṅkaḷ, ciṟu taṇṭiṉmītu poruttappaṭṭa taṭṭukkaḷ, ciṟiya akalkaḷ, ciṟiya mūkkuṭaiya ēṉaṅkaḷ, taṭṭukaḷ, tāmpāḷaṅkaḷ, vaṭṭilkaḷ, paṭikkaṅkaḷ, kuṭaṅkaḷ, ciṟitu kuṭaivāṉa pāttiraṅkaḷ, mēcaimītu malar vaikkap payaṉpaṭum nīṇṭa pāttiram pōṉṟavai, pampara uruvil amainta cīcākkaḷ, ikkālac cīcākkaḷaip pōṉṟavai, pāṉaikaḷ, caṭṭikaḷ, perun tāḻikaḷ, nīrt toṭṭikaḷ, palavakai muṭikaḷ, purimaṉaikaḷ eṉap palavakai ākum.

pūcaikkuriya maṭpāṇṭam

itaṉ uyaram 5 ce. mī. iḵtu aṭippuṟattil taṇṭu pōṉṟa vaṭivam uṭaiyatu. itaṉ mēṟpuṟam vaṭṭil oṉṟu vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivvamaippuṭaṉ irukkum ippāṇṭam pūcaikkeṉṟē payaṉpaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. ittakaiya kalaṉkaḷ pāpilōṉiya nakaraṅkaḷāṉa, ‘kiṣ, ur, pārā’ eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭaittiruttal kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu.

kaṉal caṭṭi

ituvum maṇṇāl iyaṉṟatēyām. iḵtu, ikkālattu marakkāl pōṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu niṟaiyat tuḷaikaḷ iṭappaṭṭu mēl mūṭiyuṭaṉ irukkiṉṟatu. itaṟkuḷ neruppiṭṭu aṟaikkuḷ kaṭṭilkaḷukku aṭiyil vaippiṉ, tuḷaikaḷ vaḻiyē aṉal veḷippaṭṭu aṟaikkuḷ uḷḷa kuḷirai appuṟappaṭuttum aṟai cūṭāka irukkum. ikkaṉal caṭṭikaḷ pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ikkaṉal caṭṭikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttic cukavāḻvu vāḻak kaṟṟirunta appaṇṭaip perumakkaḷ perumaiyai eṉṉeṉpatu!

kumiḻkaḷ koṇṭa tāḻi

itu mikka viyappūṭṭum pāttiramākum. itaṉaic ciṟappuṭaip putai poruḷ eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉ uṭampil muḷ, pōṉṟa amaippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa; aḵtāvatu, kumiḻkaḷ varicai varicaiyāka vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.itaṉ aṭippuṟam kūmpi oru taṇṭu pōl amaintuḷḷatu. ittakaiya nūtaṉa pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mikac cilavē kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.ivai pōṉṟvai irākkiṉmēlpakutikaḷil uḷḷa ṭel asmar eṉṉum iṭattil kaṇṭeṭukkap paṭṭaṉa eṉpatu kavaṉittaṟkuriyatu.

veṇkaṟ pāṉaikaḷ

veṇ kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pāṇṭaṅkaḷ cilavēṉum moheñco-tarōvil iruntaṉa eṉpatai uṟutippaṭuttac cila citainta oṭukaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.ivai nīlaṅ kalanta cāmpal niṟattuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. uṭainta oṭukaḷ nirampak kiṭaikkāmaiyiṉ, ippāttiraṅkaḷ moheñco-tarōvil arukiyē iruntaṉa eṉṟu niṉaikkalām. āṉāl, ivai cumēriyāvil nirampak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. palucistāṉ-īrāṉ ellaip piratēcattil kaṟuppum nīlamum tīṭṭappaṭṭa ittakaiya veṇkaṟ pāttira ōṭukaḷ palavaṟṟai ṭākṭar makkē kaṇṭu piṭittaṉar. eṉavē, ‘ivai, cintup piratēcattiṟku mēṟkē pēraḷavil payaṉpaṭṭaṉa; cintup piratēcattil mikakkuṟainta aḷavilēyē payaṉpaṭṭaṉa’, eṉṟu avar kūṟukiṉṟār.

kaippiṭi koṇṭa kalaṉkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaitta vāṇal caṭṭi oṉṟu irupuṟamum kaippiṭi koṇṭatāka irukkiṉṟatu. periya tāḻikaḷiṉ kalayam pōṉṟa mēlmūṭikaḷiṉ mēṟpuṟattil maṭṭum kaippiṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. vēṟu cila maṭkalaṉkaḷil kaippiṭikkup patilākak kaiviral nuḻaiyat takkavāṟu tuḷaikaḷ amaintuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ, harappāvil oru puṟam kaippiṭi koṇṭa kalayamum irupuṟam kaippiṭi koṇṭa kalayamum kiṭaittaṉa. iraṭṭaik kaippiṭi uḷḷa pāttiraṅkaḷ pala irākkil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.[2] kaippiṭi kaḷuṭaṉ ōviyam tīṭṭappaṭṭa pāttiraṅkaḷ pala mālṭṭāvil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.[3] kaippiṭiyuḷḷa mūṭikaḷ jemṭeṭnasr eṉṉum iṭattil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa[4]

maikkūṭukaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil āṭukaḷ pōlac ciṟiya kaṟkaḷil cetukkappaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mēṟpuṟam kuḻiyāka iruntatu. akkuḻi mai ūṟṟikkoḷḷap payaṉpaṭṭatām. ipporuḷkaḷ aṅku mikutiyākak kiṭaikkavillai. āṉāl, ivai pōṉṟavai pala kirisai aṭutta ējiyaṉ tīvukaḷil akappaṭṭuḷḷaṉavām. ivai ‘ur’ eṉṉum iṭattil ki.mu. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil maik kūṭukaḷākavē payaṉpaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu sar ārtar ivāṉs aṟaikiṉṟār. iḵtu uṇmaiyāyiṉ, cintup piratēca makkaḷ 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē maiyaik koṇṭu eḻutum muṟaiyaik kaiyāṭi vantaṉar eṉpatu aṟiyattakum.

purimaṇaikaḷ

pala vakai maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷai vaikkap purimaṉaikaḷ payaṉ paṭṭaṉa. avai maṇṇālum marattālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai uyaramākavum parumaṉākavum amaintuḷḷaṉa. harappāvil 20 ce.mī. uyaramuḷḷa purimaṇaikaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. periyatāḻikaḷai vaikka marappurimaṉaikaḷē payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. cātāraṇa māṉa purimaṇai pōṉṟu karuṅkallaik koṇṭu 30 ce.mī. uyarattil ceyyappaṭṭa purimaṇaikaḷ pala moheñco-trōvil kiṭaittaṉa. ivai iliṅkaṅkaḷiṉ aṭiyil vaikkattakka ‘yōṉikaḷ’, eṉṟu ivaṟṟaik kaṇṭu piṭitta irāypahatūr tayārām cahaṉi kūṟiyuḷḷār. āyiṉ, ṭākṭar makkē, ivai tūṇkaṭku aṭiyil vaikkattakka kal puri maṇaikaḷ’ eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.

putaikkappaṭṭa tāḻikaḷ

pala vīṭukaḷil tāṉiyaṅkaḷaik koṭṭivaikkap payaṉ paṭṭa periyatāḻikaḷ pātiyaḷavu nilattiṟ putaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mēṟpākam mikka vaḻavaḻappāka amaintuḷḷatu. elikaḷ ēṟātiruttaṟkeṉṟē ivvaḷavu vaḻavaḻappāka immēṟpakuti amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē karutukiṉṟār. nilattiṟ putaitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṟṟāṉ nakaikaḷum piṟa vilai uyarnta poruḷkaḷum vaittup pātukākkappaṭṭaṉa. ippaḻakkam cintup piratēcak kirāmaṅkaḷil iṉṟum iruppatāka aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

elip poṟikaḷ

elikaḷaip piṭikkum poṟikaḷ cuṭṭa kaḷimaṇṇāl iyaṉṟavai. ivaṟṟil iraṇṭu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. poṟikkuḷ uṇavu mutaliyavaṟṟai iraiyāka vaittu elikaḷ piṭikkappaṭṭu vantaṉa.

piṅkāṉ ceyyum muṟai

cila periya tāḻikaḷiṉ veḷippuṟamum ciṟiya pāṇ ṭaṅkaḷum taṭṭukaḷum pīṅkāṉ pōlap paḷapaḷappākkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. itaṉāl, cintup piratēca makkaḷ appaṇṭaik kālattilēyē pīṅkāṉ ceyyum muṟaiyai oruvāṟu uṇarntiruntaṉar eṉpatu pulaṉākiṟatu. ippiṅkāṉ pōṉṟa paḷapaḷappu uṇṭākkap pala poruḷkaḷaic cērttu araitta kalavaiyaiyē payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum. cintup piratēca makkaḷ akkalavaiyaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatu veḷiyākiṟatu.āyiṉ, ippiṅkāṉ pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ ēlam, cumēr eṉṉum nāṭukaḷil irunta cavak kuḻikaḷil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ataṉāl, ipporuḷkaḷ aṅkiruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭ ṭiruttalum kūṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar niṉaikkiṉṟaṉar.

ammi, ēntiram, ural

cintup piratēca makkaḷ ammi, kuḻavi, ural ivaṟṟaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. māvaraikkum kal ēntiraṅkaḷ nirampak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.ivai periya kaltaṭṭukaḷ mītu vaikkappaṭṭē māvaraikkap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. araikkumpoḻutu mā citaṟik kīḻē viḻāmal iruppataṟkākavē kal taṭṭu ēntirattiṉ aṭiyil vaikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. harappāvilum ittakaiya ēntiraṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. kalluralkaḷum harappāvil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.

palavakai viḷakkukaḷ

harappāvil muṭṭai vaṭivattil viḷakkoṉṟu kiṭaittatu. itaṉ vāyppuṟam kuvintu, tiri iṭuvataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivviḷakkiṟkuḷ oruvakai eṇṇey iṭṭut tiriyiṭṭuk koḷuttum vaḻakkam iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. vēṟu palavakai viḷakkukaḷum kiṭaittuḷa. moheñco-tarōvil oru vakai meḻukuvartti vaikkum taṭṭukaḷ kaḷimaṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ‘appaḻaṅkālattil meḻuku vattikaḷ upayōkattil iruntaṉa eṉpatu cuvai payakkum ceytiyē ākum’, eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē kūṟi viyakkiṉṟār.[5]

piṟa poruḷkaḷ

katāyutam pōṉṟu cāmpal niṟak kallāl ceyyappaṭṭa oru karuvi moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaittatu. kal ūci, elumpu ūci, kaṟkōṭari, cempu arivāḷ, marakkaṭṭilkaḷ, ! nāṇaṟpāykaḷ, kōraippāykaḷ, mēcaikaḷ, nāṟkāli pōṉṟa uyarnta marappīṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa iruntamaikkuriya kuṟikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, moheñco-tarōvil illattukkuriya ellāp poruḷkaḷum kuṟaiviṉṟi oruvāṟu amaintiruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟutal poruntum

viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil vāḻnta kuḻantaikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ palavākum: maṇ koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavaikaḷ, ūrvaṉa, nīrvāḻvaṉa, āṇpeṇ patumaikaḷ, ciṟu ceppukkaḷ mutaliyaṉa nirampak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. kucci oṉṟil kayiṟu iṇaikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. akkayiṟṟai acaittaṟku ēṟṟavāṟu akkucci mītu paṟavai oṉṟu ēṟuvatum iṟaṅkuvatumāka iruttaṟkuriya vacatiyōṭu kūṭiya karuvikaḷ cila kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. cintup piratēca vēṭkōvar ipporuḷkaḷaic ceyvatil kāṭṭiyuḷḷa tiṟamai peritum viyappukkuriyatākum. apperu makkaḷ 1.5 ce. mī. uyarattilum aḻaku mikunta poruḷkaḷaic ceytuḷḷaṉar eṉiṉ, avartam paṇpaṭṭa toḻiṟ ciṟappai eṉṉeṉap pārāṭṭuvatu icciṟiya poruḷkaḷum oḻuṅkākavum veḷippuṟam miṉumiṉuppākavum eḻil mikuntu kāṇumāṟu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷamai ikkālattār pārāṭṭutaṟku uriyatēyākum. toḻilāḷar caṅku tantam, kiḷiñcal oṭukaḷ, elumpukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟālum viḷaiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷaic ceytuḷḷaṉar. ittakaiya poruḷkaḷ cintup piratēcattil putaiyuṇṭuḷḷa piṟa nakaraṅkaḷilum kiṭaittalāl, iv vēlaippāṭu appaḻaṅkālattil uyariya nilaiyil irun tiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu, kaḷimaṇ poruḷkaḷ palavum pala tiṟaṅkaḷ koṇṭavai.

iṉṟum ūtum ūtukuḻal

kaḷimaṇṇaik kuṭaivāṉa uruṇṭaiyākac ceytu ataṉ uḷḷē ciṟitu kaṟkaḷai iṭṭuk kalakala eṉṟu olikkumāṟu ceyyappaṭṭa kilukiluppaikaḷ pārkkattakkavai ākum. maṇ pantukaḷ, vālpuṟam tuḷaiyuṭaiya kōḻi, kuruvi pōṉṟa ūtu kuḻalkaḷ kaṇṇaik kavarvaṉavākum. ‘yāṉ ōr ūtukuḻalai eṭuttu ūtiṉēṉ. atu naṉṟāka ūtiyatu. 5000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ payaṉpaṭṭa ūtukuḻal iṉṟum aṅṅaṉamē payaṉpaṭal viyattaṟkuriyatu aṉṟō!’ eṉṟu aṟiñar ci.ār.rāy kūṟiyuḷḷatu viyappūṭṭum ceytiyākum. oru paṟavai vaṭivam amainta ūtukuḻal palavakai ocaikaḷ uṇṭākumāṟu tiṟampaṭac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

talai acaikkum erutu

vēṭkōvar, kaḷimaṇṇaikkoṇṭu vilaṅkukaḷiṉ talaivēṟu-uṭalvēṟākac ceytu, piṟaku iraṇṭaiyum iṇaippataṟku iyaṉṟavāṟu tuḷaikaḷ iṭṭuc cūḷai yiṭṭaṉar. piṉṉar iraṇṭaiyum kayiṟukoṇṭu piṇaittu viṭṭaṉar. iṅṅaṉam ceyyappaṭṭa vilaṅkup patumaikaḷ kāṟṟuppaṭiṉum kayiṟṟai iḻuppiṉum talaiyai acaittuk koṇṭē irukkum. ivvāṟu talai acaikkum vilaṅkup patumaikaḷuḷ erutukaḷē palavākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. kaikaḷai acaikkum kuraṅkup patumaikaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷamai kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

vaṇṭikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kaḷimaṇ (viḷaiyāṭṭu) vaṇṭikaḷ cil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ivai mikka vaṉappuṭaiyavai: naṉku uruṇṭu oṭakkūṭiyavāṟu amaintuḷḷavai. ivai, ki.mu.3200 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa ‘ur’ eṉṉum nakarattiṟ kiṭaitta kalliṟ cetukkappaṭṭa tēr oṉṟiṉaip peritum ottuḷḷaṉavām. ittakaiya vaṇṭikaḷ kaḷimaṇṇālum veṇkalattālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. veṇkala vaṇṭip patumaikaḷiliruntu, akkālattavar vaṇṭikku mēṟkūraiyai amaittup palar uḷḷē amarntu celluvataṟku ēṟṟavāṟē vaṇṭikaḷai amaittup payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatai aṟiyalām: avvaṇṭikaḷaip pōṉṟavaiyē iṉṟum cintup piratēcak kirāmaṅkaḷil iruppavai ākum. maṇ vilaṅkukaḷai maṇ taṭṭukaḷil niṟuttic cakkaram amaittuk kuḻantaikaḷ iḻuppu vaṇṭikaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatum aṟiyattakkatu.

cokkaṭṭāṉ karuvikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kaḷimaṇṇālum kaṟkaḷālum ceyyappaṭṭa pala tiṟappaṭṭa cokkaṭṭāṉ kāykaḷ, kavaṟukaḷ, tāyakkaṭṭaikaḷ mutaliyaṉa kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. kavaṟukaḷ āṟu paṭṭaikaḷai uṭaiyaṉavākak kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru paṭṭaiyilum vaṭṭappuḷḷikaḷ iṭṭu, vevvēṟuvita eṇkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tantattāl ceyyappaṭṭapāyccikaikaḷ ikkālattaṉa pōlavē amaintuḷḷaṉa. ivai nimittam pārppōrāl kuṟi kūṟuvataṟkākap payaṉpaṭṭavai. ivaṟṟiṉ mītu puḷḷikaḷ illai; oviyaṅkaḷum nīlakkōṭukaḷum varaiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ātaliṉ, ivai cokkaṭṭāṉ āṭap payaṉpaṭṭavai alla eṉṟu oru cār aṟiñar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar. ivai allāmal, cokkaṭṭāṉ kāykaḷ pala kallālum kaḷimaṇṇālum ceyyappaṭṭu aḻakiya niṟaṅkaḷ pala pūcappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ, ārāycciyāḷar cilar ivaṟṟaic civaliṅka vaṭivaṅkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟiṉ mātirikaḷ cila ceṉṉaip poruṭkāṭcic cālaiyil vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai ikkālattil caturaṅkam āṭuvōr payaṉpaṭuttum kāykaḷ (Pawns) pōlavē irukkiṉṟaṉa.



↑ Drr Mackay’s ‘The Indus Civilization’, p.142

↑ ‘Iraq’ Vols. 1-4 published by the British School of Archaeology

↑ M.A.Murray’s ‘Excavations in Malta’, part II, pp. 26-28.

↑ Dr.Mackay’s ‘The Indus Civilization’, p.151.

↑ In any case, it is extremely interesting to discover that candles were also in use at such an early date’. Dr.Macky’ The Indus Civilization’, p. 137





* * *





﻿8. kaṇip poruḷkaḷ

payaṉpaṭṭi kaṇipporuḷkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil vāḻnta paṇṭai makkaḷ poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam, veḷḷīyam, kārīyam ivaṟṟaip payaṉpaṭuttippala aṇikaḷum poruḷkaḷum ceytu, koṇṭaṉar. āṉāl, avarkaḷ mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭuttiya ulōkaṅkaḷ cempum veṇkalamumē ākum.avarkaḷ iraṇṭu ulōkaṅkaḷait takka muṟaippaṭi cērttup putiya ulōkam ceyyavum aṟintiruntaṉar.

poṉṉum veḷḷiyum

poṉ ciṟappāka nakaikaṭkē payaṉpaṭṭatu. appoṉṉil ciṟitaḷavu veḷḷi kalantuḷḷatu. ataṉāl appoṉ kōlār, aṉantappūr pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu koṇṭu cellappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar. moheñco-tarō makkaḷ poṉṉaiyum veḷḷiyaiyum kalantu ‘elakṭram’[1] eṉṉum putiya ulōkam oṉṟaic ceyyak kaṟṟiruntaṉar eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ipputiya ulōkamum nakaikaḷ ceyyavē payaṉ paṭṭatām. ammakkaḷ īyattiliruntu veḷḷi eṭukkak kaṟṟiruntaṉar; aṅkaṉam tām eṭutta veḷḷiyaiyē payaṉpaṭuttivantaṉar veḷḷiyaik koṇṭu kālaṇikaḷum cila pāttira vakaikaḷumē ceytu payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.

cempil īyak kalavai

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaitta cempup poruḷ kaḷil īyak kalavai kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. īyak kalavai koṇṭa cempuk kaṉikaḷ irājaputaṉam, pāracīkam, palucistāṉam eṉṉum mūṉṟu iṭaṅkaḷil uṇṭu. avai cintuviṟku aṇmaiyil iruttalāl aṅkiruntē cempu koṇṭuvarappaṭ ṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu.

cempil nikkal kalavai

cempil īyattōṭu nikkalum kalantiruppatāka aṟiñar cōtittu aṟintuḷḷaṉar. ivvāṟē cumēriyāvil kiṭaitta cempilum nikkal kalantuḷḷatu. innikkal kalappuṭaiya cempuk kaṉikaḷ arēpiyāvil uḷḷa ‘ummāṉ’[2] eṉṉum iṭattiṟṟāṉ irukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, cumēriyarum cintup piratēca makkaḷum avviṭattuc cempaiyē payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉṟu cilar karutu kiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ, intiyāvilēyē cōṭṭā nākapuriyil uḷḷa cempuk kaṉikaḷil nikkal kalavai iṉṟum kāṇappaṭuvatāl, ikkaṉikaḷiliruntē cintup piratēca makkaḷ cempaik koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉarātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar makkē uṟutiyāka nampukiṉṟaṉar.[3] ituvē poruttamāṉatākavum tōṟṟukiṟatu.

cempu kalanta maṇ

cempu kalanta maṇ cintup piratēcattilēyē kiṭaittiruttal kūṭum eṉṟu eṇṇutaṟkuriyavāṟu maṇṇiliruntu cempai vēṟākap pirittamaikkuriya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. ceṅkal koṇṭu kaṭṭappaṭṭa toṭṭikaḷil cempu kalanta maṇ kuviyalkaḷum urukkappaṭṭa cempup pāḷaṅkaḷum taṭṭikaḷum kiṭaittṉa āṉāl, ivai urukkappaṭṭa muṟai uṇarumāṟu illai. ātaliṉ, potuvāka nilattiṟ kuḻi ceytu atil nilak kariyum cempum maṇṇum iṭṭut tīppaṟṟa vaittu, turutti mūlam cūṭṭai mikuttuc cempai urukac ceyyum eḷiya muṟaiyaiyē appaṇṭai makkaḷ kaiyāṇṭaṉar; iṅṅaṉam urukiya cempait taraiyil veṭṭappaṭṭa ciṟu kālvāyil pāyccip pāḷam pāḷamāka eṭuttu vantaṉar. immuṟaiyiṟ kiṭaitta cempup pāḷam oṉṟiṉ niṟai 2½ irāttal iruntatu. itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, moheñco-tarō makkaḷ cintup piratēcattilēyē kiṭaitta maṇṇuṭaṉ kalanta cempaiyum veḷiyiliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa cempaiyum payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. eṉpatai aṟiyalām.

veṇkalam

ap paṇṭaikkāla aṟiñar veḷḷīyattaiyum cempaiyum kalantu veṇkalam ākkak kaṟṟiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ toṭakkattil cempaiyē mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar; āṉāl, cempaiviṭa veṇkalam uṟuti uṭaiyatu eṉpatai aṟintatum, veṇkalattaiyum mikutiyāy payaṉpaṭuttat toṭaṅkiṉar; potuvākac cempil 100kku 9 mutal 12 paṅku veḷḷiyattaic cērppataiviṭṭu, 100kku 22 mutal 26 paṅku varai veḷḷiyattaic cērttu veṇkalamākkiyuḷḷaṉar. atē kālattil cumēriyāvilum im muṟai kaiyāḷappaṭṭatu eṉṟu ṭākṭar pīrāṅkpōrṭ, ṭākṭar ulli pōṉṟa aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ, atu tavaṟu; cumēriyarkku veṇkalam ceyyat terintilatu. avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya veṇkalam piṟanāṭukaḷiliruntē varavaḻaikkappaṭṭatu; cumēriyar cempaip payaṉpaṭuttavē aṟintiruntaṉar. āyiṉ, atē kālattil cintup piratēcamakkaḷ cempaiyum veṇkalattaiyum payaṉpaṭutta naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar eṉṟu ōr aṟiñar[4] aṟaikiṉṟaṉar. cintup piratēca makkaḷ veṇkalam ceyta muṟai mikka paṇpuṭaiyatāka iruntatāl, piṟkālattil acōkaṉ āṭciyil nālantāvil itē muṟai ciṟitum māṟṟamiṉṟik kaik koḷḷappaṭṭatu.[5]

veḷḷiyam

moheñco-tarōvil cempoṭu kalappataṟkē veḷḷiyam mikutiyāka vēṇṭiyiruntatāl, veḷḷiyattāṟ poruḷkaḷ palavum ceyyakkūṭavillai. ataṉāl, ammakkaḷ pampāy, pīhār, orissā, mutaliya piṟa maṇṭilaṅkaḷiliruntu veḷḷīyattai varavaḻaittiruttal vēṇṭum.

kārīyam

cintup piratēca makkaḷ kārīyattai mikutiyāyp payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉṟu kūṟutal iyalātu. aṅku itukāṟum kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷuḷ taṭṭu oṉṟē kārīyattāl ceyyappaṭṭatāka uḷḷatu. inta ulōkattāl ceyyappaṭṭa vēṟu poruḷkaḷ kiṭaikkavillai.

makkaḷiṉ mati nuṭpam

orē iṭattil ellāp poruḷkaḷum kiṭaippatu iyaṟkai yaṉṟu. ovvoru poruḷ ovvōr iṭattiṟ kiṭaippatē iyaṟkai. āyiṉum, nām namakkut tēvaippaṭum pala poruḷkaḷaip palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu varavaḻaittukkoḷkiṟōm allavā? nammaip pōṉṟē appaṇṭaikkāla makkaḷum taṅkaḷ mati nuṭpattāl avai avai kiṭaikkakkūṭiya iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu varavaḻaittu, avaṟṟaik kūrtta matiyuṭaṉ payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉpatu mēṟcoṉṉa ceytikaḷiliruntu aṟiyalām.acceytikaḷ uṇmaiyāyiṉ, ammakkaḷ teṉ intiyāvil kōlār varai irunta nāṭṭai naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar; teṉṉāṭṭavaruṭaṉ vāṇipam ceytaṉar eṉpaṉa aṟiyalām. maṇikaḷum piṟavum ceyyap payaṉpaṭṭa oruvakaip paccaikkal (amejāṉ kal) nīlakiriyil uḷḷa ‘toṭṭa peṭṭā’ eṉṉum iṭattiliruntu moheñco-tarōvukkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭatu eṉṟu aṟiñar kūṟutal uṇmaiyāyiṉ, accintup piratēcamakkaḷ nīlakiriyaiyum naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatu veḷiyām. ammakkaṭkuc caṅkum muttum tamiḻakattiliruntu pōyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu. aḵtāyiṉ, avarkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭuṭaṉum vāṇipat toṭarpu koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉal poruntum. iṅṅaṉam ammakkaḷ appaḻaṅkālattil carittira kālattiṟkum pala āyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar intiyā muḻuvatilum vāṇipap paḻakkam koṇṭiruntaṉar - āṅkāṅkuk kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷai vāṅkip payaṉpaṭuttic cukavāḻkkai vāḻntaṉar eṉṉum ariya ceytikaḷai aṟiya evartām viyappuṟār!

vēlai muṟai

avvaṟiñarkaḷ cemmaiyum veṇkalattaiyum urukki vārppaṭamākkiṉar; takaṭukaḷākat taṭṭi āṇi koṇṭu poruttiṉar; ivviraṇṭu muṟaikaḷāṟṟāṉ ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum ceytukoṇṭaṉar. āṉāl, avarkaḷ poṉ, veḷḷi ivaṟṟāṟ ceyta poruḷkaḷukkē poṭi vaittup paṟṟavaikkum muṟaiyaik kaiyāṇṭaṉar.

cempu-veṇkalap poruḷkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil vāḻnta cempu veṇkalak kaṉṉārkaḷ katti, vāḷ, īṭṭi, ampu mutaliya karuvikḷaiyum, palvēṟu aḷavukaḷaiyuṭaiya kōppaikaḷ nīrccāṭikaḷ, taṭṭukaḷ, tāḻikaḷ.ivaṟṟiṉ mūṭikaḷ mutaliya vīṭṭup poruḷkaḷaiyum ceyyak kaṟṟiruntaṉar; ciṟiya poruḷkaḷai urukki vārppaṭamāka vārttuc ceytuḷḷaṉar; periya poruḷkaḷait takaṭukaḷāka aṭittup poruttic ceytuḷḷaṉar. ivaṟṟuḷ perumpālāṉa poruḷkaḷukkuk kaippiṭiyē illai. iṅṅaṉamē amrī, cāṉhātarō iv viṭaṅkaḷil kiṭaitta cempu - veṇkalap poruḷkaḷukkum kaippiṭi illātatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

īṭṭikaḷ

īṭṭi nīṇṭu akaṉṟa ilaivaṭivil āṉatu kūriya nuṉi uṭaiyatu; pakkaṅkaḷ iraṇṭum kūrākkappaṭṭavai. iṅkaṉam amaintuḷḷa īṭṭi marappiṭi uṭaiyatu. ilaipōṉṟa pakuti mikavum meṉmaiyāka iruppatāl oru muṟai eṟiyappaṭṭatum vaḷaintu keṭum iyalpuṭaiyatu. itai nōkka, ammakkaḷ pakaivarai etirnōkkip pōrkkaruvikaḷaic ceytavar allar eṉpatum, vēṭṭaikkē akkaruvikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatum uṇaralām.

uṭai vāḷkaḷ

itu naṭuppakuti taṭittu, muṉai maḻuṅki, iru pakkamum kūrmai ākkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.itaṉ muṉaimaḻuṅki iruppatāl, itu māṟṟāraik kuttum poruṭṭuc ceyyappaṭṭataṉṟu eṉpatai aiyamaṟa aṟiyalām. moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaitta ittakaiya uṇṭavāḷkaḷil iraṇṭē kuṟippiṭattakkavai; oṉṟu 4.5 ce. mī. nīḷamum, 5.5 ce. mī. akalamum 1 ce. mī. kaṉamum uṭaiyatu. maṟṟoṉṟu aḷaviṟ ciṟiyatu. ittakaiya vāḷkaḷ cumēriyāvil kiṭaittila; āyiṉ, ekiptu, caipras, ciriyā mutaliya nāṭukḷil kiṭaittaṉa. ivai yāvum marak kaippiṭi uṭaiyaṉavē ākum.

iṭaivāḷkaḷum kattikaḷum

iṭuppil ceruki vaikkattakka iṭaivāḷkaḷ pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ cila nīṇṭavai; cila kuṭṭai yāṉavai; cila ilaipōṉṟa vaṭivuṭaiyavai; cila oru puṟam kūriyavai.pala irupuṟam kūriyavai. ivai aṉṟic ciṟiya kattikaḷ cila kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. katti pōlak kallāl āṉa karuvikaḷ cilavum kiṭaittirukkiṉṟaṉa.

vēlkaḷ

veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭa vēlkaḷē palavākum. avai kūriya muṉaiyuṭaṉ eḷitil pāyntōṭak kūṭiyavāṟu amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.avaṟṟuṭ periyavai37.5 ce.mī.nīḷamum 12.5 ce. mī. akalamum palvēṟu vaṭivamum koṇṭavaiyāka uḷḷaṉa. ciṟiyavaiyum kūrmai poruntik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

ampu muṉaikaḷ

ivai melliya veṇkalat takaṭu koṇṭu ceyyap paṭṭavai. ivaiyē nirampak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ivai vēṭṭaiyāṭuvōrāl peritum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu terikiṟatu. ivaṟṟuṭ pala irupuṟamum irampattiṉ amaippai uṭaiyavai. īṭṭi muṉaikaḷaip pōlavē ivaiyum marak kaippiṭi uṭaiyaṉavākum.

irampam

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaitta irampaṅkaḷ pala tiṟappaṭṭavai. oṉṟu arai vaṭṭa vaṭivattil kūriya paṟkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. veṇkala irampam oṉṟu 38, 5 ce. mī. nīḷamum, 8.5 ce.mī. akalamum, 1 ce.mī. kaṉamum uṭaiyatu.itaṟku akaṉṟa marakkaippiṭi iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. kaip piṭiyai irampattōṭu poruttutaṟkeṉṟu mūṉṟu tuḷaikaḷ irampattil amaintuḷḷaṉa. itaṉ paṟkaḷ viyappuṟu muṟaiyil amaintuḷḷaṉa. ittakaiya irampam cumēriyāvilum ekiptilum kāṇappaṭavillai. itu cintup piratēcattiṟkē taṉipperumai taravallatāka irukkiṉṟatu. ciṟiya irampaṅkaḷ caṅkaṟukkap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa pōlum! eṉṉai? iṉṟum vaṅkāḷattiṟ caṅkaṟuppavar ittakaiya ciṟiya irampaṅ kaḷaiyē payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. ātaliṉ eṉka. ‘caṅkaṟuppa teṅkaḷ kulam, caṅkaraṉārk kētukulam’ eṉṟu pāṭiya nakkīrar piṟanta tamiḻakattilum caṅkaṟukka ittakaiya irampaṅkaḷaiyē payaṉpaṭuttiyiruttal kūṭiyatē aṉṟō?

uḷikaḷ

ivai pala vakaippaṭṭavai: periyavai 12, 5 ce. mī. uyarattiṟkum mēṟpaṭṭavai; ciṟiyavai 4.5 ce. mī. uyaramuṭaiyavai. ivai irupuṟamum kūrmai uṭaiyavai. pala uruṭṭu vaṭivil amaintavai; oṉṟu maṭṭum paṭṭai vaṭivil amaintatu. ippaṭṭai vaṭiva uḷi cumēriyā mutaliya ayal nāṭukaḷil kiṭaittilatu. ivvuḷikaḷ kallaiyum marattaiyum cetukkap payaṉpaṭṭavaiyākum; muttiraikaḷaic cetukku vataṟkeṉṟē mika nuṇṇiya muṉaiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya ciṟṟuḷikaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.

tōl cīvum uḷikaḷ

tōlaik koṇṭu toḻil purintavar payaṉpaṭuttiya uḷi taṉippaṭṭa muṟaiyil amaintuḷḷatu. akkālattavar ceruppukaḷai aṇintu iruntaṉar eṉpataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ itukāṟum kiṭaittila. āyiṉum, mirutaṅkam pōṉṟa cila vāttiyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, tōl vēlai akkālattil iruntatu eṉpatai uṇaralām. attōl-toḻilāḷar mēṟcoṉṉa uḷiyaip payaṉ paṭuttiṉar pōlum! atu katti pōṉṟa takaṭu koṇṭu uḷi pōla muṉaiyil maṭṭum kūrmai uṭaiyatākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. or uḷi marappiṭiyuṭaṉ kiṭaittuḷḷatu. ammarappiṭi, ikkālattuk kattip piṭikaṭkup payaṉpaṭuttum maraṅkoṇṭē ceyyappaṭṭiruttal mikka viyappūṭṭuvatākum.

kōṭarikaḷ

kōṭarikaḷ nīṇṭum kuṟukiyum akaṉṟum uḷḷaṉa. ivai, accuc ceytu atil ulōkattai urukki vārttuc ceyyappaṭṭavai eṉṟu aṟiñar matikkiṉṟaṉar; vārppaṭam vārtta piṟaku eṭuttuc cammaṭṭi koṇṭu aṭittuc carippaṭutti oḻuṅkāṉa kōṭariyākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oru kōpari 27.5 ce.mī nīḷamum: pavuṇṭ 3 avuṉs niṟaiyum uṭaiyatākum. ikkōṭarikaḷ naṭuvil marakkāmpai tuḻaikkat tuḷaikaḷ peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.

vāycci

moheñco-tarōvil veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭa vāycci oṉṟu kiṭaittatu. atu mikunta vēlaippāṭu koṇṭatu. ataṉ naṭuvil marakkāmpu nuḻaiya iṭam viṭappṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉ nīḷam 25 ce.mī. ākum. atu, kākasas malaip piratēcattil kūpaṉ āṟṟup paṭukaiyiṟ kiṭaitta vāycciyai ottuḷḷatu viyappukkuriyatu.itaṉ kālam iṉṉatu eṉpatai iṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟal iyalavillai.

maḻittaṟ kattikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil nāṉku vitamāṉa maḻittaṟ kattikaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa: (1) arai vaṭṭa vaṭivil nīṇṭa kaippiṭi koṇṭavai. ivaiyē mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭṭavai. (2) ‘ṭa’ pōṉṟa vaṭivil amaintuḷḷavai. (3) vaḷaintirukkum katti. itaṉ kaippiṭi katti vaḷaivukku ēṟṟavāṟu piṉpuṟam vaḷaintu virittut talaipōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itupōṉṟavai, ekiptil ‘pārō maṉṉar’ tam 18ām talaimuṟai āṭciyil vaḻakkil iruntaṉavām. (4) nāṉkām vakai maḻittaṟ katti nīṇṭu nērāka muṉaiyil maṭṭum vaḷaivuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itu melliya takaṭṭil mikka kūrmaiyākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ikkatti piṟavaṟṟiṉum arukik kiṭaittiruttalāl, matippuṭaiya vakaiyākak karutappaṭṭiruttal kūṭiyatēyām. innāṉku vakai yaṉṟic ciṟu maḻittaṟ kattikaḷum pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ivai mukattil maṭṭum iṉṟi uṭalilum mayir kaḷaiyap payaṉpaṭṭavaiyākum. ivai mikutiyākak kiṭaittalāl ivaṟṟai ‘irupālarum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar pōlum!’[6] eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē karutukiṉṟār.

uḻu karuvikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaitta oruvakaik karuvikaḷ kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai 27.5 ce.mī. nīḷamum 8.5 ce. mī. akalamum, 7.5 ce.mī. uyaramum uṭaiyaṉa. avai irupuṟamum kūrākkappaṭṭavai. avai amaintuḷḷa muṟaiyiliruntum, mikka kaṉamāka iruppatiliruntum kalappaik karuvikaḷāka iruttal kūṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar apippirāyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivai pōlvaṉa cempilum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

tūṇṭil muṭkaḷ

cintu yāṟu mīṉkaḷ niṟaintatātaliṉ, akkāla makkaḷ mīṉ piṭikkuntoḻilil pērārvam kāṭṭiṉar eṉpataimeyppikkak kaṇakkaṟṟa tūṇṭil muṭkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ivai veṇkalattāl iyaṉṟuḷḷaṉa. vaḷainta muṭkaḷai uṭaiyaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ mēlmuṉaiyil nūl nuḻaiyat tuḷai uḷḷatu. attuḷaiyil uṟutiyāṉa kayiṟṟai kaṭṭi mīṉ piṭittaṉar.

piṟa karuvikaḷ

mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkāta taccarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya koṭṭāppuḷikaḷ, iḻaippuḷikaḷ kattikaḷ, tuḷaiyiṭum karuvikaḷ, necavāḷar payaṉpaṭuttiya karuvikaḷ, ūcikaḷ mutaliyaṉa palavākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. tuṇi taikkum ūcikaḷ 5 ce.mī nīḷamuṭaiyavai. avai cempālum veṇkalat tālum ceyyappaṭṭavai. ivaiyaṉṟit tantattāl iyaṉṟa taṭikaḷ cila kiṭaittaṉa. ivai 20 ce. mī. nīḷam uṭaiyavai; naṉku vaḻavaḻappākkappaṭṭu irupuṟamum kaṟuppu niṟam pūcappaṭṭavai. ivaṟṟuḷ ciṟiyavaiyum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai uṭaikaḷai māṭṭi vaikkac cuvarkaḷil cerukap payaṉpaṭṭavai (Coal Stand) ākalām. paḷiṅkuk kal, cuṇṇāmpukkal, kaṟutta paccaik kal pōṉṟa kaṭiṉa poruḷkaḷaik koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa katāyutam pōṉṟavai kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. koṇṭai ūcikaḷ, pottāṉkaḷ, mukam pārkkum kaṇṇāṭikaḷ, kaṇṇukku mai tīṭṭum ūcikaḷ, patumaikaḷ, maṇikaḷ, pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉavum cempālum veṇkalattālum ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa koṇṭai ūcikaḷ mūṉṟu kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. pottāṉkaḷ elumpukaḷālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalm, uyartarakkal mutaliyavaṟṟāl aṇikalaṉkaḷ ceyyappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam, kārīyam, kal, mīṇ mutaliyavaṟṟāl pāttiraṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē, cintup piratēca makkaḷ inta ulōkaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu: tam vīṭṭiṟku vēṇṭiya pāttiraṅkaḷaiyum, tamakku vēṇṭiya aṇikalaṉ kaḷaiyum, karuvikaḷaiyum ceytu nākarika vāḻkkai naṭatti vantaṉar eṉpatu teḷivātal kāṇka.

cāṇaik kal

moheñco-tarō makkaḷ kattikaḷaiyum piṟakaruvi kaḷaiyum tīṭṭik kūrmai ākkuvataṟkuṅ potuvākac ceṅkaṟkaḷaiyē cāṇaik kaṟkaḷākap peritum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; ciṟappākak karuṅkal, vaḻavaḻappuḷḷa kal mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum cāṇaik kaṟkaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. vēṟu cila kaṟkaḷ ulōkaṅkaḷai merukiṭutaṟkeṉṟē payaṉpaṭṭaṉa.

eṇ iṭappaṭṭa karuvikaḷ

innakarattiṟ kiṭaitta cempuk karuvikaḷiṉ kuviyal oṉṟil irunta pala karuvikaḷ cittirak kuṟikaḷ patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ittakaiyak kuṟiyuḷḷavai harappāvilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. “ivai aracāṅkattārkku uriyavai yākalām. ataṉāṟṟāṉ. kaḷavu pōkātirukka eṇ iṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam karuvikaḷ mītu eṇ iṭum paḻakkam paṇṭai ekiptilum kaiyāḷappaṭṭatu”, eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar.





* * *





↑ Electrum.

↑ ic col āṅkilattil ‘oman’ eṉappaṭum.

↑ Dr.Mackay’s, ‘The Indus Civilization’, p.122

↑ Patrick Carleton’s ‘Buried Empries’, p.155

↑ K.N.Dikshit’s ‘pre-historic Civilization ofthe Indus Valley’, p.54.

↑ His ‘The Indus Civilization’, p. 180





* * *





﻿9. vilaṅkukaḷum paṟavaikaḷum

maṉitaṉukku muṉ tōṉṟiya vilaṅkukaḷ

ulakam tōṉṟiyapōtu maṉitaṉukku muṉ tōṉṟiyavai vilaṅkukaḷē eṉpatu uyirnūṟ pulavar muṭipu. mutalil tōṉṟiya maṉitaṉ ivvilaṅkukaṭkup payantē malaik kukaikaḷil vāḻntu vantāṉ; avaṟṟaik kolla villaiyum kaṟkaruvikaḷaiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉāṉ; tāṉ vacatiyōṭu kuṭicaikaḷaik kaṭṭi vāḻa avvilaṅkukaḷai oḻikka vēṇṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ. avpoḻutu avaṉ vēṭṭaiyāṭalai mēṟkoṇṭāṉ; koṭumaiyaṟṟa vilaṅkukaḷai uṇavākak koṇṭāṉ; iṅṅaṉam maṉitaṉ vilaṅkukaḷai vēṭṭaiyāṭiyatāṟṟāṉ ‘vēṭṭuvaṉ, vēṭaṉ’ eṉṉum peyarkaḷaip peṟṟāṉ. avaṉē maṉita nākarika varalāṟṟiṉ mutaṟ pakutikku uriyavaṉ.

kālattiṟkēṟṟa māṟupāṭu

ivvāṟu mutal maṉitaṉ kaṇṭu añciyavilaṅkukaḷ pala iṉṟu illai. avai pala lakṣakkaṇakkāṉa āyirakkaṇakkāṉa yāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē iṟantu viṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ elumpuk kūṭukaḷ maṇṇuḷ maṟaintiruntaṉa. uyirnūl aṟiñar avaṟṟaic cōtittup pala ariya ceytikaḷai veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar. cintup piratēca nākarika kālattil irunta vilaṅkukaḷ, cila iṉṟu illai. ataṟku muṉ irunta vilaṅkukaḷ, cila cintup piratēca kālattil illai. kuḻantaikaḷaiyum maṉitaraiyum tūkkic cellum peruṅ kaḻukukaḷ ippoḻutu illai allavā? mikap paḻaiya kālattil avai iruntaṉa eṉṟu uyir nūṟ pulavar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avai iruntaṉa eṉpatai irāmāyaṇam - pāratam, piruhat katai pōṉṟa perunūlkaḷil uḷḷa ceytikaḷum meyppikkiṉṟaṉa. ivai niṟka, cintup piratēca vilaṅkukaḷaik kāṇpōm.

cintup piratēca vilaṅkukaḷ

ārāycciyiṟ kiṭaitta cila vilaṅkukaḷiṉ elumpuk kūṭukaḷai aṟiñar ārāyntuḷḷaṉar; viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷuḷ, pala vilaṅkuppatumaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vilaṅkukaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa muttiraikaḷ cila kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. im mūṉṟaikkoṇṭum cintup piratēcavilaṅkukaḷ iṉṉavaitām eṉpatai ārāycciyāḷar muṭivu ceytuḷḷaṉar. avaṟṟai oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāka iṅkuk kāṇpōm:

yāṉai

cintup piratēcattiṉ mēṟkellai kīrtar, intukuṣ, culaimāṉ mutaliya malait toṭarkaḷum avaṟṟaic cērnta kāṭukaḷumē ātaliṉ, kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷākiya yāṉai, puli, karaṭi, kāṇṭāmirukam, kāṭṭu erumai mutaliyavaṟṟaic cintup piratēca makkaḷ aṟintiruntaṉarātal vēṇṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ yāṉai mutaṉmai peṟṟatākum. vilaṅkiṉattil iṇaiyaṟṟa paruvuṭal peṟṟuḷḷa ivvilaṅku, ataṉ tantam paṟṟip peru matippup peṟṟiruntatu. tantattāl iyaṉṟa cila poruḷkaḷ moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaittuḷḷamaiyāl, cintup piratēca makkaḷ yāṉaikaḷai vaḷarkkavum vēṭṭaiyāṭavum aṟintiruntaṉar eṉṉal tavaṟākātu.

erutukaḷ

erutu mikap paḻaiya kālam mutalē payirt toḻiṟkup payaṉpaṭṭa vilaṅkākum. ēlam, cumēr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum ivvilaṅku uḻutoḻiṟkup payaṉpaṭṭatu eṉpatai avvan nāṭṭu muttiraikaḷ vāyilāka uṇaralām. itu vaṇṭiyiḻukkavum payaṉpaṭṭatu. itaṉ pērutavi kaṇṭē cintup piratēca makkaḷ itaṉait teyvattaṉmai vāyntatāka niṉaittaṉar. civapirāṉ yōkattil iruppatupōlavum, avaraic cuṟṟilum cila vilaṅkukaḷ niṟṟal pōlavum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa muttiraiyil erutum oṉṟāka uḷḷatu. civapirāṉ pacu (vilaṅkukaṭku pati (talaivaṉ) eṉappaṭuvataiyē ammuttirai kuṟittatātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu. appaḻaṅkāla mutalē erutu ciṟappuṭai vilaṅkākak karutappaṭṭamaiyāṟṟāṉ pōlum, atu civaṉārkkuriya ūrtiyākap piṟṟai nāḷil karutappaṭṭatu; ‘nanti’ eṉṉum peyar iṭappaṭṭatu.

vēṟōr iṉattaic cērnta erutum[1] cintup piratēcattil iruntatākat terikiṟatu. āṉāl, aḵtu iṉṟaiya airōppiya erutukaṭkut tantai eṉṟu aṟiñar tīkṣat karutukiṉṟār; ‘ivverutu uruvaṅkaḷ ērāḷamākac cumēriyar muttiraikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.ivaṟṟaic cintup piratēca makkaḷ naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar; ivai intiyāvilum iruntaṉa’, eṉṟu avar kūṟukiṉṟār.

nāykaḷ

vīṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷuḷ nāyum oṉṟu aṉṟō? nāy illāta ūr ētu? nakaram ētu? ceṅkallaik kāyavaittirunta pōtu ataṉmītunāykaḷ naṭantuceṉṟamaiyāl uṇṭāṉaaṭiccuvaṭukaḷ appaṭiyē irukka, akkaṟkaḷ cūḷaiyiṭappaṭṭu, vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭap payaṉpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. akkaṟkaḷil iṉṟum kāṇappaṭum aṭiccuvaṭu kaḷaik koṇṭum patumaikaḷaik koṇṭum aṟiñar, moheñco-tarōvilnāykaḷ iruntiruttalvēṇṭum eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. cātāraṇa nāykaḷ aṉṟi vēṭṭai nāykaḷum, ikkālattup ‘pul-ṭāk’ (Bull-dog) pōṉṟa nāykaḷum aṅku iruntaṉavām. ōriṉa nāykaḷ kuṭṭai kālkaḷuṭaṉum curuṇṭa vāluṭaṉum iruntaṉa. maṟṟōr iṉa nāykaḷ nīṇṭa akaṉṟa kātukaḷuṭaṉum nīṇṭa vāluṭaṉum iruntaṉa. cila nāykaḷ kaḻuttil paṭṭaikaḷ kaṭṭappeṟṟu iruntaṉa. “iṟutiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa nāykaḷ, intiya ōnāy iṉattiliruntu tōṉṟiyavai; cātāraṇa nāykaḷ intiya nari iṉattiliruntu tōṉṟiyavai’, eṉṟu aṟiñar payṉi piracāt aṟaikiṉṟaṉar. ittakaiya nāykaḷiṉa elumpukaḷ harappāvilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. intiya nāykaḷ marakkalaṅkaḷ mūlam pāpilōṉiyāvukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatiliruntu, nāykaḷai vaḷarkkum vaḻakkam, paṇṭaikkāla mutalē intiyāvil iruntateṉpatu teḷivām.

pūṉaikaḷ

cintup piratēcattil nāykaḷaip pōlap pūṉaikaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatu kūṟakkūṭavillai. eṉṉai? cila elumpukaḷē pūṉaikaḷaik kuṟippaṉavākak kiṭaittiruttalāl eṉka. mēlum, pūṉaikaḷ apicīṉiyāviṟṟām tōṟṟam eṭuttavai. ātaliṉ, appaṇṭaik kālattil intiyāvil mikac cilavē koṇṭuvarappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. piṉṉar nāḷaṭaivil pūṉaikaḷ intiyāviṟ perukiviṭṭaṉa.[2]

paṉṟikaḷ

paṉṟikaḷiṉ elumpukaḷ moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ivai akkālattil mikkiruntaṉa. ātaliṉ, ammakkaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ iṟaicciyai uṇṭu vantaṉar. ivai aṉṟik kāṭṭup paṉṟikaḷum uṇavukkup payaṉpaṭṭaṉa.

āṭukaḷ

malaiyāṭukaḷ, veḷḷāṭukaḷ eṉpavaṟṟaik kuṟikkum patumaikaḷ palavākum. veḷḷāṭukaḷ cintup piratēcattil mikkiruntaṉa. kāṣmīrap pakutikaḷil iṉṟum malaiyāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟaippōṉṟa āṭukaḷiṉ elumpukkūṭukaḷē moheñco - tarōvilum harappāvilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avvāṭukaḷ cintup piratēcattiṉ mēṟkumalaip piratēcattuk kāṭukaḷil ērāḷamāka iruntaṉavātal vēṇṭum. malaiyāṭukaḷaip pōṉṟē oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ cintup piratēcam mutal cumēriyā varaiyuḷḷa ciṟappāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, aḷvaraiyāṭukaḷiṉ iṟaicci cintup piratēca makkaḷiṉ uṇavup poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟākac ciṟantiruntatu eṉpatil aiyamillai.

kaḻutaikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kaḻutaikaḷ iruntaṉavākat teriyavillai; āṉāl, harappāvil iruntaṉa. eṉṉai? aṅkuk kaḻutaikaḷiṉ elumpukkūṭukaḷ kiṭaittaṉa ātaliṉ eṉka. āyiṉum, cintup piratēcattuk kaḻutaikaḷ payaṉpaṭṭa vitam uṇarumāṟillai. eṉṉai? poti cumakkavum vaṇṭi iḻukkavum erutukaḷē peritum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. ātaliṉ eṉka. cumēriyāvilum iṅṅaṉamē erutukaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa.

māṉkaḷ

palavakai māṉkaḷiṉ kompukaḷum, elumpukaḷum cintup piratēcattiṟ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. aṅkup puḷḷimāṉkaḷum kalaimāṉkaḷumē mikkiruntaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ kompukaṭkum iṟaiccikkumē cintup piratēcattavar ivaṟṟai vaḷarttu vantaṉar. vēṭṭaiyāṭi vantaṉar. māṉ kompum kuḷampum maruntukkum payaṉpaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.

erumaikaḷ

maṇṇālum cempālum iyaṉṟa erumaip patumaikaḷ cila kiṭaittuḷḷamaiyāl, cintup piratēcattil erumaikaḷ iruntaṉa eṉak kūṟalām. āyiṉ, cintup piratēcat taṭpa veppa nilaikaḷ kāraṇamāka, aṅku avai nīṇṭanāḷ uyirvāḻak kūṭavillai eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟivikkiṉṟaṉar.

oṭṭakam

mikac cila oṭṭaka elumpukkūṭukaḷē cintup piratēcattiṟ kiṭaittaṉa. eṉavē, avai aṅkuppalavāka iruntila eṉak kūṟal mikaiyākātu. mēlum ivai arēyiyāviliruntē ap paḻaṅkālattil koṇṭu varappaṭṭaṉa eṉak kūṟutal poruttamāṉatu.

muyalkaḷ

muyal mikap paḻaiyakāla vilaṅku eṉpatu uyir nūṟ pulavar muṭipu. muyalkaḷiṉ elumpukaḷ pala cintup piratēcattiṟ kiṭaittaṉa ātaliṉ, muyal uṇavu ciṟanta uṇavākak karutappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.

āmai mutaliyaṉa

āmai oṭukaḷ mikutiyākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. āmai iṟaicci uṇavākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. āmai oṭukaḷ vēṟu kāriyaṅkaṭkum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. aṅku mutalai iṟaicciyum payaṉpaṭṭatu. aṅkuk kīri, kuraṅku, aṇil, eli ivai mikkiruntaṉa eṉpatu viḷaiyāṭṭup patumaikaḷ mūlam uṇarakkiṭakkiṟatu. elippoṟikaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl, aṅku elikaḷ tuṉpam poṟukka muṭiyātapaṭi, iruntatu pulaṉākiṟatu.

paṟavaikaḷ

ivaṟṟuḷ moheñco-tarō makkaḷ peru viruppamuṟṟu vaḷarttavai kiḷikaḷē ākum. eṉṉai? kiḷikaḷ pōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷa patumaikaḷ aṅkup palavākak kiṭaittuḷḷamaiyāl eṉka. pala patumaikaḷum ūtu kuḻalkaḷum kērḻi vaṭivattil ceyyappaṭṭu uḷḷamaiyāl kōḻikaḷum palavāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷiyalām. kōḻic caṇṭai[3] akkālattil iruntatu eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē caṇṭaikku eṉṟē kōḻikaḷ tayārākkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatum aṟiyattakka ceytiyē ākum, harappāvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa tāḻikaḷ mītu mayilē ciṟappuṟat tiṭṭappaṭṭiruttalāl, paṇṭai makkaḷ mayilai naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatu teḷivām. puṟākkaḷiṉ patumaikaḷaik koṇṭu cintup piratēcattil puṟākkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatai aṟiyalām. ippatumaikaḷ mecopoṭṭēmiyāvil kiṭaitta paṟavaip patumaikaḷai ottuḷḷaṉa. oru peṇ patumaiyiṉ talai mītu iraṇṭu puṟākkaḷ iruppatupōlac ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai aṉṟi maiṉākkaḷ, paruntukaḷ pōṉṟavaiyum aṅku iruntaṉa eṉṉalām.

nākaṅkaḷ

harappāvil pāmpup patumaikaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa; cila muttiraikaḷ pāmpu paṭam virittāṟ pōlac cetukkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītum pāmpu uruvam tīṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. akkāla makkaḷ pāmpai vaṇaṅki vantaṉar; pāmpiṟkup pāl vārttaṉar eṉṟu eṇṇutaṟkuriya kuṟikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.



↑ Bostauras (parent of the modern European cattle) K. N. Dikshit in his ‘Pre - historic Civilization of the Indus Valley’, p.39.

↑ Pre-historic Civilization of the Indus Valley, p.41.

↑ kōḻiccaṇṭai mikap paḻaiya kālamutalē tamiḻakattil uṇṭu eṉpataip puṟapporuḷ nūlkaḷāl uṇarka.





* * *





﻿10. uṇavum uṭaiyum

viḷaiporuḷkaḷ

cintu āṟṟuveḷi paruva maḻaiyum āṟṟup pāyccalum ciṟappuṟap peṟṟu viḷaṅkiyatākum. ātaliṉ, aṅku irunta nilaṅkaḷ ceḻippuṟṟu iruntaṉa; vaṇṭal maṇ paṭintiruntaṉa: ciṟanta marutanilaṅkaḷāka viḷaṅkiṉa. ātaliṉ, aṅkuk kōtumai, nel, vāṟ kōtumai (pārli), parutti, paṭṭāṇi, eḷ, pērintu, mulāmpaḻam mutaliyaṉa ērāḷamākap payirākkappaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟum appakutikaḷil nellum kōtumaiyum ciṟappuṭai viḷai poruḷkaḷāka iruttal kavaṉikkat takkatu. ippalavakai viḷaiporuḷkaḷai appaṇṭai makkaḷ tāḻikaḷiṟ cēmittu vaittiruntaṉar.attāḻikaḷ nilattil pātiyaḷavu putaikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa.

maruta nilamum nakar vaḷamum

iṅṅaṉam maruta nilappaṇpu mikutippaṭṭa cintuveḷiyil, nakaraṅkaḷ ceḻippuṟṟu iruntaṉa eṉpatil viyappoṉṟum illai aṉṟō? āṟṟuppāyccal peṟṟa maruta nilamē, makkaḷ nākarika vāḻkkai naṭatta ēṟṟateṉat tamiḻnūlkaḷum cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa. aṅkuttāṉ aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷum kōṭṭai kottaḷaṅkaḷum amaikkappaṭutal peruvaḻakku āriyar, cintu-kaṅkaic camaveḷikaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa anāriyar nakaraṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟu rik vētattil kūṟiyiruttalaiyum cintu veḷiyil cumār 3200 ki. mī. tolaivu cuṟṟip pala paṇṭai nakaraṅkaḷ maṇ mūṭu paṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa; avaṟṟait tōṇṭi ārāycci naṭttutal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar pāṉarjī tam aṟikkaiyiṟ kūṟiyiruttalaiyum nōkka, cintup piratēcattiṉ vaḷamum avvaḷappaṅ kāraṇamākap pala nakaraṅkaḷ aṅku amaikkappaṭṭamaiyum naṉku viḷaṅkum.

pulāl uṇṭa makkaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil uḷḷa terukkaḷilum illaṅkaḷiliruntu veḷippaṭum kālvāykaḷilum vaṭikāl kaḷilum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum elumput tuṇṭukaḷ pala citaṟik kiṭantaṉa. avaṟṟaip pārvaiyiṭṭa ārāycciyāḷar, ‘potuvāka innakara makkaḷ pulāl uṇṇum vaḻakkattiṉar eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. ikkālattil cōṟṟilēyē iṟaicciyaik kalantu tayārikkum ‘pulavu’ (pulāl)[1] eṉṉum oruvakai uṇavu akkālattilum camaikkappaṭṭu vantatu. ammakkaḷ cōṟṟil āṭu māṭu, paṉṟi, āmai, mutalai ivaṟṟiṉ iṟaiccit tuṇṭaṅkaḷaik kalantu ‘pulavu’ ceytaṉar; palavikai mīṉ iṉaṅkaḷai uṇṭu vantaṉar; palatiṟappaṭṭa paṟavaikaḷai uṇṭu vantaṉar; piṟanāṭukaḷiliruntu kalaṉkaḷ mūlam koṇṭuvarap paṭṭa ularnta mīṉ iṉaṅkaḷum[2] iṟaicci vakaikaḷum uṇṭaṉar; iṟaiccit tuṇṭaṅkaḷai ularttip pakkuvappaṭuttip periya tāḻikaḷil aṭaittu avvappōtu payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. ‘ittāḻikaḷil veḷḷāṭu, erutu, cemmaṟiyāṭu ivaṟṟiṉ elumpukaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, ivvilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṟaiccikaḷ patappaṭutti aṭaittu vaikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu uṇmai. āṉāl, ippatappaṭuttappaṭṭa iṟaiccittuṇṭaṅkaḷ tiṉantōṟum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟō, vicēṭa kālaṅkaḷiṟṟām payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟō ippōtu tiṭṭamākak kūṟutal iyalātu’.[3]

piṟa uṇavup poruḷkaḷ

appaṇṭai makkaḷ kāykaṟikaḷaiyum pāl, veṇṇey, tayir, mōr, ney mutaliyavaṟṟaiyum uṇavupporuḷkaḷākak koṇṭiruntaṉar; palavakaip paḻaṅkaḷaiyum uṇṭu vantaṉar.

camaiyaṟ poruḷkaḷ

avarkaḷ māvaraittup palavakai uṇavup poruḷkaḷ ceyyak kaṟṟiruntaṉar. vāṇal caṭṭi, āṭṭukkal, iṭṭali ūṟṟum caṭṭi ivai kiṭaittuḷḷamaiyē immuṭipukkuriya cāṉṟākum. kuḻampu kūṭṭuvataṟkuriya vēṟu kaṟikaḷ ceyvataṟkuriya macālaip poruḷkaḷai iṭittu, avaṟṟaip pala aṟai (macālai)p peṭṭiyil vaittiruntaṉar. aṅkuk kiṭaitta ammi - kuḻavi, kal ural ivaṟṟāl macālaip poruḷkaḷ araikkap paṭṭaṉa eṉpataiyum, iṭikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpataiyum naṉkuṇaralām.

uṇavu koṇṭa muṟai

akkālattavaruḷ palar pāykaḷmītu amarntu uṇavup poruḷkaḷai etiril - vaittukkoṇṭu uṇṭaṉar. paṇakkārar nāṟkālikaḷil amarntu uṇṭaṉar eṉṟu muttiraikaḷaik koṇṭu kūṟalām. eṉiṉum, itu orōvaḻik koṇṭirunta vaḻakkamākak kōṭalē poruntuvatākum. eṉṉai? iṉṉum perumpālāṉa intiyar vīṭukaḷil nāṟkāli - mējaikaḷai uṇpataṟkup payaṉpaṭuttāmaiyiṉ eṉka. maram, cippi, caṅku, kaḷimaṇ, cempu, veṇkalam ivaṟṟālāya karaṇṭikaḷ, kaṟikaḷaiyum kuḻampu, vakaikaḷaiyum cōṟṟaiyum eṭukkap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. uṇṇaṟkutavum karaṇṭikaḷō muṭkaḷō kiṭaikkāmaiyiṉ, avarkaḷ kaikaḷālēyē uṇavu picaintu uṇṭaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu.

uṭaikaḷ

appaḻaiya makkaḷ ettakaiya uṭaikaḷai evvevvāṟu uṭuttiruntaṉar eṉpatai iṉṟu uṟutippaṭuttik kūṟutaṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaittila. eṉiṉum, avarkaḷ viṭṭuppōṉa oviyaṅkaḷ, patumaikaḷ, muttiraikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kāṇappaṭum vivaraṅkaḷaik koṇṭu, avarkaḷ uṭaikaḷai uṭuttivanta muṟaikaḷaiyum uṭai vicēṭaṅkaḷaiyum oruvāṟu uṇartal kūṭum. cintu veḷiyil parutti mikutiyākap payirāṉatālum, necavukkuriya karuvikaḷ aṅkuk kiṭaittamaiyālum ammakkaḷ nūl nūṟṟu āṭaikaḷai neytu uṭuttu vantaṉar eṉpatu aiyamaṟat terikiṟatu. eḷiyavar cātāraṇap parutti āṭaikaḷai aṇintiruntaṉar. celvar pū vēlaippāṭu poruntiya parutti āṭaikaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar; cilar kittāṉ pōṉṟa muraṭṭu āṭaikaḷai uṭuttiṉar; cilar nārāl āṉa āṭaikaḷaiyum uṭuttiruntaṉar[4] moheñco-tarōvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa vaṭivam oṉṟil pū vēlaippāṭu amainta pōrvai kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. atu ciṟanta vēlaippāṭu uṭaiyatu. attakaiya pōrvaikaḷaic celvarē payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar eṉal takum. avarkaḷ tam iṭakkaiyum mārpum maṟaiyumāṟu pōrvai pōrttiṉarātal vēṇṭum. āyiṉ, eḷiya makkaḷ iṭai āṭaiyuṭaṉē tirupti aṭaintiruntaṉarō - eḷiya vilaiyiṟ kiṭaitta pōrvaikaḷai aṇintiruttaṉarō-teriyavillai. moheñco-tarōvil palavakaip pottāṉkaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. ātaliṉ, annakara makkaḷ caṭṭaikaḷai aṇintiruntaṉar eṉpatu veḷḷiṭaimalai. kaḻuttup paṭṭaikaḷum vaḻakkil iruntaṉa.

aṇaṅkukaḷiṉ āṭaic ciṟappu

paṇṭaic cintuveḷip peṇmaṇikaḷ pāvāṭaikaḷaip peritum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatai muttiraikaḷaikkoṇṭu kūṟalām. avai muṉpuṟam oraḷavu kuṭṭaiyākavum piṉpuṟam nīṇṭum iruntirukkalām; nāṭākkaḷaik koṇṭaṉavāka irukkalām. pāvāṭaikaḷai iṟukka, maṇikaḷ cērttuc ceyyap paṭṭa (oṭṭiyāṇam pōṉṟa) araikkaccaikaḷai ammakaḷir payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. akkaccaikaḷuṭ cila muṉpuṟam mukappu vaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. celvar vīṭṭu makaḷir melliya mēlāṭaikaḷai aṇintu vantaṉar. eḷiya makaḷir mēlāṭaikaḷaippaṟṟi oṉṟum kūṟakkūṭavillai. moheñco-tarōvil iṉṉum tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭāta pakuti pattil oṉpatu paṅku. ātaliṉ, iṉṟuḷḷa nilaimaiyil etaṉaiyum uṟutiyākak kūṟak kūṭavillai eṉpatu iṅku niṉaivil iruttat takkatu.

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaitta kaḷimaṇ paṭivaṅkaḷuḷ āṇ uruvaṅkaḷ perumpālāṉa āṭai iṉṟiyē kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. peṇ uruvaṅkaḷ aṉaittum āṭaiyuṭaṉē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, ivaṟṟaik koṇṭu, ‘moheñco-tarō āṭavar paṇṭai ekiptiyaruṭ perumpālaraip pōl āṭaiyiṉṟiyē iruntaṉar’ eṉak kūṟutal iyalātu eṉṟu aṟiñar tīkṣat aṟivikkiṉṟār.[5] im muṭiyē poruttamāṉatu.

muṇṭācu kaṭṭiya makaḷir

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta peṇ vaṭivaṅkaḷuṭpala, talaiyil oruvakai muṇṭācuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila āṇ paṭivaṅkaḷilum ittakaiya muṇṭācukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam talaiyil muṇṭācu koṇṭa paṭivaṅkaḷ, āciyā maiṉaril uḷḷa aṭaliyā (Adalia) eṉṉum iṭattilum, ṭel-asmar eṉ ṉum iṭattilum, ciriyāvilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. it talai muṇṭācukaḷ paruttiyāl āṉavai; nimirntu viciṟipōla viraippuṭaṉ irukkumāṟu ōraṅkaḷil paṭṭai vaittut taikkappaṭṭavai; kaṉamāṉavai. ittakaiya muṇṭācukaḷai maṅkōliya makaḷir iṉṟum talaiyil aṇikiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu naṉku kavaṉittat takkatu.[6]

kāl caṭṭaiyō?

teyvaṅkaḷ eṉak karutappaṭum āṇ uruvaṅkaḷ cila, muḻaṅkāl aḷavuvarai tuṇṭukaḷai uṭuttiyuḷḷaṉa pōlat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉ oru paṭivam maṭṭumē muḻaṅkālukkuk kīḻē toṅkumāṟu vēṭṭikaṭṭiyuḷḷatupōlat tōṟṟukiṟatu. uṭkārntirukkum maṟṟōr āṇ uruvam nīṇṭa vēṭṭiyuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. harappāvil kiṭaitta maṭpāṇṭattiṉ mītuḷḷa oviyam oṉṟil kāṇappaṭum maṉita uruvam kāl caṭṭai aṇintiruppatu pōlat tōṟṟukiṟatu. āyiṉ, atu kālcaṭṭai aṉṟu nīṇṭavēṭṭioṉṟu kāṟṟil kālaiccuṟṟik koṇṭapōtu kāṇappaṭum nilaiyiṉaiyē atu kuṟippa tākum’, eṉṟu cilar ceppukiṉṟaṉar. uṇmai uṇarak kūṭavillai.

kūtta makaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaitta paṭivaṅkaḷuḷ veṇkalattai urukki vārttuc ceyyappaṭṭa kūtta makaḷ cilaiyē mikac ciṟantatākum. ivvoṉṟutāṉ āṭaiyē iṉṟik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaippōlavē harappāvilum veṇkalap paṭivam oṉṟu kiṭaittuḷḷatu. appeṇ paṭivaṅkaḷ nīkrō iṉappeṇṇaik kuṟippaṉavākavē tōṟṟu kiṉṟaṉavām. appatumaikaḷ iraṇṭum naṭaṉac ceyalaik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. moheñco- tarōviṟ kiṭaitta cilaiyiṉ iṭakkai niṟaiya vaḷaiyalkaḷ uḷḷaṉa; kūntal caṭaiyākap piṉṉit talaiyiṉ piṉpuṟam otukkik kaṭṭappaṭṭu valappuṟat tōḷ mītu paṭintuḷḷatu. valakkai iṭuppiṉmītu irukkiṟatu. paṇṭai ekiptil naṭaṉamātar āṭaiyiṉḷic cila vēḷaikaḷil naṭippatu vaḻakkamām.[7] appaḻakkam cintu nāṭṭilum iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.





* * *





↑ paṇṭait tamiḻ makkaḷuṭ cilar tirumaṇa viruntukaḷilum ‘pulavu’ ceytu uṇṭu vantaṉar. eṉpatu akanāṉūṟṟu 186ām ceyyuḷāl aṟika.

↑ paṇṭait tamiḻar ēṟṟumatip poruḷkaḷil uppuppaṭuttiya mīṉum oṉṟākum eṉpatu iṅku aṟiyattakkatu.

↑ Dr.E.Mackay’s ‘The Indus Civilization’, p.185.

↑ nārāṭai maravuri pōṉṟatu. iḵtu appaḻaṅkālattil upayōkikkappaṭṭatai aṟivippataṟkē pōlum, iṉṟaiya makkaḷ nārppaṭṭai vicēṭa kālaṅkaḷil aṇikiṉṟaṉar. PTS. Iyengar’s ‘The Stone Age in India’, p.38,

↑ ‘Pre-historic Civilization of the Indus Valley’, p.26.

↑ Dr.E. Mackay’s ‘The Indus Civilization’, p. 135.

↑ paṇṭait tamiḻakattil viṟaliyar (oruvakai naṭaṉa mātar) vicēṭa kālaṅkaḷil āṭaiyiṉṟit taḻaiyai araiyiṟ kaṭṭikkoṇṭu āṭutal marapu - naṟṟiṇai, 170.





* * *





﻿11. aṇikalaṉkaḷ

aṇikalaṉkaḷ

cintup piratēca makkaḷiṉ uṭai vakaikaḷai oviyaṅkaḷ, ciṟpaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaik koṇṭē aṟiya vēṇṭi irukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, avarkaḷ makiḻantēṟṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷ nirampak kiṭaittuḷḷamaiyāl, avaṟṟaip paṟṟit teḷivāṉa uṇmaikaḷai uṇara iṭam ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷatu. ammakkaḷ poṉ, veḷḷi[1], veṇpoṉ[2], cempu, veṇkalaṉ, tantam, elumpu, mākkal, palavakai irattiṉakkaṟkaḷ, kaḷimaṇ, caṅku mutaliyavaṟṟaik koṇṭu tattam takutikkēṟṟa aṇikaḷaivitam vitamākac ceytu aṇintu vantaṉar. eḷiya makkaḷ kaṟuppu-civappuk kaḷimaṇṇāl nakaikaḷ ceytu cūḷaiyiṭṭu, aṇintu vantaṉar. cilar oḷikkaṟkaḷāl nakaikaḷ ceytu merukiṭṭu, avaṟṟil, vita vitamāṉa niṟaṅkaḷait tīṭṭi oḷi vīcac ceytu aṇintuvantaṉar. cempu, calavaikkal, oruvakaik karuṅkal, caṅku elumpu, kaḷimaṇ ivaṟṟāl āṉa vaḷaiyalkaḷ mikap pala kiṭait tuḷḷaṉa. avai ikkāla vaṭuka makaḷir kālkaḷil aṇiyum veḷḷik kāppukaḷai ottuḷḷaṉa. maṇ, elumpu, caṅku, civappukkal ivaṟṟāl āṉa maṇikaḷ pala kōkkappaṭṭu mālaikaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. ammaṇikaḷ puṉṉaikkāy aḷaviliruntu cuṇṭaikkāy aḷavu varai ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cila taṭṭaiyākavum, cila vaṭṭamākavum, cila nīḷamākavum uḷḷaṉa. ellā maṇikaḷum iyaṉṟavaraiyiṟ paḷapaḷappākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

putaikkappaṭṭa nakaikaḷ

veḷḷi, cempu mutaliya kalaṉkaḷil innakaikaḷai vaittu, nilattiṉ aṭiyilum kavarkaḷilum appaṇṭai makkaḷ oḷittu vaittiruntaṉar. itaṉāl, ammakkaḷ taṅkaḷ nakarattiṟku uṇṭāka irunta aḻivai eṇṇi iṅkiṉam aṇikaḷai maṟaittu vaittaṉar eṉpatum, accam nīṅkiya piṉṉar vantu avaṟṟai eṭuttuk koḷḷalām eṉa eṇṇiṉar eṉpatum, veḷiyām. āṉāl, avarkaḷ eṇṇiyapaṭi tirumpivarak kūṭavillai pōlum!

putaiyuṇṭa veḷḷik kalaṉkaḷ

oru vīṭṭiṉ aṭiyil tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭa aṇikaḷuḷ veḷḷip pāttiram eṉṟu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. ataṉuḷ kaḻuttu mālaikaḷ palavum, kaṭiṉamāṉa uyartara maṇikaḷ palavum iruntaṉa. avveḷḷik kalaṉ putaiyuṇṭirunta iṭattil vēṟupala aṇikaḷ maṇṇuḷ putaiyuṇṭu kiṭantaṉa. avaṟṟai eṭutta irāypakatur tayārām cahṉi eṉpavar, inta veḷḷik kalaṉum itaic cuṟṟip pūmiyiṟ putaiyuṇṭu kiṭanta aṇikaḷum oru paruttit tuṇiyil kaṭṭappaṭṭup putaikkap paṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. attuṇi aḻinta piṉṉar ivai iṅṅaṉam maṇṇuḷ putaiyuṇṭaṉa eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.

piṟitōr vīṭṭiṉ aṭiyil irāypahatūr tīkṣajit eṉpār veḷḷik kalaṉ oṉṟaik kaṇṭeṭuttār. ataṉuḷ kaḻuttu mālaikaḷum paḷapaḷappuḷḷa kalmaṇikaḷum ulōkattuṇṭukaḷum poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatalai nāṭākkaḷum iruntaṉa.

putaiyuṇṭa cempuk kalaṉ

vēṟōr iṭattil cempk kalaṉ oṉṟu putaiyuṇṭu kiṭantatu.ataṟkuḷ taṅkakkoṇṭaiūcikaḷ, periya veḷḷik kātaṇikaḷ, civappuk kal maṇikaḷ, piṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷ iruntaṉa. antac cempup pāttirattiṟku uriya muṭiyum akappaṭṭatu. ammūṭi kaippiṭi koṇṭatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.harappāvil ārāycci naṭattiya aṟiñar vāṭs eṉpār pala nakaikaḷaik kaṇṭeṭuttār. avaṟṟuṭ perumpālaṉa moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaittavaṟṟai ottiruntaṉa. cila eṅkumē kāṇappaṭāta vēlaippāṭu poruntiyavaiyāka uḷḷaṉa. aṅkuk kiṭaitta nakaikaḷum taraiyiṉ aṭiyil putaiyuṇṭu kiṭantaṉavē ākum.

kāṭcikkiṉiya kaḻuttumālai

veḷḷip pāttiram oṉṟil vaikkappaṭṭirunta nakaikaḷuḷ kaḻuttu mālai oṉṟē ciṟappittuk kūṟattakka vēlaippāṭu koṇṭatu. atu poṉṉālum paccaik kaṟkaḷālum ceyta maṇikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭu nīṇṭa aṇivaṭamākak kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu. taṅka maṇikaḷ taṭṭaiyākac ceyyappaṭṭu avaṟṟiṉmītu kumiḻkaḷ paṟṟavaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaṇṇāṭi pōṉṟa kaṭiṉamāṉa paccaik kaṟkaḷ pīppāy vaṭivil ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. oru poṉmaṇiyum aṭuttu oru paccai maṇiyumāka ammālai kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉ naṭuvil ‘kaṭuttakkal’ eṉṉum oruvakai vaiṭuriyattālum, cūriya kāntam’ eṉappaṭum iravaik kallālum ceyyappaṭṭa ēḻu patakkaṅkaḷ kōttut toṅkaviṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. atil uḷḷa paccaikkal vaṭa parmāvilum tipēttilum kiṭaittalāl, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiliruntē koṇṭuvarappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.[3]

maṟṟum iru kaḻuttu mālaikaḷ

vēṟoru kaḻuttu mālaiyil kuḻavi vaṭivattilum uruṇṭai vaṭivattilum ceyyappaṭṭa taṅka maṇikaḷum, nīlap paḷiṅkuk kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa maṇikaḷum cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. appaḷiṅku maṇikaḷuṭ cila iṉṟum niṟaṅkuṉṟāmal paḷapaḷappākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟoru mālaiyil, taṅkattāl taṭṭaiyākac ceyyappaṭṭu iraṇṭu maṇikaḷait cērttup paṟṟavaittuḷḷavai nīḷamākak kōkkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai pōṉṟa maṇikaḷ pāpilōṉiyā, ekiptu, ṭrāy mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum kiṭaittaṉa. ivveḻil mikka aṇivaṭaṅkaḷiṉ vēlaippāṭṭaik koṇṭu akkāla makkaḷiṉ ciṟanta aṟivai naṉkuṇaralām.

harappāvil kiṭaitta kaḻuttu mālaikaḷ

harappāvil kiṭaitta mālaikaḷ palavākum. avai aṉaittum viyattaku vēlaippāṭu koṇṭavai. 240 maṇikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭa nāṉku cāraṅkaḷ koṇṭa mālai, 27 taṅka maṇikaḷ paṟpala vaṭiviṟceytu kōkkappaṭṭamālai, palavakai vaṭiviṟ ceyyappaṭṭa 70 taṅkamaṇikaḷ koṇṭa mālai, palavakaik kalmaṇikaḷum poṇmaṇikaḷum kōkkappaṭṭa mālai eṉap palakai mālaikaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai iṭaiyiṭaiyē 7 patakkaṅkaḷ mutal 13 patakkaṅkaḷ varai kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. irattiṉak kaṟkaḷāl āṉa mūṉṟu mālaikaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, ittuṇai vitavitamāṉa mālaikaḷ moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaittila. kaḻuttai iṟukkiya muṟaiyil aṇiyappaṭum aṭṭikaiyum akkālattil iruntatu. āṭavarum kaḻuttumālaikaḷ aṇintiruntaṉar eṉpatu aṟiyattakkatu.[4]

aḻakoḻukum iṭaippaṭṭaikaḷ

piṟa nakaikaḷil iṭuppil aṇiyap payaṉpaṭum iṭaippaṭṭaikaḷē (oṭṭiyāṇaṅkaḷē) ciṟappāṉavai. ivai paṇpaṭṭa vēlaippāṭṭuṭaṉ kūṭiyūvai. ivaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭēkuṟippiṭattakkavai; ovvoṉṟum āṟu caraṅkaḷaik koṇṭatu. ovvoru carattilum pīppāy pōlac cetukkap paṭṭa civappukkal maṇikaḷ kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ovvoru maṇiyum 12 ce. mī. nīḷam irukkiṟatu. iccivappu maṇikaṭ kiṭaiyē veṇkalamaṇikaḷum kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai uruṇṭaiyāyum malar moṭṭukaḷ pōlak kumiḻākavum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaiyaṉṟi veṇkalac cataṅkaikaḷum muttukkaḷum kōtta i aip paṭ aikaḷum cila kiṭaittaṉa. ivai cennīla niṟattuṭaṉum kaṇkavar vaṉappuṭaṉum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭṭaikaḷiṉ muṭivil mukappukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avai veṇkalattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu, āṟu caraṅkaḷaik kōttaṟku ēṟṟavāṟu āṟu tuḷaikaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivviṭaip paṭṭaikaḷ kaḷimaṇ patumaikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum iṭaippaṭṭaikaḷaip peritum ottuḷḷaṉa. ovvoṉṟum 100 ce. mī. nīḷam irukkiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟil uḷḷa kaṭiṉamāṉa civappuk kaṟkaḷ naṉku merukiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai ‘kuruntak kal’ koṇṭu merukiṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar.

tuḷai iṭṭa pēraṟivu

merukiṭappaṭṭamaṇikaḷiṉ naṭuvil caraṭu nuḻaivataṟkākat tuḷaikaḷ iṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. attuḷaikaḷ mikka oḻuṅkuṭaṉ amaintuḷḷaṉa. maṇikaḷiṉ iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷum tuḷaiyiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ellāt tuḷaikaḷum orē aḷavil amain tiruttalē viyappukkuriyatu. iṅṅaṉam tiruttamāṉa muṟaiyil tuḷaiyiṭuvataṟkeṉṟu appēraṟiñar payaṉpaṭuttiya nuṭpamāṉa karuvi yāteṉpatu viḷaṅkavillai. aṅkuk kiṭaittuḷḷa cempālāṉa nuṭpamāṉa tuḷaiyiṭum karuvikaḷē ivvaṟputa vēlaikkup payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām. ivvaṟputa vēlaippāṭu avvaṟiñar tam nuṇṇaṟivai naṉku viḷakkuvatākum.

āṟu caraṅkoṇṭa aḻakiya kaiyaṇi

moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaitta kāppukaḷ, vaḷaiyalkaḷ mutaliya kaiyaṇikaḷuḷ ciṟappittuk kūṟattakuvaṉa cila uḷa. avaiyē uruṇṭaiyāṉa taṅkamaṇikaḷai āṟu caraṅkaḷākak kōttuc ceyyappaṭṭa kaiyaṇikaḷ ākum. uruṇṭai maṇikaṭku iṭaiyē taṭṭaiyāṉa taṅka maṇikaḷum kōkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. caraṅkaḷ muṭiyum iṭattil araivaṭṭa vaṭivil taṅkattakaṭṭāl āṉa mukappukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai pōṉṟavai harappāvilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.

uḷḷē arakkiṭṭa kāppukaḷ

kaiyaṇikaḷil palavakai irukkiṉṟaṉa. taṅkam, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam, paḷiṅku veṇkal, kaṟuppukkal, caṅku, cippi, kaḷimaṇ mutaliyavaṟṟāl kaiyaṇikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila kāppukaḷil uṭpuṟam arakkum arakkuppōṉṟa vēṟoru poruḷum urukki ūṟṟappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu terikiṟatu. harappāvil pala kāppukaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu muṟṟum cempiṉāl āṉatu oṉṟu uḷḷē arakkiṭattakkatāka veḷḷiyāl ceyyappaṭṭatu. harappāvil taṅkak kaṭakaṅkaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.

palavakaippaṭṭa vaḷaiyalkaḷ

cempālum veṇkalattālum ceyyappaṭṭa kaiyaṇikaḷ, ippōtu ceyyappaṭum taṅka vaḷaiyalkaḷ pōlak kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ cila uruṇṭaiyākavum cila taṭṭaiyākavum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila vaḷaiyalkaḷil iraṇṭu muṉaikaḷum poruntiyuḷḷaṉa; cilavaṟṟuḷ vāy virintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cippiyāl ceyyappaṭṭa vaḷaiyalkaḷ akalamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. cippit tuṇṭukaḷ iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nūlāl iṇaikkappaṭṭuk kaikaḷil aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa. paḷiṅku vaḷaiyalkaḷ cilavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai ciṟiya paṭṭāṭaiyākavum, periya paṭṭāṭaiyākavum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaḷimaṇ vaḷaiyalkaḷ mikka kavaṉattuṭaṉ ceyyappaṭṭuc ciṟanta muṟaiyil cūḷaiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟaic ceyyap payaṉpaṭṭa kaḷimaṇ mikavum uyarntatākum eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu.

oviyam tiṭṭappaṭṭa vaḷaiyalkaḷ

kaḷimaṇ koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa vaḷaiyalkaḷ vevvēṟu niṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉum oviyaṅkaḷuṭaṉum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila vaḷaiyalkaḷ mītu puḷḷikaḷ iṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ittakaiya kaḷimaṇ vaḷaiyalkaḷai ēḻaip peṇmaṇikaḷē aṇintiruntaṉar pōlum! ikkālattum ēḻai mātar cempu, veṇkalam, pittaḷai, kaṇṇāṭi mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ ceyyappaṭum vaḷaiyalkaḷai aṇintuḷḷaṉar allavā? ivarkaḷaip pōlavē, akkāla ēḻai makaḷirum ivaṟṟālāya aṇikaḷai aṇintaṉar eṉṟu kōṭal poruttamākum.

kāl kāppukaḷ

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta kāppu vakaikaḷiṟ kaikkup payaṉpaṭṭavai ivai, kāliṟkup payaṉpaṭṭavai ivai, eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟutaṟku illai. āyiṉum, cila patumaikaḷiṉ kālkaḷil vaḷainta kōṇal kāppukaḷ iruppatākat terivatāl, appaḻaṅkālap peṇṭir kāl kāppukaḷai aṇintiruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟutal takum. ittakaiya kālkāppukaḷaiyē cimlāvaiccūḻavuḷḷa malaippiratēcattil vāḻum peṇṭir aṇintu varukiṉṟaṉar. mēlum kirīṭ tīvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa maṅkiya uruvam oṉṟiṉ kālkaḷilum ittakaiya kōṇaṟkāppukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.[5] ivai pōṉṟa kāppukaḷaiyē iṉṟum vaṭuka makaḷir kālkaḷil aṇikiṉṟaṉar.

neṟṟic cuṭṭikaḷ

vaṭṭamāka mukkōṇa vaṭivattiṟ kūmpiyaṉavākac ceyyapṭṭuḷḷa nakaikaḷ cila moheñco-tarōvilum harappāvilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.ivaikuḻantaikaḷiṉ neṟṟiyil toṅkaviṭappaṭum patakkaṅkaḷ pōlak kāṇkiṉṟaṉa. ivai, pañcāpilum mārvārilum uḷḷa peṇkaḷ talaiyaic cuṟṟip poṉ nāṭā oṉṟaik kaṭṭi, atiṟ kōttu aṇiyum cuṭṭi pōlavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iccuṭṭikaḷ 5 ce. mī. cuṟṟaḷavuṭaṉum 6.10 ce. mī. uyarttuṭaṉum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, pīṅkāṉ mutaliyavaṟṟāl ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai talaimayiruṭaṉ iṇaippataṟkum nāṭāviṟ kōppataṟkum ēṟṟavāṟu tuḷaiyuṭaṉ kūṭi irukkiṉṟaṉa. cippikaḷil mūṉṟu nāṉku aḻakiya tuṇṭukaḷaip - poruttiyum ittakaiya cuṭṭikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iccuṭṭikaḷaik koṇṭa kaḷimaṇ patumaikaḷ nirampak kiṭaittamaiyāl, ivai talaiyaṇikaḷē eṉpatu veḷḷiṭai malai. ikkālattil ‘neṟṟiccuṭṭi’ eṉṟu collappaṭuvatum nam peṇkaḷāl talaiyil aṇiyappaṭuvatumākiya talai yaṇiyaip pōṉṟaṉavē iccuṭṭikaḷ eṉal oruvāṟu poruntum. immātiriyuḷḷa cuṭṭikaḷ pala harappāvil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai neṟṟi mutal uccittalai varai oṉṟaṉ piṉ oṉṟāka vaittuk kūntalmītu alaṅkarikkappaṭṭaṉa. iṅṅaṉam alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa nilaiyil cila patumaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iccuṭṭikaḷ pala poṉṉāl iyaṉṟavai: aṭippuṟam kūntal iḻaikaḷ nuḻaiyat takkapaṭi veḷḷi iṇaippuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṉṟavai. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu 3 ce. mī. uyaramum aṭippākattil 4 ce.mī. akalamum uṭaiyatu.[6]

kātaṇikaḷ

cila kaḷimaṇ patumaikaḷ kātaṇikaḷuṭaṉ iruk kiṉṟaṉa.avaṟṟāl, akkāla makaḷir palavakaiyāṉa kātaṇikaḷai aṇintu vantaṉar eṉpatai aṟiyalām. cila kātaṇikaḷ, taṅkac curuḷ pōlak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila ciṟu kuḻāykaḷaiyuṭaiya kumiḻpōlac ceyyappaṭṭavai. avai, kātukaḷil uḷḷa tuḷaikaḷiṟ celuttappaṭṭa piṉṉar maṟṟoru tuṇṭu vaittut tirukāṇi pōlap poruttiviṭat takkavāṟu amaintuḷḷaṉa. ivai taṅkattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavai; ciṟitu kūmpiya kōpura. vaṭivuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ikkūmpiya pakutiyiṟṟāṉ kuḻāy vaittup paṟṟavaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. vēṟu cila kātaṇikaḷ taṅkattāl vaḷaiyaṅkaḷākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai, tamiḻnāṭṭu makaḷir vaṭikātukaḷil aṇintu koḷḷum vaḷaiyaṅkaḷākak kāṭci aḷittal kavaṉikkat takkatu.

mūkkaṇikaḷ

oraṅkaḷiṟ paṟkaḷaik koṇṭa cakkaram pōṉṟa taṅkattakaṭukaḷum piṟavum akkāla mūkkaṇikaḷākap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ, tīkṣit eṉpavar, ‘mūkkaṇikaḷ appaṇṭaik kālattil iruntaṉa eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟakkūṭavillai. eṉṉai? kaḷimaṇ patumaikaḷuḷ oṉṟilēṉum mūkkaṇikku uriya aṭaiyāḷam iṉmaiyiṉ eṉka. mēlum ki.pi.1200-kkup piṉṉarē mūkkil tuḷaiyiṭum vaḻakkam muslimkaḷāl innāṭṭiṟ pukuttappaṭṭatu’[7] eṉṟu aṟivikkiṟār. āṉāl, aṟiñar ci. ār. irāy eṉpār, ‘cintup piratēca makaḷir mūkkaṇikaḷ aṇintiruntaṉar. avvaṇikaḷ kātil uḷḷa tōṭukaḷuṭaṉ poṟcaṅkiliyāl irupuṟamum iṇaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa’,[8] eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. ‘cintup piratēca makaḷir nīlak kal patitta mukkaṇikaḷai aṇintiruntaṉar eṉak kūṟalām’ eṉṟu pāṭrik kārliṭaṉ eṉpār aṟivikkiṟār.[9] cintuppiratēca ārāyccikkeṉṟē ciṟappāṉa ārāycciyāḷarāka intiya aracāṅkattāl niyamikkappaṭṭa ṭākṭar makkē eṉpavar, ‘akkāla mātar mūkkaṇikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; nīla niṟamuṭaiya pīṅkāṉ tuṇṭukaḷaiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; oruvakai nīla arakkil mēṟpuṟam taṭṭaiyākac ceytō, kaṟkaḷ, ulōkat tuṇṭukaḷ, oviyaṅkoṇṭa tuṇṭukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaip poruttiyō mūkkaṇikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉarātal vēṇṭum’ eṉṟu tuṇukkamāka ārāyntu kūṟukiṉṟār.[10] ‘harappā nakarattu makaḷir, cuṟṟilum paṟkaḷaik koṇṭa cakkaram pōṉṟa aḻakiya ciṟiya takaṭukaḷai mūkkaṇikaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉṟu aṟiñar vāṭs varaintuḷḷār.[11]

mōtiraṅkaḷ

mōtiraṅkaḷ palavākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. cila, kampikaḷākac ceytu paṭṭaiyākat taṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pala, ulōkattaic curuṭṭi vaittu mōtiramāka aṭittuc ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. perumpālāṉa mōtiraṅkaḷ cempu, veṇkalam, caṅku cippi ivaṟṟāl ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. harappāvil taṅkamōtiram oṉṟum veḷḷi mōtiram oṉṟum kiṭaittaṉa. oru mōtiram catura mukappu vaittuc ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ammukappiṉ mītu kuṟukkum neṭukkumākak kōṭukaḷ varaiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pīṅkāṉ mōtiram oṉṟum harappāvil kiṭaittatu.

pottāṉkaḷ

cempu, veṇkalam, pīṅkāṉ, mākkal mutaliyavaṟṟāl vaṭṭa vaṭivattil ceyyappaṭṭa pottāṉkaḷ pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai potuvāka, mālṭṭā, pōrccukal, teṉ pirāṉs mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṟ kiṭaitta paṇṭaik kālap pottāṉkaḷai ottuḷḷaṉa. avai taṭṭaiyākac ceytu caṭṭai mītu poruttit taippataṟkēṟṟavāṟu iraṇṭu tuḷaikaḷ iṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ulōkattāl āṉa pottāṉkaḷ muṉpuṟam kumiḻākac ceyyappaṭṭu, aṭiyil iraṇṭu tuḷaikaḷ iṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila pottāṉkaḷ piṟai vaṭivattil ceyyap paṭṭut tuṇiyil iṇaittut taittuk koḷḷattakka vacatiyuṭṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. harappāvil veḷḷip pottāṉkaḷ cila kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ naṭuvē cippit tuṇṭukaḷ aḻakāka vaittup patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai pārvaikku eḻil mikuntu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

talai nāṭākkaḷ

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta cila kaḷimaṇ patumaikaḷ vāyilāka, akkāla makkaḷ talaiyil oru vakai nāṭāvaic cuṟṟik kaṭṭikkoṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu veḷi yākiṟatu. akkāla āṭavarum peṇṭirum, annāṭākkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ippaḻakkam cumēriyariṭaiyum mikuntiruntatu. annāṭākkaḷ curuṇaikaḷākac cuṟṟi vaikkappaṭṭu, nakaik kuviyalkaḷuṭaṉ kiṭaittaṉa. ivaṟṟuṭ cila maṅkiviṭāmalum uṟuti iḻavāmalum irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivai 1 ce. mī. akalattil melliya taṅkat takaṭṭāl carikaipōlac ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cila nāṭākkaḷ 40 ce mī nīḷamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. anta nāṭākkaḷiṉ irumuṉaikaḷum tuḷaiyiṭappaṭṭu, iḻuttuk kaṭṭa vacati uṭaiyaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. cilavaṟṟil orupuṟam muḻuvatum varicaiyākat tuḷaiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ittuḷaikaḷil piṟa aṇikaḷ ētēṉum māṭṭit toṅkaviṭappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[12] cumēriyar ittakaiya nāṭākkaḷ mītu vilaṅkukaḷiṉ cittiraṅ kaḷaiyum, veṟum puḷḷikaḷaiyum tīṭṭip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar.

koṇṭai ūcikaḷ

āṭavarum peṇṭirum kūntalaik kōtik koṇṭai iṭuvutum curuṭṭik kaṭṭik koḷvatum vaḻakkam. akkaṭṭukaḷ nekiḻṉamal irukkak koṇṭaiūcikaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. aṅkuk kiṭaitta paṭivaṅkaḷaik kāṇkaiyil, ammakkaḷ palatiṟappaṭṭa koṇṭai ūcikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatu teḷivām. citaṟuṇṭa ūcikaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ ciṟap puṭaiyatu veṇkalak koṇṭai ūci oṉṟē ākum. atu 11 ce. mī. nīḷam irukkiṟatu. ataṉ ucciyil iraṇṭu kaṟutta kalaimāṉ talaikaḷ vevvēṟu ticaiyai nōkkuvaṉavāy amaintuḷḷaṉa. akkalaimāṉkaḷiṉ kompukaḷ aḻakuṟa muṟukkik koṇṭiruppaṉapōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maṟṟoru veṇkala ūciyil curuṇṭa kompuṭaiya talai oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivvakai ūcikaḷ cumēriyar, ekiptu, kākasas, naṭu airōppā ākiya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭaitta ūcikaḷaip pōlavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. tantam, elumpu mutaliyavaṟṟāl āṉa koṇṭai ūcikaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.cila ūcikaḷ talaippuṟam maṭṭum ulōkattālum, kuttikkoḷḷum pakuti marattālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ, avaṟṟiṉ talaippakutiyē kiṭaittatu. marappakuti aḻintuviṭṭatu. ivvāṟu kiṭaitta talaippakuti oṉṟu 1 ce. mī. uyaram irukkiṉṟatu; ataṉmītu mūṉṟu kuraṅkukaḷ tōḷkaḷiṟ kaikōttuk koṇṭu cuṟṟi uṭkārntiruppaṉapōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maṟṟoṉṟiṉ talaippuṟam veṇkallāl āṉatu. aḵtu, iraṇṭu kuraṅkukaḷ oṉṟai oṉṟu aṇaittukkoṇ ṭiruppaṉapōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. maṟṟuñ cila talaip puṟaṅkaḷ tāmarai vittukaḷ pōlavum, vēṟu palavakaiyākavum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

camayat toṭarpuḷḷa patakkam

kaḻuttu mālaikaḷil kōkkappaṭṭaṉa pōla iṉṟit taṉippaṭṭa vēlaippāṭu koṇṭa patakkam oṉṟu moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaittuḷḷatu. itu 7 ce. mī. nīḷamum 6 ce.mī akalamum uḷḷatu; vaṭṭa vaṭivamāṉatu. itu veṇmai kalanta mañcaḷ niṟa mākkallil ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉ mītu piṟai pōlac cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ippiṟaiyil, oṟṟaik kompuṭaiya erutiṉ uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉ etiril toṭṭi oṉṟu vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu; piṟaiyiṉ muṉaikaḷ cērumiṭattil ētō oṉṟu toṅkutal pōlak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ippatakkattil cetukku vēlai nirampiyuḷḷatu. itaṉuḷ palaniṟac cāntukaḷ appiyiruttal kūṭiyatē. ippatakkam taṅkat takaṭṭil vaittup patikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. attakaṭṭiṉ talaippuṟattil māṭṭikkoḷḷat takkapaṭi vaḷaiyam iṇaikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. itu camayat toṭarpuṭaiya patakkamāka iruttal vēṇṭum.[13]

palavakaik kaṟkaḷ

cintuppiratēcattiṟ kiṭaitta palavakaik kaiyaṇikaḷ, kaḻuttu mālaikaḷ, iṭaippaṭṭaikaḷ, mōtiraṅkaḷ iṉṉapiṟa aṇikaḷil patikkappaṭṭuḷḷavaiyum taṉiyē kiṭaittuḷḷavai yumāṉa palavakai viyattaku maṇikaḷ yāvai? avai iravaik kaṟkaḷ, civappu, mañcaḷ, nīlam, cūriya kāntak kaṟkaḷ, vaiṭuriyam, irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ, tūymai ceyyappaṭāta vaiṭūriyam, kōmētakam, irattam pōṉṟa cenniṟamuṭaiya māṇikkam, vayiram, pāmpukkal, kūriya oruvakaik kaṟkaḷ, ‘amesāṉ’ eṉappaṭum oruvakaip paccaik kal, vēṟu (palvēṟupaṭṭa) kaṟkaḷ,[14] spaṭikam, ulōkak kalavaikkal, paḷiṅkukkal mutaliyaṉa ākum. nīlam oṉṟait tavira, piṟa kal vakaikaḷ aṉaittum āpkāṉistāṉam, kāṣmīr, tipēt, vaṭaparmā, piṟa intiya maṇṭilaṅkaḷ ākiya pala iṭaṅkaḷiliruntē varavaḻaikkappaṭṭavai.

ik kalmaṇikaḷ iṉṟic caṅku, cippi, pīṅkāṉ, kaḷimaṇ mutaliyavaṟṟālum maṇikaḷ ceytu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.

maṇikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭa vitam

cintup piratēca makkaḷ kaṟkaḷaic cōtittu avaṟṟiṉ taṉmaikkēṟṟa muṟaiyil uruvākki vaḻavaḻappākat tēytatu merukiṭṭuḷḷaṉar ivvēlaippāṭṭiṟkut tēvaippaṭṭa kaṭaiccal iyantiraṅkaḷ avarkaḷiṭam iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. avai iṉṟēl, ivvēlaippāṭukaḷ kaṟkaḷil kāṇappaṭā. kaṟkaḷ uruvākkappaṭṭa piṉṉarē nuṇṇiya kūrmaiyāṉa karuvikaḷaik koṇṭu tuḷaiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. aṅkuk kiṭaitta pala maṇikaḷ tuḷaiyiṭappaṭāmal iruppataikkoṇṭu iṅṅaṉam karutappaṭukiṟatu. attuḷaiyiṭappaṭāta kaṟkaḷ moheñco-tarōviṉ iṟutik kālattiṉavātal vēṇṭum. vēlai muṭivataṟkuḷ nakaram tuṟakkappaṭṭuviṭṭatu. ammakkaḷ, cila cantarppaṅkaḷil iraṇṭu mūṉṟu vakaiyāṉa kaṟkaḷai oṭṭa vaittu, avaṟṟiṉ maṇikaḷaik kaṭaintirukkiṉṟaṉar. ittakaiya maṇikaḷē mikka vēlaippāṭṭuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu pīppāy pōṉṟa 1ce.mī nīḷattil uḷḷa maṇi oṉṟu civappu, veḷḷai, nīlam mutaliya niṟaṅkaḷaik koṇṭa aintuvitak kaṟkaḷai oṭṭavaittucceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āyiṉ, immaṇiyaip pārttavuṭaṉ itaṉ kalappai uṇarntu kūṟutal iyalātu eṉiṉ, itaṉ vēlaippāṭṭai eṉṉeṉpatu! immaṇi ippoḻutu uṭaintu kiṭappatāl itaṉ marmam veḷippaṭṭatu. kōmētakamō eṉṟu aiyuṟattakkavāṟu oru maṇi kiṭaittuḷḷatu. aḵtu orupuṟam cippi, maṟṟoru puṟam caṟṟuc civanta cuṇṇakkal ākiyavai koṇṭu pakkuvapṭuttip pōlik kōmētaka maṇiyākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu viyattaṟ kuriyatē ittakaiya pōli maṇikaḷ maṟṟa maṇikaḷaiviṭac ciṟantaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai pōṉṟa pōli maṇikaḷ ‘niṉaveh’ eṉṉum iṭattilum kiṭaittaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar kām pel tāmcaṉ kūṟukiṉṟār.

oviyam amainta maṇikaḷ

aṟiñar, oviyaṅkoṇṭa maṇikaḷē mikka ciṟap puṭaiyavai eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. civappuk kallil paṭṭai maṇikaḷ, ceytu, avaṟṟiṉmītu veḷḷai niṟaṅkoṇṭa cittiraṅkaḷait tīṭṭit tīyiliṭṭu, accittiraṅkaḷaip patiyac ceyyum muṟai kaiyāḷappaṭṭatu. ittakaiya maṇikaḷ pala moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ivaipōṉṟa pala, ur nakarattu araca parmparaiyiṉar cavakkuḻikaḷiliruntu eṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. avai aḷavilum uruvilum moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta maṇikaḷai ottuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, immaṇikaḷ cintu veḷiyiṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉavā? cumēriyāviṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉavā? aṉṟi vēṟeṅkēṉum ceyyappaṭṭaṉavā? tuṇintu kūṟutaṟku iyalavillai. āyiṉ, immaṇikaḷ cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭait tiruppatāṟṟāṉ ivaṟṟiṉ kālamum cumēriyariṉ uyar nākarikak kālamum oṉṟeṉak kūṟavum, akkālam ki.mu. 3250 - ki.mu. 2750 eṉa muṭivu kaṭṭavum vacati ēṟpaṭṭatu. moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta immaṇikaḷ pōṉṟaṉavum, 8 pōṉṟa amaippuṭaiya civappu maṇikaḷ cilavum cāṉhu-tarōvil kiṭaittaṉa. piṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭa maṇikaḷ veṇṇiṟa oviyaṅkaḷaik koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. ovvoru maṇiyum 2 ce. mī. uyaram irukkiṉṟatu. aṅku, veṇ maṇikaḷ mītu cenniṟam tīṭṭi, veṇṇiṟa oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa maṇikaḷum cila kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.

paṭṭai veṭṭappaṭṭa maṇikaḷ

moheñco-tarōvilum harappāvilum paṭṭai veṭṭappaṭṭa maṇikaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ittakaiya maṇikaḷ ekiptait tavira vēṟu ennāṭṭilum kiṭaittila; ekiptilum piṟkāla urōmar tam kallaṟaikaḷilēyē kiṭaittaṉa. ivai oviya maṇikaḷ alla; aṟukōṇam, eṇkōṇam eṉṉum muṟaiyil paṭṭaikaḷāka amaintavai.

kaṟkaḷiṟ pulamai

cintu veḷiyiṉar, iṅṅaṉam pala tiṟappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷaic cōtittu, avaṟṟiṉ eḻil kuṉṟāmal irukkumpaṭi ariya vēlaippāṭukaḷ ceytu, palavakai maṇikaḷākki mālaikaḷākac ceytum, vēṟu pala ulōkaṅkaḷālāṉa maṇikaḷuṭaṉ cērttum, poṉ veḷḷi mutaliyavaṟṟāl āṉa palavakai aṇikaḷiṟ poruttamuṟap patittum aṇintu vantaṉar. varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa appaḻaṅkālattil - iṉṟaikku 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar - apperu makkaḷ kaṟkaḷilum pulamai cāṉṟavarāka iruntaṉar eṉiṉ, ammamma! avartam uyariya nākarikattai eṉṉeṉap pukaḻa vallēm![15]

'taṅkak kavacam koṇṭa maṇikaḷ'

immaṇikaḷuṭ cila mālaiyākak kōkkappaṭṭa pōtu, maṇikaḷiṉ irupuṟattum taṅka villaikaḷ kavacam pōla oṭṭavaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṟu nammavar palar immuṟaippaṭi uruttirākka maṇikaḷaik kōttu aṇintuḷḷaṉar. ippaḻakkam harappāvil mikutiyāka iruntateṉṟu aṟiñar vāṭs aṟivikkiṟār. itu cumēriyariṭattum iruntatām.

cīppukaḷ

appaṇṭai makkaḷ palatiṟappaṭṭa cīppukaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar; avai tantam, māṭṭukkompu, elumpu, maram eṉpaṉavaṟṟāl āṉavai; pala vaṭivaṅkaḷil ceyyap paṭṭavai. avaṟṟuḷ cila talaiyil cerukik koḷḷavum payaṉ paṭṭaṉa. iṅṅaṉam makaḷir cīppukaḷaik kūntaliṟ cerukik koḷḷum paḻakkam iṉṟu parmā nāṭṭil iruntu varukiṉṟatu. mēṉāṭṭu mātar cilar melliya vaḷainta cīppukaḷai aṇikiṉṟaṉar. oru kallaṟaiyil iḷam peṇ oruttiyiṉ maṇṭai oṭṭiṉ arukil irupuṟamum paṟkaḷaik koṇṭa aḻakiya cittiraṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟa tantac cīppu oṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭatu. ‘V’ pōṉṟa vaṭivil ceyyappaṭṭa cīppoṉṟu moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaittatu. itu nīṇṭa kūntalaic cikkiṉṟik kōta utaviyatākum. iccīppu neruṅkiya paṟkaḷaiyuṭaiyatāy aḻakuṟac ceyyappaṭṭiruntamaiyāl, talaiyaic cīvavum talaiyil vaittuk koḷḷavum payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. itupōṉṟa cīppu ekiptil ‘paṭari’ eṉṉum iṭattiṟṟāṉ kiṭaittatu vēṟu eṅkum akappaṭavillai.

kaṇṇāṭikaḷ

cintu veḷi makkaḷ mukam pārkkak kaṇṇāṭikaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiyavai yāvai? avarkaḷ ceppu-veṇkalat takaṭukaḷai naṉku merukiṭṭup paḷapaḷappākkik kaṇṇāṭika ḷākap payaṉpaṭuttikkoṇṭaṉar; avaṟṟiṉ paḷapaḷappu maṅkiviṭāmal irukka, oraṅkaḷai maṭakki araṇ ceytiruntaṉar. iṅṅaṉam pakuttaṟivuṭaṉ ceyyappaṭṭa kaṇṇāṭikaḷ pala aḷavukaḷai uṭaiyavai; marak kaippiṭikaḷaik koṇṭavai. moheñco-tarōvilum harappāvilum kiṭaitta cila kaṇṇāṭikaḷ muṭṭai vaṭivil amaintuḷḷaṉa! cila vaṭṭa vaṭivil amaintuḷḷaṉa. oru kaṇṇāṭi 22 ce. mī. uyaram irukkiṟatu.ittakaiya kaṇṇāṭikaḷ ekiptilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.

makaḷir kūntal oppaṉai

cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭaitta peṇ paṭivaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu, akkāla makaḷir taṅkaḷ kūntalai evvem muṟaiyil oppaṉai ceytukoṇṭaṉar eṉpatai oruvāṟu uṇaralām. ammakaḷir kūntalai naṉṟākac cīppiṭṭu vārip piṉṉi viṭṭiruntaṉar. kōtic curuṭṭi nāṭāvāl kaṭṭi iṭappuṟam cāyttuk koṇṭaipōlamuṭintiruntaṉar. kūntalaippiṉṉināṭāvaik koṇṭu muṭintiruntaṉar; kūntalaip piṉṉi nāṭāvaik koṇṭu pūppōla muṭippiṭṭu, vitavitamāka (kaṇṇaṉ koṇṭai pōṭṭiruppatupōla) mēlē tūkki muṭintu, ammuṭippukaḷil perṉ, veḷḷi, tantam, elumpu mutaliya vaṟṟāl āṉa koṇṭai ūcikaḷait tattam takutik kēṟṟavāṟu cerukik koṇṭaṉar; palavakai naṟumalarkaḷaic cūṭivantaṉar. maintar kūntal oppaṉai

āṭavar cilar tam cikaiyaik kuṟukalāka veṭṭik koṇṭiruntaṉar; cilar nīḷamāka vaḷarttuc curuṭṭip piṉpuṟam muṭintiruntaṉar; atu carintuviṭāmal irukkak koṇṭai ūcikaḷaiyum nāṭākkaḷaiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar; cilar talai naṭuvil vaṭu eṭuttumayirai alai alaiyākac cīviviṭṭiruntaṉar. vēṟu cilar talaimayiraik kōtic curuṭṭi ucciyiṟ kaṭṭik koṇṭiruntaṉar. iṅṅaṉam kūntalai muṭital cumēriyāvilum paḻakkamāka iruntatu. potuvākac cintuveḷi makkaḷiṉ kūntal alai alaiyākavum curuṇṭu curuṇṭum toṅkikkoṇṭiruntatu eṉpatu pala patumaikaḷ vāyilāka aṟiyalām. cilar kūntalaip piṉṉip pakkaṅkaḷil viṭṭiruntaṉar; ippaḻakkam pāpilōṉiyariṭamum iruntatēyākum.[16]

mīcai illā āṭavar

cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭaittuḷḷa āṇ patumaikaḷ perumpālāṉa teyvaṅkaḷaiyō - teyvamaṉṉa yōkiyaraiyō kuṟippaṉa. eṉiṉum, maṉitaṉ taṉṉai uḷḷattiṟkoṇṭē teyvaṅkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷaic camaippatu iyalpu. annilaiyai niṉaintu ārāyiṉ, paṇṭaic cintu veḷi makkaḷ mīcaiyai vaittilar tāṭi oṉṟaiyē vaittiruntaṉar eṉpatai aḻuttamākak kūṟalām. ittāṭiyum pala vakaiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. kātu mutal vaḷarntuḷḷa mayirum kīḻ utaṭṭaṭiyil vaḷarntuḷḷa mayirum oṉṟākat tōṟṟamaḷikkumāṟu tāṭi vaittiruttal oru muṟai; atai oḻuṅkupeṟak kattirittiruttal oru muṟai: mukavāyk kaṭṭaiyiṉ aṭiyilmaṭṭum ciṟitaḷavu tāṭivaḷarttal mūṉṟām muṟai. attāṭiyiṉ nuṉi uḷnōkki vaḷaintirukkum paṭi viṭutal nāṉkām muṟai. immuṟaippaṭiyē cumēriyarum tāṭikaḷai vaittiruntaṉar; āṉāl palar nīḷa vaḷarttuviṭṭiruntaṉar. mīcai vaḷarntu mēl utaṭṭil toṭutalāl uṭkoḷḷum uṇavil mācupaṭum eṉṟu eṇṇi, avvaṟiñar mīcaiyai aṭiyōṭu ciraittu vantaṉar eṉṟē karutavēṇṭum. cukātāra muṟaiyil uṟaiviṭaṅkaḷai amaittu vāḻnta appēraṟiñarkaḷ, uṇavilum cukātāra muṟaiyaik kaiyāṇṭu mīcaiyai aṟavē oḻittaṉar pōlum!

inta aḷavōṭēṉum avarkaḷ niṉṟaṉarō? illai! illai! āṭavarum peṇṭirum uṭaliṉ piṟa pakutikaḷil vaḷarum mayiraiyum kaḷaintu vantaṉar eṉpatu cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭaitta kaṇakkaṟṟa maḻittaṟ kattikaḷaik koṇṭum kūrmaiyāṉa ciṟiya takaṭukaḷaik koṇṭum kūṟalām’ eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē uraikkiṉṟār.[17]

kaṇṇukku mai

appaṇṭaik kāla makaḷir evvakaiyilum iṉṟaiya makaḷirkkup piṉṉiṭaintavarākat teriyavillai. avarkaḷ kaṇkaḷil kaṟuppu mai tīṭṭik koṇṭaṉar. peṇṭir maṭṭum illai. āṭavarum mai iṭṭuk koṇṭaṉar. irupālarum kaṇṇukku mai iṭac ceppuk kuccikaḷaiyum piṟa melliya kuccikaḷaiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. makaḷir ciṟiya nattai oṭukaḷil kuṅkumam pōṉṟa ceṅkāvip poṭikaḷai vaittukkoṇṭu, avaṟṟai neṟṟiyil poṭṭiṭap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ivai pōṉṟa nattai oṭukaḷaic cumēriya makaḷir vaittiruntaṉar. āyiṉ, avarkaḷ avvōṭukaḷil mukattiṟ pūcum poṭi vakaikaḷaiyē vaittiruntaṉar.

mukattiṟkup poṭi,

cintu veḷi makaḷir oru vakai veṇpoṭiyait taṅkaḷ mukattiṟ pūci vantaṉar. ippoṭi moheñco-tarōvilum harappāvilum kiṭaittuḷḷatu. itē poruḷ kaṇṇukku iṉiya maruntākavum payaṉ paṭṭirukkalām eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu. ‘aritāram pōṉṟa oruvakaip poṭiyum mukattiṟ pūcik koḷḷap payaṉpaṭṭatu. oruvakaip paccai maṇṇum naṉṟāyp poṭi ceyyappaṭṭu mukattiṟ pūcap payaṉpaṭṭatām. ippaccai maṇ kaṭṭi kaṭṭiyāka moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaittuḷḷatu. itu kaṇṇukku maiyākavum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu paccai niṟam pūcavum payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē karutukiṟār. itupōṉṟa paccai niṟapporuḷaip paṇṭai ekiptiyar kaṇṇukkē payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. ivvāṟu mukattiṟ poṭi pūcik koḷḷum paḻakkam paṇṭaik kiris, cīṉam ākiya nāṭukaḷilum iruntaṉa.



↑ veḷḷi eṉpatu āpkāṉistāṉattiliruntu koṇṭu varappaṭṭa īyattiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭatātaliṉ, avvariya poruḷāl āṉa kalaṉkaḷ arukiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

↑ veṇ poṉ (Electrum) eṉpatu nam nāṭṭil kiṭaikkiṟatu. itil veḷḷiyē mikutiyākak kalantirukkum. itiṉiṉṟum poṉṉaip pirikka vakai aṟiyāta ammakkaḷ, appaṭiyē nakaikaḷ ceytukoṇṭaṉar.

↑ Dr.E.J.H.Mackay’s ‘The Indus Civilization’, p. 109.

↑ M.S.Vats’s ‘Excavations at Harappa’, Vol. I, p.64

↑ K.N.Dikshit’s ‘Pre-historic Civilization of the IV’, p.27

↑ His Article on ‘Mohenjo-Daro. In the ‘Indian World’.

↑ ‘His Buried Empires’, p.154.

↑ Dr.E.Mackay’s The Indus Civilization’ p. 115.

↑ M.S.Vat’s ‘Excavations at Harappa’, Vol. I pp.60-64.

↑ innāṭākkaḷum muṉcoṉṉa talaiccuṭṭikaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭut tirumaṇaṅkaḷil maṇamakaḷ aṇiyum talaiyaṇikaḷaip peritum ottaṉavē ākum. ivai mottamākat talaic cāmāṉ (talai aṇikaḷ) eṉṟu ikkālattiṟ collappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
