↑ M.S.Vats’s ‘Excavations at Harappa’, Vol. pp.292, 293. Dr.E.J.H.Mackays’ “The Indus Civilization’, pp.104, 105.

↑ His ‘The Civilisation’, p. 120.





* * *





﻿12. vāṇipam

uḷ nāṭṭu vāṇipam

cintu veḷi makkaḷ koṇṭirunta vāṇipam uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipam, veḷināṭṭu vāṇipam eṉa iraṇṭākak kūṟalām. moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta cempu mutaliya kaṇip poruḷkaḷ irājaputaṉam, mattiya mākāṇam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiviruntum, māṉ kompukaḷ kāṣmīriliruntum, vairamum veḷḷi kalanta īyamum āpkāṉistāṉattiliruntum, oruvakai uyartarap paccaikkal vaṭaparmāviliruntum allatu tipēttiliruntum, poṉ kōlār, aṉantappūr eṉṉum ceṉṉai mākāṇa iṭaṅkaḷiliruntum, oruvakaip paccaikkal maicūriliruntum, uyartara ‘amesāṉ’ eṉṉum paccaikkal nīlakiriyiliruntum, caṅku muttu mutaliyaṉa pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum koṇṭu cellappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvatāl, appaṇṭaikkālattil intiya uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipam ciṟappuṟa naṭantu vantatu eṉpataiyum, cintu veḷi makkaḷ intiyā muḻuvataiyum cuṟṟuppuṟa nāṭukaḷaiyum naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpataiyum oruvāṟu uṇaralām. haitarāpātil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa cavakkuḻikaḷ mītum, tirunelvēlik kōṭṭattiṟ kiṭaitta nāṇayaṅkaḷ mītum, moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaitta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu. kāṇappaṭum cila kuṟiyīṭukaḷ pōṉṟavai kāṇappaṭṭamai iṅkuk karutat takkatu.[1] iṅṅaṉam ‘teṉṉintiyāviṉ kaṭaṟkarai,kattiyavār, vaṭamēṟku maṇṭilam, cintu-pañcāp maṇṭilaṅkaḷ, kaṅkaic cama veḷiyiṉ vaṭapakuti, irājaputaṉam mutaliya intiyap pakutikaḷ ak kāla nākarikattiṟku uriyavaiyākalām'[2] eṉṟu tīkṣat avarkaḷ kūṟutalāl, akkāla uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipacciṟappai oruvāṟu uṇaralām.

veḷi nāṭṭu vāṇipam

cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭaitti eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ, muttiraikaḷ, oviyaṅkaḷ,karuvikaḷ, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai ārāynta nipuṇar avaṟṟukkum ēlam, cumēr mocopoṭṭēmiyā, ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpai naṉku aṟintu viyantuḷḷaṉar. ipporuḷkaḷiṉ orumaippāṭṭālum ēṟṟumati iṟakkumatip poruḷkaḷālum, nām, cintu nāṭṭavar ceytuvanta veḷināṭṭu vāṇipattai oruvāṟu uṇaralām.

(1) moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta uyarnta calavaikkal muttiraikaḷaip pōṉṟavai mikap palavāka ‘ēlam’ eṉṉum nāṭṭiṟ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. calavaikkal poruḷkaḷ mikutiyākac cintu nāṭṭiṟ kiṭaikkāmaiyāṉum, avaṟṟiṉ mītu amaintuḷḷa vēlaippāṭu annāṭukaṭkē uriyavai ātalālum, avai ēlam-cumēriyanāṭukaḷil mikutiyākak kiṭait talālum, avai annāṭukaḷiliruntē cintuviṟkuk koṇṭu varappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu veḷḷiṭai malai.[3]

(2) cila intiya muttiraikaḷ paḻaiya ur, kiṣ, ṭel asmar eṉṉum mocopoṭṭēmiyā nakaraṅkaḷil kiṭait tuḷḷaṉa. aṅku irunta piṟa poruḷkaḷum, annakaraṅkaḷiṉ nākarika kālamum ki.mu.3000 ki.mu.2500kku uṭpaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē, cintu nākarikamum veḷināṭṭu vāṇipamum mēṟcoṉṉakālattaṉavēeṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar muṭivākum. ṭelasmaril ṭākṭar pirāṅkpōrṭ eṉṉum aṟiñar kaṇṭeṭutta nīṇṭa uruṇṭaiyāṉa muttirai cintu veḷiyiṉatē ākum. atil poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa yāṉai, kāṇṭā mirukam, mutaliya ākiya mūṉṟum cumēriyāvil illātavai.

(3) atē nakarattil, uṭalil tuvāramuḷḷa muṭkaḷaip pōṉṟa amaippaik koṇṭa maṇ pāttiraṅkaḷ cila kiṭaittaṉa. avai irākkil ki.mu.2800-ki.mu.2500kku uṭpaṭṭa kālattup poruḷkaḷōṭu kalantiruntaṉa. avai intiyāviṟkē uriyavai eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu.[4]

(4) oruvakai veḷiṟiya paccaik kallāl ceyyappaṭṭa pāttiram oṉṟu pāy vaṭiva vēlaippāṭu koṇṭatāka moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaittatu. attakaiya pāttiraṅkaḷ pala ṭel asmar, kiṣ, cucā mutaliya paṇṭai nakaraṅkaḷil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.

nanti vaḻipāṭu

(5) 1936 il ṭel akrap (Tel Agrab) eṉṉum iṭam putitākat tōṇṭappaṭṭatu. aṅkuk ki.mu. 2800 kku uriya kōvil oṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭatu. ataṉuḷ pūcaikkuriya pāṇṭaṅkaḷ pala kiṭaittaṉa. oruvakaip paccaik kallāl āṉa pāttirattiṉ mītu toḻuvattiṉ muṉ periya intiya pirāmaṉi erutu niṟpatākavum, atu niṉṟuḷḷa kaṭṭiṭattiṉ muṉpuṟam cumēriya maṉitaṉ uṭkārntiruppatu pōlavum cittiram tīṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. ippaṭattiṉāl, appaṇṭaik kālattilēyē nanti vaḻipāṭu (Bull Cult) cintuviliruntu cumēriyāvukkuc ceṉṟiruttal viḷaṅkukiṟatu aṉṟō!

(6) muttiraikaḷum piṟa pāttiraṅkaḷum ceytaṟkuriya uyartarak kaṟkaḷ cumēriyāviṟku aṉuppappaṭṭuc cemmaip paṭuttappaṭṭa piṟaku koṇṭuvarappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṉai? uyartarap paccaik kaṟkaḷum vēṟu kaṟkaḷum koṇṭu ceyyap paṭṭa maṇikaḷ moheñco-tarōvaiviṭa ‘ur’ nakaril (cumēriyāvil) mikutiyākak kiṭaittuḷḷamaiyāl eṉka.

(7) naṉṟāka vaḷaintu muṟukkuṇṭa kompukaḷaiyuṭaiya cemmaṟiyāṭukaḷaik kuṟikkum cittiraṅkaḷ cila moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai morēviyā, kirīṭ tīvu, aṉaṭōliyā, ekiptu cumēriyā, ēlam eṉṉum paṇṭai nāṭukaḷil mikkiruntaṉa.

(8) moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta putuvakaik kōṭāri oṉṟu cumēriyāviliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. eṉṉai? attakaiya pala kōṭarikaḷ aṅkuk kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ātaliṉ eṉka.

(9) mīṉ piṭikkap payaṉpaṭṭa valaiyiṉ oraṅkaḷil kaṭṭappaṭṭirunta maṇ kuṇṭukaḷ iru nāṭukaḷilum oru vitamākavē kiṭaittaṉa.[5]

(10) ‘ur’ nakarattil tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭa aracar tam kallaṟaikaḷil tīṭṭappaṭṭa cittiraṅkaḷuṭ kuraṅku oṉṟākum. aḵtu annāṭṭu makkaṭkut teriyāta vilaṅku. eṉavē, an nāṭṭavar nerukkamāṉa vāṇipattoṭarpukoṇṭirunta cintu veḷi makkaḷiṭamiruntē kuraṅkiṉaip peṟṟavarātal vēṇṭum.

(11) mēṟ collappaṭṭa kallaṟaikaḷil ciṟanta vēlaippāṭṭuṭaṉ kūṭiya irattiṉa maṇikaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. avaiyum cintuveḷiyiliruntē ceṉṟaṉavātal vēṇṭum.

(12) kūntalait talaiyaic cuṟṟilum vāric cerukip piṉpuṟam kaṟṟaiyāka muṭitalum, vevvēṟu muṟaikaḷil muṭitalum avaṟṟiṉmītu koṇṭai ūcikaḷaic cerukutalum iru nāṭṭārum potuvākak koṇṭirunta paḻakkamākum.[6]

(13) cintu veḷi makkaḷ yāṉait tantam, yāṉait tantattālāṉa cippukaḷ, caturaṅka viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvi kaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaic cumēriyāvukku ēṟṟumati ceytiruk kiṉṟaṉar.[7]

(14) cintu veḷi maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu pūcappaṭṭa ceṅ kāvippoṭi intiyāvil iṉṉa iṭattiliruntu koṇṭu cellappaṭṭateṉpatu tuṇitaṟkillai. āyiṉ, pāracīka vaḷaikuṭāvai aṭuttuḷḷa hormus (Hormuz) eṉṉum iṭattiliruntu uyartarac ceṅkāvip poṭi iṉṟum intiyāvil iṟakkumati āki varuvatāl, avviṭattiliruntē appaḻaṅkālattilum vantirukkalām.

(16) vaṭanāṭṭu miṭṭāy vakaikaḷait taṭṭukaḷil vaittu terukkaḷil viṟkum vaṭanāṭṭavar, attaṭṭukaḷait tāṅka mūṅkiṟ kuccikaḷāl ākiya taṇṭukaḷai eṭuttu varutal kāṇkiṟōm. attakaiya nāṇalālāṉa taṇṭukaḷ palavaṟṟai moheñco-tarō makkaḷ payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar.ataip pōṉṟa taṇṭukaḷ cumēriyā, ekiptu, ēlam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kallālum, maṇṇālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. eṉiṉum, immūṉṟu nāṭṭavarum cintuveḷi makkaḷiṭamiruntē ittaṇṭukaḷ ceytalaik kaṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.

(16) irutaya vaṭivattil elumpukaḷaik koṇṭu ceyyap paṭṭa nuṇukka vēlaippāṭu amainta vēṟu aṇikaḷil patikkattakka ciṟiya aḻakiya poruḷkaḷ ṭelasmaril kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭaitta cippikaḷāl āṉa poruḷkaḷaip peritum ottuḷḷaṉa. ivai iruvakai yiṉavum orē kālattaṉa.

(17) kirēkka ciluvai (Greek Cross) vaṭivam poṟikkap paṭṭa muttirai oṉṟu vaṭa kiḻakkuk kirīsil kiṭaittatu. atu putiya kaṟkālattiṟku uriyatu. atupōṉṟavai pala moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, avai cintu veḷikkē uriyaṉa eṉak kūṟutal poruntum. avaṟṟiṉ amaippu muṟai harappāviṟ kiṭaittavaṟṟiliruntu naṉku aṟiyak kiṭakkiṟatu.[8] avai cumēriyāvilum, kīrīṭ tīvilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.

(18) moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta paṭivaṅkaḷil uḷḷapaṭi mīcaiyaik ciraittut tāṭiyaip palavāṟu vaittukkoḷḷum muṟai cumēriyāvilum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.

(19) pūcaṉaikkuriya taṭṭukaḷait tāṅkum taṇṭukaḷ pala cintu veḷiyilum cumēriyāvilum oṉṟu pōlavē kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.

(20) eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ ciṟitu vēṟupaṭiṉum aṭippaṭai oṉṟākavē iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.[9]

(21) cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭaitta kōṭarikaḷaip pōṉṟa veṇkalak kōṭari oṉṟu kākasas malaināṭṭaic cērnta ‘kūpaṉ’ āṟṟup paṭukaiyiṟ kiṭaittatu.

(22) viciṟipōṉṟa talaimuṇṭācu turukkiyil uḷḷa ‘aṭaliyā’ eṉṉum iṭattiṟ kiṭaitta patumaikaḷilum, ciriyāviṟ kiṭaitta cittiram oṉṟilum cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭaitta cittiraṅkaḷilum ottē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.

(23) nāṇaṟ pēṉā oṉṟu moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaittatu. atu pōṉṟa eḻutukōl kirīṭ tīvil pāttiraṅkaḷ mītu eḻutap payaṉpaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. appēṉar urōmarkaḷ kālam varai ekiptiyarukkut teriyātatākum.[10]

(24) akkēṭiyar nakaramāṉa ‘eṣṇaṉṉā’vil kaṇṭaṟiyap paṭṭa araṇmaṉaiyuḷ nīrāṭum aṟai amaikkappaṭṭatu, akkēṭiyar cintu veḷi makkaḷōṭu koṇṭirunta kūṭṭuṟaviṉālēyām eṉṉai vēṟu en nāṭṭilum nīrāṭum aṟaikaḷ appaḻaṅkālattil amaikkap paṭāmaiyiṉ eṉka.[11]

ekiptiyaruṭaṉ vāṇipam

cintu veḷi makkaḷ ekiptiyaruṭaṉ nērākavō aṉṟic cumēriyar mūlamākavō vāṇipam ceytu vantaṉar eṉpatu kīḻvarum uṇmaikaḷāl uṇaralām:

(1) cintu veḷiyilum ekiptilum kiṭaitta kaḻuttu mālaikaḷ vaṭṭamākavē amaintuḷḷaṉa.

(2) ekiptil kiṭaitta pīṭaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṉ kālkaḷ erutiṉ kālkaḷaip pōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaṭṭil kālkaḷum aṅṅaṉamē amaintuḷḷaṉa. cintu veḷiyil attakaiya pīṭam oṉṟiṉmītu civaṉār uruvam amarn tiruppatāka uḷḷa muttirai kiṭaittuḷḷatu.[12]

(3) ekiptil uḷḷa piramiṭ kōpuraṅkaḷākiya aracar cavakkuḻikaḷil, peṇ uruvaṅkaḷ cetukkappaṭṭa naṉmaṇikaḷ pala kiṭaittaṉa. avai iṟantavariṉ maṉaiviyaraik kuṟippaṉa; ammaṉaiviyar maṟupiṟappil tam kaṇavaraik kūṭi vāḻvar eṉpatu appaṇṭaikkāla ekiptiyar eṇṇam. avveṇṇam cintu veḷiyilum cumēriyāvilum iruntatākat terikiṟatu.

(4) tāy kuḻantaikkup pāl koṭuttal pōṉṟa cittiraṅkaḷ pala ekiptil kiṭaittaṉa. āṉāl, avai cumēriyāvil kāṇappaṭavillai. moheñco-tarōvil iraṇṭoru cittiraṅkaḷē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila harappāvilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.

(5) pala mukappukaḷaik koṇṭa uyartara maṇikaḷ moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai ekiptait tavira vēṟu entap paṇṭai nāṭṭilum kiṭaittila; ekiptilum, piṟkāla rōmarkaḷiṉ kallaṟaikaḷiṟṟām kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē, avai intiyāviṟkē uriyavai eṉpatu aṟiyattakkatu.

(6) ī vaṭivattil ceyyappaṭṭa maṇikaḷ ekiptilum moheñco-tarōvilum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivai, cuṟucuṟuppaiyum toḻil muṟukkaiyum aṟivikkum aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

(7) ekiptil irunta 13, 17 ām araca talaimuṟaiyiṉar kālattavai ākiya cittira eḻuttukkaḷaik koṇṭa mūṉṟu cemput takaṭukaḷ, moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaittavaṟṟaiyē peritum ottuḷḷaṉa.

(8) meḻuku varttiyait tāṅkum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, iraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷilum ēṟakkuṟaiya oṉṟākavē kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.[13]

- (9) kaṟpalakaikoṇṭu cippi vaṭiviṟ ceyyappaṭṭa karaṇṭikaḷ pala ekiptil kiṭaittaṉa. cintu veḷiyilum attakaiyaṉa kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ivai kaḷimaṇṇālum cempiṉālum ceyyappaṭṭavai. ovvoṉṟaṉ nuṉiyilum kaippiṭi iṇaippukku uriya tuḷai kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.[14] iṉṉa piṟa cāṉṟukaḷāl, appaḻaṅkālac cintuveḷi makkaḷiṉ vāṇipa ulakam, ekiptuvarai paravi iruntatu eṉpatai naṉkaṟiyalām aṉṟō?

nīrvaḻi vāṇipam

uḷ nāṭṭu vāṇipattiṉ oru pakuti cintu āṟṟiṉ mūlamākavē naṭantatu. appaṇṭaik kālattil orē vita nākarikattil irunta cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷ pala cintu āṟṟiṉ vaḻiyē tamakkuḷ vāṇipam naṭattik koṇṭaṉa; arapik kaṭal tuṟaimukap paṭṭiṉaṅkaṭkut tam poruḷkaḷaip paṭakukaḷil ēṟṟic cintu āṟṟiṉ mūlam aṉuppiṉa: mēṟkup puṟa nāṭukaḷiliruntu tuṟaimukap paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷai aṭainta poruḷkaḷai muṉcoṉṉa paṭakukaḷ mūlamē peṟṟukkoṇṭaṉa.

paṇṭai makkaḷ, iṅṅaṉam cintuyāṟṟu vaḻiyē vanta poruḷkaḷaik kappalkaḷil ēṟṟikkoṇṭu tuṟaimukaṅkaḷai viṭṭuk karai ōramākavē teṉ palucistāṉam, teṉ pāracīkam, irāk, apicīṉiyā, ekiptu eṉṉum nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vāṇipam ceytaṉarātal vēṇṭum. appaṇṭai makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya muttirai oṉṟil kappal paṭam oṉṟum, pāṉai mītu ōṭattiṉ paṭam oṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṭaṅkaḷāl, avarkaḷ kaṭaliṟ cellum kappalkaḷaiyum āṟṟiṟ cellum oṭaṅ kaḷaiyum naṉku aṟintu payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatai aṟiyalām.

kāmpē vaḷaikuṭāvaic cērtta purōc(Broach) kāmpē (Combay) eṉṉum tuṟaimukaṅkaḷ iraṇṭum cintuveḷi nākarika kālattil ceḻippuṟṟu iruntaṉavātal vēṇṭum. attuṟai mukaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻiyē cenniṟakkal maṇikaḷaip paṟṟiya vāṇipam uyariya nilaiyil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum narumatai yāṟṟup paḷḷat tākkukaḷil oḻuṅkāṉa ārāycci naṭaipeṟumāyiṉ, pala uṇmaikaḷ veḷiyākum.[15]

nilavaḻi vāṇipam

cintu veḷikku jēṭ (lade) eṉṉum oruvakaik kal naṭu āciyāviliruntu vantukoṇṭiruntatu. akkal cintu veḷi mūlam teṉ pāracīkam, irark, cumēriyā, ciriyā, ekiptu ākiya nāṭukaṭku aṉuppappaṭṭatu. eṉavē, akkālat taraivaḻi vāṇipam ēṟakkuṟaiya 4800 ki.mī. tolaivil naṭantu vantatu eṉpatu aṟiyattakum.[16]

ittarai vaḻi vāṇipattiṟku appaṇṭai makkaḷ erutukaḷaiyum kōvēṟu kaḻutaikaḷaiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avaṟṟuḷḷum erutukaḷē mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, erutukaḷē appaṇṭaikkāla vāṇipattai vaḷarttaṉa eṉṉalām. intak kāraṇam oṉṟaik koṇṭē appaṇṭaimakkaḷ erutai uyarvāka matittu aṉpu kāṭṭiṉar eṉpatum mikaiyākātu.

paṇṭaikkālap palucistāṉam

appaḻaṅkālattil ւlucistāṉam ceḻumaiyuṭaiyatāka iruntiruttal vēṇṭum makkaṭ perukkam uṭaiyatākavum iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. avaruṭ cilarēṉum mēṟcoṉṉa taraivaḻi vāṇipattiṟ paṅkeṭuttaṉarātal vēṇṭum cintu veḷi makkaḷōṭu neruṅkiya uṟavāṉavarākavum iruttal vēṇṭum; ammakkaḷatu mēṟkuppuṟa vāṇipattiṟkup pērutavi purintiṉarātal vēṇṭum.[17]

cintuveḷi makkaṭkup piṉ

iṅṅaṉam peruñ ciṟappuṭaṉ naṭaipeṟṟu vanta mēṟkuppuṟa vāṇipam, āriyar vantavuṭaṉ tiṭīreṉa niṉṟu viṭṭatu. āriyar pañcāpiliruntu, piṉ kaṅkaic camaveḷiyiṟ kuṭi pukkaṉar. avarkaḷ iṅṅaṉam uḷnāṭṭil taṅkiviṭṭatālum cintuveḷi makkaḷ nilai kulaintu viṭṭaṉar ātalālum avvāṇipam aṭiyōṭu niṉṟuviṭṭatu. ataṉāṟṟāṉ, cintu veḷinākarika kālattiṟkuppiṟpaṭṭa ārampa aciriyap pēraraciṉ kālattilum, ekiptiyar iṭai-kaṭai āṭcik kālaṅkaḷilum avviru nāṭukaḷilum intiyap poruḷkaḷē kiṭaittila pōlum![18]

niṟaik kaṟkaḷ

kallāl ceyyappaṭṭa niṟaik kaṟkaḷ mikutiyākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai aṉaittum vaḻavaḻappuṭaṉum paḷapaḷappuṭaṉum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nīḷam, akalam, uyaram ākiya mūṉṟu aḷavukaḷum orē muṟaiyil irukkumpaṭi ceytuḷḷa kaṟkaḷē palavākum. ivai mikac ciṟiya aḷavu mutal 274938 kirām (gramme) varai palvēṟu niṟaikaḷaik kuṟippaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa, ucciyilum aṭiyilum taṭṭaiyākkappaṭṭa uruṇṭai niṟaikaḷ aṭuttāṟpōl mikutiyāka iruppavai ākum. ivaṟṟuḷ cila viyattaku pēraḷavaik kuṟippaṉa. mūṉṟām vakaiyāṉa niṟaikaḷ uruṇṭu nīṇṭaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. immātiri niṟaikaḷ cumēriyā, ēlam, ekiptu ākiya nāṭukaḷilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. vēṟu cila niṟaikaḷ iṉṟaiya viṟaku viṟkum kaṭaikaḷil iruppavai pōlap periyaṉavum ucciyil vaḷaiyattiṟku amainta tuḷai uṭaiyaṉavumākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai kūmpiya ucciyai uṭaiyaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu 25 kilōvukku mēṟpaṭṭa niṟaiyuṭaiyatāka irukkiṟatu. periya viyāpārattiṟkē akkaṟkaḷ payaṉpaṭṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum. in nālvakaik kaṟkaḷum karuṅkal, paḷiṅkukkal, paccaikkal, maṅkiya civappukkal mutaliya palatiṟappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷāl aḻakuṟac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cintu veḷiyiṉar. ikkaṟkaḷai vēṇṭiya vaṭivattil veṭṭi, cāṉaikkal pōṉṟa kaṟpalakaimītu naṉṟākat tēyttu, iṟutiyil meruku koṭuttu vantaṉar. oru niṟaikkallilēṉum ‘eṇ’ iṭappaṭātatu viyappukku uriyatu. ciṟu kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷum niṟaikaḷākap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa.

niṟai aḷavukaḷ

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta niṟaikaḷaiyum harappāviṟ kiṭaittavaṟṟaiyum cevvaiyākac cōtitta aṟiñar ē.es.hemmi eṉpavar. avar, ciṟiya niṟaikaḷ iraṭṭai eṇkaḷaik kuṟippaṉa. periyavai tacāmca piṉṉa aḷavai uṭaiyaṉa. avai 1, 2, ⅓x8, 4, 8, 16, 32, 54, 160, 200, 320, 640, 1500, 3200, 5400, 8000, 12800 ākiya eṇkaḷaik kuṟippaṉa. ‘oṉṟu’ (kuṟippiṭṭa oru nilaitta niṟai - ‘unit') eṉpatu 0.8565 kirām ākum. ivai muṟṟum cintu veḷikkē uriya niṟaikaḷ ākum. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiraṇṭu piṟa nāṭṭu niṟaikaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭat takkaṉa āyiṉum, inniṟaikaḷ iyalpākavē ceyyappaṭṭaṉavē yām’, eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār. in niṟaik kaṟkaḷ aḷaviṟantaṉavāka iruttalaik kāṇiṉ, moheñco-tarō ciṟanta vāṇipap perukkamuṭaiya nakaramāka iruntatu eṉpatu aṅkaik kaṉipōl aiyamaṟa viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

tarācukaḷ

taṭṭukaḷ cempālum taṇṭu veṇkalattālum ceyyappaṭṭa tarācu oṉṟu moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaittatu. ataṉ tōṟṟam uyariyatāka illai. eṉiṉum, ataik koṇṭu annakarat tarācukaḷ eḷiyavai eṉak kūṟutal tavaṟu. niṟaikaḷai nōkka tarācukaḷ vaṉmaiyuṭaṉ iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu teḷivākum. nam nāṭṭu maṇṭik kaṭaikaḷilum viṟakuk kaṭaikaḷilum payaṉpaṭum periyatarācukaḷ pala akkālattil iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē karutukiṟār.

aḷavu kōl

akkālattil aḷavu kōlkaḷum iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. āṉāl, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟēṉum kiṭaittilatu. oru cippiyiṉ uṭainta pakutiyil cila aḷavuk kuṟikaḷ kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. appakuti nīḷappakuti oṉṟiṉ tuṇṭākum. atil iraṇṭu vita aḷavukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai tacāmca piṉṉattaik kuṟippaṉavākum. akkālattavar, icciṟpip pakutikaḷ mītu tacāmca piṉṉa aḷavukaḷaik kuṟippiṭṭu, appakutikaḷai ulōkat takaṭṭil poruttip patintirukkumāṟu ceytu, nīṭṭal aḷavaikkup payaṉpaṭuttiṉar pōlum! intat tacāmca piṉṉa aḷavaic cintu veḷi nākarika kālattilum ataṟkucciṟitu muṉṉarum ēlam, mecopoṭṭēmiyāvilum vaḻakkil iruntatu. eṉṉai? inta aḷavai paḻaiya elamaiṭ cāsaṉaṅkaḷilum, mecopoṭṭēmiyāvil uḷḷa jemṭeṭ nasr (Jemdet Nasr) eṉṉum iṭattiṟ kiṭaitta paṇṭaic cāsaṉaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭalām eṉka.[19]

muttirai patittal

cintu veḷi makkaḷ viṟpaṉaiyāṉa poruḷkaḷ mītum, poruḷkaḷ niṟainta tāḻikaḷ vāyppuṟattil uḷḷa mūṭi mītum pūṭṭappeṟṟa vīṭṭuk katavil iruntapūṭṭukaḷ mītum muttiraiyiṭṭu vantaṉar.[20] ivvaḻakkam iṉṉum iruntu varukiṟataṉṟō? ittakaiy muttiraikaḷum piṟa ilacciṉai patitta tāyittukaḷum piṟa paṇṭai nāṭukaḷil kaḷimaṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. āṉāl cintu veḷiyil avai cempālum kaḷimaṇṇālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta cuṭṭa kaḷimaṇ tuṇṭu oṉṟil, neṟṟi naṭuvē nīṇṭa oṟṟaik kompuṭaiya kutiraipōṉṟa nūtaṉa vilaṅkiṉ uruvam oru puṟam patintiruntatu. maṟupuṟam nāṇaṟkaṭṭu oṉṟu kayiṟukoṇṭu iṟukkik kaṭṭappaṭṭu iruppatu pōṉṟa uruvam patintuḷḷatu. ākavē, inta muttirai, nāṇalāl ceyyappaṭṭa peṭṭikaṭkō katavukaṭkō poruttum taṉic ciṟappuṭaiyatāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu tīkṣat karutukiṉṟār. ittakaiya nūtaṉa vilaṅku patikkappaṭṭa muttiraikaḷ pala, moheñcotrōvilum harappāvilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. cuṭṭa maṇṇālāṉa muttiraikaḷ cila kuṟippiṭṭa poruḷkaḷukku maṭṭumē muttiraiyiṭap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa.[21]

﻿13. viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷ - toḻilkaḷ - kalaikaḷ

palavakai viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷ

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta viḷaiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷil kōlikaḷē mikkuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, annakarac ciṟār kōli viḷaiyāṭṭil peru viruppam koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu tēṟṟam kaḷimaṇ pantukaḷ pala mātirikkākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟāl, ciṟār pantu viḷaiyāṭṭilum paṇpaṭṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu veḷiyākiṟatu. periyavarkaḷ caturaṅkam cokkaṭṭāṉ mutaliya āṭṭaṅkaḷai āṭivantaṉar. pāyccikaḷ ikkālattaṉa pōlavē puḷḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. caturaṅkam āṭutaṟkuriya kāykaḷ maṇṇālum uṟutiyāṉa kaṟkaḷālum aḻakuṟac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai pala niṟaṅkaḷaip peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟai vaittu āṭutaṟkuriya caturaṅkappalakaikaḷ marattāl ceyyappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum; nāḷaṭaivil avai aḻintu pōyiruttal vēṇṭum! appalkaikaḷil caturac cippikaḷ patittuk kaṭṭaṅkaḷ pirikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. iccaturac cippikaḷ pala innakara ārāycciyiṟ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam caturaṅka viḷaiyāṭṭiṟkuriya cātaṉaṅkaḷ pala ‘ur’ nakarattilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta oru ceṅkal mītu nīḷ caturak kaṭṭaṅkaḷ nāṉku orē varicaiyil iruppaṉa pōla mūṉṟu varicaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. akkal maṟṟoru vakaic caturaṅkappalakaiyākap payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām. ataṉ mītuḷḷa kaṭṭaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu kuṟukkuk kōṭukaḷuṭaṉ (ippoḻutu kāṇappaṭum ‘malai’ pōlak) kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. piṟitoru kalmītu tāya viḷaiyāṭṭukku uriya kōṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ittakaiya viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷ pala ur, ekiptu ākiya iṭaṅkaḷilum paḻakkattil iruntaṉa.

vēṭṭaiyāṭal

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta muttiraikaḷ cilavaṟṟil uḷḷa aṭaiyāṅkaḷālum aṅkuk kiṭaitta māṉ kompukaḷālum vēṭṭai nāyp patumaikaḷālum am mānakarattār vēṭṭaiyil viruppaṅ koṇṭaṉar eṉpatai aṟiyalām. oru muttiraiyil, oru marakkiḷaiyil iraṇṭu malaiyāṭukaḷiṉ talaikaḷ toṅkaviṭappaṭṭiruttalaik kāṇalām. malaiyāṭukaḷ kīrtarmalait toṭaril mikkirun taṉavātal vēṇṭum. vēṟoru muttiraiyil, oruvaṉ kaiyil villum ampum koṇṭu niṟpatu pōlavum, avaṉ arukē nāyoṉṟu iru vilaṅkiṉ vālaip paṟṟi iḻuppatu pōlavum cittiram cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cila muttiraikaḷil maṉitar māṉaiyum malaiyāṭṭaiyum vēṭṭaiyāṭuvatu pōlavum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ivvēṭṭaikku uriya cempu veṇkala ampu muṉaikaḷ akappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṟavaikaḷai vēṭṭai āṭuvōr kaḷimaṇ uruṇṭaikaḷaik kavaṇil vaittu eṟintamaikkuriya cāṉṟukaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

kōḻi keḷatāric caṇṭaikaḷ

oru muttiraiyil, iraṇṭu kāṭṭuk kōḻikaḷ caṇṭai iṭutal pōlap poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉāl, ikkālattil naṭaipeṟṟu varum māṭṭuccaṇṭai kōḻic caṇṭai mutaliyaṉa akkālattil naṭaipeṟṟirukkalām eṉṟu karututal takum. keḷatārikaḷai vaḷarppavaruṭaiya vīṭukaḷil iruntaṉavākac cila kaṇimaṇ kūṇṭukaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. vēṟoru maṇkūṇṭil keḷatāri nuḻaivatu kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ‘iṉṟu moheñco-tarōvaik kāṇa varum cilar, tām vaḷarkkum keḷatārikaḷaik kūṇṭukaḷil vaittukkoṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. tiruṣṭi tōṣātikaḷ tākkā tiruppataṟkāka eṉṟē akkūṇṭukaḷ nīlaniṟa maṇikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. contakkārar avaṟṟai aḻaippatum, avai avarkaṭku maṟu kural koṭuppatum moheñco-tarōvil irunta paḻaiya kavutāri vaḷarppai niṉaip pūṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.[1]

kottuvēlai

cintu veḷiyil vāḻnta kottarkaḷ tam toḻiliṟ paṇpaṭṭa pulamai uṭaiyavar āvar. avarkaḷ ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷaiyum cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷaiyum aṭukki, oḻuṅkāṉa cuvarkaḷaiyum vīṭukaḷaiyum māḷikaikaḷaiyum potu iṭaṅkaḷaiyum kōṭṭaikaḷaiyum appaṇṭaik kālattilēyē paṇpaṭa amaikkak kaṟṟiruntaṉar eṉpatu viyappūṭṭuñ ceytiyē ākum. avarkaḷ oru māḷikaiyil 2700 ce.mī. kūṭam amaittu, ataṉ mēl aṭukkait tāṅka 20 tuṇkaḷai naṉ muṟaiyil niṟuttiyuḷḷaṉar; vīṭṭut taraiyai vaḻavaḻappākki, atil vaṭṭak kōṭukaḷum caturak kōṭukaḷum vakaiyuṟa iḻuttuḷḷaṉar; maḻaiyil naṉaiyattakka vīṭṭuppuṟac cuvarkaḷaic cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷālum uṭpuṟaccuvarkaḷai ularnta ceṅkaṟkaḷālum kaṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. iḵtoṉṟē avarkaḷ aṟivaiyum aṉupavattaiyum naṉku viḷakka vallatu; kiṇaṟṟiṉ uṭpuṟattiṟkeṉṟē taṉippaṭṭa kaṟkaḷ ceytuḷḷamai viyattaṟku uriyatu. cuvarkaḷukkuḷḷum kaḻinīrk kuḻikaḷai amaittu vīṭu kaṭṭum āṟṟal peṟṟa apperumakkaḷai evvāṟu pukaḻa vallēm! vīṭu - kiṇaṟu - kuḷam paṟṟiya pakutiyil pala viyattaku uṇmaikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭamaiyāl, iṅku, avaṟṟai mīṭṭum kūṟal kūṟiyatu kūṟalā’kum. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, 5000 āṇṭu kaṭku muṉ irunta cintu veḷik kottarkaḷ, tam toḻil muṟaiyai aṟivu koṇṭu ceytu vantaṉar: toḻilai vayiṟṟukkākacceyyavillai; kalaikkākac ceytu vantaṉar eṉṉalām. ivvuṇmaiyai avarkaḷ kaṭṭiyuḷḷa nīrāṭum kuḷattiṉ nallamaippaik koṇṭum harappāvil kaṭṭiyuḷḷa mikapperiya kaḷañciya vēlaippāṭṭaik koṇṭum naṉkaṟiyalām. itaṉai mēlum virittal vēṇṭā.

maṭpāṇṭat toḻil

cintuveḷi nākarikattil ciṟappiṭam peṟattakkavai maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷē ākum. ākavē, avaṟṟaic ceyta vēṭkōvar tiṟamaiyē tiṟamai! avartam perumaiyē perumai! avarkaḷē avveḷiyil mutal iṭampeṟṟiruntaṉar eṉṉal mikaiyākātu. viyappūṭṭum vitavitavitamāṉa viḷaiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ, nuṇṇiya vēlaippāṭu koṇṭa mikac ciṟiya poruḷkaḷ, elumpukaḷaiyum cāmpalaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum putaittu aṭakkam ceyvataṟku amainta tāḻikaḷ,[2] palavakai oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟa pāṇṭaṅkaḷ, kaḻinīrk kuḻaikaḷ, oli perukkik kuḻaikaḷ, poruttamāṉa aḷavukaḷ amainta palavakaic ceṅkaṟkaḷ, vīṭṭu vēlaikaṭku uriya palvakaip poruḷkaḷ iṉṉa piṟavum vēṇṭiyōr vēṇṭiyavāṟu ceytu tanta vēṭkōvar pērāṟṟalai eṉṉeṉpatu!

moheñco-tarōvil vāḻnta vēṭkōvarai viṭa harappā vēṭkōvar piṉṉum uyarntavarāka iruttal kūṭumō eṉṟu aṟiñar aiyuṟukiṉṟaṉar. aṅku or iṭattil 16 kāḷavāykaḷ kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. avai aḷaviṟ ciṟiyavai: āṉāl, viraivil cūṭēṟattakka muṟaiyil amaikkappaṭṭavai: kaḷimaṇ ceppukaḷ, patumaikaḷ, maṇikaḷ nakaikaḷ ākiya vaṟṟaic cūḷaiyiṭavum, kal vaḷaiyalkaḷ, paṭṭukkalkaḷimaṇ muttiraikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaic cuṭṭu merukiṭvum, civappu maṇikaḷaic ceyyavum payaṉpaṭṭavai. vēṇṭiya aḷavu veppattaiyum kuḷircciyaiyum ūṭṭi, vitavitamāṉa niṟaṅkaḷai ēṟṟutaṟku vacatiyāka iruntavai. moheñco-tarōvil oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap peṟāta maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ vēlaippāṭu kaṇkavar vaṉappuṭaiyatu. 1 ce. mī. uyarattil aḻakiya patumaikaḷ ceyyak kaṟṟirunta avvēṭkōvar tiṟamaiyai paṇpaṭṭa kalaiyaṟivai iṉṟu eṇṇi eṇṇi viyavāta aṟiñar illai!

kaltaccar toḻil

cintu veḷiyiṟ kaṟ caṭṭikaḷ, nīr parukum paccaik kal ēṉaṅkaḷ, ammikaḷ, kuḻavikaḷ, uralkaḷ, entiraṅkaḷ, āṭṭukkaṟkaḷ, tūṇkaḷait tāṅkum kuḻiyamainta kaṟkaḷ, ciṟṟurukkaḷ, liṅkaṅkaḷ, yōṉikaḷ, muttiraikaḷ iṉṉa piṟavum ceyyappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟaik koṇṭu, cintu veḷiyil kal taccarkaḷ iruntaṉar eṉpatum kal vēlai naṭaipeṟṟatu eṉpatum naṉkaṟiyalām. harappāvil aḷavaṟṟa liṅkaṅkaḷ pala vaṭiviṉavākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. maṉita urukkaḷ (cilaikaḷ) ceyyappaṭṭaṭuḷḷaṉa. avvēlaippāṭu viyakkat takkatāka uḷḷateṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟi viyantuḷḷaṉar.

marattaccar toḻil

marapporuḷkaḷ viraivil aḻiyum iyalpiṉa. ātaliṉ, cintu veḷiyil iṉṟu kiṭaittila eṉiṉum, aṅku marattāl iyaṉṟa kaṭṭilkaḷ, nāṟkālikaḷ, mēcaikaḷ, mukkālikaḷ, pīṭaṅkaḷ, maṉaikaḷ, katavukaḷ, peṭṭikaḷ, tūṇkaḷ, palavakaik karuvikaḷiṉ kaippiṭikaḷ, viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷ mutaliyaṉa iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpataṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. erutiṉ kālkaḷaip pōlac ceyyappaṭṭa kālkaḷaik koṇṭamarattāl āṉa mukkālippīṭaṅkaḷum erumaik kālkaḷ vaitta cāy maṉaikaḷum iruntaṉa eṉpatai muttiraikaḷaik koṇṭu aṟiyalām. ammarattaccarkaḷ kāṭṭiya paḻakkattiliruntē, ciṅkak kālikaḷaik koṇṭa pīṭaṅkaḷ piṟkālattil tōṟṟameṭuttaṉa pōlum!

kaṉṉāra vēlai

cempu, veṇkalakkaruvikaḷum piṟaporuḷkaḷum nirampak kiṭaittamai koṇṭu, cintu veḷiyil kaṉṉāra vēlaiyum ciṟappāka naṭaipeṟṟamai aṟiyalām. ikkāla aṟiñarkaḷ peru viyappukkoḷḷumpaṭi urukki vārkkappaṭṭa ciṟanta vēlaippaṭāmainta veṇkala (naṭaṉamātiṉ) uruvaccilai oṉṟē akkaṉṉārap perumakkaḷiṉ kaittoḻiṟ ciṟappai uṇarttap pōtumāṉatu! melliya takaṭukaḷāka amaintuḷḷa īṭṭikaḷum, ciṟiya aḻakiya kūrmaiyāṉa paṟkaḷaik koṇṭa irampaṅkaḷum, kōṭarikaḷum, arivāḷ maṇaikaḷum. iṭai vāḷkaḷum, nīṇṭa vāḷkaḷum, ampukaḷum piṟavum avar tam toḻiṟ pulamaiyait teṟṟeṉat terivippaṉavākum. mikap palavāka-vakaivakaiyākak kiṭaitta maḻittaṟ kattikaḷ, maḻittal takaṭukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟaik kāṇum ikkāla ārāycci aṟiñar, akkālak kaṉṉāratu tiṟamaiyai eṇṇip peruviyappuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar eṉiṉ, avartam paṇpaṭṭa toḻil muṟaiyai eṉṉeṉap pukaḻvatu!

araṇmaṉaiyuḷ kāḷavāykaḷ

moheñco-tarōvil araṇmaṉai eṉṟu karutappaṭṭa periya kaṭṭiṭattiṉ tiṟantaveḷiyil ciṟiyavaṭivil cila kāḷavāykaḷ akappaṭṭaṉa. avai aracāṅkattāruṭaiya paṭaikkalaṅkaḷaic ceyyavum, paḻutu pārkkavum payaṉpaṭṭa kāḷavāykaḷāka irukkalām. avai vaṭṭamāka amaintavai. - ittakaiya kāḷavāykaḷ ‘kiṣ’ nakarattu araṇmaṉaiyuḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. avai nīḷ caturamāka amaintavai. avaṟṟiṉ kālam ki.mu.2800 ākum.[3]

poṟkollar toḻil

cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭaittuḷḷa poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikaḷil amaintuḷḷa vēlaippāṭu ciṟappuṭaiyatākum. kāppukaḷ, vaḷaiyalkaḷ, aṭṭikaikaḷ, kaḻuttu mālaikaḷ, am mālaikaḷil kōkkappaṭṭa palavakaip poṉmaṇikaḷ, pū vēlaippāṭu amainta maṇikaḷ, pala uruvaṅkoṇṭamaṇikaḷ, nuṇṇiya vēlaippāṭamainta talaic cāmāṉkaḷ, cuṭṭikaḷ, kātaṇikaḷ, kaṭakaṅkaḷ, mōtiraṅkaḷum, iṉṉapiṟavum akkālap poṟkollar vēlaippāṭṭai viḷakkuvaṉa ākum. ivvaṇikaḷil iḻaippu vēlaippāṭu ciṟantu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. moheñco-tarōvil kiṭaittavaṟṟaiviṭa harappāvilēyē poṉ aṇikaḷ mikutiyākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.

irattiṉakkal cōtaṉai

aṇikaḷ ceytaṟkamainta pala vakai irattiṉak kaṟkaḷaiyum, vēṟu pala niṟak kaṟkaḷaiyum cōtippataṟkeṉṟē palar iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. eṉṉai? ‘navarattiṉaṅkaḷ’ eṉṟu collappaṭum maṇikaḷ aṉaittum cintuveḷi makkaḷāl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa: paṇpaṭṭa vēlaippāṭu koṇṭuḷḷaṉa; nīlakiri mutaliya neṭun tolaivil uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷiliruntum kaṟkaḷ koṇṭu cellappaṭṭaṉa eṉṉum vivaraṅkaḷāl eṉka.

cetukku vēlai

cintu veḷiyil ituvarai 1000kku mēṟpaṭṭa palavakai muttiraikaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. muttiraiyiṉ oru puṟam ētēṉum or uruvamum cittira eḻuttukaḷum maṟupuṟam vēṟu kuṟiyīṭukaḷ palavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai ceyyappaṭṭu 5000 āṇṭu kaṭku mēlākiyum, iṉṟum puttam putiyaṉavākak kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟaṉa eṉiṉ, ivaṟṟaic ceytavar toḻiṟ pulamaiyai eṉṉeṉpatu? cempu nāṇayaṅkaḷ mītum muttiraikaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa[4]; eḻuttukkaḷum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. immuttiraikaḷil kāṇappaṭum kuṟippukaḷaikkoṇṭē appaṇṭai makkaḷ moḻiyaiyum eḻuttukkaḷaiyum, camayac ceytikaḷaiyum, ōviyam, ciṟpam, cetukku vēlai iṉṉa piṟavaṟṟaiyum aṟiya iṭam ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉiṉ, ivaṟṟait tayāritta pēraṟiñar nam pōṟṟutaṟkuriyarē yāvar allarō? pala muttiraikaḷ veṇkal koṇṭu ceytu cūḷaiyiṭappaṭṭu merukiṭappaṭṭavai. ivai 1 ce. mī. mutal 6 ce. mī. varai caturamāka uḷḷaṉa. cila 2 ce. mī. mutal 3 ce.mī. varai caturamāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. cittiraṅkaḷ maṭṭumē koṇṭavai cila; cittiraṅkaḷōṭu eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷ koṇṭavai pala. ivai aṉaittum irampam koṇṭu oḻuṅkāka aṟukkappaṭṭavai; ciṟṟuḷiyālō - kattiyālō oḻuṅkupeṟac cetukkappaṭṭavai; piṉṉarē merukiṭappaṭṭavai. ivai ivvāṟu tayārikkap paṭṭa piṟakē, ivaṟṟiṉmītu uruvaṅkaḷum eḻuttukkaḷum cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. piṉṉar ivai, ētō oruvkaip poruḷ mēlē pūcappaṭṭuc cuṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.appaṇṭaikkālattil ittuṇai muṟaikaḷum oru muttirai ceyyak kaiyāḷappaṭṭaṉa eṉiṉ, muttirait toḻil īṭupaṭṭirunta māntar tam toḻil aṟivaiyum kalaiyuṇarvaiyum eṉṉeṉpatu!

caṅkut toḻil

akkālattavar caṅkaikkoṇṭu palavakai nakaikaḷaiyum, nakaikaḷiṟpatikkum ciṟu patakkaṅkaḷaiyum piṟa poruḷkaḷaiyum ceytukoṇṭaṉar caṅkai aṟuttu, aram cetukkum karuvi mutaliyavaṟṟaikkoṇṭu palcakkaram, caturattuṇṭu, mukkōṇat tuṇṭu. irutayam pōṉṟa vaṭivaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟaic ceytaṉar; ivaṟṟaip poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu ivaṟṟāl āṉa nakaikaḷiṟ patittu vantaṉar. ivvāṟu ceyyappaṭṭu vanta vēlai viyappūṭṭuvatēyākum.

mīṉ piṭittal

moheñco-tarō nakaram cintu yāṟṟiṉ karai mītē irunta nakaramātalālum, harappā rāviyāṟṟiṉ aṇmaiyil iruntatātalālum, avveḷikaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷ mīṉ piṭikkun toḻilaiyum ciṟappāka mēṟkoṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpatu tavaṟākātu.aṅku mīṉ piṭikkap payaṉpaṭṭacempu-veṇkalat tūṇṭilkaḷ pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. mīṉ piṭikkum valaikaḷiṉ oraṅkaḷiṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa maṇ uruṇṭaikaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ‘moheñco-tarō makkaḷ mīṉ uṇavaip peritum payaṉ paṭuttiṉar eṉṟu kūṟalām’ eṉṟu makkē pōṉṟa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvatiliruntu, mīṉ piṭikkun toḻil akkālattil ciṟappuṭaiyatākak karutappaṭṭu vantatu eṉpatu veḷiyām.[5]

vaṇṭi oṭṭutal .

cintu veḷiyil perumpālum māṭṭu vaṇṭikaḷē mikkiruntaṉa. avaṟṟai oṭṭuvataṟkeṉṟē vaṇṭikkārar palar iruntaṉar. ‘cāṭṭai’ iruntatu. vaṇṭi kūṇṭu uṭaiyatu. ikkuṟippukaḷai uṇarttum viḷaiyāṭṭu maṇ vaṇṭi oṉṟu harappāvil kiṭaittatu. harappāvil kiṭaitta viḷaiyāṭṭu vaṇṭi cempāṟceyyappaṭṭatu. vaṇṭi oṭṭiyiṉ mukam vaṭṭamāṉatu: mūkkut taṭṭaiyākavum oraḷavu uyarntum irukkiṟatu; kūntal civip piṉpuṟam muṭiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cintuveḷi nākarikattu vaṇṭikaḷē ulakiṟ paḻaimaiyāṉavai eṉṉalām. cintu veḷi nākarika kālattiṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaṭkup piṉṉarē ekiptil vaṇṭikaḷ tōṉṟalāyiṉa. ṭākṭar makkē cāṉhu-tarōvil iraṇṭu maṇ vaṇṭikaḷaik kaṇṭeṭuttār. oṉṟu naṉṉilaiyil iruntatu; maṟṟatu cakkaraṅkaḷ illāmal iruntatu.

harappāviṟ kiṭaitta vaṇṭikaḷ (1) iṉṟaiya vaṭa intiya vaṇṭikaḷaip pōluḷḷa catura vaṇṭikaḷ, (2) mōṭṭār lāriyaip pōṉṟa cāmāṉ ēṟṟic cellum vaṇṭikaḷ, (3) ik kālattuc cāraṭṭu vaṇṭiyaip pōla etiretir ācaṉaṅkaḷaik koṇṭa vaṇṭikaḷ, (4) paṭaku pōṉṟa vaṇṭikaḷ eṉa nāl vakaippaṭum. ivaiyaṉṟi iraṇṭu uruḷaikaḷaik koṇṭa irataṅkaḷ eṉpaṉavum iruntaṉa. ivai marattāl ceyyap paṭṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum. ittakaiya viḷaiyāṭṭu vaṇṭikaḷ iṉṉum pañcāppil hoṣiyarp pūr eṉṉum iṭattiṟ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.[6]

nāvitat toḻil

kaṇakkaṟṟa cuvarak kattikaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷamaiyāl cintu veḷi makkaḷ kṣavarattil mikka akkaṟaikoṇṭu iruntaṉar eṉpatum, attoḻilāḷar palar iruntaṉarātal vēṇṭum eṉpatum kūṟāmalē viḷaṅkum ceytikaḷām.

tōṭṭi vēlai

‘nakara cukātārattaik kavaṉitta perumai, appaṇṭaikkāla nākarika nakaraṅkaḷ aṉaittaiyum viṭa moheñco - tarōvukkē uriyatākum’ eṉṟu sar jāṉ mārṣal kūṟutalāl, annakarāṇmaik kaḻakattiṉar ṉar tōṭṭikaḷai vaittiruntaṉar eṉpatai naṉku uṇaralām.

kāval toḻil

periya māḷikaikaḷiṉ muṉpuṟa aṟaikaḷum teruk kōṭikaḷil irunta aṟaikaḷum kāvaṟkārarkku uriyavai eṉpatu muṉṉark kūṟappaṭṭa taṉṟō? moheñco-tarō, harappā eṉṉum periya nakaraṅkaḷ vāṇipap perukkuṭaiyaṉa - ātaliṉ, avaṟṟaikkākka vēṇṭiya poṟuppu annakarak kaḻakat tāraiyō-aracaraiyō cārntatākum. avarkaḷāl amarttap paṭṭa pātukāvalar palar nakaraṅkaḷaip pātukāttu vantaṉar.[7]

kappal toḻil

cintu veḷi makkaḷ āṟṟu vāṇipattilum kaṭal vāṇipattilum ciṟantiruntaṉar. ātaliṉ, āṟukaḷukku vēṇṭiya paṭakukaḷaiyum kaṭaṟ celavukkuriya kappalkaḷaiyum kaṭṭat terintiruntaṉarātal vēṇṭum. moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta muttirai oṉṟil paṭakiṉ uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. atiṟ pāymaram illai. cukkāṉum, paṭakiṉ naṭuvil or aṟaiyum, mīkāmaṉ irukka iṭamum uḷḷaṉa. appaṭakiṉ amaippaik koṇṭu, akkālap paṭakukaḷai nām oruvāṟu aṟintukoḷḷalām. avai nāṇaṟkōlkaḷ koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam ceyyappaṭṭa paṭakukaḷ paṇṭai ekiptilum iruntirukkiṉṟaṉa. maṭpāṇṭa oṭṭil tīṭṭappaṭṭirunta oviyam oṉṟu paṭakiṉum vēṟupaṭṭatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉ irupuṟaṅkaḷum vaḷaintu uyarntu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. naṭuvilaṟaiyē illai; 180 ce.mī. uyarattil pāy maram irukkiṟatu. cukkāṉum uḷatu. appaṭaku āṟṟilum kaṭalilum payaṉpaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum.[8] eṉavē, cintu veḷi makkaḷ paṭakukaḷaik kaṭṭavum vēṟu vakaik kappalkaḷaik kaṭṭavum aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatu teḷivām.

payirt toḻil

nel, kōtumai, vāṟkōtumai, eḷ, parutti mutaliyaṉa payiriṭappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē, uḻavu ciṟappuṟṟa toḻilākavē iruntuvanta teṉṉalām. vayalkaṭku vēṇṭiya nīraic cintu yāṟē tantu vantatu. ammakkaḷ cintu yāṟṟunīraik kālvāykaḷ mūlam koṇṭu ceṉṟu vayalkaḷiṟ pāyac ceytiruttal vēṇṭum. kalappaikkārarkaḷum arivāḷkaḷum kiṭaittamai koṇṭum ciṟitu kōtumai iruntataik koṇṭum payirttoḻil ciṟappuṭaiya toḻilākak karutappaṭṭateṉpatu veḷiyākum.

necavut toḻil

moheñco-tarōvil uḷḷa vīṭukaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ necavut toḻil naṭantu vantatu eṉpatai aṟivippaṉ pōlak ‘katirkaḷ’ pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai paḷiṅkālum caṅkiṉālum kaḷi maṇṇālum ceyyappaṭṭavai. eṉavē, celvar mutal vaṟiyar īṟāka iruntavar aṉaivarum nūl nūṟṟalil īṭupaṭṭiruntaṉar eṉa oruvāṟu kūṟalām. cintu veḷiyiṟ paruttiyē mikutiyākap payaṉpaṭṭatu. kampaḷa āṭaikaḷ ‘iruntila’ eṉṟu cilarum, iruntirukkalām eṉṟu cilarum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. nāriṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa āṭaikaḷ piṟa iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu taruvikkappaṭṭaṉa. ‘kittāṉ’ pōṉṟa taṭitta tuṇiyum payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭatu. moheñco-tarōvil 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ irunta pañcu kiṭaittuḷḷatu. aḵtu iṉṟaiya intiyap pañcaippōlavē irukkiṟatu. ippañcaittāṉ pāpilōṉiyar cintu[9] eṉṟum, kirēkkar ciṉṭaṉ eṉṟum vaḻaṅki vantaṉar. celvarkaḷ cittira vēlaippāṭamainta āṭaikaḷai aṇintu vantaṉar; pūt taiyalkaḷaik koṇṭa mēlāṭaikaḷaip pōrttu vantaṉar eṉpataic cila vaṭivaṅkaḷaik koṇṭē kūṟalām.

taiyalum piṉṉalum

ivai iraṇṭum aṅku iruntaṉa eṉpatu mēlē kūṟappaṭṭatu. ivvēlaikaṭku uriya ūcikaḷ pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai ceppu, veṇkalam, elumpu, tantam, taṅkam ivaṟṟāl ceyyappaṭṭavai. avaṟṟuḷ cila nūl kōttut taippataṟku utavum ūcikaḷ; cila pūt taiyalkaḷ pōṭavum, cittiraṅkaḷ piṉṉavum payaṉpaṭṭavaiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

tanta vēlai

moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta poruḷkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu yāṉaik kōṭukaḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavai. or aḻakiya pāttirattiṉ aṭippuṟattiṟkō aṉṟi mēṟpuṟattiṟkō payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa tantat takaṭu oṉṟu kiṭaittuḷḷatu. atu cila aḻakiya vēlaippāṭu kaḷaik koṇṭuḷḷatu. tantap pāttiraṅkaḷ iruntirukkalām; periya pāttiraṅkaḷai aḻaku ceyyat tantat takaṭukaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti irukkalām: tanta ūcikaḷ, tantak katirkaḷ mutaliyaṉa kiṭaittuḷḷataik koṇṭum muttiraikaḷ palavaṟṟil kōṭuḷḷa yāṉaikaḷ kāṇappaṭalaikkoṇṭum, cintuveḷiyil yāṉaikaḷ putiyaṉa alla eṉpatum tantat toḻil naṭaipeṟṟu irukkalām eṉṟum kūṟalām,[10] tantap poruḷkaḷ cila cumēriyāviṟ kāṇappaṭu talaikkoṇṭu. avai cintu veḷiyiliruntē pōyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvatāl, tanta vāṇipamum naṭaipeṟṟirukkalām eṉṟu karuta iṭam uṇṭu.

maṇi ceyyun toḻil

paṟpala iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu uyartarak kaṟkaḷai varavaḻaittu, avaṟṟai aṟuttup paṟpalavita maṇikaḷākki merukiṭṭuc cevvaippaṭuttum toḻil vallār palar iruntaṉar eṉpatu, cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭaittuḷḷa aḷaviṟanta maṇikaḷaik koṇṭu eḷitil aṟiyalām. ammaṇikaḷil orē aḷavuḷḷa vaḻavaḻap pāṉa tuḷaikaḷ kiṭaintuḷḷataik kaṇṭu ārāycciyāḷar viyappuṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉiṉ, attoḻil vallār vaittirunta nuṇṇiya karuvikaḷaiyum avartam āṟṟalaiyum eṉṉeṉap pārāṭṭuvōm! curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, avvēlai muṟaiyē iṉṟuḷḷa maṇi vēlaikaṭkup piṟappiṭam eṉṉal mikaiyākātu.

pāy piṉṉutal

māṭaṅ kaṭṭiyavar payaṉpaṭuttiya kōraip pāy oṉṟu paḻutuṟṟa nilaiyiṟ kiṭaittatu. cintu āṟṟup paṭukaiyil kōraiyum nāṇalum iyalpākak kiṭaittamaiyiṉ, cintu veḷi makkaḷ avaṟṟaik koṇṭu pāykaḷaip piṉṉik koṇṭaṉar; kūraikaḷai amaittuk koṇṭaṉar. eṉavē, pāy piṉṉun toḻil aṅku iruntatu eṉpatu tōṟṟam.

eḻutak kaṟṟavar

cintu veḷi makkaḷ eḻutak kaṟṟavar āvar. itaṉai, avarkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa cempu naḷṇayaṅkaḷālum 1000kku mēṟpaṭṭa muttiraikaḷālum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa kuṟiyīṭukaḷālum naṉkuṇaralām. avarkaḷ, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu eḻuta oruvakai nāṇaṟ pēṉāvaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. attakaiya pēṉākkaḷaik kirīṭ tīviṉar payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avai nākarikam mikunta ekiptilum urōmarkaḷ kālattukku muṉ iruntatillai. eḻutap payaṉpaṭṭa civanta kaḷimaṇ palakaikaḷ cila kiṭaittaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ nīḷam 10 ce.mī. akalam 8 ce.mī, kaṉam 180 ce. mī. avaṟṟiṉ mītu oruvakaip poruḷ taṭavappaṭṭu, apporuḷmītu eḻutappaṭṭirukkalām eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu.[11] kaṇakkaṟṟa muttiraikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum cittira eḻuttukaḷaik kāṉiṉ, cintu veḷi makkaḷatu eḻuttu muṟaiyai naṉku aṟiyalām. avveḻuttukaḷ iṉṟu vacikkappaṭavillai. āyiṉum, ‘avaiyē intiya mutal eḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟu itukāṟuṅ karutappaṭṭa pirāmi eḻuttukaṭkē piṟappiṭamākum’ eṉṟu lāṅṭaṉ (Langdon) haṉṭṭar pōṉṟa moḻillunar aṟaintuḷḷaṉar. avveḻuttukaḷai ārāyntu pākupaṭutti haṉṭṭar eṉpavar ēṟakkuṟaiya 250 pakkaṅkaḷ koṇṭa perunūl[12] oṉṟai 1934 il veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. hīrās eṉṉum pātiriyār oruvarum ittakaiya ārāyccip paṇiyil iṟaṅkiyuḷḷār.avarum viraivil nūl oṉṟai veḷiyiṭuvār. iṅṅaṉam aṟiñar palar kavaṉattaiyum īrttuḷḷa aṟputa eḻuttukkaḷaip paṇṭaic cintup piratēca makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar.

vāṇipat toḻil

cintu makkaḷ toḻilkaḷiṟ ciṟantiruntavar eṉpataṟku avarkaḷatu vāṇipamē ciṟanta cāṉṟu pakarum, avarkaḷ ēṟakkuṟaiya 4800 ki.mī. tolaivil naṭu āciyāviliruntu ekiptu varai vāṇipam naṭatti vantaṉar. ikkālap pukai vaṇṭiyō, vāṉa ūrtiyō, nīrāvik kappalō, mōṭṭār lārikaḷō illāta 5000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ avarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ paṇṭap potikaḷai erutukaḷ mītu ēṟṟikkoṇṭu ceṉṟum, kappalkaḷ mūlam karaiyōramākavē ceṉṟum kaṭal vāṇipam - ākiya iraṇṭaiyum veṟṟi peṟa naṭatti vantaṉar eṉpatu muṟpakutiyil viḷakkamuṟak kūṟappaṭṭatu. avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ nāṭṭup poruḷkaḷaiyum kalaiyuṇarvaiyum piṟa nāṭṭiṉarkkuk koṭuttu, avar tam poruḷkaḷaiyum kalaiyuṇarvaiyum peṟṟu, vāṇipattaiyum kalaiyuṇarvaiyum vaḷarttu vantaṉar. ‘tiraikaṭal oṭiyum tiraviyam tēṭu’ eṉpataṟku apperumakkaḷē cāṉṟāvar. avarkaḷ intiyāviṉ pala pakutiyiliruntum tamakku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷaip peṟṟu uḷnāṭṭu vāṇipattaiyum vaḷarttu vantaṉar.

ciṟpak kalai

cintu veḷi makkaḷ ciṟpak kalaiyait tōṟṟuvittavar eṉṉal tavaṟākātu. moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaitta cuṇṇāmpukkal koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭirunta cilai oṉṟu aṟiñar kavaṉattai īrttatu. avvuruvam yōkiyiṉ uruvam eṉṟum, camayat talaivar uruvam eṉṟum aṟiñar palavāṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. avvuruvam cittirat taiyalaikkoṇṭa pōrvai oṉṟaip pōrttuḷḷatu; mīcaiyiṉṟit tāṭiyai maṭṭum vaittuḷḷatu; attāṭi oḻuṅkupeṟa amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. talaimayir oḻuṅkākac cīvappeṟṟu, vaṭṭap patakkattuṭaṉ kūṭiya talaiyaṇiyāl aḻuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu.inta amaippu muṟai kaṇkavar vaṉappiṉatu.

harappāvil kiṭaittuḷḷa kaṟcilaikaḷ cintu veḷi makkaḷiṉ ciṟpakkalai aṟivait tiṟampaṭak kāṭṭuvaṉavām. avai viyakkattakka muṟaiyil ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaikaḷ taṉiyē ceytu iṇaippataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu accilaikaḷil tuḷaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa; aṅṅaṉamē talaikaḷum iṇaikkappaṭum pōlum kaṟkaḷil iṇaippu vēlaiyaic ceyyat tuṇinta ammakkaḷiṉ kalaiyaṟivai eṉṉeṉpatu! ivvāṭavar cilaikaḷ maṇaṟ kallālum paḻuppu niṟak kallālum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ikkaṟkaḷ cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭaippaṉa alla; veḷi iṭaṅkaḷiliruntē koṇṭuvarap paṭṭavai. ikkaṟkaḷaip piṟkāla intiyar iṉṟaḷavum cilaikaṭkup payaṉpaṭutta aṟintilar eṉpatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.

ciṟpak kalai kirēkkariṭamiruntutāṉ intiyāviṟku vantatu eṉṟu itukāṟum kūṟi vantatu iṉṟu tavaṟākiviṭṭatu. avartam kalai tōṉṟaṟku muṉṉarē cintuveḷi makkaḷ ciṟpak kalaiyait tōṟṟuvittaṉarātal vēṇṭum. ‘intiyac ciṟpak kalai intiyāviṟkē uriyatu’ eṉṟu carjāṉ mārṣal kūṟiyuḷḷatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.[13]

veṇkalattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa naṭaṉamātiṉ uruvam oṉṟum cintuveḷi makkaḷiṉ ciṟpa aṟivaic ciṟappuṟak kāṭṭuvatākum. ataṉaippaṟṟi viyantu eḻutāta aṟiñar ilar eṉiṉ, ataṉ vēlaippāṭṭuc ciṟappai eṉṉeṉpatu! veṇkala vārppaṭaṅ koṇṭu ceyyappaṭṭa avvuruvam vaḻavaḻappākavum oḻuṅkākavum amaintuḷḷatu viyappukku uriyatēyākum. cila uruvac cilaikaḷ naṭaṉam ceyvatṟku ēṟṟavāṟu niṟpaṉapōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ovvōr uruvamum iṭatu kālait tūkki valatu kālmītu niṉṟu naṭaṉam ceyvatu pōla amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iccilaikaḷ, civaperumāṉatu piṟkāla naṭaṉattiṟku tōṟṟuvāy ākalām eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttu.

cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭaitta vilaṅkup paṭivaṅkaḷaik koṇṭum ammakkaḷatu ciṟpa aṟivai uṇaralām; oru kuraṅku taṉ kuṭṭiyuṭaṉ uṭkārntiruppatu pōlac ceyyappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. vēṟu oru kuraṅku taṉmuḻaṅkālkaḷ mītu kaikaḷai ūṉṟikkoṇṭu uṭkārntiruppatu pōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. iṅṅaṉam ceyyappaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷil kaṟkaḷum cippikaḷum kaṇkaḷāka vaittuppatikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. immuṟai ekiptilum cumēriyāvilum kaiyāḷappaṭṭatu. veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭa erumai, kuraṅku, veḷḷāṭu eṉpaṉa pārkkat takkavai; ciṟanta muṟaiyil amaintuḷḷavai.

ōviyak kalai

cintu veḷi makkaḷiṉ oviyat tiṟamai avar tam maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiliruntē aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. aṅkuk kiṭaittuḷḷa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa oviyaṅkaḷ mikat teḷivāka akkāla nilaiyaiyum nākarikattaiyum uṇartta vallavaiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. avai varalāṟṟuk kālattu oviyaṅkaṭku muṟpaṭṭa ōviyak kalaiyai aḷaviṭṭu aṟiyap pērutavi purikiṉṟaṉa. oru vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ pala vaṭṭaṅkaḷ iṭṭuḷḷa muṟai pōṟṟutaṟkuriyatu. pala pāṇṭaṅkaḷ mītu maraṅkaḷ, ilaikaḷ, ilaikkottukaḷ, catūkkaṭṭaṅkaḷ, kuṟukkum neṭukkumāka uḷḷa mukkōṇaṅkaḷ, paṟavaikaḷ, pāmpukaḷ, mīṉkaḷ, kāṭṭu erumaikaḷ, malai āṭukaḷ,mayilkaḷ, māṉkaḷ, kappalkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ oviyaṅkaḷākat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

toṭarntu varumtoṉmai nākarikam

ivaiyaṉṟi, mikka vēlaippāṭukaḷ aṟṟa cilaikaḷum oviyaṅkaḷum pala kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai cintu kaṅkai veḷikaḷilum paravi irukkiṉṟaṉa. ātaliṉ, cintu veḷip paṇṭai nākarikam kaṅkai veḷiyilum paravi iruntamai naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu. mēlum appaṇṭaik kālac ciṟpak kalaiyum ōviyak kalaiyum varalāṟṟukkālam varai toṭarntu kaik koḷḷappaṭṭu vantiruntamai veḷiyākiṉṟatu. pāṭalipurattil akappaṭṭa mōriyar kālattu maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum cintu veḷiyiṉ maṭpāṇṭat toṭarcciyākavē karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cintu veḷi nākarika kālattiṟkum varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟkum - iṭaiyil pala āyiram āṇṭukaḷ kaḻintirunta pōtilum vāḻkkaiyil pala māṟutalkaḷ uṇṭāki iruppiṉum oviyak kalaiyum ciṟpak kalaiyum varalāṟṟuk kālamvarai toṭarntē vantuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟāl, intiyac ciṟpak kalaikkum oviyak kalaikkum cintu veḷiyē tāyakamāṉatu eṉpatu teḷivākum aṉṟō?

icaiyum naṭaṉamum

kaḷimaṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa āṇ uruvam oṉṟaṉ kaḻuttil ‘tavul’ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. veṇkalattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa naṭaṉamātiṉ vaṭivam oṉṟu kiṭaittuḷḷatu. cila kaṟcilaikaḷ naṭaṉamuṟaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭu muttiraikaḷil ‘mirutaṅkam’ pōṉṟa tōṟ karuvikaḷ poṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ikkālattu ‘vīṇai’ pōla oviyam oṉṟu tīṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ikkālattup pākavatarkaḷ kaikaḷil vaittuk koṇṭu tāḷamiṭum karuvikaḷ pōṉṟavaiyum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ivai aṉaittaiyum nōkka, cintu veḷi makkaḷ icaik kalaiyilum naṭaṉak kalaiyilum ōraḷavu payiṟci uṭaiyavar eṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkukiṟatu.

kaṇitap pulamai

cintu veḷiyiṟ kiṭaittuḷḷa niṟaik kaṟkaḷ mikappala. avai perumpālum ellā vīṭukaḷilum payaṉpaṭṭaṉa eṉak kūṟalām. niṟaik kaṟkaḷaip paṟṟiya viḷakkam ceṉṟa pakutiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭamaiyiṉ īṇṭu virittal vēṇṭā. niṟaiyiṉ mikac ciṟiya aḷavu tacāmca piṉṉattiṟ celkiṉṟatu; pēraḷavu 15 pavuṇṭ varai celkiṉṟatu eṉiṉ, akkāla makkaḷatu kaṇitap pulamai teṟṟeṉat terikiṟataṉṟō? niṟaikaḷ 64 varai iraṭṭai eṇkaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. piṉṉa aḷavaik kuṟikkum niṟaikaḷ oru kirāmiṉ 0.8565 aḷavu varai pōkiṉṟaṉa eṉiṉ ammakkaḷatu. kaṇitak kalai vaḷarcciyai eṉṉeṉṟu kūṟi viyappatu. ‘inniṟaik kaṟkaḷ mecopoṭṭēmiyā pōṉṟa akkāla nākarika nāṭukaḷil irunta niṟaik kaṟkaḷai viṭa eṭaiyil cariyāṉavaiyākavum aḷavil oḻuṅkāṉavaiyākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟaintu viyakkiṉṟaṉar.

maruttuvak kalai

iṉṟu intiyā muḻutilum āyurvēta yūṉāṉi maruttuvap pulavar payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟa ‘cilā cittu’ eṉṉum maruntu moheñco-tarōviṟ kiṭaittuḷḷatu. cintu veḷiyiṉar, campar (Sambur) māṉ kompukaḷaip poṭiyākki maruntākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. māṉ kompukaḷum, māṭṭuk kompukaḷum pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. māṭṭukkompukaḷ cila kiṇṇam pōlak kuṭaiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai maruntu vaittuk koḷḷap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa ākum. nāṭṭu maruttuvattil ‘cilā cittu’ uyartara maruntākum. atu vayiṟṟil uṇṭākum kuṭal, nuraiyīral mutaliyaṉa paṟṟiya nōykaḷaik kuṇappaṭuttum āṟṟal peṟṟatu. atu, imayamalaip pāṟaikaḷiliruntu kacintu varuvatu. ataṉai malai vāḻnar koṇṭuvantu iṉṟum uḷ nāṭukaḷil koṭukkiṉṟaṉar. cintu veḷiyilēyē moheñco-tarōvai otta nākarikanakaramāka irunta ‘otmañco-puti’ eṉṉum iṭattil maruntukkuriya oruvakai elumpukaḷ cila kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai kātu, kaṇ, toṇṭai tōl paṟṟiya nōykaḷaik kuṇappaṭuttum vaṉmaiyuṭaiyaṉa. ‘moheñco-tarō makkaḷ viruntil viruppuṭaiyar eṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu. ātalāl, avarkaḷ acīraṇattāl tuṉpuṟṟaṉarātal iyalpē. avarkaḷaik kuṇappaṭutta makaḷir cila maruttuva muṟaikaḷai aṟintiruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟal tavaṟākātu’.[14] ‘...iṉṉa piṟavaṟṟāl, iṉṟuḷḷa āyurvēta maruttuvak kalai paṟṟiya mūla uṇarccikaḷ cintuveḷi maruttuvarkaḷiṭamiruntē tōṟṟamāyiṉa eṉṉal naṉku veḷiyām’.[15]

vāṉa nūṟ pulamai

cintu veḷi makkaḷ vīṭukaḷ kaṭṭiyuḷḷa muṟaiyiliruntu irāci kaṇaṅkaḷiṉ iyakkaṅkaḷaik kavaṉittu vantaṉar eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu, muttiraikaḷiṟ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷa cila kuṟiyīṭukaḷ irācikaḷait tām kuṟikkiṉṟaṉar; ammakkaḷatu āṇṭut toṭarcci ēṟakkuṟaiyat tai mātattiliruntē toṭakkamāka iruntatu eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[16] āṉāl, avaṟṟait tiṭṭamākak kūṟakkūṭavillai eṉṟu irāv pakatūr tīkṣit pōṉṟōr kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.[17] hirās pātiriyār, ‘cintu veḷi makkaḷ irāci maṇṭalattai naṉku aṟintavarkaḷ, avarkaḷ kālattil eṭṭu irācikaḷē iruntaṉa, cumēriyar kālattiṟ pattu irācikaḷ kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭaṉa. cintu makkaḷiṉ irāci maṇṭalak kuṟippaik kaṇittup pārkkaiyil, akkālam, ēṟakkuṟaiya ki.mu. 5610 eṉak kūṟalām. itu, cumēriyar nākarikamē tōṉṟāta kālam ākum eṉṟu pēciyuḷḷār.[18]

uṭaṟ payiṟci

harappāviṟ kiṭaitta muttirai oṉṟil or āṭavaṉ uṭaṟ payiṟci ceyvatākap poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉāl, cintuveḷi makkaḷ ōraḷavu uṭaṟpayiṟci muṟaikaḷai aṟintiruntaṉar eṉṟu kūṟutal tavaṟākātu.[19]

nakara makkaḷ

toḻilkaḷaiyum kalaikaḷaiyum nōkka, cintu veḷi nakaraṅkaḷil moḻip pulavar, nimittaṅ kūṟuvōr, kālakkaṇitar, maruttuvar, icaivāṇar, kūttap pulavar, kūtta makaḷir, araciyaṟ makaḷir, araciyaṟ paṇiyāḷar, nakarakkāvalar, camayak kurumār (purōkitr), vēḷāḷar, uṇavup poruḷ viṟpōr, kūla vāṇikar, poṟkollar, kaṉṉār, taccar, mīṉvāṇikar, appa vāṇikar, uppuvāṇikar, marakkalam oṭṭuvōr, vaṇṭi oṭṭuvōr, marakkalam kaṭṭuvōr, vēṭkōvar, irattiṉap paṇiyāḷar, cetukku vēlaiyāḷar, oviyan tīṭṭuvōr, ciṟpikaḷ, kāl naṭai vaḷarppōr, caṅku aṟuppōr, ciṟpa vēlai ceypavar, veṇkal-mākkal mutaliya palavakaik kalvēlai ceyvōr, tantavēlai ceypavar, viḷaiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ ceyvōr, nīrkoṇṭu varupavar, kottarkaḷ, muttirai ceypavar, nāvitar, tōṭṭikaḷ mutaliya pala toḻil puriyum makkaḷ vāḻntu vantaṉar eṉpatait teḷivuṟa aṟiyalām. ivarkaḷ allāmal ekiptu, asiriyā, pāpilōṉiyā, ēlam, cumēriyā, pāracīkam naṭu āciyā, parmā, teṉ intiyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu vāṇipattiṉ poruṭṭuk kuṭiyēṟiyirunta makkaḷ cilarāvar. ātaliṉ, moheñco-tarō ikkālattuk karācci nakaraippōlap pala nāṭṭavaraik koṇṭirunta vāṇikap perunakar ākum: paṇṭaip pūmpukār , koṟkai, muciṟi, toṇṭi pōṉṟa vāṇipa nakaram ākum eṉpatu aṟiyattakkatu. itaṉāṟṟāṉ aṅkuk kiṭaitta elumpuk kūṭukaḷum maṇṭai oṭukaḷum maṅkōliyar, alpaiṉar, matyataraik kaṭaliṉar, āsṭrēliyā iṉattavar ākiya pal nāṭṭu makkaḷuṭaiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.

﻿14. camayanilai

cāṉṟukaḷ

moheñco-tarōvilum harappāvilum kaḷimaṇṇāṟ ceytu cuṭappeṟṟa ciṟiya vaṭivaṅkaḷum muttiraikaḷum ulōkat takaṭukaḷum tāyittukaḷum nirampak kiṭaittaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mūlam akkālattavar tam iṟaivaṇakkattiṟakuriya poruḷkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉavai eṉpatai oruvāṟu aṟital kūṭum.perumpālum ovvoru vīṭṭilum kaṭavuḷ uruvam vaikkap peṟṟu vaḻipaṭap peṟṟatu eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. kaṭavuḷ uruvam vīṭṭuc cuvaril irunta māṭaṅkaḷilēṉum cuvaril marappalakai aṭittu ataṉ mītēṉum vaikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē karutukiṟār.

taraip peṇ vaṇakkam

cintu veḷiyiṟ peṇ vaṭivaṅkaḷ mikap palavākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ivai pōṉṟavai palucistāṉam mutal kirīs varaiyil uḷḷa ellā iṭaṅkaḷilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, paṇṭai makkaḷ, tamakku vēṇṭiya ellā uṇavup poruḷkaḷaiyum utavi vanta taraiyaip peṇ teyvamāka vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar eṉpatu veḷiyākiṟatu.[1] ittaraip peṇ teyvam piṟa nāṭukaḷil piṟa kaṭavuḷaruṭaṉ cērttē vaṇaṅkappaṭṭatu. atteyvattiṟkuk kaṇavaṉ, makaṉ iraṇṭu teyvaṅkaḷ cērkkappaṭṭu iruntaṉa. āṉāl, cintu veḷiyil taraip peṇ tēvatai taṉiyākavē vaṇaṅkappaṭṭatu eṉṉalām. at teyvam, amaitiyāṉa mukat tōṟṟaṅ koṇṭatākavum, accamūṭṭum tōṟṟamuṭaiyatākavum, kuḻantaiyai vaittiruppatu pōlavum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. iṉṟaḷavum civaperumāṉ maṉaiviyārāṉa umaiyammaiyār pārvati, turkkai, kāḷi eṉap pala uruvaṅkaḷil vaḻipaṭappaṭutalaiyum, piṉ iraṇṭu uruvaṅkaḷum accamūṭṭuvaṉavāka iruttalaiyum cintu veḷip peṇ teyva uruvaṅkaḷōṭu oppiṭṭuk kāṇa, taraippeṇ vaṇakkam nāḷaṭaivil cakti vaṇakkamāka māṟiyateṉpatai naṉku uṇaralām.[2]

iṉṟu kirāmantōṟum kāṇappaṭum kirāma tēvataikaḷ aṉaittum taraip peṇṇiṉ piratinitikaḷē eṉṉal poruntum. itteyvaṅkaḷ aṉaittum kaṉṉit taṉmai vāyntavai ākum. ivaṟṟuṭ pala piṟaku ekkāraṇam koṇṭō, āṇ teyvaṅkaḷuṭaṉ iṇaikkappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa. appoḻutu tāṉ ‘cakti vaṇakkam’ ēṟpaṭṭatu. taraip peṇ vaṇakkam iṉṟaḷavum koṇṭarkaḷiṭam iruttalum, avarkaḷ nilamakaḷai (pūmi tēviyai)t taraip peṇ[3] eṉṟē kūṟutalum kāṇa, itu vaṭa intiyāvil irunta paḻan tamiḻar vaḻipāṭu eṉpatai naṉkuṇaralām. taraip peṇ nalla viḷaiccalai nalkun teyvam eṉpatu koṇṭar koḷkai. akkoḷkaiyē cintu veḷi makkaḷiṭattum iruntateṉpatu veḷiyām.

kaviṉ peṟu kaṟpaṉai

taraiyait tāyāka uruvakappaṭutti, atu namakkāka aḷikkum poruḷkaḷaik karuvuyirppatākak kāṭṭum cittiram oṉṟu cintuveḷiyiṟ kiṭaitta muttirai oṉṟil kāṇap paṭukiṟatu. oru peṇ karuvuyirkkum nilaiyil talaikīḻāy niṟkiṟāḷ; avaḷatu karuviliruntu ceṭi oṉṟu veḷippaṭukiṟatu. kuptar kālattiya ittakaiya ōviyaṅkaḷ aikkiya maṇṭilattil uḷḷa ‘pītā’vil kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.

narapali uṇṭā?

oru muttiraiyil oruvaṉ vāḷēnti niṟpatupōlavum avaṉukku aṭiyil oru peṇ muḻaṅkāṟ paṭiyiṭṭu iruppatu pōlavum kāṇappaṭutalai nōkki, ārāycciyāḷar, akkālattil taraip peṇṇukku narapali koṭukkum vaḻakkam iruntatō eṉṟu aiyuṟukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, ivvārāyccikku muṉṉarē aṟiñar palar, paṇṭai intiyāvil narapali iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu tam ārāycci nūlkaḷil varaintuḷḷaṉar.[4] itē paḻakkam āsṭrēliya iṉattavarāṉa koṇṭarkaḷiṭamum 80 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ varai iruntu vantatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. vēṟu oru muttiraiyil, iṟaivi muṉ oruvaṉ veḷḷāṭu oṉṟaip piṭittukkoṇṭu niṟkiṟāṉ; palar varicaiyāka vaḻipaṭa niṟkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl, akkāla makkaḷ vilaṅkukaḷaip paliyiṭum paḻakkamuṭaiyavr eṉpatu pulaṉākum.

talaiyil vicirip pākai

taraip peṇ uruvaṅkaḷ aṉaittum nakaikaḷ nirampap pūṇṭavaiyākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ talaimītu viciṟi pōṉṟa pākaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. araiyil mikac ciṟiya tuṇi cuṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

civa vaṇakkam

cintu veḷi makkaḷ liṅka vaṇakkattiṉar eṉpatai, aṅkuk kiṭaitta pala liṅkaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu aḻuttamākak kūṟalām. iṭakkālait tūkki āṭum naṭarācaratu uruvam kiṭaittuḷḷatu. oru muttiraiyil, mūṉṟumukaṅkaḷaik koṇṭamaṉita uruvam oṉṟu, kālkaḷai maṭakki iru kutikālkaḷum oṉṟu cērntu kāṟ peru viralkaḷ kīḻnōkki uḷḷavāṟu ācaṉamiṭṭu yōkattil amarntiruppatupōlak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. mārpil mukkōṇa vaṭivap patakkaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kaikaḷ niṟaiyak kaṭakaṅkaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṭuppil iraṭṭaip paṭṭaiyāka or araik kaccai kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivvuruvattiṉ valappuṟam yāṉaiyum puliyum, iṭappuṟam erutum kāṇṭāmirukamum niṟkiṉṟaṉa. irukkaiyiṉ aṭiyil iraṇṭu māṉkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa.[5] yōkiyiṉ talaimītu vaḷainta erumaik kompukaḷ uḷa. naṭuvil taṭitta malark kottō ilaik kottō nimirtti vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ‘itu civaṉaik kuṟippatu’ eṉṟu car jāṉ mārṣal karutukiṟār. talaimītuḷḷa kompukaḷum pūṅkottum, piṟkālattil tiricūlamāka māṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar. oru cārār ‘ik kompukaḷ cāñci stūpattiṉ vāyilil poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa cūlattaip pōṉṟuḷḷaṉa. yōkiyai aṭuttu vilaṅkukaḷ iruppatu civaṉ-pacupati eṉpataik kuṟikkiṉṟataṉṟō? mūṉṟu talaikaḷ naṉku terivatāl, piṉpuṟam iraṇṭu talaikaḷ iruttal kūṭum eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vēṟu oru cārār, ‘mūṉṟu talaikaḷum ākkal, aḷittal, aḻittal eṉṉum muttoḻilkaḷaik kuṟippaṉa’ eṉpar. itupōṉṟa muttalai uruvaṅkaḷ ‘āpu’ malaikkarukil aḻintu kiṭakkum kōyilkaḷilum, vaṭa āṟkāṭu jillāvil kāvērip pākkattai aṭutta mēlaic cēriyilum, pelkām kōṭṭattil uḷḷa kōkāk’ nīr vīḻccikku arukilum, utayapuri camastāṉattaic cērnta ‘cittōrkār’ eṉṉum iṭattilum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. āyiṉ, ivai varalāṟṟuk kālattil vaḻakkāṟu aṟṟuppōyiṉa.

liṅka vaḻipāṭu

periya liṅkaṅkaḷum ciṟiya liṅkaṅkaḷum mikap palavākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. harappāvil maṭṭum 600kku mēṟpaṭṭavai kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. paṇṭai makkaḷ ciṟiya liṅkaṅkaḷait tāyittukaḷ pōlak kaḻuttilō kaiyilō kaṭṭiyiruntaṉarātal vēṇṭum:[6] ivaiyaṉṟi muttiraikaḷum, cempu villaikaḷ pōṉṟaṉavum aṇiyappaṭṭu vantaṉa.

puttar perumāṉā? kaṇṇapirāṉā?

muttirai oṉṟil, or uruvam uṭkārntiruppatu pōlavum itaṉ irupuṟaṅkaḷilum varicaiyāka makkaḷ niṉṟu vaṇaṅkuvatu pōlavum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paktartam talaimītu paṭam - virittāṭum pāmpu pōṉṟa talaiyaṇikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. immuttiraiyil uḷḷa kāṭci, ‘cāñci, parhat’ eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷil puttar perumāṉai nāka vamcattu aracarkaḷ vaṇaṅkutal pōlak kāṇappaṭum oviyaṅkaḷai niṉaippūṭṭukiṟatu.[7] kaṇṇapirāṉ, kaliyaṉ eṉṉum nāka aracaṉai veṉṟa piṉ, avaṉum avaṉaic cērntavarum kaṇṇaṉai vaḻipaṭum kāṭciyai immuttiraikaḷil uḷḷa cittiram kuṟikkiṟatu.[8]

kompuḷḷa teyvaṅkaḷ

pacupatiyiṉ talaiyil kāṇappaṭṭa kompukaḷ pōla vēṟupala uruvaṅkaḷiṉ talaikaḷil kompukaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. vēta kālattilum tāca, vētāḷam, cuṣṉē mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷ kompuṭaiyaṉavākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷa. aracar purōkitar mutaliya uyarnilai makkaḷ tam mēmpāṭu tōṉṟak kompukaḷaiyuṭaiya kavacaṅkaḷait talaiyil aṇital paṇṭai marapu. cumēriyāvilum pāpilōṉiyāvilum ittakaiya kavacaṅkaḷ pala kiṭaittaṉa. ittakaiya kavacaṅkaḷ cintu veḷiyilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.[9]

nāṉku kait teyvaṅkaḷ

cintu veḷiyil nāṟkai uruva oviyaṅkaḷ cila citaintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ‘ittakaiya nāṟkai maṉita uruvaṅkaḷē, piṟkālattil āriyar pirammā, viṣṇu, ruttirarkaḷai nāṟkaiyiṉarākak kuṟikkap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa ākalām’ eṉṟu ār.pi. caṇṭā kūṟukiṟār.

camaṇa camayamum paṇṭaiyatō?

cila muttiraikaḷil aṟuvar niṉṟu yōkam ceytal pōlat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa oviyaṅkaḷai nōkkic caṇṭā eṉpavar, “ivai camaṇa yōkikaḷaippōla irukkiṉṟaṉa; vaṭamaturaip poruṭkāṭciccālaiyil uḷḷa ki.pi.iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭaic cērnta camaṇa tēvar cilaikaḷai ottuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. niṟkum nilaiyil yōkam purital camaṇarkkē ciṟappāṉatu. erutu camaṇaratu aṭaiyāḷakkuṟi ākum. cila muttiraikaḷil yōkiyiṉ muṉpuṟam erutu iruttal kuṟippiṭat takkatu. ākavē, ivvuruvam riṣapa tēvaraik kuṟippatākalām. aḵtāyiṉ, caiva camayam pōlavē camaṇa camayamum mikavum paḻaiya camayamākalām” eṉpar. vēṟu cilar, “yōkiyiṉ uruvam civaṉaik kuṟippatu; erutu. nantiyaik kuṟippatu”, eṉpar.

nanti vaḻipāṭu

cintuveḷi vilaṅku vaṇakkattil mutaliṭam peṟṟatu . erutē ākum. yōkikku muṉ timil parutta erutu niṟpatu pōlavum paṭuttiruppatu pōlavum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. oru kōvilukkuḷ uḷḷa ciṟu kōvil muṉ (mūlastāṉattiṉ muṉ) nanti niṟpatu oru muttiraiyiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cintu veḷi makkaḷ vāṇipattiṟ pērutavi purintu vantatu erutē ākum. ātaliṉ, nāḷaṭaivil atu vaṇakkattiṟku uriya vilaṅkāka māṟiviṭṭatu eṉṟu aṟiñar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar. nanti vaṇakkam mutalil taṉiyē iruntu, piṉṉarc civa vaṇakkattuṭaṉ iṇaikkappaṭṭirukkalām eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttu.

oṟṟaik kompu erutu

erutukaḷil iruvakaiyiṉa muttiraikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. oruvakaiyiṉa timil parutta erutukaḷ, maṟṟoru vakaiyiṉa timiliṉṟit talaiyil oṟṟaik kompuṭaiyaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ muṉṉarp piṉṉiya kiḷaikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya maram oṉṟu irukkiṟatu. oṟṟaik kompuṭaiya erutukaḷ vaṇakkattiṉ poruṭṭuk kaṟpikkappaṭṭavai eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ varalāṟu ciṟappuṭaiyatāka irukkalām eṉṟum aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

āṟu talai vilaṅku; katiravak kaṭavuḷ

oru muttiraiyil, orē uṭalil āṟu vevvēṟu vilaṅkut talaikaḷ tōṉṟi iruppatu pōṉṟa uruvam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. inta āṟu talaikaḷuḷ mūṉṟu erutiṉ talaikaḷ ākum. oṉṟu pulittalai, maṟṟa iraṇṭu citaintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai āṟum vaṭṭamākak katiravaṉ katirkaḷai ottirukkumāṟu oṭṭa vaikkappaṭṭu irukkiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟoru tāyittil or erututtalai āṟu katirkaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivvāṟu kāṇappaṭuvatu katiravaṉaik kuṟippatākum eṉṟu aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

kalappu uruvaṅkaḷ

cintu veḷi muttiraikaḷil maṉita vilaṅkukaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ōr uruvam maṉitat talaiyuṭaṉum yāṉai uṭaluṭaṉum erutiṉ kompukaḷuṭaṉum puliyiṉ kālkaḷuṭaṉum vāluṭaṉum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivvuruvam, pala teyvaṅkaḷai - orē vilaṅku uruvamākac cērttuk kāṭṭac ceyyappaṭṭatātal vēṇṭum. erutuk kompukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya maṉita uruvam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. immaṉitavuruviṉ kālkaḷ erutiṉ kālkaḷ pōl uḷa. piṉpuṟam erutiṉ vāl pōṉṟu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. immaṉita uruvam oṟṟaik kompuṭaiya puliyōṭu pōr purivatākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ittakaiya vinōta uruvaṅkaḷ cumēriyāvil nirampak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa; immaṉitar ‘teyva cakti vāyntavar eṉpataiyum, ivar aṭikkaṭi kaṭavuḷarākak karutappaṭṭavaruṭaṉ pōr toṭuttuk koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpataiyum ivai terivikkiṉṟaṉapōlum! maṉitattalai, arai uruvam āṭu, pāti uruvam erutu, pāti uruvam yāṉai pōṉṟa maṉita vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ pala muttiraikaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oru maṉitaṉ pulikaḷōṭu pōr purivatu cila muttiraikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ekiptilum cumēriyāvilum ciṅkaṅkaḷōṭu pōr purivatu kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. oru muttiraiyil peṇṇum puliyum cērnta uruvam oṉṟu poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu. puliyai orutēvataiyākak kuṟippatu pōlum[10] peṇṇuruvattiṉ mutukil talaiyaṟṟa puliyuṭal poruttap peṟṟuḷḷatu. ittakaiya uruvam tēvataiyaik kuṟippatu eṉṟē aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar.

piṟa vilaṅkukaḷ

cintuveḷi makkaḷ nīṇṭa kompukaḷum parutta timilkaḷum uṭaiya erutai vaṇaṅkiyatōṭu erumai, kāṭṭu erumai, yāṉai, vēṅkai, karaṭi, māṉ, kāṇṭāmirukam, muyal, veḷḷiāṭu mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷaiyum vēṟu teyvaṅkaṭkup piratinitikaḷākavō aṭaiyāḷamākavō eṇṇi vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar. harappāvil kiṭaitta muttirai oṉṟil, oru vilaṅku uṭkārntirukkiṟatu; ataṉ muṉṉar niṉṟu or āṭavaṉ kūppiya karaṅkaḷuṭaṉ ataṉai vaḻipaṭukiṟāṉ. niṟka, cintuveḷi makkaḷ veḷḷāṭṭuk kompaic ciṟappuṭaiyatākak karutiṉar pōlum! āṇ teyvat talai mītum pulit tēvataikaḷ talaimītum veḷḷāṭṭuk kompu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.

nāka vaṇakkam

cintuveḷi nākarika kālattiliruntu iṉṟaḷavum nāka vaṇakkam iruntu varukiṟatu. kaḷimaṇ tāyittu oṉṟil, mukkāli oṉṟaṉmītu pāl kiṇṇam irukkiṟatu; ataṉ etiril nākam oṉṟu irukkiṟatu. ituvaṉṟic cila muttiraikaḷil kompu muḷaitta pāmpukaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

puṟā vaṇakkam

maṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa puṟākkaḷ pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avai mecapoṭṭēmiyāvil kiṭaittavaṟṟaiyē peritum ottuḷḷaṉa. taraippeṇ paṭivattiṉ talaimītu iraṇṭu puṟākkaḷ niṟpatupōlac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iccilaikaḷ ekiptilum kirīṭtīvilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē, paṇṭai nākarika nāṭukaḷil ellām taraippeṇtēvataiyuṭaṉ puṟākkaḷ iṇaikkap peṟṟu vaḻipaṭap peṟṟaṉa eṉpatu terikiṟatu.

karuṭa vaṇakkam

tāyittu oṉṟil karuṭap paṟavai paṟappatu pōlap poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉ kālkaḷ maṉitar kālkaḷ pōlak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvuruvattaik koṇṭa tāyittukaḷ moheñco-tarōvilum, harappāvilum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.[11]

mara vaṇakkam

cintuveḷi makkaḷ maraṅkaḷaiyum avaṟṟil uṟaiyum teyvaṅkaḷaiyum vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar eṉpatu moheñco. tarōvilum harappāvilum kiṭaitta cila muttiraikaḷiliruntum villaikaḷiliruntum terikiṟatu. ippaḻakkam tamiḻakattil iṉṟum peruvaḻakku uṭaiyatākum. araca maramē ciṟappuṭaiya vaṇakkattiṟku uriyatu. āṉāl, aḵtu erutuṭaṉ iṇaikkap paṭṭē muttiraikaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. cila muttiraikaḷil vēppamaram kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻ makkaḷ araca, vēppa maraṅkaḷaic cuṟṟutalaiyum iraṇṭu maraṅkaḷaiyum ciṟappuṭai yaṉavākak karututalaiyum iṉṟaḷavum kāṇalām. marattaṭiyil mēṭaiyiṭṭiruttalum akkāla muṟai eṉpatu cila tāyittukaḷiliruntu aṟiyak kiṭakkiṟatu. marattaṭiyil erumai oṉṟu niṟkiṟatu. ataṉ kompukaḷāl tākkuṇṭa oruvaṉ uṭpuṟam vīḻntu kiṭakkiṟāṉ. iṅṅaṉam erumaikaḷōṭu viḷaiyāṭal paṇṭai vaḻakkam ivvaḻakkam kirīṭ tīvilum iruntatām.

mara tēvataikaḷ

cila muttiraikaḷil uḷḷa cittiraṅkaḷ vinōtamākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru marattiṉ iraṇṭu kiḷaikaṭku iṭaiyil āṭaiyiṉṟip peṇ uruvam oṉṟu niṟkiṉṟatu. ataṉ kūntal aviḻntu toṅkukiṟatu. marattaṭiyil āṭaiyaṟṟa maṟṟoru peṇṇuruvam maṇṭiyiṭṭuvaṇṅkukiṟatu. marattiṉmītu niṟkum peṇṇuruvattiṉ talaiyil iraṇṭu kompukaḷum avaṟṟukku iṭaiyil malarkkottum irukkiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭu uruvaṅkaḷiṉ kaikaḷ nirampa vaḷaiyalkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvuruvaṅkaṭku etiril ciṟiya kuṭṭaip pāvāṭaikaḷ kaṭṭiya peṇṇuruvaṅkaḷ ēḻu varicaiyāka niṟkiṉṟaṉa. maṇṭiyiṭṭu vaṇaṅkum peṇṇuruviṟkup piṉpuṟam maṉitattalaiyuṭaṉ araiyuruvam āṭākavum maṟupāti erutākavum uḷḷa vilaṅkuruvam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itu, mara tēvataiyiṉ ūrtiyāka irukkalām

oru marattiṟku muṉṉar erutiṉ kompukaḷaiyuṭaiya puli oṉṟai, vāl - erutiṉ kālkaḷ - erutiṉ talai - maṉita uṭal ivai koṇṭa vinōta vilaṅkoṉṟu etirppatāka uḷḷa tōṟṟam oru muttiraiyil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. vēṟoru muttiraiyil iraṇṭu maraṅkaḷ mītu iruvar uṭkārntiruppatu pōlak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avartam talaikaḷil mara muṭi kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. iṅṅaṉam kuṟikkappaṭum palavakai uruvaṅkaḷ mara tēvataikaḷ eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar. ‘maraṅkaḷil maṉita āvikaḷ taṅkutal uṇṭu’ eṉṉum koḷkai akkālamakkaṭku uṇṭu pōlum! maratēvataikaḷuṭaṉ pulikaḷē toṭarpu paṭuttappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, mara tēvataikaṭkum pulikaṭkum ētō toṭarpu iruttal vēṇṭum. itaṉāl, mara vaṇakkam akkālattil ciṟappuṭaiyatāka iruntatu eṉpatu naṉku veḷiyākiṉṟataṉṟō?

koṇṭar iravil maramacaikka añcuvar; maratēvataikaḷ maraṅkaḷil uṟaṅkum, irāk kālattil maram acaittalō paḻaṅkaḷaip paṟittalō avaṟṟiṟku ākātu eṉpatu avar koḷkaimarattai veṭṭupavar. mutalilmarattiṟkup pūcai iṭṭa piṉṉarē veṭṭat toṭaṅkutal iṉṟaḷavum uḷḷa paḻakkamākum.cilaceṭikaṭku mantiram kūṟik kāppukkaṭṭiya piṉṉarē ilai kaḷaiyō, paḻaṅkaḷaiyō paṟippatu vaḻakkam. cilar marattiṟku mutalil tāli kaṭṭiya piṟakē peṇṇukkut tāli kaṭṭutal vaḻakkam.

oru muttiraiyil iru uruvaṅkaḷ iraṇṭu maraṅkaḷai naṭuvataṟku ēnti niṟkiṉṟaṉa. avaikaṭku iṭaiyil maratēvatai uruvam oṉṟu niṟkiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam maraṅkaḷai naṭutal tūya ceyal eṉṟu nammavar niṉaittal neṭuṅkālap paḻakkamākum.

āṟṟu vaṇakkam

vāyil ciṟu mīṉkaḷai vaittuḷḷa mutalai, āmai pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷai āṟukaḷuṭaṉ cērttup poṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷa muttiraikaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟāl akkāla makkaḷ āṟṟu vaṇakkam[12] ceytu vantaṉar eṉpatai naṉku aṟiyalām.

pali iṭum paḻakkam

cintuveḷi makkaḷ mara tēvataikaṭkum piṟa kaṭavuḷarkkum veḷḷāṭu, erumai mutaliyavaṟṟaip pali yiṭṭaṉar. cintu veḷiyiṟ perumpālum veḷḷāṭē paliyiṭap paṭṭatām.[13]

tāyittu aṇital

maṇ tāyittukkaḷē makkaḷāl peritum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. avai tēyntu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tāyittukkaḷil kāṇappaṭum uruvaṅkaḷ camaya campantamāṉa pala nikaḻccikaḷaik kūṟuvaṉavākalām. aṇil, cemmaṟiyāṭu, muyal, māṉ, nāy ākiya vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ - kal, pīṅkāṉ, veṇkalam, cuṭṭa kaḷimaṇ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ villaikaḷil poṟikkappaṭṭu mēlē tuḷaiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai kayiṟu koṇṭu kaṭṭik koḷḷap payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. puṟāvaṭivilāṉa tāyittukkaḷum aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa. talaippil muṭiyiṭappeṟṟa cippiyālāṉa tāyittu oṉṟu mantira cakti uṭaiyatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. iṅṅaṉam muṭiyiṭutal mantira vaṉmai uṭaiyateṉṟu mēṟkup puṟa nāṭukaḷilum appaḻaṅkālattil karutappaṭṭatām. attāyittiṉ naṭuvil iraṇṭu ciṟiya tuḷaikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, atu pottāṉākavō tāyittākavō payaṉpaṭṭirukkalām. cila tāyittukkaḷil aṭi-muṭi aṟṟa varivaṭivaṅkaḷ cumēriyar muttiraikaḷai ottuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maṇikaḷait tāyittuk kaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkamum cintu veḷi makkaḷiṭai iruntatu. ippaḻakkam mēṟkup puṟa nāṭukaḷilum iruntatākum.

cila tāyittukkaḷil svastikā kirēkkac ciluvai pōṉṟa kuṟikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai iraṇṭum ēlam, cumēriyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil irunta paṇṭaik kuṟiyīṭukaḷē ākum. svastikā’ kuṟi yōkak kuṟiyāka innāṭṭil iṉṟaḷavum karutappaṭukiṟatu. ikkuṟikaḷ cintu veḷi illac cuvarkaḷ mītum iruntaṉa.

camayap patakkam

camaya campantamāṉa patakkaṅkaḷum paṇṭaikkālac cintuveḷi makkaḷāl aṇiyappaṭṭu vantaṉa. vaṭṭappiḷavu pōṉṟa oru patakkattil, oṟṟaik kompuṭaiya. vilaṅkoṉṟu niṟkiṟatu. ataṉ etiril palipīṭam oṉṟu irukkiṟatu. piṟaiyiṉ muṉaikaḷ muṭiyum iṭattil nīḷ caturavaṭivam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ituvum vilaṅku vaṭivamum naṭuvil kuṭaiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ tuḷaikaḷil civappuc cāntu pūcappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. ap patakkam ulōka vaḷaiyattil poruttap peṟṟuk kōvil atikārikaḷāl makkaṭku aḷikkappaṭum ciṟappuṭaip poruḷāka iruntiruttal kūṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. uṇmai uṇarntavar yāvar?

kaṭavuḷ uruvaṅkaḷiṉ ūrvalam

camayak kuṟikaḷ eṉak karutappaṭum erutu mutaliya vilaṅku kaḷai makkaḷ tūkkic celvatu iraṇṭu muttiraikaḷil kāṇap paṭukiṟatu. oruvaṉ oru kampattil erutu uruvattai vaittut tukkic celkiṟāṉ. avaṉ piṉṉar, vēṟu oruvaṉ palipīṭam allatu toṭṭi pōṉṟa oṉṟait tūkkiccelkiṟāṉ.mūṉṟām maṉitaṉ vēṟu or uruvattaic cumantu celkiṟāṉ. ivvūrvalak kāṭci, iṉṟaik kōvil viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil tūkki varappaṭum nanti vāhaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟai niṉaippūṭṭukiṉṟatu aṉṟō?



nērttik kaṭaṉ

karuppavatikaḷaik kuṟikkum patumaikaḷ, kaik kuḻantai ēnti niṟkum tāyaik kuṟikkum patumaikaḷ, tavaḻntu cellum kuḻantaikaḷaik kuṟikkum patumaikaḷ mutaliyaṉavum vilaṅkup patumaikaḷum nērttik kaṭaṉ poruṭṭuk kōvilkaḷil vaikkappeṟṟaṉavāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. ip paḻakkam iṉṟaḷavum iruntu varukiṉṟatu kavaṉikkat takkatu. iṅṅaṉam celuttappaṭṭa nērttikkaṭaṉ poruḷkaḷ ellāk kōvilkaḷilum iruttalaik kāṇalām.

icaiyum naṭaṉamum

camayat toṭarpuṭaiya kaḷimaṇ tāyittu oṉṟil cila uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oruvaṉ mattaḷam aṭikkiṟāṉ; cilar avvōcaik kēṟpa naṭikkiṉṟaṉar. tāyittuc camayat toṭarpuṭaiyatāka iruttaliṉ, atil kuṟikkappaṭṭa naṭaṉamum camayat toṭarpuṭaiyatāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu poruttamē aṉṟō? harappāvil kiṭaitta tāyittu oṉṟil, puli muṉ oruvaṉ mattaḷam aṭippatu pōlap poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. vēṟoru muttiraiyil erutiṉ muṉpu maṅkai orutti kūttāṭutal kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. nāṭṭiya makaḷaik kuṟikkum veṇkalap paṭivam oṉṟu harappāvil kiṭaittatu. ataṉ vēlaip pāṭṭaik kaṇṭu viyavāta ārāycciyāḷar illai. accilai kōyil naṭaṉamātiṉ cilaiyāka irukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñar cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar.[14]

paṭaittal paḻakkam

kaṭavuḷar uruvaṅkaḷiṉ muṉ uṇavupporuḷkaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum paṭaittal akkāla makkaḷiṉ paḻakkamāka iruntatu eṉpatu cila tāyittukkaḷ koṇṭum muttiraikaḷ koṇṭum kūṟalām; taṇṭiṉmītu poruttappeṟṟa taṭṭukaḷ (Offering Stands) pala kiṭaittamai koṇṭum kūṟalām; oru muttiraiyil peṇkaḷ palar paṭaikkut taṭṭukaḷai ēnti niṟpatākac cittiram tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.[15] ‘harappāvil kōvil aṇikaḷ cila kiṭaittaṉa; avai caṅku, kaḷimaṇ, kal, uyartarak kal mutaliyavaṟṟāl ceyyappeṟṟavai. avai kōvilkaḷukku uriyaṉavākalām eṉṟu em.es.vāṭs eṉṉum aṟiñar aṟaikiṉṟār.[16]

kōvil vaḻipāṭu

moheñco-tarōvil peḷatta stūpiyuḷḷa iṭattiṉ aṭiyil - iṉṟaḷavum tōṇṭip pārāta iṭattil cintu veḷi makkaḷiṉ kōvil irukkalām eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar. ‘pala kōvilkaḷ aḻiyum poruḷkaḷaik koṇṭu kaṭṭappeṟṟamaiyāl, aḻintu viṭṭiruttal iyalpu. avai iṉṟu iṉmaikoṇṭē kōvilkaḷ iruntila eṉak kūṟal iyalātu’ eṉṟu ṭākṭar makkē pōṉṟa aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. uyaramāṉa kōvil kaḷaik kaṭṭi vāḻnta cumēriyarōṭu vāṇipat toṭarpum piṟa pala tuṟaikaḷil toṭarpum koṇṭirunta cintu veḷi makkaḷ kōvilkaḷ iṉṟi vāḻntaṉar eṉpatu poruntāk kūṟṟākum. eṉavē, kōvilkaḷ iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatil aiyamillai eṉpatu aṟiñar karuttākum.

cintu veḷic camayam yātu?

cintu veḷi makkaḷ, (1) taraippeṇ vaṇakkam (2) liṅka-yōṉi vaṇakkam (3) civa vaṇakkam (4) nanti vaṇakkam (5) cūriya vaṇakkam (6) vilaṅku vaṇakkam (7) paṟavai vaṇakkam (8) nāka vaṇakkam (9) mara vaṇakkam (10) āṟṟu vaṇakkam (11) ciṟu teyva vaṇakkam (12) vinōta-teyva vaṇakkam (13) kaṇṇaṉ vaṇakkam ivaṟṟai uṭaiyavar eṉṟu oruvāṟu kūṟalām; mēlum avarkaḷ, (1) camayat toṭarpāṉa tāyittukkaḷaiyum muttiraikaḷaiyum patakkaṅkaḷaiyum aṇintu vantārkaḷ, (2) paliyiṭṭu vantārkaḷ, (3) palavakaip poruḷkaḷaip paṭaittu vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ, (4) kaṭavuḷukku viḻākkaḷai naṭatti vantārkaḷ, (5) nērttik kaṭaṉ celutti vantārkaḷ, (6) camayat toṭarpāka icaiyum naṭaṉamum vaḷarttārkaḷ, (7) mantira māyaṅkaḷil paḻaki iruntārkaḷ, (8) yōkap payiṟciyai naṉku aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉpavaṟṟai oruvāṟu uṇaralām.

curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, iṉṟu hintu matattil uḷḷa perumpālāṉa vaḻipāṭukaḷum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum cintu veḷi makkaḷuṭaiya camayattaṉavē eṉak kūṟi muṭittal tavaṟākātu.

caivattiṉ paḻaimai

ippala vakai vaḻipāṭukaḷ iṉṟum irukkiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum, ivai aṉaittaiyum. ikkāla hintukkaḷ aṉaivarum piṉpaṟṟutal illai aṉṟō? uyarntavar civaliṅkam, kaṇṇaṉ mutaliya perun teyvaṅkaḷaiyē vaḻipaṭukiṉṟaṉar. kirāmattavarum pāmara makkaḷumē maravaṇakkam mutaliyavaṟṟaik kaikkoṇṭuḷḷaṉar. iṅṅaṉamē, appaṇṭaik kālac cintu veḷi makkaḷuḷ kalvi kēḷvikaḷāl uyarnta perumakkaḷ peruṅ kaṭavuḷarai vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar? tāḻnta nilaiyil iruntavarkaḷ palavakait teyvaṅkaḷai vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉarātal vēṇṭum. iḵtu eṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, ivvārāycciyāl aṟiyappaṭum uṇmai oṉṟuṇṭu. aḵtāvatu, caiva camayam cintuveḷi nākarikattukkum (ki.mu.4000kku) muṟpaṭṭa paḻaimaiyuṭaiyatu eṉpatē ākum. aḵtu iṉṉum evvaḷavu paḻaimaiyuṭaiya teṉpatai varaiyaṟukkakkūṭavillai. accamayam ulakattilēyē mikka paḻaimai vāyntu, iṉṟaḷavum iruntuvarum camayam eṉpatai maṟattal ākātu.[17]

↑ koṇṭarum kōyikaḷum (kōtāvari, mattiya mākāṇam orissā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa malaivāṇar) iṉṟum tam naṭaṉattiṉpōtu talaippākaikaḷil erumaik kompukaḷaic ceruki vaittuk koṇṭu āṭutal marapu - Thurston’s ‘Castes and Tribes ofS. India’, Vo!. IV, p.61

↑ viyākrapātamuṉivar uruvam iṅkuc cintikkaṟ pālatu. āsṭrēliya iṉattavarāṉa koṇṭar, iṟantavaṉaippaṟṟic ceyyum iraṇṭām nāṭ caṭaṅkil, avaṉai eritta iṭattil paṭaiyal iṭṭu avaṉatu āviyai viḷittu. ‘nī picācākavō puliyākavō vantu eṅkaḷait tuṉpuṟuttalākātu’ eṉṟu vēṇṭuvatu iṉṟum vaḻakkam eṉpatai aṟika. mēlum akkoṇṭarkaḷ. tām tam pakaivarai aḻikkum poruṭṭup puliyākavō pāmpāvō urumāṟum āṟṟal peṟṟavar eṉpatai nampukiṉṟaṉar; irākkālaṅkaḷil tūṅkumpoḻutu āvi veḷic ceṉṟu cuṟṟuvatāka nampukiṉṟaṉar; atu puliyuruvil alaivatāka nampukiṉṟaṉar; ciṟuttaippuvi teyvattaṉmai yuṭaiyateṉpatai nampukiṉṟaṉar; pulittōl mītu kai aṭittuc cattiyam ceykiṉṟaṉar. ‘nāṉ ikkuṟṟam ceytavaṉāyiṉ. puliyāl kollap paṭuvēṉ ākuka’ eṉac cūḷ uraikkiṉṟaṉar. kōtāvari jillāvil uḷḷa kōyikaḷ eṉṉum malaivāṇar tam tēvarāṭṭi iravil pulimītu ēṟikkoṇṭu ēḻu kirāmaṅkaḷaic cuṟṟuvatāka nampappaṭukiṟāḷ. mēlum, “avaḷ iravil puli vēṭaṅ koḷvāḷ; oru kāl maṉitar kālāka irukkum. annilaiyil avaḷ ‘maruṭpuli’ eṉappaṭuvāḷ; iravil taṉiyē varum āḷmītu pāyntu accuṟuttikkolvāṉ” eṉṟu avaḷaippaṟṟik kōyikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. -'Thurston’s ‘Castes and Tribes of S.India’, Vol.III pp.395, 405, 406, 407; and Vol. IV, pp. 69, 70. ivvivaraṅkaḷai nōkkac cintuveḷimakkaḷ puliyai vaṇaṅki vantamaikkuriya karuttait teṟṟeṉat teḷiyalām aṉṟō?

↑ ikkaruṭavaṇakkam piṟkālattil tirumāl vaṇakkattōṭu iṇaippuṇṭu viṭṭatu.eṉiṉum, hintukkaḷ karuṭaṉaik kāṇumpoḻu tellām vaṇaṅkutalai iṉṟum vaḻakkamākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉar.

↑ āṟṟil putuveḷḷam varumpoḻutu āṇṭukkoru muṟai āṟṟai vaṇaṅkutal paṇṭait tamiḻar marapu eṉpataip paripāṭalāl aṟika.

↑ murukapperumāṉukku āṭṭaippaliyiṭal paṇṭait tamiḻar marapu. itaṉait tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭaiyiṟ kāṇka, kāḷi tēvikku erumaip pali iṭutal iṉṟum kāṇalām.

↑ tamiḻakattil kōvil naṭaṉamātar iruntaṉar. irukkiṉṟaṉar: avarkaṭku ‘māṉiyam’ uṇṭu. viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil kōvil nikaḻccikaḷiṟ kalantukoṇṭu icai pāṭalum naṭittalum avartam toḻil. avarkaḷ kūttāṭiyā eṉappaṭuvar. avar paramparaiyiṉar iṉṟum kapistalam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil irukkiṉṟaṉar.

↑ M.S.Vats’s ‘Excavations at Harappa’, Vol. I, p.299

↑ Ibid p.443.

↑ “Among the many revelations that Mohenjo-Daro and Harappa have had in store for us, none perhaps is more remarkable than this discovery that, Saivism has a history going back to the Chalcolithic Age or perhaps even further still, and it thus takes its place as the most ancient living faith in the world”, - Sir John Marshall in his preface to Mohenjo-Daro and the Indus Civilization, ‘Vol, I. P. vii.





* * *





﻿15. iṭutalum cuṭutalum

cuṭutal

cintuveḷi makkaḷ iṟantārai eṉ ceytaṉar eṉpatu moheñco-tarō ārāycciyai maṭṭuṅ koṇṭu tiṭṭamākak kūṟutaṟkillai. harappā ārāycciyaiyum uḷaṅ koṇṭu nōkkiṉ, cila vivaraṅkaḷai aṟiyalām. eṉiṉum, it tuṟaikkuriya ārāyccip poruḷkaḷ ekiptilum cumēriyāvilum kiṭaittaṉa pōlap pēraḷavu cintuveḷiyiṟ kiṭaittila. moheñco-tarōvil camātiyō, iṭukāṭō kiṭaittila, āyiṉ, cantukaḷil, vīṭukaṭku aṭiyil nīr aruntum kuvaḷaikaṭkuḷ irunta eritta cāmpalum elumput tuṇṭukaḷum ērāḷamākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟai nōkka, cintu veḷi makkaḷ iṟantār uṭalai erittuc cāmpalaiyum elumpukaḷaiyum cēmittup putaittu vantaṉar eṉpatu teḷivākiṟatu. ippaḻakkam iṉṟum hintukkaḷiṭai iruntu varuvatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.

iṭutal

harappāvil kiṭaitta cavak kuḻikaḷil iraṇṭu mūṉṟu aṭukkukaḷ uṇṭu. mikavum aṭiyil irunta cavakkuḻikaḷil piṇaṅkaḷai nērākap paṭukkavaittuk kaikaḷai mārpiṉ mītu maṭakkivaittup putaittu vantaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. nilattiṟkaṭiyil putaikkappaṭṭa uṭalkaḷ mūṉṟu vakaiyāka aṭakkam ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; (1) cila uṭalkaḷ taṉittaṉiyē iṭam viṭṭup putaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; (2) cila nerukkamākap putaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; (3) cila oṉṟiṉkīḻ oṉṟākap putaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟu piṇaṅkaḷaip putaikkum paḻakkam palucistāṉattilum peruvaḻakkuṭaiyatāka iruntatu. avvuṭalkaḷiṉ arukil nīruṭaiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, kōppaikaḷ, taṭṭukaḷ, iṟantār payaṉpaṭuttiya piṟa poruḷkaḷ mutaliyaṉa putaikkappaṭṭaṉa. ippaḻakkam cumēriyāvilum ekiptilum ciṟappāka iruntu vantatu. itē paḻakkam, putukkōṭṭai, āticcanallūr mutaliya tamiḻnāṭṭup pakutikaḷiṟ kiṭaitta tāḻikaḷaik koṇṭu, tamiḻnāṭṭilum iruntateṉat tuṇintu kūṟalām.

īrāṉiyar paḻakkam

mēl aṭukkukaḷiṟ kiṭaitta cavak kuḻiyil taṉittaṉi uṭaṟpakutikaḷum elumpukaḷum putaikkappaṭṭaṉa pōlum! iṅṅaṉam uṭaṟpakutikaḷaip putaittuvantavar muṉ collap paṭṭavariṉum vēṟāṉavarātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar karuttu. iṅṅaṉam uṭaṟpakutikaḷ putaikkappaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ harappāvil nūṟṟukku mēlākak kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. āyiṉ, ivaṟṟil uḷḷa maṇṭai oṭukaḷaiyum piṟa elumpukaḷaiyum kāṇkaiyil, uṭaṟ pakutikaḷaik kaḻuku, nari mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkup pōṭṭuc cataippakutikaḷ tiṉṉappaṭṭapiṟaku, elumpukaḷai maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷil iṭṭup putaittu vantaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. ippaḻakkam pāracīkariṭamē celvākkup peṟṟatātaliṉ, īrāṉiyar harappāvil kuṭiyēṟi iruntaṉar; annakara makkaḷuḷ īrāṉiyarum oru pakutiyiṉarāka iruntaṉar eṉpatai aṟiyalām.

tāḻikaḷ mītu oviyaṅkaḷ

ittāḻikaḷ ellām oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟaṉa. ovvoṉṟilum vitavitamāṉa oviyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru tāḻiyiṉ kaḻuttarukil karuṭap paṟavaikaḷ[1] iraṇṭu varicaikaḷil paṟappaṉa pōlat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avvaricaikaṭku iṭaiyē ilaikkottuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭum toṭṭikaḷ aṇiyaṇiyāka vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. piṟa tāḻikaḷ mītu, cempaṭavaṉ valai pōṭṭu mīṉ piṭippatu pōlavum, mīṉ āmai paṟavaikaḷ. ilaikaḷ mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷaip pōlavum cittiraṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

mayilkaḷ teyvikattaṉmai peṟṟavaiyā?

akkāla makkaḷ mayilkaḷait teyvikac ciṟappuṭaiya paṟavaikaḷāka matittaṉar eṉpatu terikiṟatu. immayilkaḷiṉ tuṇaiyaik koṇṭu maṉitar āvikaḷ mēl ulakam pōvaṉa eṉpatai akkālattavar eṇṇi vantaṉar pōlum! aṉṟic cūkkum uṭalē mayilāka uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭatō aṟiyōm. oru tāḻi mītuḷḷa cittiraṅkaḷil mūṉṟu mayilkaḷ paṟappatu pōlat tiṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ammayilkaṭku iṭaiyiṭaiyē viṇmīṉkaḷ varaiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ātaliṉ, ‘mayilkaḷ cūkkuma uṭalkaḷaik kuṟippaṉa: avai mēl ulakattai aṭaivatākap paṇṭaiyōr eṇṇiṉar’ eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar aṟaikiṉṟaṉar.[2] cila tāḻikaḷ mītu ciṟiya mayilkaḷ aṇiyaṇiyāka iruppaṉapōla oviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vēṟu cilavaṟṟiṉ mītu aintu mayilkaḷ paṟappaṉa pōlak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila tāḻikaḷ mītu kāṇappaṭum mayilkaḷiṉ tōkai tiricūlam pōlak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. cila mayilkaḷiṉ talaimītu iraṇṭu kompukaḷum avaṟṟukku iṭaiyil nimirnta malarkkottum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

paṟavai muka maṉita uruvaṅkaḷ

cila tāḻikaḷ mītu paṟavai mukkup pōṉṟa nīṇṭa mūkkukaḷaiyuṭaiya maṉita uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai, mayilkaḷ varaiyappaṭṭa karuttuk koṇṭē varaiyappaṭṭaṉavākalām: aḵtāvatu cūkkuma uṭampaiccumantu cellum vaṉmai vāyntavaiyākalām eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. oru tāḻimītu paṟavai mukkuṭaiya maṉita uruvam tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. avvuruvam iṭakkaiyil ampum villum vaittuḷḷatu. ataṉ irupuṟamum iraṇṭu erutukaḷ niṟkiṉṟaṉa. avverutukaḷaik kayiṟu koṇṭu kaṭṭi, avaṟṟiṉ kayiṟṟai maṉita vuruvam taṉ valakkaiyil piṭittuḷḷatu. iṭappuṟamuḷḷa erutai nāyoṉṟu turattivantu vālaip piṭittuk kaṭittu iḻukkiṟatu,nāykkup piṉ, talaimītu kompu muḷaitta mayilkaḷ iraṇṭu paṟakkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṟku arukil periya veḷḷāṭu oṉṟu niṟkiṉṟatu. ataṉ kompukaḷ eṭṭut tiricūlaṅkaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. veḷḷāṭṭiṟkum piṟa uruvaṅkaṭkum iṭaiyil ilaikaḷum viṇmīṉkaḷum varaiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṅṅaṉam pala uruvaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappeṟṟa cittiram, ‘kālaṉ, uyiraik kavarntu celvataik kuṟippatākalām’ eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar. paṟavai mukattaik koṇṭa maṉita uruvaṅkaḷait tīṭṭum paḻakkam paṇṭaik kirēkkariṭamum mecopoṭṭēmiyariṭamum iruntatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.[3]

tāḻikaḷuṭ pala poruḷkaḷ

iṟantavar aṇintirunta aṇikaḷum payaṉpaṭuttiya pāṇṭaṅkaḷum piṟa poruḷkaḷum uṇavup poruḷkaḷum tāḻikaḷil iruntu eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟai ellām iṟantavar cāmpaluṭaṉ tāḻikaḷil iṭṭup putaittuviṭal appaṇṭai makkaḷ marapu eṉpatu veḷiyākiṟatu. tāḻikaḷil cūḷaiyiṭap peṟṟa maṇ appaṅkaḷ kiṭaittataik kuṟippatākak kūṟalām. pala tāḻikaḷil taṅkanakaikaḷ akappaṭṭaṉa: navarattiṉaṅkaḷ kiṭaittaṉa. iṅṅaṉam tāḻikaḷil iṟantār aṇikaḷaip putaittal paṇṭai mēṟkuppuṟa nāṭukaḷilum iruntuvanta paḻakkamē ākum.

uṭaṉ iṟakkum vaḻakkam

oru piṇaik kuḻiyiṟ kiṭaitta muttirai oṉṟil, kaṭṭilmītu maṅkai orutti cāyntukoṇṭu iruppatu pōla, uruvam cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itaṉāl iṟanta āṭavaṉuṭakkuriya maṉaivi kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ iṟakkum vaḻakkam ak kālattil iruntirukkalām eṉṟu aṟiñar karutukiṉṟaṉar; ‘iṟantavar aṭutta vāḻkkaiyil ippiṟaviyil iruntavāṟē irukka viḻaintaṉar; avviḻaivāṟṟāṉ maṉaiviyum uṭaṉ iṟantavaḷātal vēṇṭum[4] eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.

muṭivu

moheñco-tarō, harappā mutaliyaṉa ciṟanta nākarikamum celvap perukkamum uṭaiya nakaraṅkaḷ; vāṇipap pukaḻpeṟṟa nakaraṅkaḷ. ātaliṉ, avaṟṟil pala nāṭṭu makkaḷ kuṭipukuntiruntaṉar. avaravar tattam nāṭṭup paḻakkattiṟkēṟpa iṟantār uṭalaip putaittum, erittum, nāy narikaṭku iṭṭum vantaṉar eṉpatai harappāviṟ kiṭaitta putaikuḻi vivaraṅkaḷāl naṉku aṟiyalām. appalavakai aṭakka muṟaikaḷ iṉṟum ceṉṉai, kalkattā, pampāp pōṉṟa vāṇipap perukkamuṭaiya nakaraṅkaḷil palanāṭṭu makkaḷ uṟaikiṉṟa nakaraṅkaḷil kāṇalām.[5] eṉiṉum, cintuveḷi makkaṭ keṉṟē ciṟappāka irunta paḻakkam iṭutalum cuṭutalumē eṉpatai harappāvil kiṭaitta muḻuvuṭal putai muṟaiyālum (avaiyē āḻattil putaikkappaṭṭavai). moheñco-tarōvil vīṭukaṭkaṭiyil kiṭaitta cāmpal koṇṭa maṭkalaṅkaḷālum naṉkaṟiyalām. mēlum, cintu veḷi makkaḷ maṟupiṟavi uṇarcci uṭaiyaṉarāka iruntaṉar eṉpatum aṟiyattakkatu.[6]





* * *





↑ iṉṟum karuṭaṉai vaḻipaṭal kavaṉikkattakkatu. itu paṇṭaik kālattiruntu iṉṟaḷavum toṭarntu varum vaḻipāṭākum.

↑ mayilēṟi viḷaiyāṭu kukaṉē eṉa varum aṭiyum, mayil murukaṉait tāṅka muṭiyāta paṟavaiyāka iruntum, karuṭaṉaip pōla ūrtiyākak kūṟappaṭṭataṉ uṭkaruttup paṇṭaiyōr nampikkaiyai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭatē pōlum! naṭuvīṭṭil āṇmayilaippōla oviyam eḻuti ataṉai maṭṭum vaḻi paṭum vaḻakkam iṉṟun tamiḻnāṭṭil uṇṭu. ataṉai mayilēṟu viḻā eṉpar.

↑ 'cātal añcēṉ: añcuval cāvil

piṟappuppiṟi tākuva tāyiṉ
